      if (btn && btn.textContent !== 'Commitment') btn.textContent = 'Commitment';
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  function wa55Watch() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel) return;
    if (!panel.dataset.w55mo) {
      panel.dataset.w55mo = '1';
      var mo = new MutationObserver(function () { wa55Rename(); });
      mo.observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
    wa55Rename();
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp55 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp55(id);
      try { if (id === 'hrcentre') wa55Watch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa55 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa55();
      try { wa55Watch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v56 gather hours on demand
   (03/08/26)
   The Commitment tab gets an update bar: when the hours were last
   gathered from Slack, and an "Update hours from Slack" button that
   runs the hours-pull right now (instead of waiting for the schedule),
   waits for the fresh numbers to land, reloads them, and re-renders
   the dashboard.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa56) return;
  window.__wa56 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa56css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w56bar{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:12px;color:#797773;margin:0 0 8px;}' +
    '[data-w56go]{display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 13px;' +
    'font-size:11.5px;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;line-height:1.5;}' +
    '[data-w56go]:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.4);}' +
    '[data-w56go][disabled]{opacity:.6;cursor:default;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  function wa56MaxUpd() {
    var top = null;
    (typeof DB_HOURS !== 'undefined' ? DB_HOURS : []).forEach(function (h) {
      if (h.updated_at && (!top || h.updated_at > top)) top = h.updated_at;
    });
    return top;
  }
  function wa56When(iso) {
    if (!iso) return 'not yet';
    try {
      var d = new Date(iso);
      return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) + ' '
        + d.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    } catch (e) { return String(iso).slice(0, 16); }
  }

  function wa56Ensure() {
    try {
      var sec = document.getElementById('w52hours');
      if (!sec || document.getElementById('w56bar')) return;
      sec.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',
        '<div class="w56bar" id="w56bar"><span>Hours last gathered from Slack: <b style="color:#1F201E">' + wa56When(wa56MaxUpd()) + '</b></span>'
        + '<button data-w56go="1">Update hours from Slack</button>'
        + '<span style="font-size:11px">also runs automatically on the schedule</span></div>');
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  async function wa56Top() {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('slack_hours').select('updated_at').order('updated_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1);
      return (r && r.data && r.data[0] && r.data[0].updated_at) || null;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }

  var WA56_BUSY = false;
  async function wa56Run(btn) {
    if (WA56_BUSY) return;
    WA56_BUSY = true;
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = 'Updating from Slack...';
    var before = await wa56Top();
    var invoked = true;
    try { await db.functions.invoke('hours-pull', { body: {} }); } catch (e) { invoked = false; }
    var fresh = false;
    if (invoked) {
      var deadline = Date.now() + 100000;
      while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        await new Promise(function (res) { setTimeout(res, 6000); });
        var top = await wa56Top();
        if (top && top !== before) { fresh = true; break; }
      }
    }
    /* reload the hours window the app uses (same as loadAll) */
    try {
      var rr = await db.from('slack_hours').select('*').gte('week_start', new Date(Date.now() - 42 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10));
      if (rr && rr.data) DB_HOURS = rr.data;
    } catch (e) { /* keep old data */ }
    WA56_BUSY = false;
    toast(fresh ? 'Hours updated from Slack' : (invoked ? 'The Slack pull is still finishing, check back in a minute' : 'Could not reach the Slack pull'));
    /* re-render the tab through the normal path (v52 rebuild, v54 rewrite) */
    var tab = document.querySelector('.w47tab[data-w47v="hours"]');
    var sec = document.getElementById('w52hours');
    if (tab && sec && sec.style.display !== 'none') tab.click();
  }

  if (!document.body.dataset.wa56Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa56Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var go = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w56go]');
      if (go) { wa56Run(go); return; }
      var tb = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w47tab');
      /* after v52 rebuilds and v54 rewrites, put the bar back */
      if (tb && tb.getAttribute('data-w47v') === 'hours') setTimeout(wa56Ensure, 0);
    });
  }
  function wa56Watch() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel) return;
    if (!panel.dataset.w56mo) {
      panel.dataset.w56mo = '1';
      var mo = new MutationObserver(function () { wa56Ensure(); });
      mo.observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
    wa56Ensure();
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp56 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp56(id);
      try { if (id === 'hrcentre') wa56Watch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa56 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa56();
      try { wa56Watch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v57 bulk training assignment + folded onboarding subtasks
   (03/08/26)
   1. Bulk assign trainings: a "Bulk assign" flow in the HR centre
      Trainings dashboard. Pick the audience (specific people with a
      searchable list, a whole team, or the whole organization), pick
      the trainings, and everything lands in one shot. People who
      already have a training keep their record (done stays done,
      nothing duplicates); the toast says how many were new.
   2. Folded subtasks: in onboarding checklists (the personal one on
      the Training tab, the staff view of someone's checklist, and the
      master onboarding editor in the HR centre) parent steps now
      carry a "N subtasks" dropdown pill and their subtasks stay
      hidden until opened, so the list reads short.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa57) return;
  window.__wa57 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa57css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w57tg{display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;padding:0 9px;margin-left:8px;' +
    'font-size:10.5px;color:#103F39 !important;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;line-height:1.7;vertical-align:middle;text-decoration:none !important;}' +
    '.w57tg:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.4);}' +
    '[data-w57bulk]{display:inline-block;background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2 !important;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 13px;margin-left:8px;' +
    'font-size:11.5px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;line-height:1.5;text-decoration:none !important;}' +
    '[data-w57bulk]:hover{background:#103F39;}' +
    '.w57who label{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;padding:2px 0;font-size:13px;}' +
    '.w57cnt{font-size:11.5px;color:#797773;margin-left:6px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* ================= folded subtasks ================= */
  function wa57Fold(root) {
    try {
      if (!root || typeof WA37 === 'undefined') return;
      var tmplById = {};
      (WA37.tmpl || []).forEach(function (t) { tmplById[String(t.id)] = t; });
      var stepTmpl = {};
      (WA37.steps || []).forEach(function (s) { stepTmpl[String(s.id)] = String(s.template_id); });
      var rows = Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('.w37step, .w46st'));
      var byTmpl = {};
      rows.forEach(function (row) {
        var tid = row.dataset.w57id || null;
        if (!tid) {
          var chk = row.querySelector('[data-w37chk]');
          if (chk) tid = stepTmpl[String(chk.getAttribute('data-w37chk'))] || null;
          if (!tid) {
            var e = row.querySelector('[data-w37e]');
            if (e) tid = String(e.getAttribute('data-w37e'));
          }
          if (tid) row.dataset.w57id = tid;
        }
        if (tid) byTmpl[String(tid)] = row;
      });
      rows.forEach(function (row) {
        var tid = row.dataset.w57id;
        if (!tid) return;
        var t = tmplById[String(tid)];
        if (!t || !t.parent_id || !byTmpl[String(t.parent_id)]) return;
        if (!row.hasAttribute('data-w57p')) {
          row.setAttribute('data-w57p', String(t.parent_id));
          if (row.dataset.w57shown !== '1') row.style.display = 'none';
        }
      });
      rows.forEach(function (row) {
        var tid = row.dataset.w57id;
        if (!tid || row.querySelector('.w57tg')) return;
        var kids = root.querySelectorAll('[data-w57p="' + tid + '"]');
        if (!kids.length) return;
        var open = kids[0].style.display !== 'none';
        var nm = row.querySelector('.nm') || row;
        var a = document.createElement('a');
        a.className = 'w57tg';
        a.setAttribute('data-w57t', tid);
        a.textContent = (open ? '\u25BE ' : '\u25B8 ') + kids.length + ' subtask' + (kids.length === 1 ? '' : 's');
        nm.appendChild(a);
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  function wa57FoldAll() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.w37wbody').forEach(wa57Fold);
    var master = document.getElementById('w46master');
    if (master) wa57Fold(master);
  }

  /* ================= bulk training assignment ================= */
  function wa57Depts() {
    var seen = {}, out = [];
    (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []).forEach(function (p) {
      var d = p.department || '';
      if (d && !seen[d]) { seen[d] = 1; out.push(d); }
    });
    return out;
  }
  function wa57BulkModal() {
    var people = (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []).slice()
      .sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var dl = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined') ? DEPT_LABEL : {};
    var trainings = (typeof WA36_TRAININGS !== 'undefined' ? WA36_TRAININGS : []).slice();
    trainings.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.category).localeCompare(String(b.category)) || String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title)); });
    var html = '<h3>Bulk assign trainings</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">Pick who, pick what, assign in one go. Anyone who already has a training keeps their record; done stays done.</p>'
      + '<label>Who gets them</label>'
      + '<select id="w57scope">'
      + '<option value="people">Specific people</option>'
      + '<option value="team">A whole team</option>'
      + '<option value="org">The whole organization (' + people.length + ' people)</option>'
      + '</select>'
      + '<select id="w57dept" style="display:none;margin-top:6px">'
      + wa57Depts().map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + esc(d) + '">' + esc(dl[d] || d) + '</option>'; }).join('')
      + '</select>'
      + '<div id="w57people" class="w57who" style="max-height:170px;overflow:auto;margin-top:6px;border:1px solid #ECEBE3;border-radius:10px;padding:6px 10px">'
      + people.map(function (p) {
        return '<label><input type="checkbox" class="w57pcb" value="' + esc(p.name) + '" style="width:15px;height:15px" /> '
          + esc(p.name) + ' <span style="color:#797773;font-size:11px">' + esc(dl[p.department] || p.department || '') + '</span></label>';
      }).join('')
      + '</div>'
      + '<label style="margin-top:10px">Trainings to assign</label>'
      + '<input type="search" id="w57q" placeholder="Search trainings..." style="width:100%;margin:2px 0 6px" />'
      + '<div style="max-height:190px;overflow:auto;border:1px solid #ECEBE3;border-radius:10px;padding:6px 10px" class="w57who">'
      + trainings.map(function (t) {
        return '<label data-w57tr="1" data-search="' + esc((t.title + ' ' + (t.category || '')).toLowerCase()) + '">'
          + '<input type="checkbox" class="w57tcb" value="' + t.id + '" style="width:15px;height:15px" /> '
          + esc(t.title) + ' <span style="color:#797773;font-size:11px">' + esc(t.category || '') + '</span></label>';
      }).join('')
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w57go">Assign them</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w57cx">Cancel</button></div>';
    openModal(html);
    var scope = document.getElementById('w57scope');
    scope.addEventListener('change', function () {
      document.getElementById('w57dept').style.display = scope.value === 'team' ? '' : 'none';
      document.getElementById('w57people').style.display = scope.value === 'people' ? '' : 'none';
    });
    document.getElementById('w57q').addEventListener('input', function () {
      var q = this.value.toLowerCase();
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-w57tr]').forEach(function (l) {
        l.style.display = !q || l.getAttribute('data-search').indexOf(q) >= 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
      });
    });
    document.getElementById('w57cx').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w57go').addEventListener('click', function () { wa57Go(this); });
  }
  async function wa57Go(btn) {
    var scope = (document.getElementById('w57scope') || {}).value || 'people';
    var people = (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []);
    var targets = [];
    if (scope === 'org') targets = people.map(function (p) { return p.name; });
    else if (scope === 'team') {
      var d = (document.getElementById('w57dept') || {}).value;
      targets = people.filter(function (p) { return p.department === d; }).map(function (p) { return p.name; });
    } else {
      document.querySelectorAll('.w57pcb:checked').forEach(function (c) { targets.push(c.value); });
    }
    var tids = [];
    document.querySelectorAll('.w57tcb:checked').forEach(function (c) { tids.push(Number(c.value)); });
    if (!targets.length) { toast('Pick who gets the trainings'); return; }
    if (!tids.length) { toast('Pick at least one training'); return; }
    var have = {};
    (typeof WA36_PROG !== 'undefined' ? WA36_PROG : []).forEach(function (p) {
      have[String(p.training_id) + '|' + String(p.person_name || '').toLowerCase()] = 1;
    });
    var by = (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'HR';
    var now = new Date().toISOString();
    var rows = [], skipped = 0;
    targets.forEach(function (nm) {
      tids.forEach(function (tid) {
        if (have[String(tid) + '|' + String(nm).toLowerCase()]) { skipped++; return; }
        rows.push({ training_id: tid, person_name: nm, status: 'assigned', assigned_by: by, updated_at: now });
      });
    });
    if (!rows.length) { toast('Everyone picked already has those trainings'); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    var r = await db.from('training_progress').upsert(rows, { onConflict: 'training_id,person_name' });
    btn.disabled = false;
    if (r.error) { toast('Could not assign them'); return; }
    try {
      await db.from('change_log').insert({
        entity_type: 'training', entity_id: 'bulk', field: 'bulk_assign',
        old_value: null,
        new_value: tids.length + ' training' + (tids.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' to ' + targets.length + ' people (' + scope + '): ' + rows.length + ' new',
        changed_by: by
      });
    } catch (e) { /* log only */ }
    closeModal();
    toast('Assigned ' + rows.length + ' new' + (skipped ? ', ' + skipped + ' already in place' : ''));
    try { if (typeof wa36Load === 'function') await wa36Load(true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { location.hash = '#hrcentre/training'; applyHash(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* the entry pill in the Trainings dashboard */
  function wa57Ensure() {
    try {
      var add = document.querySelector('#w46body [data-w46addtr]');
      if (add && !document.querySelector('[data-w57bulk]')) {
        add.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', ' <a data-w57bulk="1">Bulk assign (teams, people, or everyone)</a>');
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* ================= wiring ================= */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa57Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa57Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var tg = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w57t]');
      if (tg) {
        e.preventDefault();
        var tid = tg.getAttribute('data-w57t');
        var root = (tg.closest && (tg.closest('.w37wbody') || tg.closest('#w46master'))) || document;
        var kids = root.querySelectorAll('[data-w57p="' + tid + '"]');
        if (!kids.length) return;
        var show = kids[0].style.display === 'none';
        kids.forEach(function (k) {
          k.style.display = show ? '' : 'none';
          k.dataset.w57shown = show ? '1' : '';
        });
        tg.textContent = (show ? '\u25BE ' : '\u25B8 ') + kids.length + ' subtask' + (kids.length === 1 ? '' : 's');
        return;
      }
      var bk = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w57bulk]');
      if (bk) { if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) wa57BulkModal(); return; }
    });
  }

  if (typeof wa37Mount === 'function') {
    var __mt57 = wa37Mount;
    wa37Mount = async function () {
      var out = await __mt57.apply(this, arguments);
      try { wa57FoldAll(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa37OpenModal === 'function') {
    var __om57 = wa37OpenModal;
    wa37OpenModal = function () {
      var out = __om57.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(function () {
        var m = document.getElementById('w37modal');
        if (m) m.querySelectorAll('.w37wbody').forEach(wa57Fold);
      }, 60);
      return out;
    };
  }
  function wa57Watch() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel) return;
    if (!panel.dataset.w57mo) {
      panel.dataset.w57mo = '1';
      var mo = new MutationObserver(function () {
        wa57Ensure();
        var master = document.getElementById('w46master');
        if (master) wa57Fold(master);
      });
      mo.observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
    wa57Ensure();
    var master = document.getElementById('w46master');
    if (master) wa57Fold(master);
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp57 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp57(id);
      try {
        if (id === 'hrcentre') wa57Watch();
        if (id === 'training') setTimeout(wa57FoldAll, 300);
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa57 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa57();
      try { wa57Watch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      try { wa57FoldAll(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v58 training page order + quiet completed onboardings
   (03/08/26)
   1. Order: on the Training page your onboarding comes first, your
      assigned trainings sit right under it, and the full catalog of
      trainings and paths to choose from stays below.
   2. Once your onboarding is COMPLETE the whole checklist minimizes to
      a single "complete" bar (with a view link). The exception: when
      HR edits a step in the master onboarding after you finished, only
      that changed step surfaces, with its new instructions and a
      confirm button ("Got it, I am up to date"). Confirmations are
      stored per person per change (public.onboarding_update_acks);
      master edits stamp onboarding_template.updated_at via trigger.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa58) return;
  window.__wa58 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa58css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '#w58bar{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:12px;padding:10px 14px;margin:6px 0 4px;}' +
    '#w58bar b{color:#103F39;font-size:13.5px;}' +
    '#w58bar a{cursor:pointer;color:#103F39;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;}' +
    '.w58upd{background:rgba(220,165,39,.1);border:1px solid rgba(220,165,39,.35);border-radius:12px;padding:10px 14px;margin:8px 0 4px;}' +
    '.w58upd h4{margin:0 0 4px;font-size:11.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;color:#8a6414;}' +
    '.w58st{padding:7px 0;border-top:1px solid rgba(220,165,39,.25);}' +
    '.w58st:first-of-type{border-top:none;}' +
    '.w58st .nm{font-size:13.5px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w58st .sec{font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.4px;color:#8a6414;margin-left:8px;}' +
    '.w58st .ds{font-size:12.5px;color:#797773;margin:3px 0 6px;line-height:1.5;}' +
    '[data-w58ack]{display:inline-block;background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2 !important;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 13px;' +
    'font-size:11.5px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;line-height:1.5;border:none;}' +
    '[data-w58ack]:hover{background:#103F39;}' +
    '.w58hid{display:none !important;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* ---- 1. order: onboarding, then your trainings, then the catalog ---- */
  function wa58Order() {
    try {
      var body = document.getElementById('w36body');
      if (!body) return;
      var me = body.querySelector('.w44me');
      var onb = body.querySelector('.w36onb');
      if (!me || !onb) return;
      /* if "Your training" currently sits above onboarding, move it below */
      if (me.compareDocumentPosition(onb) & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING) {
        onb.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', me);
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* ---- 2. minimized completed onboarding + update confirmations ---- */
  var WA58 = { acks: {}, ackedFor: null };
  window.w58 = { state: WA58 };   /* wave194: closing an update elsewhere keeps this list in step */
  async function wa58LoadAcks(onbId) {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('onboarding_update_acks').select('*').eq('onboarding_id', Number(onbId));
      WA58.acks = {};
      ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (a) {
        var k = String(a.template_id);
        if (!WA58.acks[k] || WA58.acks[k] < a.template_updated_at) WA58.acks[k] = a.template_updated_at;
      });
      WA58.ackedFor = onbId;
    } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
  }
  function wa58Pending(mine) {
    if (!mine || !mine.completed_at || typeof WA37 === 'undefined') return [];
    return (WA37.tmpl || []).filter(function (t) {
      if (!t.active || !t.updated_at) return false;
      if (String(t.updated_at) <= String(mine.completed_at)) return false;
      var ack = WA58.acks[String(t.id)];
      return !(ack && String(ack) >= String(t.updated_at));
    });
  }
  async function wa58Onb() {
    try {
      var host = document.querySelector('#training .w36onb');
      if (!host || host.querySelector('#w58bar')) return;
      var me = (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null;
      if (!me || typeof WA37 === 'undefined') return;
      var mine = (WA37.onbs || []).filter(function (o) {
        return o.status === 'completed' && String(o.person_name || '').toLowerCase() === String(me.name || '').toLowerCase();
      })[0];
      if (!mine) return;
      if (WA58.ackedFor !== mine.id) await wa58LoadAcks(mine.id);
      if (host.querySelector('#w58bar')) return;
      /* hide the checklist, the intro line and the progress row */
      Array.prototype.slice.call(host.children).forEach(function (ch) {
        var isWeek = ch.classList && ch.classList.contains('w37week');
        var isIntro = ch.tagName === 'P' && ch.classList.contains('read');
        var isProgress = false;
        try { isProgress = !!ch.querySelector(':scope > .w37bar'); } catch (e2) { /* old browsers */ }
        if (isWeek || isIntro || isProgress) ch.classList.add('w58hid');
      });
      if (window.w194 && typeof w194.loadOpens === 'function') { try { await w194.loadOpens(mine.id); } catch (e3) { /* fine */ } }   /* wave194 */
      if (host.querySelector('#w58bar')) return;
      var pend = wa58Pending(mine);
      var html = '<div id="w58bar"><b>Onboarding complete</b>'
        + '<span style="font-size:12px;color:#797773">finished ' + esc(String(mine.completed_at || '').slice(0, 10)) + '</span>'
        + '<a data-w58show="1">View the checklist</a></div>';
      if (pend.length) {
        html += '<div class="w58upd"><h4>Updated since you finished (' + pend.length + ')</h4>'
          + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin:0 0 4px">' + (window.w194 ? 'HR changed these onboarding steps after you finished. Open each one to go through it again, then close it.' : 'HR changed these onboarding steps. Read each one and confirm you are up to date.') + '</p>'
          + pend.map(function (t) {
            return '<div class="w58st" data-w58row="' + esc(String(t.id)) + '" data-w194row="' + esc(String(t.id)) + '"><span class="nm">' + esc(t.name) + '</span><span class="sec">' + esc(t.section || '') + '</span>'
              + (t.description ? '<div class="ds">' + (typeof wa37Md === 'function' ? wa37Md(t.description) : esc(t.description)) + '</div>' : '<div class="ds"></div>')
              + (window.w194 && typeof w194.actsHtml === 'function' ? '<span class="w194acts">' + w194.actsHtml(t, mine.id) + '</span></div>'   /* wave194: open it, then close it */
                : '<button data-w58ack="' + esc(String(t.id)) + '" data-w58onb="' + mine.id + '" data-w58up="' + esc(String(t.updated_at)) + '">Got it, I am up to date</button></div>');
          }).join('')
          + '</div>';
      }
      var h3 = host.querySelector('h3');
      if (h3) h3.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', html);
      else host.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', html);
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  async function wa58Ack(btn) {
    var tid = btn.getAttribute('data-w58ack');
    var onbId = btn.getAttribute('data-w58onb');
    var up = btn.getAttribute('data-w58up');
    btn.disabled = true;
    var by = (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff';
    var r = await db.from('onboarding_update_acks').insert({
      onboarding_id: Number(onbId), template_id: tid, template_updated_at: up, acked_by: by
    });
    if (r.error) { btn.disabled = false; toast('Could not save the confirmation'); return; }
    WA58.acks[String(tid)] = up;
    toast('Noted, thanks');
    var row = document.querySelector('[data-w58row="' + tid + '"]');
    if (row) row.remove();
    var box = document.querySelector('.w58upd');
    if (box && !box.querySelector('.w58st')) box.remove();
  }

  if (!document.body.dataset.wa58Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa58Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var sh = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w58show]');
      if (sh) {
        var host = document.querySelector('#training .w36onb');
        if (!host) return;
        var hidden = host.querySelectorAll('.w58hid');
        if (hidden.length) {
          hidden.forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('w58hid'); x.setAttribute('data-w58was', '1'); });
          sh.textContent = 'Hide the checklist';
        } else {
          host.querySelectorAll('[data-w58was]').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.add('w58hid'); });
          sh.textContent = 'View the checklist';
        }
        return;
      }
      var ak = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w58ack]');
      if (ak) { wa58Ack(ak); return; }
    });
  }

  function wa58Apply() {
    wa58Order();
    wa58Onb();
  }
  if (typeof wa37Mount === 'function') {
    var __mt58 = wa37Mount;
    wa37Mount = async function () {
      var out = await __mt58.apply(this, arguments);
      try { wa58Apply(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  function wa58Watch() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('training');
    if (!panel) return;
    if (!panel.dataset.w58mo) {
      panel.dataset.w58mo = '1';
      var mo = new MutationObserver(function () { wa58Order(); });
      mo.observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
    wa58Apply();
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp58 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp58(id);
      try { if (id === 'training') setTimeout(wa58Watch, 250); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa58 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa58();
      try { wa58Watch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v59 roles on the steps themselves
   (03/08/26)
   Steps that belong to a role (sop_roles) now show it inline, in both
   the Master program SOP and every upcoming event checklist: a role
   chip plus a dropdown of everyone in the org. Picking a person
   assigns the WHOLE role: every linked step gets that owner in the
   master SOP (sop_template.default_owner) AND in every planned
   upcoming event (sop_event_steps.owner), in one action. The roles
   block dropdown at the top of the SOP now also pushes to upcoming
   events (v47 only updated the master).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa59) return;
  window.__wa59 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa59css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w59role{display:inline-block;background:rgba(111,100,171,.12);color:#6F64AB;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 9px;' +
    'font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;margin-right:6px;vertical-align:middle;}' +
    '.w59sel{max-width:170px;font-size:11.5px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var WA59 = { roles: [], loaded: false };
  async function wa59Load(force) {
    if (WA59.loaded && !force) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('sop_roles').select('*').order('name');
      WA59.roles = (r && r.data) || [];
      WA59.loaded = true;
    } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
  }
  function wa59RoleOf(tid) {
    for (var i = 0; i < WA59.roles.length; i++) {
      var ids = Array.isArray(WA59.roles[i].step_ids) ? WA59.roles[i].step_ids : [];
      if (ids.indexOf(tid) >= 0) return WA59.roles[i];
    }
    return null;
  }
  function wa59Tmpl(row) {
    var ds = String(row.getAttribute('data-vlstep') || '');
    if (ds.indexOf('tmpl-') === 0) return ds.slice(5);
    var det = (typeof WA_VOL_DETAIL !== 'undefined') ? WA_VOL_DETAIL : null;
    if (det && det.steps) {
      for (var i = 0; i < det.steps.length; i++) {
        if (String(det.steps[i].id) === ds) return det.steps[i].template_id;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  function wa59Decorate() {
    try {
      if (!(typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff())) return;
      if (!WA59.roles.length) return;
      var people = (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []);
      document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering .vl-step').forEach(function (row) {
        if (row.querySelector('.w59role')) return;
        var tid = wa59Tmpl(row);
        if (!tid) return;
        var role = wa59RoleOf(tid);
        if (!role) return;
        var meta = row.querySelector('.vl-meta');
        if (!meta) return;
        var ownerIn = meta.querySelector('[data-vlowner]');
        var cur = ownerIn ? String(ownerIn.value || '').trim() : '';
        var html = '<span class="w59role" title="Assigning this role sets the owner on every linked step, master SOP and all upcoming events">' + esc(role.name) + '</span>'
          + '<select class="vl-in w59sel" data-w59r="' + role.id + '"><option value="">assign the role to...</option>'
          + people.map(function (p) {
            return '<option value="' + esc(p.name) + '"' + (p.name === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(p.name) + '</option>';
          }).join('')
          + '</select> ';
        meta.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', html);
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  async function wa59Events(ids, name) {
    /* push the owner into every planned upcoming event */
    var today = new Date();
    var iso = today.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (today.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + today.getDate()).slice(-2);
    var evs = null;
    try { evs = await db.from('volunteer_events').select('id').eq('status', 'planned').gte('event_date', iso); } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
    var evIds = ((evs && evs.data) || []).map(function (x) { return x.id; });
    if (!evIds.length) return 0;
    var r = await db.from('sop_event_steps').update({ owner: name }).in('event_id', evIds).in('template_id', ids);
    if (r.error) return -1;
    try {
      if (typeof WA_VOL_DETAIL !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_DETAIL && WA_VOL_DETAIL.steps) {
        WA_VOL_DETAIL.steps.forEach(function (s) { if (ids.indexOf(s.template_id) >= 0) s.owner = name; });
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return evIds.length;
  }
  async function wa59Assign(roleId, name, el) {
    var role = WA59.roles.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(roleId); })[0];
    if (!role || !name) return;
    var ids = Array.isArray(role.step_ids) ? role.step_ids : [];
    if (!ids.length) { toast('This role has no steps yet'); return; }
    if (el) el.disabled = true;
    var r = await db.from('sop_template').update({ default_owner: name, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).in('id', ids);
    if (r.error) { if (el) el.disabled = false; toast('Could not assign the role'); return; }
    var evN = await wa59Events(ids, name);
    try {
      (typeof WA_VOL_TMPL !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_TMPL : []).forEach(function (t) {
        if (ids.indexOf(t.id) >= 0) t.default_owner = name;
      });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try {
      await db.from('change_log').insert({
        entity_type: 'sop_role', entity_id: String(role.id), field: 'assigned',
        old_value: null, new_value: role.name + ' -> ' + name + ' (' + ids.length + ' steps, ' + Math.max(0, evN) + ' upcoming events)',
        changed_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff'
      });
    } catch (e) { /* log only */ }
    if (el) el.disabled = false;
    toast(name + ' now owns "' + role.name + '": ' + ids.length + ' steps'
      + (evN > 0 ? ' across ' + evN + ' upcoming event' + (evN === 1 ? '' : 's') : '') + (evN === -1 ? ' (events did not update)' : ''));
    try {
      if (typeof WA_VOL_DETAIL !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_DETAIL && typeof WA_VOL_EVENT !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_EVENT && WA_VOL_DETAIL.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) {
        waVolRenderEvent();
      } else if (typeof waVolRenderSop === 'function') {
        waVolRenderSop();
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    setTimeout(wa59Decorate, 80);
  }

  if (!document.body.dataset.wa59Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa59Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
      var sel = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w59r]');
      if (sel && sel.value) {
        if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) wa59Assign(sel.getAttribute('data-w59r'), sel.value, sel);
        return;
      }
      /* the roles block at the top of the SOP (v47) only updates the
         master; ride along and push to upcoming events too */
      var rb = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47rb]');
      if (rb && rb.value && typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) {
        wa59Load(false).then(function () {
          var role = WA59.roles.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(rb.getAttribute('data-w47rb')); })[0];
          if (!role) return;
          var ids = Array.isArray(role.step_ids) ? role.step_ids : [];
          if (ids.length) wa59Events(ids, rb.value);
        });
      }
    });
  }

  if (typeof waVolRenderSop === 'function') {
    var __sop59 = waVolRenderSop;
    waVolRenderSop = function () {
      __sop59.apply(this, arguments);
      wa59Load(false).then(function () { setTimeout(wa59Decorate, 60); });
    };
  }
  if (typeof waVolRenderEvent === 'function') {
    var __ev59 = waVolRenderEvent;
    waVolRenderEvent = function () {
      __ev59.apply(this, arguments);
      wa59Load(false).then(function () { setTimeout(wa59Decorate, 60); });
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa59 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa59();
      try { await wa59Load(false); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      try { wa59Decorate(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();

  /* ---- tiny mock builders ---- */
  function mTask(pills, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    return '<div class="w60task' + (opts.late ? ' late' : '') + '"><span class="cb' + (opts.done ? ' on' : '') + '"></span><span class="ln"></span>'
      + (pills || []).map(function (p) { return '<span class="w60p ' + (p[1] || '') + '">' + p[0] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function mBar(pct) {
    return '<div class="w60barw"><span class="w60bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></span><b>' + pct + '%</b></div>';
  }
  function mPills(arr) {
    return '<div class="w60pr">' + arr.map(function (x) { return '<span class="w60p ' + (x[1] || '') + '">' + x[0] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function mCal() {
    var h = '<div class="w60cal">';
    for (var i = 0; i < 14; i++) h += '<i' + (i === 9 ? ' class="on"' : '') + '></i>';
    return h + '</div>';
  }
  function mPeople(names) {
    return '<div>' + names.map(function (n) { return '<span class="w60per"><i></i>' + n + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function mDoc() { return '<div class="w60doc"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>'; }
  function mSecret() { return '<div class="w60task"><span class="w60p">Canva login</span><span class="w60mask">&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;</span><span class="w60p grn">reveal</span></div>'; }

  function card(vis, cap) { return '<div class="w60card"><div class="vis">' + vis + '</div><div class="cap">' + cap + '</div></div>'; }
  function flow(steps) {
    return '<div class="w60flow">' + steps.map(function (s, i) {
      return '<span class="w60step"><i>' + (i + 1) + '</i>' + s + '</span>' + (i < steps.length - 1 ? '<span class="ar">&rarr;</span>' : '');
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }

  var T = {
    mypage: 'Home', overview: 'Decisions', dashboard: 'Dashboard', dept: 'Department pages',
    requests: 'Team Requests', agendas: 'Agendas', importantinfo: 'Important info', goals: 'Goals',

  function wa60Html(k) {
    var sub = '', fl = [], cards = [];
    if (k === 'mypage') {

      ];

      fl = ['Scan the tiles', 'Review open decisions', 'Tick to record the call'];
      cards = [

        card(mBar(64), 'Stat tiles: the org right now')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'dashboard') {
      sub = 'How work is moving, per department.';
      fl = ['Pick a department', 'Spot the red', 'Click any row'];
      cards = [
        card(mBar(45), 'Completion and cycle times'),

        card(mPills([['ClickUp'], ['decisions aging']]), 'Movement and aging, linked')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'dept') {
      sub = 'Everything this department is doing.';
      fl = ['Read the updates', 'Work the tasks', 'File into projects'];
      cards = [
        card(mTask([['project: Flag Football'], ['due Aug 12', 'gold']]), 'Tasks carry project + due pills'),

      ];
    } else if (k === 'requests') {

      fl = ['Pick the type', 'Fill the short form', 'Track the status'];
      cards = [
        card(mPills([['requested', 'gold'], ['in progress'], ['done', 'grn']]), 'Statuses update live, both sides'),
        card(mTask([['from a meeting', 'vio']]), 'Asks the Hub suggests: accept or dismiss')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'agendas') {
      sub = 'Agendas the Hub drafts, grounded in your real work.';
      fl = ['Pick type + people', 'Type the purpose', 'Generate'];
      cards = [
        card(mDoc(), 'The purpose box shapes the whole agenda'),
        card(mTask([], { done: true }), 'Agenda items tick real tasks'),

        card(mPills([['only your meetings', 'vio']]), 'You see agendas you are part of')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'importantinfo') {
      sub = 'Shared links, tools and credentials.';
      fl = ['Find it', 'Reveal if masked', 'Add your own'];
      cards = [
        card(mSecret(), 'Secrets stay masked until revealed'),
        card(mPills([['Canva'], ['Keela'], ['Drive']]), 'One place for every tool')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'goals') {
      sub = 'Quarterly goals, rolling up to the organization.';
      fl = ['Pick the quarter', 'Open a department', 'Link the work'];
      cards = [
        card(mBar(58), 'Progress per goal'),
        card(mPills([['project', 'grn'], ['&rarr;'], ['goal', 'vio']]), 'Projects roll up into goals'),
        card(mDoc(), 'Goals the Hub suggests per department')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'create') {
      sub = 'Branded content in the WA voice.';
      fl = ['Describe it', 'Generate', 'Refine or download'];
      cards = [
        card(mDoc(), 'Emails, posts, reports, letters'),
        card(mPills([['graphic'], ['+ photo'], ['send for review', 'grn']]), 'Graphics too, with formats')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'finances') {
      sub = 'Budget vs actual, and every transaction.';
      fl = ['Check the budget', 'Drill into the ledger', 'Log or import'];
      cards = [
        card(mBar(81), 'Budget vs actual by category'),
        card(mTask([['reallocate'], ['duplicate', 'red']]), 'Fix transactions inline'),
        card(mPills([['FY: Oct 1 to Sep 30', 'gold']]), 'The fiscal year')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'orgchart') {
      sub = 'The live structure, from the roster.';
      fl = ['Find the person', 'Open their profile', 'Jump to their page'];
      cards = [
        card(mPeople(['Valex', 'Natasha', 'Tuna']), 'Green dot = online now'),
        card(mBar(66), 'Their page: plate, stats, training')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'training') {
      sub = 'Onboarding first, then your trainings, then the catalog.';
      fl = ['Finish onboarding', 'Do what is assigned', 'Browse the rest'];
      cards = [
        card(mBar(40), 'Your onboarding progress'),
        card(mTask([['2 subtasks']]), 'Big steps fold their subtasks'),
        card(mTask([['assigned', 'gold']]), 'Assigned trainings sit up top'),
        card(mPills([['confirm updates', 'gold']]), 'Done? Only HR changes resurface')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'volunteering') {
      sub = 'The serving programme, end to end.';
      fl = ['Open an event', 'Work the checklist', 'Confirm the roster'];
      cards = [
        card(mCal(), 'Calendar of events'),
        card(mTask([['Coordinating volunteers', 'vio'], ['assign to...']]), 'Role pills assign every linked step'),
        card(mPills([['SOP'], ['inventory'], ['recipes'], ['texting']]), 'Master SOP flows to upcoming events'),
        card(mPeople(['Mary', 'cooks x4']), 'Roster and confirmations per event')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'transcripts') {
      sub = 'Drop in any meeting; the Hub does the rest.';
      fl = ['Paste or import', 'Tag the department', 'Save'];
      cards = [
        card(mDoc(), 'Summary, tasks and updates extracted'),
        card(mPills([['comments', 'vio'], ['requests', 'gold'], ['due dates']]), 'The Hub\u2019s suggestions flow to the boards'),
        card(mPills([['posted to Slack', 'grn']]), 'The team sees the summary')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'activity') {
      sub = 'The change log of everything.';
      fl = ['Filter', 'Scan', 'Click through'];
      cards = [
        card(mDoc(), 'Every change, who and when')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'adminpanel') {
      sub = 'Who sees what.';
      fl = ['Pick a person', 'Set the role', 'Override tabs if needed'];
      cards = [
        card(mPills([['admin', 'grn'], ['leader'], ['member'], ['viewer', 'gold']]), 'Roles set the default pages'),
        card(mDoc(), 'Applies on their next load')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'projectpage') {
      sub = 'One project, its tasks and goal.';
      fl = ['Tick things done', 'Quick-add', 'Move tasks'];
      cards = [
        card(mTask([['move']], { done: true }), 'Move tasks between projects'),
        card(mPills([['rolls up to a goal', 'vio']]), 'Linked to the quarterly goals')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'personpage') {
      sub = 'One person, in full.';
      fl = ['Read the responsibilities', 'See their plate and projects', 'Check hours and training'];
      cards = [
        card(mTask([['3 open', 'gold']]), 'Their current plate'),
        card(mBar(75), 'Training progress')
      ];
    } else if (k === 'hrcentre') {
      sub = 'People operations, in four dashboards.';
      fl = ['Pick a tab', 'Act', 'Check Commitment'];
      cards = [
        card(mPills([['Onboarding', 'grn'], ['Trainings'], ['Recruitment'], ['Commitment']]), 'Four dashboards, one page'),
        card(mPills([['Bulk assign', 'grn'], ['team'], ['everyone']]), 'Assign trainings to many at once'),
        card(mTask([['interviewing', 'gold']]), 'Hiring pipeline with candidates'),
        card(mBar(52), 'Hours vs minimums, tasks per week')
      ];
    } else {
      return null;
    }
    return '<h3>' + (T[k] || 'This page') + '</h3>'
      + '<p class="w60sub">' + sub + '</p>'
      + flow(fl)
      + '<div class="w60grid">' + cards.join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn ghost" id="w60c">Close</button></div>';
  }

  function wa60Open(k) {
    var html = wa60Html(k);
    if (!html) return;
    openModal(html);
    var c = document.getElementById('w60c');
    if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  }

  /* run after the old handlers so this content wins */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa60Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa60Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w43help]');
      if (b) { setTimeout(function () { wa60Open(b.getAttribute('data-w43help')); }, 0); return; }
      var h = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w46help]');
      if (h) { setTimeout(function () { wa60Open('hrcentre'); }, 0); return; }
    });
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v61 deep page guides
   (03/08/26)
   The help popups grow into full, scrollable guides. Same shape as
   v60 up top (one-line purpose + numbered flow), then a "How it
   works, in detail" area: one visual + a couple of precise sentences
   per feature, drawn from how the Hub actually behaves. Department
   pages get the fullest guide (views, gating, flags, confidential,
   what completing does, and why tasks never disappear). Reuses the
   v60 mock styles; replaces the v60 popup content the same way v60
   replaced v43's (later listener wins the modal).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa61) return;
  window.__wa61 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa61css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w61wrap{max-height:58vh;overflow-y:auto;padding-right:8px;margin-top:4px;}' +

    '.w61sec .t b{display:block;font-size:12.5px;color:#103F39;margin-bottom:2px;}' +

    '.w61pipe .bx.grn{background:#103F39;border-color:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '.w61pipe .ar{color:#9ab3ab;font-weight:800;font-size:11px;}' +

  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* ---- mocks (v60 classes are global; these builders remake them) ---- */
  function mTask(pills, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    return '<div class="w60task' + (opts.late ? ' late' : '') + '"><span class="cb' + (opts.done ? ' on' : '') + '"></span><span class="ln"></span>'
      + (pills || []).map(function (p) { return '<span class="w60p ' + (p[1] || '') + '">' + p[0] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function mBar(pct) { return '<div class="w60barw"><span class="w60bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></span><b>' + pct + '%</b></div>'; }
  function mPills(arr) {
    return '<div class="w60pr">' + arr.map(function (x) { return '<span class="w60p ' + (x[1] || '') + '">' + x[0] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function mCal() {
    var h = '<div class="w60cal">';
    for (var i = 0; i < 14; i++) h += '<i' + (i === 9 ? ' class="on"' : '') + '></i>';
    return h + '</div>';
  }
  function mPeople(names) {
    return '<div>' + names.map(function (n) { return '<span class="w60per"><i></i>' + n + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function mDoc() { return '<div class="w60doc"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>'; }
  function mPipe(steps, hi) {
    return '<div class="w61pipe">' + steps.map(function (s, i) {
      return '<span class="bx' + (i === hi ? ' grn' : '') + '">' + s + '</span>' + (i < steps.length - 1 ? '<span class="ar">&rarr;</span>' : '');
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function flow(steps) {
    return '<div class="w60flow">' + steps.map(function (s, i) {
      return '<span class="w60step"><i>' + (i + 1) + '</i>' + s + '</span>' + (i < steps.length - 1 ? '<span class="ar">&rarr;</span>' : '');
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function sec(vis, title, txt) {
    return '<div class="w61sec"><div class="v">' + vis + '</div><div class="t"><b>' + title + '</b><span>' + txt + '</span></div></div>';
  }

  var T = {
    mypage: 'Home', overview: 'Executive dashboard', dashboard: 'Delivery numbers', dept: 'Department pages',
    requests: 'Team Requests', agendas: 'Agendas', importantinfo: 'Important info', goals: 'Department goals',
    create: 'Create studio', finances: 'Finance', orgchart: 'Org chart', training: 'Avenue Academy',
    volunteering: 'Volunteering', transcripts: 'Transcript archive', activity: 'Activity',
    adminpanel: 'Admin', projectpage: 'Project pages', personpage: 'People pages', hrcentre: 'HR centre'
  };

        + sec(mPills([['edit'], ['reassign'], ['move']]), 'Edit, reassign, move',
          'Edit changes title, owner, due date and more. Reassign switches the owner. Move sends the task to another department. Every change is written to the Activity log with who and when.')
        + sec(mTask([['project: Flag Football'], ['due Aug 12', 'gold']]), 'Project and due date pills',
          'Every task carries two pills: link it to a project (or General), and set or clear a due date with quick picks. Both are one click, both are logged.')
        + sec(mTask([], { late: true }), 'Yellow means overdue',
          'A task past its due date turns light yellow everywhere it appears until it is done or re-dated.')

  function wa61Open(k) {
    var html = wa61Body(k);

  /* registered after v60, so this content wins the modal */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa61Wired) {

      var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w43help]');
      if (b) { setTimeout(function () { wa61Open(b.getAttribute('data-w43help')); }, 0); return; }
      var h = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w46help]');
      if (h) { setTimeout(function () { wa61Open('hrcentre'); }, 0); return; }
    });
  }

/* =====================================================================
   WA v62 leadership and confidential meetings
   (03/08/26)
   The transcript archive learns meeting types. Uploading now offers:
   - Department meeting: visible to everyone, as before.
   - Leadership meeting: the transcript AND everything it produces
     (updates, tasks, decisions) visible only to leaders and admins.
   - Confidential: pick exactly who can see it; only those people (and
     admins) see the transcript and its updates, tasks and decisions.
   How it holds together: the transcript row carries visibility +
   allowed_people; a DB trigger (wa_stamp_restricted) stamps the same
   restriction onto the updates, tasks and decisions the AI files from
   that meeting; this wave filters every cache (archive, boards,
   decisions) per signed-in user; and the server functions (ask v6,
   build-agenda v6, transcript-requests v4) exclude restricted
   meetings so nothing leaks through chat, agendas or suggestions.
   Client-side gating until RLS phase 2, like the rest of the Hub.
   ===================================================================== */

  /* transcripts: creator always sees their own upload */
  function wa62SeeTx(t) {

    return false;
  }
  /* updates and decisions */
  function wa62SeeRow(r) {

  /* merge the meeting type into the transcript insert */
  function wa62Merge(payload) {
    var sel = document.getElementById('w62type');
    if (!sel || sel.value === 'department') return payload;
    var patch = { visibility: sel.value };
    if (sel.value === 'private' || sel.value === 'speakers') {
      var names = [];
      document.querySelectorAll('.w62pcb:checked').forEach(function (c) { names.push(c.value); });
      var mine = (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.name) || (typeof who === 'function' ? who() : '');
      if (mine && names.indexOf(mine) < 0) names.push(mine);
      patch.allowed_people = names;
    }
    var out = Object.assign({}, payload, patch);
    /* reset the picker for the next upload */
    setTimeout(function () {

        document.getElementById('w62note').style.display = 'none';
        document.querySelectorAll('.w62pcb:checked').forEach(function (c) { c.checked = false; });

  }

  /* ---- visibility chips on archive rows ---- */

/* =====================================================================
   WA v63 editable inventory + sharing with volunteer leaders
   (03/08/26)
   1. In the Hub: the Volunteering > Inventory table becomes editable
      for staff. In, Remaining and Reorder are inline inputs that save
      on change (change-logged), and there is an add-item row.
   2. Outside the Hub: "Share an editing link" creates a tokened link
      to a standalone editor page (hosted in the public web bucket).
      Volunteer leaders open it without an account, put in their name,
      update counts or add items, and Save. Writes go through the
      inventory-share edge function, which validates the token
      (hub_settings 'inventory_share') and change-logs every save.
      Revoke kills the link instantly.
   ===================================================================== */

  async function wa63Save(input) {
    var id = Number(input.getAttribute('data-w63id'));
    var f = input.getAttribute('data-w63f');
    if (!id || !f) return;
    var v = (input.value || '').trim() || null;
    var patch = { updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    patch[f] = v;
    var r = await db.from('volunteer_inventory').update(patch).eq('id', id);
    if (r.error) { toast('Could not save'); return; }
    try {
      (typeof WA_VOL_INV !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_INV : []).forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(id)) x[f] = v; });
      await db.from('change_log').insert({
        entity_type: 'inventory', entity_id: String(id), field: f, old_value: null, new_value: v,
        changed_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff'
      });
    } catch (e) { /* log only */ }
    toast('Saved');
  }

  function wa63AddModal() {

  async function wa63ShareModal() {

  /* ---- wiring ---- */

/* =====================================================================
   WA v64 tasks route to the owner's department
   (03/08/26)
   Adding a NEW task from any department page now checks who it is
   for: if the owner is on the roster and belongs to a different
   department, the task files into THAT department instead, with a
   toast saying where it went. Rules to stay safe: only new tasks
   (edits never move), only when the owner matches exactly one active
   roster member, and the workstream is cleared on reroute (it
   belonged to the other department). Everything else about the task
   modal is untouched.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa64) return;
  window.__wa64 = 1;

  function wa64Match(ownerText) {
    var q = String(ownerText || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return null;
    var members = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false && m.department; });
    /* exact full-name match first */
    var exact = members.filter(function (m) { return String(m.name || '').toLowerCase() === q; });
    if (exact.length === 1) return exact[0];
    /* then unique first-token match */
    var ft = q.split(/\s+/)[0];
    if (!ft) return null;
    var firsts = members.filter(function (m) { return String(m.name || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0].toLowerCase() === ft; });
    if (firsts.length === 1) return firsts[0];
    return null;
  }

  if (typeof saveTaskModal === 'function') {
    var __stm64 = saveTaskModal;
    saveTaskModal = async function (deptId, task) {
      try {
        if (!task) { /* only brand new tasks reroute */
          var ownerEl = document.getElementById('tmOwner');
          var m = wa64Match(ownerEl ? ownerEl.value : '');
          if (m && m.department && m.department !== deptId
            && typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' && DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(m.department) >= 0) {
            var subEl = document.getElementById('tmSub');
            if (subEl) subEl.value = '';
            var out = await __stm64.call(this, m.department, task);
            try {
              toast('Filed to ' + ((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[m.department]) || m.department)
                + " (" + m.name.split(' ')[0] + "'s department)");
            } catch (e2) { /* toast only */ }
            return out;
          }
        }
      } catch (e) { /* fall through to normal save */ }
      return __stm64.apply(this, arguments);
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v65 leadership meetings, upload preview, goal wins, mentions,
   request refinements, transcript pipeline guide, and drag to reorder
   the master SOPs
   (04/08/26)
   1. The transcript form's "Meeting type" is now "Meeting visibility",
      and the department dropdown gains "Leadership meeting (all
      departments)": it locks visibility to leadership and the new
      process-leadership function spreads its updates and tasks across
      every department (the request scanner spreads suggestions too).
   2. Uploading a transcript first shows a preview popup of everything
      the system intends to do (updates, tasks, closures, decisions,
      requests, goal wins, mentions); nothing saves until approved.
   3. Goal wins: concrete progress detected in meetings appears under
      each goal in the department Quarterly goals block.
   4. Mentions: people discussed in a meeting they did not attend get
      a Mentions section on My page with a "Got it" button.
   5. Requests: suggestions that refine an existing open request are
      labelled and apply onto that request instead of duplicating it.
   6. The transcripts "How this page works" popup becomes a full
      pipeline guide.
   7. Both master editors (onboarding and the volunteering SOP) get
      drag handles: drag steps up and down, across sections, and next
      to a main task to promote a subtask (or next to a subtask to
      nest). Reorders do not resurface steps for completed members.
   ===================================================================== */

  function w65Esc(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s || ''); }
  function w65Col(d) { return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || '#797773'; }
  function w65Lab(d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; }
  function w65Date(s) {
    if (!s) return '';
    try { return new Date(String(s) + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); }
    catch (e) { return String(s); }
  }
  var W65_ADMINS = ['steel.macdonald', 'suhaib.abdillahi', 'ian.odhiambo', 'valex.sanon', 'tuna.kaynak', 'michael.michailidis'];
  var W65_LEAD = /\b(lead|leader|director|manager|chair|co-)/i;
  function w65IsAdmin() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin') return true;
      var e = String((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase();
      return W65_ADMINS.indexOf(e.split('@')[0]) >= 0;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function w65IsLeader() {
    try {
      var me = (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined') ? WA_ME : null;
      return !!(me && (me.department === 'board' || W65_LEAD.test(me.role || '')));
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function w65SeeRow(r) {
    var v = r.visibility;
    if (!v || v === 'department') return true;
    if (v === 'speakers') return true; /* wave165: the server shows a one-on-one only to its speakers */
    if (w65IsAdmin()) return true;
    if (v === 'leadership') return w65IsLeader();
    return false;
  }

  function w65TxForm() {

      if (sel && sel.parentElement) {
        var lab = sel.parentElement.querySelector('label');
        if (lab && lab.textContent === 'Meeting type') lab.textContent = 'Meeting visibility';
      }

      if (dep && !dep.querySelector('option[value="Leadership meeting"]')) {
        var op = document.createElement('option');
        op.value = 'Leadership meeting';
        op.textContent = 'Leadership meeting (all departments)';
        dep.appendChild(op);
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  function w65DeptSync() {
    try {
      var dep = document.getElementById('txDept');
      var sel = document.getElementById('w62type');
      if (!dep || !sel) return;
      var isLead = dep.value === 'Leadership meeting';
      if (isLead) {
        sel.value = 'leadership';
        sel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
        sel.disabled = true;
        if (!document.getElementById('w65leadnote')) {
          sel.parentElement.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
            '<div id="w65leadnote">Leadership meeting: leaders from every department attend, so its updates, tasks and suggested requests spread across ALL departments. Visible to leaders and admins only.</div>');
        }
      } else {
        sel.disabled = false;
        var n = document.getElementById('w65leadnote');
        if (n) n.remove();
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* =========== 2. the upload preview popup =========== */
  function w65PlanRows(items, fn) {
    if (!items || !items.length) return '<p class="w65mut" style="margin:2px 0">Nothing.</p>';
    return items.map(fn).join('');
  }
  function w65Plan(p, isLead) {
    var h = '<h3>What this upload will do</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">' + w65Esc(p.summary || '') + '</p>'
      + (isLead ? '<p class="w65mut" style="margin:0 0 8px">Leadership meeting: items spread across every department and stay visible to leaders and admins only.</p>' : '')
      + '<div class="w65wrap">';
    h += '<div class="w65sec"><h4>New tasks (' + (p.new_tasks || []).length + ')</h4>'
      + w65PlanRows(p.new_tasks, function (t) {
        return '<div class="w65row"><span class="w65dep" style="background:' + w65Col(t.department) + '">' + w65Esc(w65Lab(t.department)) + '</span>'
          + '<span>' + w65Esc(t.title) + (t.bank_until ? ' <span class="w65mut">(to the bank until ' + w65Esc(t.bank_until) + ')</span>' : '') + '</span><span class="w65mut" style="margin-left:auto">' + w65Esc(t.owner || '') + '</span></div>';
      }) + '</div>';
    var closes = p.close_tasks || [];
    h += '<div class="w65sec"><h4>Tasks it will close (' + closes.length + ')</h4>';
    if (closes.length) {
      h += '<div class="w65warn" style="margin-bottom:4px">Closing needs clear proof in the meeting; double-check these.</div>'
        + closes.map(function (c) {
          return '<div class="w65row"><span>' + w65Esc(c.title) + '</span><span class="w65mut" style="margin-left:auto">&quot;' + w65Esc((c.evidence || '').slice(0, 90)) + '&quot;</span></div>';
        }).join('');
    } else h += '<p class="w65mut" style="margin:2px 0">Nothing.</p>';
    h += '</div>';
    h += '<div class="w65sec"><h4>Updates (' + (p.new_updates || []).length + ')</h4>'
      + w65PlanRows(p.new_updates, function (u) {
        return '<div class="w65row"><span class="w65dep" style="background:' + w65Col(u.department) + '">' + w65Esc(w65Lab(u.department)) + '</span><span>' + w65Esc(u.gist) + '</span></div>';
      }) + '</div>';
    h += '<div class="w65sec"><h4>Suggested requests (' + (p.requests || []).length + ', confirm-first)</h4>'
      + w65PlanRows(p.requests, function (r) {
        return '<div class="w65row"><span class="w65dep" style="background:' + w65Col(r.department) + '">' + w65Esc(w65Lab(r.department)) + '</span>'
          + '<span>' + w65Esc(r.title) + (r.kind === 'refine' ? ' <span class="w65mut">(updates an existing request)</span>' : '') + '</span></div>';
      }) + '</div>';
    h += '<div class="w65sec"><h4>Executive decisions (' + (p.new_decisions || []).length + ')</h4>'
      + w65PlanRows(p.new_decisions, function (d) { return '<div class="w65row"><span>' + w65Esc(d) + '</span></div>'; }) + '</div>';
    h += '<div class="w65sec"><h4>Goal wins (' + (p.goal_wins || []).length + ')</h4>'
      + w65PlanRows(p.goal_wins, function (w) {
        return '<div class="w65row"><span class="st" style="color:#8a6414;font-weight:800">&#10038;</span><span>' + w65Esc(w.win) + '</span><span class="w65mut" style="margin-left:auto">' + w65Esc((w.goal || '').slice(0, 60)) + '</span></div>';
      }) + '</div>';
    h += '<div class="w65sec"><h4>People to keep in the loop (' + (p.mentions || []).length + ')</h4>'
      + w65PlanRows(p.mentions, function (m) { return '<div class="w65row"><span>' + w65Esc(m) + '</span></div>'; }) + '</div>';
    /* wave136: a Marketing meeting's content ideas go to the Content calendar's Ideas (the far ones to the bank) */
    if ((p.content_ideas || []).length) h += '<div class="w65sec"><h4>Content ideas for the calendar (' + p.content_ideas.length + ')</h4>'
      + w65PlanRows(p.content_ideas, function (c) { var far = c.for_date && (Date.parse(c.for_date + 'T12:00:00') - Date.now()) >= 90 * 86400000; return '<div class="w65row"><span>' + w65Esc(c.title) + '</span>' + (c.for_date ? '<span class="w65mut" style="margin-left:auto">for ' + w65Esc(c.for_date) + (far ? ', waits in the bank' : '') + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'; }) + '</div>';
    h += '</div>'
      + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px">This is a close estimate from the same reading the Hub will do when it processes the meeting; exact wording can differ. Nothing is saved until you approve. Suggested requests, comments and due dates always wait for a human to accept them.</p>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w65go">Approve: upload and process</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w65cx">Cancel</button></div>';
    return h;
  }
  function w65Approve() {
    window.__wa65ok = 1;
    try { closeModal(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var b = document.getElementById('txSave');
    if (b) b.click();
    setTimeout(function () { window.__wa65ok = 0; }, 1500);
  }
  async function w65Preview() {
    openModal('<h3>Checking what this will do</h3><p class="m-sub">Reading the transcript against the board, one moment...</p>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn ghost" id="w65cx">Cancel</button></div>');
    var cx = document.getElementById('w65cx');
    if (cx) cx.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    var payload = {
      department: (document.getElementById('txDept') || {}).value || '',
      title: ((document.getElementById('txTitle') || {}).value || '').trim(),
      meeting_date: (document.getElementById('txDate') || {}).value || null,
      body: ((document.getElementById('txBody') || {}).value || '').trim(),
      visibility: (document.getElementById('w62type') || {}).value || 'department'
    };
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('transcript-preview', { body: payload });
      if (r.error || !(r.data && r.data.ok)) throw (r.error || new Error('failed'));
      openModal(w65Plan(r.data.preview || {}, !!r.data.is_leadership));
    } catch (e) {
      openModal('<h3>Could not build the preview</h3>'
        + '<p class="m-sub">The preview service did not answer. You can still upload; processing works the same either way.</p>'
        + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w65go">Upload anyway</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w65cx">Cancel</button></div>');
    }
    var go = document.getElementById('w65go');
    if (go) go.addEventListener('click', w65Approve);
    var cx2 = document.getElementById('w65cx');
    if (cx2) cx2.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#txSave');
    if (!b || window.__wa65ok) return;
    var t = ((document.getElementById('txTitle') || {}).value || '').trim();
    var body = ((document.getElementById('txBody') || {}).value || '').trim();
    if (!t || !body) return; /* let the normal validation speak */
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    w65Preview();
  }, true);

  /* =========== 3. goal wins under the quarterly goals =========== */
  var WA65W = { rows: null, at: 0, loading: false };
  async function w65LoadWins() {
    if (WA65W.loading) return;
    if (WA65W.rows && (Date.now() - WA65W.at) < 120000) return;
    WA65W.loading = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('goal_wins').select('*').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(200);
      WA65W.rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      WA65W.at = Date.now();
    } catch (e) { WA65W.rows = WA65W.rows || []; }
    WA65W.loading = false;
  }
  function w65PaintWins(deptId) {
    try {
      var panel = document.getElementById(deptId);
      if (!panel || !WA65W.rows || !WA65W.rows.length) return;
      panel.querySelectorAll('.w39goal').forEach(function (g) {
        if (g.querySelector('.w65wins')) return;
        var ed = g.querySelector('[data-w39ge]');
        if (!ed) return;
        var gid = String(ed.getAttribute('data-w39ge'));
        var wins = WA65W.rows.filter(function (w) { return String(w.goal_id) === gid && w65SeeRow(w); }).slice(0, 3);
        if (!wins.length) return;
        g.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<div class="w65wins">' + wins.map(function (w) {
          return '<div class="w65win"><span class="st">&#10038;</span><span>' + w65Esc(w.win) + '</span>'
            + '<span class="dt">' + w65Esc(w65Date(w.meeting_date)) + '</span></div>';
        }).join('') + '</div>');
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (typeof wa39Recompose === 'function') {
    var __rc65 = wa39Recompose;
    wa39Recompose = function (id) {
      __rc65.apply(this, arguments);
      if (WA65W.rows) { w65PaintWins(id); }
      else { w65LoadWins().then(function () { w65PaintWins(id); }); }
    };
  }

  /* =========== 4. mentions on My page =========== */
  async function w65Mentions() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA_ME === 'undefined' || !WA_ME || !WA_ME.name) return;
      var grid = document.querySelector('#mpBody .mp-grid');
      if (!grid || document.getElementById('w65menbox')) return;
      var r = await db.from('mentions').select('*').eq('person', WA_ME.name).order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(10);
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      if (!rows.length) return;
      var unseen = rows.filter(function (m) { return !m.seen; }).length;
      var html = '<div class="mp-sec" id="w65menbox"><h3>Mentions' + (unseen ? ' (' + unseen + ' new)' : '') + '</h3>'
        + '<p class="w65mut" style="margin:0 0 4px">You came up in these meetings you were not in, so you stay in the loop.</p>'
        + rows.map(function (m) {
          return '<div class="w65men' + (m.seen ? ' seen' : '') + '" data-w65mid="' + m.id + '">'
            + '<div class="mt">' + w65Esc(m.meeting_title || 'A meeting') + ' <span class="w65mut">' + w65Esc(w65Date(m.meeting_date)) + '</span>'
            + (m.seen ? '' : '<span class="w65new">new</span>') + '</div>'
            + (m.context ? '<div>' + w65Esc(m.context) + '</div>' : '')
            + (m.quote ? '<div class="mq">&quot;' + w65Esc(m.quote) + '&quot;</div>' : '')
            + (m.seen ? '' : '<a class="w65got" data-w65got="' + m.id + '">Got it</a>')
            + '</div>';
        }).join('') + '</div>';
      grid.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', html);
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa65Men) {
    document.body.dataset.wa65Men = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
      var a = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w65got]');
      if (!a) return;
      var id = Number(a.getAttribute('data-w65got'));
      try { await db.from('mentions').update({ seen: true }).eq('id', id); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ }
      var row = a.closest('.w65men');
      if (row) { row.classList.add('seen'); a.remove(); var nb = row.querySelector('.w65new'); if (nb) nb.remove(); }
    });
  }
  /* dead wrap removed in Phase 0 compaction: wave74 replaced renderMyPage outright and renders its own mentions list (.w74men) plus a decoy #w65menbox */

  /* =========== 5. refine suggestions on the requests page =========== */
  var WA65R = { map: null };
  async function w65LoadRefs() {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('request_suggestions').select('id,target_request_id,details,quote,title')
        .eq('status', 'pending').eq('kind', 'refine').limit(30);
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      var map = {};
      if (rows.length) {
        var ids = rows.map(function (x) { return x.target_request_id; }).filter(Boolean);
        var t = await db.from('dept_requests').select('id,title,details').in('id', ids);
        var byId = {};
        ((t && t.data) || []).forEach(function (x) { byId[String(x.id)] = x; });
        rows.forEach(function (s) {
          var tgt = byId[String(s.target_request_id)];
          if (tgt) map[String(s.id)] = { s: s, t: tgt };
        });
      }
      WA65R.map = map;
    } catch (e) { WA65R.map = WA65R.map || {}; }
  }
  function w65DecorateRefs() {
    try {
      if (!WA65R.map) return;
      Object.keys(WA65R.map).forEach(function (id) {
        var a = document.querySelector('[data-w50a="' + id + '"]');
        if (!a) return;
        var row = a.closest('.w50row');
        if (!row || row.dataset.w65r) return;
        row.dataset.w65r = '1';
        var tgt = WA65R.map[id].t;
        row.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',
          '<div class="w65ref">Not a new request: this refines the open request <b>' + w65Esc(tgt.title) + '</b>. Accepting adds the update onto it.</div>');
        a.textContent = 'Apply to the existing request';
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (typeof renderRequestsModule === 'function') {
    var __rr65 = renderRequestsModule;
    renderRequestsModule = function () {
      var out = __rr65.apply(this, arguments);
      w65LoadRefs().then(function () {
        [400, 1000, 2200].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(w65DecorateRefs, ms); });
      });
      return out;
    };
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var a = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w50a]');
    if (!a || !WA65R.map) return;
    var id = String(a.getAttribute('data-w50a'));
    var pair = WA65R.map[id];
    if (!pair) return; /* a normal suggestion: let wave50 handle it */
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    a.textContent = 'Applying...';
    try {
      var by = (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff';
      var add = '\n\nUpdate from a meeting: ' + (pair.s.details || pair.s.title || '')
        + (pair.s.quote ? '\n"' + pair.s.quote + '"' : '');
      var r1 = await db.from('dept_requests').update({
        details: (pair.t.details || '') + add,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: by
      }).eq('id', pair.t.id);
      if (r1.error) throw r1.error;
      await db.from('request_suggestions').update({
        status: 'accepted', request_id: pair.t.id,
        decided_by: by, decided_at: new Date().toISOString()
      }).eq('id', Number(id));
      try {
        await db.from('change_log').insert({
          entity_type: 'request', entity_id: String(pair.t.id), field: 'refined',
          old_value: null, new_value: (pair.s.title || '').slice(0, 200), changed_by: by
        });
      } catch (e3) { /* log only */ }
      delete WA65R.map[id];
      toast('Added onto the existing request');
      var row = a.closest('.w50row');
      if (row) row.remove();
    } catch (e2) {
      toast('Could not apply it');
      a.textContent = 'Apply to the existing request';
    }
  }, true);

  /* =========== 6. the transcripts pipeline guide =========== */
  function w65Pipe(items, hot) {
    return '<div class="w61pipe">' + items.map(function (x, i) {
      return '<span class="bx' + (i === hot ? ' grn' : '') + '">' + x + '</span>' + (i < items.length - 1 ? '<span class="ar">&#8594;</span>' : '');
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function w65Sec(vis, title, text) {
    return '<div class="w61sec"><div class="v">' + vis + '</div><div class="t"><b>' + title + '</b><span>' + text + '</span></div></div>';
  }
  function w65TxGuide() {
    var pill = function (t, c) { return '<span style="display:inline-block;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 9px;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;margin:1px 3px 1px 0;background:' + (c || '#ECEBE3') + ';color:' + (c ? '#FFFAF2' : '#103F39') + '">' + t + '</span>'; };
    return '<h3>Transcript archive</h3>'
      + '<p class="w60sub">Drop in any meeting; the Hub reads it and runs the boards.</p>'
      + w65Pipe(['Paste the transcript', 'Preview the actions', 'Approve', 'The Hub files everything'], 3)
      + '<div class="w61wrap">'
      + w65Sec(w65Pipe(['transcript', 'preview', 'approve'], 1), 'You approve before anything saves',
        'Saving first shows a popup of every action the system intends to take: new tasks, closures, updates, decisions, suggested requests, goal wins and mentions. Nothing is stored until you approve it.')
      + w65Sec(pill('summary') + pill('updates') + pill('tasks', '#103F39'), 'What the Hub files on the boards',
        'A one line summary (posted to Slack), 1 to 3 update entries, and new tasks with owners from the roster and due dates when the meeting states them. It never edits or deletes existing content, it only adds or closes.')
      + w65Sec(pill('close', '#b3402f') + pill('evidence'), 'Closing needs proof',
        'A task is closed only when the meeting clearly says the work is done, shipped, or dropped, and every closure carries a quote as evidence. Merely discussing a task adds a comment instead, which also stops duplicate tasks.')
      + w65Sec(pill('projects', '#103F39') + pill('serving events'), 'Tasks land in the right place',
        'Each new task is routed to the project it belongs to (project descriptions carry routing rules), and operations tasks for a serving Sunday attach to that event&#39;s checklist. Everything else sits in the department&#39;s General list.')
      + w65Sec(pill('requests', '#DCA527') + pill('due dates') + pill('comments'), 'Suggestions wait for a human',
