        'Cross team asks become request suggestions routed to the team that actions them; if the ask matches an open request it becomes an update on that request, not a duplicate. Due dates and task comments are suggested the same confirm-first way, always with a quote.')
      + w65Sec(pill('&#10038; goal wins', '#8a6414'), 'Progress shows up under the goals',
        'Concrete progress on a quarterly goal (something shipped, raised, signed or measured) is recorded as a win and appears under that goal on the department page.')
      + w65Sec(pill('mentions', '#6F64AB'), 'Absent people stay in the loop',
        'If someone on the roster is discussed in a meeting they were not in, a Mentions card appears on their My page with the context and a Got it button.')
      + w65Sec(pill('department') + pill('leadership', '#6F64AB') + pill('confidential', '#b3402f') + pill('one-on-one', '#b3402f'), 'Meeting visibility',
        'Department meetings are visible to everyone. Leadership meetings spread their tasks and updates across ALL departments but stay visible to leaders and admins only. Confidential meetings are visible only to the people you pick (and admins). A one-on-one is visible only to the people in it, not even admins. Restricted meetings stay out of agendas and shared suggestions; Ask the Hub answers from one only for the people who can see it.')
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn ghost" id="w65gc">Close</button></div>';
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa65Guide) {
    document.body.dataset.wa65Guide = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w43help]');
      if (b && b.getAttribute('data-w43help') === 'transcripts') {
        setTimeout(function () {
          openModal(w65TxGuide());
          var c = document.getElementById('w65gc');
          if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
        }, 0);
      }
    });
  }

  /* =========== 7. drag to reorder the master editors =========== */
  var W65D = { id: null, kind: null };
  function w65HandleHtml(kind, id) {
    return '<span class="w65h" title="Drag to move" data-w65h="' + kind + ':' + w65Esc(String(id)) + '">&#8942;&#8942;</span>';
  }
  function w65ClearMarks(scope) {
    (scope || document).querySelectorAll('.w65before,.w65after').forEach(function (x) {
      x.classList.remove('w65before'); x.classList.remove('w65after');
    });
  }
  function w65WireRow(row, kind, id) {
    if (row.dataset.w65d) return;
    row.dataset.w65d = kind + ':' + id;
    row.setAttribute('draggable', 'true');
    row.addEventListener('dragstart', function (e) {
      W65D.id = String(id); W65D.kind = kind;
      row.classList.add('w65dragging');
      try { e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', String(id)); e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; } catch (e2) { /* fine */ }
    });
    row.addEventListener('dragend', function () {
      row.classList.remove('w65dragging');
      w65ClearMarks();
      W65D.id = null;
    });
    row.addEventListener('dragover', function (e) {
      if (!W65D.id || W65D.kind !== kind || String(id) === W65D.id) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      var r = row.getBoundingClientRect();
      var before = (e.clientY - r.top) < r.height / 2;
      w65ClearMarks();
      row.classList.add(before ? 'w65before' : 'w65after');
    });
    row.addEventListener('drop', function (e) {
      if (!W65D.id || W65D.kind !== kind || String(id) === W65D.id) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      var r = row.getBoundingClientRect();
      var before = (e.clientY - r.top) < r.height / 2;
      w65ClearMarks();
      if (kind === 'onb') w65OnbDrop(W65D.id, String(id), before);
      else w65SopDrop(W65D.id, String(id), before);
      W65D.id = null;
    });
  }

  /* ---- onboarding master (the wa37Master modal) ---- */
  async function w65OnbDrop(dragId, targetId, before) {
    try {
      var all = (typeof WA37 !== 'undefined' && WA37.tmpl) ? WA37.tmpl : [];
      var drag = all.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === dragId; })[0];
      var target = all.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === targetId; })[0];
      if (!drag || !target) return;
      if (String(target.parent_id || '') === dragId) { toast('A step cannot nest under its own subtask'); return; }
      var kids = all.filter(function (x) { return String(x.parent_id || '') === dragId; })
        .sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0); });
      var block = [drag].concat(kids);
      var blockIds = block.map(function (x) { return String(x.id); });
      /* the new home: the target's section and parent */
      var sec = target.section;
      var list = all.filter(function (x) { return x.section === sec && blockIds.indexOf(String(x.id)) < 0; })
        .sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0) || String(a.id).localeCompare(String(b.id)); });
      var ti = list.map(function (x) { return String(x.id); }).indexOf(targetId);
      if (ti < 0) return;
      list.splice(before ? ti : ti + 1, 0, block[0]);
      /* children ride right behind the parent */
      for (var k = 1; k < block.length; k++) list.splice(list.indexOf(block[0]) + k, 0, block[k]);
      drag.section = sec;
      drag.parent_id = target.parent_id || null;
      kids.forEach(function (c) { c.section = sec; });
      var ups = [];
      list.forEach(function (x, i) {
        var want = (i + 1) * 10;
        if (x.sort !== want || String(x.id) === dragId || blockIds.indexOf(String(x.id)) >= 0) {
          x.sort = want;
          ups.push(db.from('onboarding_template').update({
            sort: want, section: x.section, parent_id: x.parent_id || null
          }).eq('id', x.id));
        }
      });
      await Promise.all(ups);
      try {
        await db.from('change_log').insert({
          entity_type: 'onboarding', entity_id: String(drag.id), field: 'moved',
          old_value: null, new_value: sec + (drag.parent_id ? ' (as a subtask)' : ''),
          changed_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff'
        });
      } catch (e2) { /* log only */ }
      toast('Moved');
      if (typeof wa37Load === 'function') await wa37Load(true);
      if (typeof wa37Master === 'function') wa37Master();
    } catch (e) { toast('Could not move it'); }
  }
  function w65OnbDecorate() {
    try {
      var edits = document.querySelectorAll('[data-w37e]');
      if (!edits.length) return;
      var any = false;
      edits.forEach(function (a) {
        var row = (a.closest && a.closest('.w46st')) || a.parentElement;
        if (!row || row.dataset.w65d) return;
        var id = a.getAttribute('data-w37e');
        row.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', w65HandleHtml('onb', id));
        w65WireRow(row, 'onb', id);
        any = true;
      });
      if (any && !document.getElementById('w65onbhint')) {
        var host = document.getElementById('w46master')
          || ((edits[0].closest && edits[0].closest('.modal-card, .modal')) || null);
        if (host) {
          host.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',
            '<div class="w65hint" id="w65onbhint">Drag any step by its handle: up or down, into another week, next to a main task to make it a main task, or next to a subtask to nest it there. Reordering never re-alerts people who already finished.</div>');
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (typeof wa37Master === 'function') {
    var __ms65 = wa37Master;
    wa37Master = function () {
      __ms65.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(w65OnbDecorate, 60);
    };
  }
  /* the HR centre paints the master editor directly, so watch for it */
  try {
    var __w65mo;
    var w65MoKick = function () {
      clearTimeout(__w65mo);
      __w65mo = setTimeout(function () {
        if (document.querySelector('[data-w37e]:not([data-w65seen])')) {
          document.querySelectorAll('[data-w37e]').forEach(function (a) { a.setAttribute('data-w65seen', '1'); });
          w65OnbDecorate();
        }
      }, 250);
    };
    new MutationObserver(w65MoKick).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }

  /* ---- volunteering master SOP ---- */
  function w65SopKidsOf(all, id) {
    var out = [];
    var direct = all.filter(function (x) { return String(x.parent_id || '') === String(id); });
    direct.forEach(function (c) { out.push(c); out = out.concat(w65SopKidsOf(all, c.id)); });
    return out;
  }
  async function w65SopDrop(dragId, targetId, before) {
    try {
      var all = (typeof WA_VOL_TMPL !== 'undefined') ? WA_VOL_TMPL : [];
      var drag = all.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === dragId; })[0];
      var target = all.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === targetId; })[0];
      if (!drag || !target) return;
      var block, insertAt, rest, ti;
      if ((drag.depth || 0) === 0) {
        /* a section header: move the whole section around the target's section */
        block = all.filter(function (x) { return x.section === drag.section; });
        var blockIds0 = block.map(function (x) { return String(x.id); });
        if (target.section === drag.section) { toast('That is the same section'); return; }
        rest = all.filter(function (x) { return blockIds0.indexOf(String(x.id)) < 0; });
        var secRows = rest.map(function (x) { return x.section; });
        insertAt = before ? secRows.indexOf(target.section) : secRows.lastIndexOf(target.section) + 1;
        if (insertAt < 0) return;
      } else {
        var kids = w65SopKidsOf(all, dragId);
        var blockIds = [dragId].concat(kids.map(function (x) { return String(x.id); }));
        if (blockIds.indexOf(targetId) >= 0) { toast('A step cannot move into itself'); return; }
        rest = all.filter(function (x) { return blockIds.indexOf(String(x.id)) < 0; });
        ti = rest.map(function (x) { return String(x.id); }).indexOf(targetId);
        if (ti < 0) return;
        var newParent, newDepth;
        if ((target.depth || 0) === 0) {
          /* dropped on a section header: become its first step */
          newParent = target.id;
          newDepth = 1;
          insertAt = ti + 1;
        } else {
          newParent = target.parent_id || null;
          newDepth = target.depth || 1;
          insertAt = before ? ti : ti + 1;
          if (!before) { /* after a step means after its whole subtree */
            var tKids = w65SopKidsOf(rest, targetId).map(function (x) { return String(x.id); });
            while (insertAt < rest.length && tKids.indexOf(String(rest[insertAt].id)) >= 0) insertAt++;
          }
        }
        var delta = newDepth - (drag.depth || 0);
        drag.section = target.section;
        drag.parent_id = newParent;
        drag.depth = newDepth;
        kids.forEach(function (c) { c.section = target.section; c.depth = Math.max(1, (c.depth || 1) + delta); });
        block = [drag].concat(kids);
      }
      var merged = rest.slice(0, insertAt).concat(block, rest.slice(insertAt));
      /* global renumber (the SOP loads ordered by sort across sections) */
      var ups = [];
      merged.forEach(function (x, i) {
        var want = (i + 1) * 10;
        var isMoved = block.indexOf(x) >= 0;
        if (x.sort !== want || isMoved) {
          x.sort = want;
          ups.push(db.from('sop_template').update({
            sort: want, section: x.section, parent_id: x.parent_id || null, depth: x.depth || 0
          }).eq('id', x.id));
        }
      });
      /* keep the in-memory order in sync so the re-render is right */
      WA_VOL_TMPL.length = 0;
      merged.forEach(function (x) { WA_VOL_TMPL.push(x); });
      await Promise.all(ups);
      try {
        await db.from('change_log').insert({
          entity_type: 'sop', entity_id: String(drag.id), field: 'moved',
          old_value: null, new_value: (drag.section || '') + (drag.parent_id ? ' (nested)' : ' (main step)'), /* audit 26/09/26: was an undeclared sec, so no move was logged */
          changed_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff'
        });
      } catch (e2) { /* log only */ }
      toast('Moved in the master; future events pick it up');
      if (typeof waVolRenderSop === 'function') waVolRenderSop();
    } catch (e) { toast('Could not move it'); }
  }
  function w65SopDecorate() {
    try {
      if (!(typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff())) return;
      var panel = document.getElementById('volunteering');
      if (!panel) return;
      var rows = panel.querySelectorAll('.vl-step[data-vlstep^="tmpl-"]');
      if (!rows.length) return;
      rows.forEach(function (row) {
        if (row.dataset.w65d) return;
        var id = String(row.getAttribute('data-vlstep')).slice(5);
        row.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', w65HandleHtml('sop', id));
        w65WireRow(row, 'sop', id);
      });
      if (!panel.querySelector('#w65sophint')) {
        var firstPhase = panel.querySelector('.vl-phase');
        if (firstPhase) firstPhase.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin',
          '<div class="w65hint" id="w65sophint">Drag any step by its handle: up or down, into another section, next to a main step to promote a subtask, or next to a subtask to nest. Changes apply to the master and future events.</div>');
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (typeof waVolRenderSop === 'function') {
    var __sop65 = waVolRenderSop;
    waVolRenderSop = function () {
      __sop65.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(w65SopDecorate, 80);
    };
  }

  /* =========== wiring =========== */
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp65 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp65(id);
      if (id === 'transcripts') setTimeout(function () { w65TxForm(); w65DeptSync(); }, 220);
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa65 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa65();
      try { setTimeout(function () { w65TxForm(); }, 300); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      try { w65LoadWins(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v66 leadership meetings can be open to everyone
   (04/08/26)
   v65 locked a Leadership meeting to leaders-and-admins visibility.
   Now the Meeting visibility select stays usable: the fan-out across
   all departments happens either way, and the uploader chooses whether
   the meeting (and everything it files) is open to everyone, kept to
   leaders and admins, or confidential to picked people. The note under
   the form explains the current choice, and the pipeline guide and
   preview popup wording follow along. Server side: process-leadership
   v2 stamps rows with the transcript's visibility (null = open), and
   transcript-requests v6 keys the leadership spread off the department
   field too.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa66) return;
  window.__wa66 = 1;

  function w66IsLeadDept() {
    var dep = document.getElementById('txDept');
    return !!(dep && dep.value === 'Leadership meeting');
  }

  function w66Note() {
    var note = document.getElementById('w65leadnote');
    if (!note) return;
    var sel = document.getElementById('w62type');
    var v = sel ? sel.value : 'leadership';
    if (v === 'department') {
      note.textContent = 'Leadership meeting, open to everyone: its updates, tasks and suggested requests spread across ALL departments, and the whole team can see them.';
    } else if (v === 'private') {
      note.textContent = 'Leadership meeting, confidential: it spreads across ALL departments, but only the people you pick (plus admins) see the meeting and what it files.';
    } else if (v === 'speakers') {
      note.textContent = 'Leadership meeting as a one-on-one: it spreads across ALL departments, but only the people you pick see the meeting and what it files, not even admins.';
    } else {
      note.textContent = 'Leadership meeting: its updates, tasks and suggested requests spread across ALL departments. Visible to leaders and admins only.';
    }
  }

  /* runs after wave65's sync (registered later on the same targets) */
  function w66Sync() {
    try {
      var sel = document.getElementById('w62type');
      if (!sel) return;
      if (w66IsLeadDept()) {
        sel.disabled = false; /* v65 locked it; the choice is yours again */
        w66Note();
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  if (!document.body.dataset.wa66Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa66Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
      if (!e.target) return;
      if (e.target.id === 'txDept') setTimeout(w66Sync, 0);
      if (e.target.id === 'w62type' && w66IsLeadDept()) setTimeout(w66Note, 0);
    });
  }

  /* keep the popups honest about the choice */
  function w66PatchModals() {
    try {
      /* the v65 preview modal note */
      document.querySelectorAll('.modal-back p').forEach(function (p) {
        var t = p.textContent || '';
        if (t.indexOf('stay visible to leaders and admins only') >= 0) {
          var sel = document.getElementById('w62type');
          var v = sel ? sel.value : 'leadership';
          if (v === 'department') {
            p.textContent = 'Leadership meeting, open to everyone: items spread across every department and the whole team can see them.';
          } else if (v === 'private') {
            p.textContent = 'Leadership meeting, confidential: items spread across every department but only the people you picked (plus admins) see them.';
          }
        }
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('.modal-back .w61sec .t span').forEach(function (s) {
        var t = s.textContent || '';
        if (t.indexOf('Leadership meetings spread their tasks and updates across ALL departments but stay visible to leaders and admins only.') >= 0) {
          s.textContent = t.replace(
            'Leadership meetings spread their tasks and updates across ALL departments but stay visible to leaders and admins only.',
            'Leadership meetings spread their tasks and updates across ALL departments; you choose whether they are open to everyone, kept to leaders and admins, or confidential.');
        }
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  try {
    var __w66mo;
    new MutationObserver(function () {
      clearTimeout(__w66mo);
      __w66mo = setTimeout(w66PatchModals, 120);
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }

  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp66 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp66(id);
      if (id === 'transcripts') setTimeout(w66Sync, 350);
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa66 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa66();
      setTimeout(w66Sync, 450);
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v67 the training catalog becomes a browsable library
   (04/08/26)
   The Training page catalog and the HR centre Trainings tab get a
   Netflix-style browser: one carousel row per training path (a tool or
   learning path) and per category, a search box, and filter chips.
   Categories are linked to departments (training_categories table) and
   show as bubble tags in that department's colour. Cards open a detail
   modal with the full description, the link, mark-done, assign, and
   (admins and leaders) the existing edit modal. A new "Using the Hub"
   path of 8 modules teaches the Hub itself.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa67) return;
  window.__wa67 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa67css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w67bar{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:2px 0 10px;}' +
    '.w67q{flex:1;min-width:180px;max-width:320px;padding:7px 12px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:999px;font-size:13px;background:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '.w67chips{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;}' +
    '.w67chip{display:inline-block;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 12px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid transparent;user-select:none;}' +
    '.w67chip.path{background:#FFFAF2;border-color:var(--wa-line);color:#1F201E;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w67chip.on{outline:2px solid rgba(16,63,57,.55);outline-offset:1px;}' +
    '.w67rowwrap{margin:0 0 16px;}' +
    '.w67rh{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:baseline;margin:0 0 6px;}' +
    '.w67rh b{font-size:13.5px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w67rh .n{font-size:11px;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w67rh .nav{margin-left:auto;display:flex;gap:4px;}' +
    '.w67rh .nav button{border:1px solid var(--wa-line);background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;width:26px;height:26px;cursor:pointer;color:#103F39;font-weight:800;line-height:1;}' +
    '.w67row{display:flex;gap:10px;overflow-x:auto;padding:4px 2px 10px;scroll-behavior:smooth;scrollbar-width:thin;}' +
    '.w67card{flex:0 0 205px;max-width:205px;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:12px;padding:11px 12px;cursor:pointer;transition:box-shadow .12s, transform .12s;}' +
    '.w67card:hover{box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(16,63,57,.12);transform:translateY(-2px);}' +
    '.w67card h5{margin:0 0 5px;font-size:12.8px;line-height:1.35;color:#103F39;display:-webkit-box;-webkit-line-clamp:2;-webkit-box-orient:vertical;overflow:hidden;min-height:34px;}' +
    '.w67meta{font-size:10.5px;color:#797773;margin:0 0 6px;}' +
    '.w67tag{display:inline-block;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 9px;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;margin:0 4px 4px 0;}' +
    '.w67done{display:inline-block;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 9px;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;background:#ECEBE3;color:#2c5a44;}' +
    '.w67done.todo{background:#f6ecd2;color:#7a5a12;}' +
    '.w67empty{font-size:12.5px;color:#797773;padding:6px 0;}' +
    '#w67m .w67desc{max-height:44vh;overflow-y:auto;font-size:13px;line-height:1.55;color:#1F201E;margin:8px 0;}' +
    '#w67m .w67desc p{margin:0 0 8px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var WA67 = { cats: null };
  async function w67Cats() {
    if (WA67.cats) return WA67.cats;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('training_categories').select('*').order('sort');
      WA67.cats = {};
      ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (c) { WA67.cats[c.name] = c.department || null; });
    } catch (e) { WA67.cats = {}; }
    return WA67.cats;
  }
  function w67Dept(cat) { return (WA67.cats && WA67.cats[cat]) || null; }
  function w67Color(cat) {
    var d = w67Dept(cat);
    return (d && typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || '#797773';
  }
  function w67Tag(cat) {
    var c = w67Color(cat);
    return '<span class="w67tag" style="background:' + c + '1f;color:' + c + '">' + esc(cat || '') + '</span>';
  }
  function w67Me() { return (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null; }
  function w67Mine(tid) {
    var me = w67Me();
    if (!me) return null;
    var pr = (typeof wa36ProgFor === 'function') ? wa36ProgFor(tid) : [];
    return pr.filter(function (x) { return String(x.person_name || '').toLowerCase() === me.name.toLowerCase(); })[0] || null;
  }

  function w67Card(t) {
    var mine = w67Mine(t.id);
    var meta = [];
    if (t.duration_sec && typeof wa36Dur === 'function') meta.push(wa36Dur(t.duration_sec));
    meta.push(t.source === 'clickup' ? 'ClickUp' : (t.source === 'loom' ? 'Loom' : 'In the Hub'));
    return '<div class="w67card" data-w67t="' + t.id + '" data-w67cat="' + esc(t.category || '') + '"'
      + ' data-w67path="' + esc(t.path_group || '') + '"'
      + ' data-w67s="' + esc(((t.title || '') + ' ' + (t.description || '') + ' ' + (t.category || '') + ' ' + (t.path_group || '')).toLowerCase()) + '">'
      + '<h5>' + esc(t.title) + '</h5>'
      + '<div class="w67meta">' + esc(meta.join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</div>'
      + w67Tag(t.category)
      + (mine ? '<span class="w67done' + (mine.status === 'done' ? '' : ' todo') + '">' + (mine.status === 'done' ? 'done' : 'assigned to you') + '</span>' : '')
      + '</div>';
  }

  function w67Row(key, title, subtitle, items) {
    if (!items.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w67rowwrap" data-w67row="' + esc(key) + '">'
      + '<div class="w67rh"><b>' + esc(title) + '</b><span class="n">' + items.length
      + (subtitle ? ' \u00b7 ' + esc(subtitle) : '') + '</span>'
      + '<span class="nav"><button data-w67nav="-1">&#8249;</button><button data-w67nav="1">&#8250;</button></span></div>'
      + '<div class="w67row">' + items.map(w67Card).join('') + '</div></div>';
  }

  function w67Browser(idSuffix) {
    var all = (typeof WA36_TRAININGS !== 'undefined' ? WA36_TRAININGS : []).filter(function (t) { return t.active !== false; });
    if (!all.length) return '';
    var me = w67Me();
    /* paths first */
    var paths = {};
    all.forEach(function (t) { if (t.path_group) (paths[t.path_group] = paths[t.path_group] || []).push(t); });
    var pathKeys = Object.keys(paths).sort();
    /* categories for everything else */
    var cats = {};
    all.forEach(function (t) { if (!t.path_group) (cats[t.category || 'General'] = cats[t.category || 'General'] || []).push(t); });
    var catKeys = Object.keys(cats).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a).localeCompare(String(b)); });

    /* chips cover every category, including ones only seen inside paths */
    var chipCats = {};
    all.forEach(function (t) { chipCats[t.category || 'General'] = 1; });
    var chips = '<span class="w67chip path on" data-w67c="all" style="background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;border-color:#103F39">All</span>';
    Object.keys(chipCats).sort().forEach(function (c) {
      var col = w67Color(c);
      chips += '<span class="w67chip" data-w67c="cat:' + esc(c) + '" style="background:' + col + '1f;color:' + col + '">' + esc(c) + '</span>';
    });
    pathKeys.forEach(function (p) {
      chips += '<span class="w67chip path" data-w67c="path:' + esc(p) + '">' + esc(p) + '</span>';
    });

    var rows = '';
    pathKeys.forEach(function (p) {
      var items = paths[p].slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0); });
      var sub = 'training path';
      if (me) {
        var d = items.filter(function (t) { var m = w67Mine(t.id); return m && m.status === 'done'; }).length;
        sub = 'training path \u00b7 you: ' + d + ' of ' + items.length + ' done';
      }
      rows += w67Row('path:' + p, p, sub, items);
    });
    catKeys.forEach(function (c) {
      rows += w67Row('cat:' + c, c, w67Dept(c) ? ((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[w67Dept(c)]) || '') : '', cats[c].slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0); }));
    });

    return '<div class="w67wrap" id="w67' + idSuffix + '" data-w67f="all" data-w67q="">'
      + '<div class="w67bar"><input class="w67q" type="search" placeholder="Search the trainings..." />'
      + '<div class="w67chips">' + chips + '</div></div>'
      + rows
      + '<p class="w67empty" style="display:none">Nothing matches that search.</p>'
      + '</div>';
  }

  function w67Apply(wrap) {
    var q = (wrap.dataset.w67q || '').toLowerCase();
    var f = wrap.dataset.w67f || 'all';
    var any = false;
    wrap.querySelectorAll('.w67rowwrap').forEach(function (row) {
      var key = row.getAttribute('data-w67row');
      var rowVisible = 0;
      row.querySelectorAll('.w67card').forEach(function (card) {
        var ok = true;
        if (q && (card.getAttribute('data-w67s') || '').indexOf(q) < 0) ok = false;
        if (ok && f !== 'all') {
          if (f.indexOf('path:') === 0) ok = key === f;
          else if (f.indexOf('cat:') === 0) ok = ('cat:' + card.getAttribute('data-w67cat')) === f;
        }
        card.style.display = ok ? '' : 'none';
        if (ok) rowVisible++;
      });
      row.style.display = rowVisible ? '' : 'none';
      if (rowVisible) any = true;
    });
    var empty = wrap.querySelector('.w67empty');
    if (empty) empty.style.display = any ? 'none' : '';
  }

  function w67Modal(tid) {
    var t = null;
    (typeof WA36_TRAININGS !== 'undefined' ? WA36_TRAININGS : []).forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(tid)) t = x; });
    if (!t) return;
    var mine = w67Mine(t.id);
    var pr = (typeof wa36ProgFor === 'function') ? wa36ProgFor(t.id) : [];
    var doneN = pr.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'done'; }).length;
    var people = (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []).slice()
      .sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var canEdit = false;
    try { canEdit = typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && (WA40_MINE.role === 'admin' || WA40_MINE.role === 'leader'); } catch (e) { /* no */ }
    var desc = t.description
      ? ((typeof waVolMd === 'function') ? waVolMd(t.description) : '<p>' + esc(t.description) + '</p>')
      : '<p style="color:#797773">No description yet.</p>';
    openModal('<div id="w67m"><h3 style="margin-bottom:2px">' + esc(t.title) + '</h3>'
      + '<div style="margin:4px 0 2px">' + w67Tag(t.category)
      + (t.path_group ? '<span class="w67tag" style="background:#ECEBE3;color:#1F201E">' + esc(t.path_group) + '</span>' : '')
      + (t.duration_sec && typeof wa36Dur === 'function' ? '<span class="w67tag" style="background:#ECEBE3;color:#1F201E">' + wa36Dur(t.duration_sec) + '</span>' : '')
      + (doneN ? '<span class="w67tag" style="background:#ECEBE3;color:#2c5a44">' + doneN + ' people done</span>' : '')
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w67desc">' + desc + '</div>'
      + '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center">'
      + (t.resource_url ? '<a class="btn mini" href="' + esc(t.resource_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="text-decoration:none">Open the training</a>' : '')
      + '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-w36me="' + t.id + '" data-st="' + (mine && mine.status === 'done' ? 'done' : 'todo') + '">'
      + (mine && mine.status === 'done' ? 'Mark not done' : 'Mark it done for me') + '</button>'
      + '<select class="w36sel" data-w36assign="' + t.id + '"><option value="">Assign to...</option>'
      + people.map(function (pp) { return '<option value="' + esc(pp.name) + '">' + esc(pp.name) + '</option>'; }).join('')
      + '</select>'
      + (canEdit ? '<a data-w43te="' + t.id + '" style="font-size:12px">Edit this training</a>' : '')
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn ghost" id="w67mc">Close</button></div></div>');
    var c = document.getElementById('w67mc');
    if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  }

  /* ---- wiring (once, delegated) ---- */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa67Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa67Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
      var q = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w67q');
      if (!q) return;
      var wrap = q.closest('.w67wrap');
      wrap.dataset.w67q = q.value.trim().toLowerCase();
      w67Apply(wrap);
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var chip = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w67c]');
      if (chip) {
        var wrap = chip.closest('.w67wrap');
        var cur = wrap.dataset.w67f;
        var val = chip.getAttribute('data-w67c');
        wrap.dataset.w67f = (cur === val) ? 'all' : val;
        wrap.querySelectorAll('.w67chip').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('on'); });
        var sel = wrap.dataset.w67f === 'all' ? wrap.querySelector('[data-w67c="all"]') : chip;
        if (sel) sel.classList.add('on');
        w67Apply(wrap);
        return;
      }
      var nav = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w67nav]');
      if (nav) {
        var row = nav.closest('.w67rowwrap').querySelector('.w67row');
        if (row) row.scrollBy({ left: 430 * Number(nav.getAttribute('data-w67nav')), behavior: 'smooth' });
        return;
      }
      var card = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w67t]');
      if (card) { w67Modal(card.getAttribute('data-w67t')); return; }
    });
  }

  /* ---- the personal Training page: swap the old flat catalog ---- */
  async function w67TrainingPage() {
    try {
      var body = document.getElementById('w36body');
      if (!body || body.querySelector('#w67me')) return;
      if (typeof WA36_TRAININGS === 'undefined' || !WA36_TRAININGS.length) return;
      await w67Cats();
      var html = w67Browser('me');
      if (!html) return;
      var anchor = null;
      body.querySelectorAll('.w44cat').forEach(function (x) {
        if ((x.textContent || '').indexOf('The full catalog') >= 0) anchor = x;
      });
      var first = body.querySelector('.w36cat');
      var host = document.createElement('div');
      host.innerHTML = html;
      if (anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', host);
      else if (first) first.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', host);
      else body.appendChild(host);
      /* retire the flat grid */
      body.querySelectorAll('.w36cat, .w36grid').forEach(function (x) { x.remove(); });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (typeof wa36Render === 'function') {
    var __tr67 = wa36Render;
    wa36Render = async function () {
      await __tr67.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(w67TrainingPage, 250);
    };
  }

  /* ---- the HR centre Trainings tab ---- */
  async function w67HrCentre() {
    try {
      var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
      if (!body || body.querySelector('#w67hr')) return;
      var h3s = body.querySelectorAll('.w46sec h3');
      var sec = null;
      h3s.forEach(function (h) { if ((h.textContent || '').trim() === 'Trainings') sec = h.parentElement; });
      if (!sec) return;
      if (typeof WA36_TRAININGS === 'undefined' || !WA36_TRAININGS.length) {
        if (typeof wa36Load === 'function') { try { await wa36Load(false); } catch (e2) { /* offline */ } }
      }
      await w67Cats();
      var html = w67Browser('hr');
      if (!html) return;
      var tiles = sec.querySelector('.w44tiles');
      var host = document.createElement('div');
      host.innerHTML = '<div style="margin:10px 0 4px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;font-size:13px">Browse the catalog</div>' + html;
      if (tiles) tiles.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', host);
      else sec.appendChild(host);
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  try {
    var __w67mo;
    new MutationObserver(function () {
      clearTimeout(__w67mo);
      __w67mo = setTimeout(function () {
        var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
        if (body && !body.querySelector('#w67hr')) w67HrCentre();
      }, 300);
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa67 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa67();
      try { w67Cats(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v68 the Goals page becomes Strategy: four tabs
   (04/08/26)
   The Work menu tab renames from Goals to Strategy, and the page splits
   into four tabs:
   1. Projects: every active project grouped by department, with its
      status, linked goal, and open task count; click through to the
      project page.
   2. Department goals: the existing quarter-by-quarter view (edit,
      link projects, AI suggestions), unchanged, minus the org tier.
   3. Organizational goals: the yearly and multi-year direction that
      department goals and projects roll up into.
   4. The map: a three-column linkage diagram (projects, department
      quarterly goals, organizational goals) with drawn connectors;
      hover any node to light up everything it links to.
   The underlying page machinery (waGoalRow, edit modals, suggestions,
   the "How this fits together" button) is untouched; v68 only
   reorganizes the rendered DOM and adds the two new views.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa68) return;
  window.__wa68 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa68css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '#w68tabs{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 14px;}' +
    '.w68tab{border:1px solid var(--wa-line);background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;padding:6px 16px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:#1F201E;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w68tab.on{background:#103F39;border-color:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '.w68sub{font-size:12.5px;color:#6b7a75;margin:0 0 10px;}' +
    '.w68pgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(280px,1fr));gap:10px;align-items:start;}' +
    '.w68pdept{margin:0 0 16px;}' +
    '.w68pdept h4{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:13px;}' +
    '.w68proj{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-left:4px solid #ccc;border-radius:12px;padding:11px 13px;}' +
    '.w68proj b{font-size:13px;color:#103F39;display:block;margin-bottom:3px;}' +
    '.w68proj .d{font-size:12px;color:#797773;line-height:1.45;display:-webkit-box;-webkit-line-clamp:2;-webkit-box-orient:vertical;overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:6px;}' +
    '.w68pill{display:inline-block;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 9px;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;margin:0 4px 4px 0;background:#ECEBE3;color:#1F201E;}' +
    '.w68pill.goal{background:rgba(220,165,39,.15);color:#8a6414;}' +
    '.w68pill.none{background:#f2f2f0;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w68open{font-size:11.5px;color:#103F39;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '#w68map{position:relative;}' +
    '#w68map svg{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;pointer-events:none;}' +
    '.w68cols{position:relative;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:70px;z-index:1;}' +
    '.w68col h4{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:12px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w68col .hint{font-size:11px;color:#797773;margin:0 0 10px;}' +
    '.w68node{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-left:4px solid #ccc;border-radius:10px;padding:7px 10px;font-size:12px;color:#103F39;margin:0 0 8px;cursor:default;line-height:1.35;}' +
    '.w68node .m{font-size:10.5px;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w68node.hl{box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(16,63,57,.55);}' +
    '.w68node.dim{opacity:.32;}' +
    'path.w68e{fill:none;stroke:#c9d4cf;stroke-width:1.6;}' +
    'path.w68e.hl{stroke:#103F39;stroke-width:2.4;}' +
    'path.w68e.dim{opacity:.15;}' +
    '@media (max-width:800px){.w68cols{gap:26px;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var WA68 = { view: 'dept', edges: [], projects: null };
  function w68Col(d) { return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || '#797773'; }
  function w68Lab(d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; }

  async function w68Projects() {
    var p = (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' && WA27_PROJECTS && WA27_PROJECTS.length) ? WA27_PROJECTS : WA68.projects;
    if (p && p.length) return p;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('projects').select('*').order('department').order('sort');
      WA68.projects = (r && r.data) || [];
    } catch (e) { WA68.projects = []; }
    return WA68.projects;
  }
  function w68GoalById(id) {
    var out = null;
    (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).forEach(function (g) { if (String(g.id) === String(id)) out = g; });
    return out;
  }
  function w68OpenTasks(pid) {
    try {
      return (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) {
        return !t.done && String(t.project_id) === String(pid);
      }).length;
    } catch (e) { return 0; }
  }
  function w68ProjName(p, all) {
    var nm = p.name;
    if (p.parent_id) {
      all.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(p.parent_id)) nm = x.name + ' > ' + nm; });
    }
    return nm;
  }

  /* ---------- the Projects view ---------- */
  function w68ProjView(projects) {
    var act = projects.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'active'; });
    var order = (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined') ? DEPT_ORDER.slice() : [];
    act.forEach(function (p) { if (order.indexOf(p.department) < 0) order.push(p.department); });
    var html = '<p class="w68sub">Every active project, by department. Tasks from meetings route into these automatically; each project rolls up into a goal.</p>';
    order.forEach(function (d) {
      var ps = act.filter(function (p) { return p.department === d; });
      if (!ps.length) return;
      html += '<div class="w68pdept"><h4 style="color:' + w68Col(d) + '">' + esc(w68Lab(d)) + '</h4><div class="w68pgrid">';
      ps.forEach(function (p) {
        var g = p.goal_id ? w68GoalById(p.goal_id) : null;
        var open = w68OpenTasks(p.id);
        html += '<div class="w68proj" style="border-left-color:' + w68Col(d) + '">'
          + '<b>' + esc(w68ProjName(p, projects)) + '</b>'
          + (p.description ? '<div class="d">' + esc(p.description) + '</div>' : '')
          + '<div>'
          + (g ? '<span class="w68pill goal">&#127919; ' + esc(g.title.slice(0, 60)) + (g.title.length > 60 ? '...' : '') + '</span>'
               : '<span class="w68pill none">no goal linked yet</span>')
          + '<span class="w68pill">' + open + ' open ' + (open === 1 ? 'task' : 'tasks') + '</span>'
          + '</div>'
          + '<a class="w68open" data-w39pp="' + p.id + '">Open the project page &raquo;</a>'
          + '</div>';
      });
      html += '</div></div>';
    });
    if (html.indexOf('w68proj') < 0) html += '<p class="read">No active projects yet.</p>';
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- the map view ---------- */
  function w68Node(kind, id, dept, title, meta) {
    return '<div class="w68node" data-w68n="' + kind + id + '" style="border-left-color:' + w68Col(dept) + '">'
      + esc(title) + (meta ? '<div class="m">' + esc(meta) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function w68MapView(projects) {
    var goals = (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).filter(function (g) {
      return ['closed', 'done', 'archived'].indexOf(String(g.status)) < 0;
    });
    var org = goals.filter(function (g) { return g.department === 'org'; });
    var dept = goals.filter(function (g) { return g.department !== 'org'; });
    var act = projects.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'active'; });
    var orgIds = {}; org.forEach(function (g) { orgIds[String(g.id)] = 1; });
    var deptIds = {}; dept.forEach(function (g) { deptIds[String(g.id)] = 1; });

    WA68.edges = [];
    act.forEach(function (p) {
      if (!p.goal_id) return;
      if (deptIds[String(p.goal_id)]) WA68.edges.push(['P' + p.id, 'G' + p.goal_id]);
      else if (orgIds[String(p.goal_id)]) WA68.edges.push(['P' + p.id, 'O' + p.goal_id]);
    });
    dept.forEach(function (g) {
      if (g.parent_goal_id && orgIds[String(g.parent_goal_id)]) WA68.edges.push(['G' + g.id, 'O' + g.parent_goal_id]);
    });

    var html = '<p class="w68sub">How it all connects: projects feed department quarterly goals, and both roll up into the long term organizational goals. Hover anything to light up its links.</p>'
      + '<div id="w68map"><svg></svg><div class="w68cols">'
      + '<div class="w68col"><h4>Projects</h4><div class="hint">the work underway</div>'
      + act.map(function (p) { return w68Node('P', p.id, p.department, w68ProjName(p, projects), w68Lab(p.department)); }).join('')
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w68col"><h4>Department goals</h4><div class="hint">quarterly</div>'
      + dept.map(function (g) { return w68Node('G', g.id, g.department, g.title, w68Lab(g.department) + (g.quarter ? ' \u00b7 ' + g.quarter : '')); }).join('')
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w68col"><h4>Organizational goals</h4><div class="hint">yearly and multi-year</div>'
      + org.map(function (g) { return w68Node('O', g.id, 'board', g.title, g.quarter || 'long term'); }).join('')
      + '</div>'
      + '</div></div>';
    return html;
  }
  function w68Draw() {
    var map = document.getElementById('w68map');
    if (!map || map.offsetParent === null) return;
    var svg = map.querySelector('svg');
    if (!svg) return;
    var box = map.getBoundingClientRect();
    svg.setAttribute('viewBox', '0 0 ' + box.width + ' ' + map.scrollHeight);
    svg.style.height = map.scrollHeight + 'px';
    var pos = {};
    map.querySelectorAll('.w68node').forEach(function (n) {
      var r = n.getBoundingClientRect();
      pos[n.getAttribute('data-w68n')] = {
        l: r.left - box.left, r: r.right - box.left,
        y: r.top - box.top + r.height / 2
      };
    });
    var d = '';
    var parts = [];
    WA68.edges.forEach(function (e) {
      var a = pos[e[0]], b = pos[e[1]];
      if (!a || !b) return;
      var x1 = a.r, y1 = a.y, x2 = b.l, y2 = b.y;
      var mx = (x1 + x2) / 2;
      parts.push('<path class="w68e" data-w68e="' + e[0] + '|' + e[1] + '" d="M' + x1 + ' ' + y1 + ' C' + mx + ' ' + y1 + ', ' + mx + ' ' + y2 + ', ' + x2 + ' ' + y2 + '" />');
    });
    svg.innerHTML = parts.join('');
  }
  function w68Connected(id) {
    var set = {};
    set[id] = 1;
    var grew = true;
    while (grew) {
      grew = false;
      WA68.edges.forEach(function (e) {
        if (set[e[0]] && !set[e[1]]) { set[e[1]] = 1; grew = true; }
        if (set[e[1]] && !set[e[0]]) { set[e[0]] = 1; grew = true; }
      });
    }
    return set;
  }
  function w68Hover(id) {
    var map = document.getElementById('w68map');
    if (!map) return;
    if (!id) {
      map.querySelectorAll('.w68node, path.w68e').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('hl'); x.classList.remove('dim'); });
      return;
    }
    var set = w68Connected(id);
    map.querySelectorAll('.w68node').forEach(function (n) {
      var k = n.getAttribute('data-w68n');
      n.classList.toggle('hl', !!set[k]);
      n.classList.toggle('dim', !set[k]);
    });
    map.querySelectorAll('path.w68e').forEach(function (p) {
      var e = (p.getAttribute('data-w68e') || '').split('|');
      var on = set[e[0]] && set[e[1]];
      p.classList.toggle('hl', !!on);
      p.classList.toggle('dim', !on);
    });
  }

  /* ---------- tabs ---------- */
  function w68Show(view) {
    WA68.view = view;
    ['proj', 'dept', 'org', 'map'].forEach(function (v) {
      var el = document.getElementById('w68' + v + 'view');
      if (el) el.style.display = v === view ? '' : 'none';
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.w68tab').forEach(function (t) {
      t.classList.toggle('on', t.getAttribute('data-w68v') === view);
    });
    if (view === 'map') setTimeout(w68Draw, 60);
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa68Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa68Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w68v]');
      if (t) w68Show(t.getAttribute('data-w68v'));
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('mouseover', function (e) {
      var n = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w68node');
      if (n) w68Hover(n.getAttribute('data-w68n'));
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('mouseout', function (e) {
      var n = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w68node');
      if (n) w68Hover(null);
    });
    window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
      clearTimeout(window.__w68rz);
      window.__w68rz = setTimeout(w68Draw, 200);
    });
  }

  async function w68Tabs() {
    try {
      var body = document.getElementById('glBody');
      if (!body || document.getElementById('w68tabs')) return;
      var projects = await w68Projects();

      /* keep the "How this fits together" button on top, split the rest */
      var keepTop = null, orgBlock = null, rest = [];
      Array.from(body.children).forEach(function (ch) {
        if (ch.querySelector && ch.querySelector('#w34how')) keepTop = ch;
        else if (ch.classList && ch.classList.contains('w30org')) orgBlock = ch;
        else rest.push(ch);
      });

      var tabs = document.createElement('div');
      tabs.id = 'w68tabs';
      tabs.innerHTML = '<button class="w68tab" data-w68v="proj">Projects</button>'
        + '<button class="w68tab" data-w68v="dept">Department goals</button>'
        + '<button class="w68tab" data-w68v="org">Organizational goals</button>'
        + '<button class="w68tab" data-w68v="map">The map</button>';

      var projView = document.createElement('div');
      projView.id = 'w68projview';
      projView.innerHTML = w68ProjView(projects);

      var deptView = document.createElement('div');
      deptView.id = 'w68deptview';
      deptView.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<p class="w68sub">Quarterly goals by department; they roll up into the organizational tier. Edit, link projects, and review the Hub\u2019s suggestions here.</p>');
      rest.forEach(function (ch) { deptView.appendChild(ch); });

      var orgView = document.createElement('div');
      orgView.id = 'w68orgview';
      orgView.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<p class="w68sub">The yearly and multi-year direction of Welfare Avenue (one to ten years out). Department quarterly goals and projects roll up here.</p>');
      if (orgBlock) orgView.appendChild(orgBlock);

      var mapView = document.createElement('div');
      mapView.id = 'w68mapview';
      mapView.innerHTML = w68MapView(projects);

      body.innerHTML = '';
      if (keepTop) body.appendChild(keepTop);
      body.appendChild(tabs);
      body.appendChild(projView);
      body.appendChild(deptView);
      body.appendChild(orgView);
      body.appendChild(mapView);

      var head = document.querySelector('#goals .card h2');
      if (head) head.textContent = 'Department goals';
      var intro = document.querySelector('#goals .card p.read');
      if (intro) intro.textContent = 'Projects flow into department quarterly goals; both roll up into the long term organizational goals. Browse each tier, or open the map to see how everything links.';

      w68Show(WA68.view || 'dept');
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  if (typeof renderGoalsPage === 'function') {
    var __gp68 = renderGoalsPage;
    renderGoalsPage = async function () {
      await __gp68.apply(this, arguments);
      await w68Tabs();
    };
  }

  /* ---------- rename the nav tab (the bar is rebuilt by the nav registry) ---------- */
  function w68Rename() {
    try {
      var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
      if (bar) {
        bar.querySelectorAll('button, a').forEach(function (el) {
          if ((el.textContent || '').trim() === 'Goals') {
            el.innerHTML = el.innerHTML.replace('Goals', 'Department goals');
          }
        });
      }
      document.querySelectorAll('.w39tool').forEach(function (el) {
        if ((el.textContent || '').trim() === 'Goals') {
          el.innerHTML = el.innerHTML.replace('Goals', 'Department goals');
        }
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  try {
    var __w68mo;
    new MutationObserver(function () {
      clearTimeout(__w68mo);
      __w68mo = setTimeout(w68Rename, 200);
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa68 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa68();
      setTimeout(w68Rename, 400);
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v69 the Strategy map learns rollups and focus
   (04/08/26)
   1. Rollup counts everywhere: project nodes and cards show open and
      done task counts; department goal nodes show how many projects
      sit under them plus the task rollup; organizational goal nodes
      roll up their direct projects AND everything under their child
      quarterly goals.
   2. Click to focus: clicking any node on the map pulls its whole
      linked chain to the TOP of all three columns (so a project at the
      top and an org goal at the bottom sit next to each other),
      redraws the connectors, and dims the rest. Click again or hit
      Clear to restore the original order. Hover still previews.
   3. The project modal's goal dropdown is grouped: organizational
      goals first, then each department's quarterly goals, so linking
      a project to either tier is obvious. (Dept goal -> org goal
      linking already lives in the goal modal's rolls-up select.)
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa69) return;
  window.__wa69 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa69css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w69c{display:block;font-size:10px;color:#797773;margin-top:2px;}' +
    '.w69c b{color:#103F39;}' +
    '#w69bar{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;background:rgba(16,63,57,.07);border-radius:10px;padding:7px 12px;margin:0 0 10px;font-size:12.5px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '#w69bar button{border:1px solid var(--wa-line);background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 12px;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;margin-left:auto;}' +
    '.w68node{cursor:pointer;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var WA69 = { tasks: null, focus: null };

  async function w69Tasks() {
    if (WA69.tasks) return WA69.tasks;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').select('id,project_id,done').not('project_id', 'is', null).limit(2000);
      var by = {};
      ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (t) {
        var k = String(t.project_id);
        by[k] = by[k] || { open: 0, done: 0 };
        if (t.done) by[k].done++; else by[k].open++;
      });
      WA69.tasks = by;
    } catch (e) { WA69.tasks = {}; }
    return WA69.tasks;
  }
  function w69Projects() {
    var p = (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' && WA27_PROJECTS && WA27_PROJECTS.length) ? WA27_PROJECTS : [];
    return p.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'active'; });
  }
  function w69PC(pid) { return (WA69.tasks && WA69.tasks[String(pid)]) || { open: 0, done: 0 }; }
  function w69GoalRoll(gid, isOrg) {
    var projs = w69Projects().filter(function (p) { return String(p.goal_id) === String(gid); });
    if (isOrg) {
      var kids = (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).filter(function (g) {
        return String(g.parent_goal_id) === String(gid);
      });
      kids.forEach(function (k) {
        w69Projects().forEach(function (p) {
          if (String(p.goal_id) === String(k.id) && projs.indexOf(p) < 0) projs.push(p);
        });
      });
    }
    var open = 0, done = 0;
    projs.forEach(function (p) { var c = w69PC(p.id); open += c.open; done += c.done; });
    return { projects: projs.length, open: open, done: done };
  }
  function w69Line(txt) { return '<span class="w69c">' + txt + '</span>'; }

  /* ---------- decorate the map nodes and project cards ---------- */
  async function w69Decorate() {
    try {
      await w69Tasks();
      /* map nodes */
      document.querySelectorAll('#w68map .w68node').forEach(function (n, i) {
        if (!n.dataset.w69i) n.dataset.w69i = String(i);
        if (n.dataset.w69c) return;
        n.dataset.w69c = '1';
        var id = n.getAttribute('data-w68n') || '';
        var kind = id.slice(0, 1), raw = id.slice(1);
        var html = '';
        if (kind === 'P') {
          var c = w69PC(raw);
          html = w69Line('<b>' + c.open + '</b> open \u00b7 ' + c.done + ' done');
        } else if (kind === 'G') {
          var r1 = w69GoalRoll(raw, false);
          html = w69Line('<b>' + r1.projects + '</b> ' + (r1.projects === 1 ? 'project' : 'projects') + ' \u00b7 <b>' + r1.open + '</b> open \u00b7 ' + r1.done + ' done');
        } else if (kind === 'O') {
          var r2 = w69GoalRoll(raw, true);
          html = w69Line('<b>' + r2.projects + '</b> ' + (r2.projects === 1 ? 'project' : 'projects') + ' \u00b7 <b>' + r2.open + '</b> open \u00b7 ' + r2.done + ' done');
        }
        if (html) n.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
      });
      /* each department can add its own project right from the Projects tab */
      if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff() && typeof wa27ProjectModal === 'function') {
        document.querySelectorAll('#w68projview .w68pdept h4').forEach(function (h) {
          if (h.querySelector('[data-w69add]')) return;
          var label = (h.childNodes[0] && h.childNodes[0].textContent ? h.childNodes[0].textContent : h.textContent).trim();
          var dept = null;
          if (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined') {
            Object.keys(DEPT_LABEL).forEach(function (k) { if (DEPT_LABEL[k] === label) dept = k; });
          }
          if (!dept) dept = label;
          h.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
            ' <a data-w69add="' + esc(dept) + '" style="font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;margin-left:8px">+ Add a project</a>');
        });
      }
      /* project cards on the Projects tab: open + done */
      document.querySelectorAll('#w68projview .w68proj').forEach(function (card) {
        if (card.dataset.w69c) return;
        card.dataset.w69c = '1';
        var link = card.querySelector('[data-w39pp]');
        if (!link) return;
        var c = w69PC(link.getAttribute('data-w39pp'));
        card.querySelectorAll('.w68pill').forEach(function (pill) {
          if (pill.classList.contains('goal') || pill.classList.contains('none')) return;
          pill.textContent = c.open + ' open \u00b7 ' + c.done + ' done';
        });
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* ---------- click to focus: pull the chain to the top ---------- */
  function w69Edges() {
    var out = [];
    document.querySelectorAll('#w68map path.w68e').forEach(function (p) {
      var e = (p.getAttribute('data-w68e') || '').split('|');
      if (e.length === 2) out.push(e);
    });
    return out;
  }
  function w69Connected(id) {
    var set = {};
    set[id] = 1;
    var edges = w69Edges();
    var grew = true;
    while (grew) {
      grew = false;
      edges.forEach(function (e) {
        if (set[e[0]] && !set[e[1]]) { set[e[1]] = 1; grew = true; }
        if (set[e[1]] && !set[e[0]]) { set[e[0]] = 1; grew = true; }
      });
    }
    return set;
  }
  function w69Apply() {
    var map = document.getElementById('w68map');
    if (!map) return;
    if (!WA69.focus) {
      map.querySelectorAll('.w68node, path.w68e').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('hl'); x.classList.remove('dim'); });
      return;
    }
    var set = w69Connected(WA69.focus);
    map.querySelectorAll('.w68node').forEach(function (n) {
      var k = n.getAttribute('data-w68n');
      n.classList.toggle('hl', !!set[k]);
      n.classList.toggle('dim', !set[k]);
    });
    map.querySelectorAll('path.w68e').forEach(function (p) {
      var e = (p.getAttribute('data-w68e') || '').split('|');
      var on = set[e[0]] && set[e[1]];
      p.classList.toggle('hl', !!on);
      p.classList.toggle('dim', !on);
    });
  }
  function w69Order(set) {
    var map = document.getElementById('w68map');
    if (!map) return;
    map.querySelectorAll('.w68col').forEach(function (col) {
      var nodes = Array.from(col.querySelectorAll('.w68node'));
      if (!nodes.length) return;
      nodes.sort(function (a, b) {
        var ac = set ? (set[a.getAttribute('data-w68n')] ? 0 : 1) : 0;
        var bc = set ? (set[b.getAttribute('data-w68n')] ? 0 : 1) : 0;
        return ac - bc || Number(a.dataset.w69i || 0) - Number(b.dataset.w69i || 0);
      });
      nodes.forEach(function (n) { col.appendChild(n); });
    });
  }
  function w69Redraw() {
    try { window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize')); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function w69Bar(on, label) {
    var old = document.getElementById('w69bar');
    if (old) old.remove();
    if (!on) return;
    var map = document.getElementById('w68map');
    if (!map) return;
    map.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin',
      '<div id="w69bar">Showing everything linked to <b>&nbsp;' + esc(label) + '&nbsp;</b> (pulled to the top).'
      + '<button id="w69clear">Show everything</button></div>');
  }
  function w69Focus(id, label) {
    if (WA69.focus === id) { w69Clear(); return; }
    WA69.focus = id;
    w69Order(w69Connected(id));
    w69Apply();
    w69Bar(true, label);
    w69Redraw();
  }
  function w69Clear() {
    WA69.focus = null;
    w69Order(null);
    w69Apply();
    w69Bar(false);
    w69Redraw();
  }

  if (!document.body.dataset.wa69Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa69Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var add = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w69add]');
      if (add) {
        if (typeof wa27ProjectModal === 'function') wa27ProjectModal(add.getAttribute('data-w69add'), null);
        return;
      }
      if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w69clear')) { w69Clear(); return; }
      var n = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w68map .w68node');
      if (n) {
        var label = (n.childNodes[0] && n.childNodes[0].textContent) ? n.childNodes[0].textContent.trim() : 'this';
        w69Focus(n.getAttribute('data-w68n'), label.slice(0, 60));
      }
    });
    /* after wave68's mouseout wipes the classes, put the focus back */
    document.body.addEventListener('mouseout', function (e) {
      var n = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w68node');
      if (n && WA69.focus) setTimeout(w69Apply, 0);
    });
  }

  /* ---------- grouped goal dropdown in the project modal ---------- */
  function w69GoalSelect() {
    try {
      var sel = document.getElementById('w27pg');
      if (!sel || sel.dataset.w69g) return;
      sel.dataset.w69g = '1';
      var cur = sel.value;
      var goals = (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).filter(function (g) {
        return ['closed', 'done', 'archived'].indexOf(String(g.status)) < 0;
      });
      var org = goals.filter(function (g) { return g.department === 'org'; });
      var dl = function (d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d; };
      var html = '<option value="">No goal link</option>';
      if (org.length) {
        html += '<optgroup label="Organizational goals (long term)">' + org.map(function (g) {
          return '<option value="' + g.id + '">' + esc(String(g.title).slice(0, 70)) + '</option>';
        }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
      }
      var depts = [];
      goals.forEach(function (g) { if (g.department !== 'org' && depts.indexOf(g.department) < 0) depts.push(g.department); });
      depts.forEach(function (d) {
        html += '<optgroup label="' + esc(dl(d)) + ' (quarterly)">' + goals.filter(function (g) { return g.department === d; }).map(function (g) {
          return '<option value="' + g.id + '">' + esc((g.quarter ? g.quarter + ': ' : '') + String(g.title).slice(0, 70)) + '</option>';
        }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
      });
      sel.innerHTML = html;
      sel.value = cur;
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  try {
    var __w69mo;
    new MutationObserver(function () {
      clearTimeout(__w69mo);
      __w69mo = setTimeout(function () {
        w69GoalSelect();
        if (document.querySelector('#w68map .w68node:not([data-w69c])')
          || document.querySelector('#w68projview .w68proj:not([data-w69c])')) {
          WA69.focus = null;
          w69Decorate();
        }
      }, 250);
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }

  if (typeof renderGoalsPage === 'function') {
    var __gp69 = renderGoalsPage;
    renderGoalsPage = async function () {
      WA69.focus = null;
      WA69.tasks = null; /* refresh counts on each page render */
      await __gp69.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(w69Decorate, 200);
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v70 projects show who is working in them
   (04/08/26)
   Projects belong to a department but the work inside them often does
   not: operations people carry tasks under Rally Paper (a fundraising
   project), marketing carries tasks under Flag Football. So project
   cards on the Strategy page now show an "open tasks by team" row of
   department-coloured chips with counts, and the map's project nodes
   carry matching coloured dots, so every department can spot the
   projects from other departments' initiatives that they have tasks
   in. Data comes straight from dept_tasks (task department vs project
   department).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa70) return;
  window.__wa70 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa70css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w70row{margin-top:5px;font-size:10px;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w70chip{display:inline-block;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 8px;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;margin:2px 3px 0 0;}' +
    '.w70chip.x{box-shadow:0 0 0 1px currentColor inset;}' +
    '.w70dots{margin-top:3px;line-height:1;}' +
    '.w70dot{display:inline-block;width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:999px;margin-right:4px;vertical-align:middle;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var WA70 = { by: null };
  async function w70Load() {
    if (WA70.by) return WA70.by;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').select('id,project_id,department,done')
        .not('project_id', 'is', null).eq('done', false).limit(2000);
      var by = {};
      ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (t) {
        var k = String(t.project_id);
        by[k] = by[k] || {};
        by[k][t.department] = (by[k][t.department] || 0) + 1;
      });
      WA70.by = by;
    } catch (e) { WA70.by = {}; }
    return WA70.by;
  }
  function w70Col(d) { return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || '#797773'; }
  function w70Lab(d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; }

  async function w70Decorate() {
    try {
      var by = await w70Load();
      /* project cards: open tasks by team */
      document.querySelectorAll('#w68projview .w68proj').forEach(function (card) {
        if (card.dataset.w70) return;
        var link = card.querySelector('[data-w39pp]');
        if (!link) return;
        card.dataset.w70 = '1';
        var pid = String(link.getAttribute('data-w39pp'));
        var depts = by[pid] ? Object.keys(by[pid]) : [];
        if (!depts.length) return;
        var projDept = null;
        try {
          (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []).forEach(function (p) {
            if (String(p.id) === pid) projDept = p.department;
          });
        } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        depts.sort(function (a, b) { return by[pid][b] - by[pid][a]; });
        var chips = depts.map(function (d) {
          var c = w70Col(d);
          return '<span class="w70chip' + (projDept && d !== projDept ? ' x' : '') + '" style="background:' + c + '1f;color:' + c + '" title="'
            + esc(w70Lab(d)) + ': ' + by[pid][d] + ' open">' + esc(w70Lab(d)) + ' ' + by[pid][d] + '</span>';
        }).join('');
        link.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', '<div class="w70row">Open tasks by team: ' + chips + '</div>');
      });
      /* map project nodes: coloured dots per team with open tasks */
      document.querySelectorAll('#w68map .w68node[data-w68n^="P"]').forEach(function (n) {
        if (n.dataset.w70) return;
        n.dataset.w70 = '1';
        var pid = String(n.getAttribute('data-w68n')).slice(1);
        var depts = by[pid] ? Object.keys(by[pid]) : [];
        if (!depts.length) return;
        n.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<div class="w70dots">' + depts.map(function (d) {
          return '<span class="w70dot" style="background:' + w70Col(d) + '" title="' + esc(w70Lab(d)) + ': ' + by[pid][d] + ' open"></span>';
        }).join('') + '</div>');
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  try {
    var __w70mo;
    new MutationObserver(function () {
      clearTimeout(__w70mo);
      __w70mo = setTimeout(function () {
        if (document.querySelector('#w68projview .w68proj:not([data-w70])')
          || document.querySelector('#w68map .w68node[data-w68n^="P"]:not([data-w70])')) {
          w70Decorate();
        }
      }, 350);
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }

  if (typeof renderGoalsPage === 'function') {
    var __gp70 = renderGoalsPage;
    renderGoalsPage = async function () {
      WA70.by = null; /* refresh on each page render */
      await __gp70.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(w70Decorate, 350);
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v71 department tasks split into workstream tabs
   (04/08/26)
   Every department page gets a tab row above its tasks: All plus the
   department's workstreams (marketing: Website, Content, Digital
   advertising and Google Ads, Branding; fundraising: Fundraising
   events, Donor program, Grants; Ops Montreal: General, Logistics,
   Inventory, Program development; Ops Toronto: General, Logistics;
   HR: Organizational structure, Onboarding and recruitment, Trainings,
   Team and community). Picking a tab filters ALL views (list, board,
   calendar, the assignee filter) because the filter sits inside
   getTasks during the render. Tabs show open counts; unfiled tasks
   live in General; unknown subsections get their own auto tab so
   nothing hides. The task modal's Workstream select now always offers
   the canonical list (SECTIONS[..].subsections is patched at load).
   Every existing task feature (comments, project pill, due pill,
   flags, confidential, assignees) is untouched; the rows just get a
   cleaner card look.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa71) return;
  window.__wa71 = 1;

  var W71_WS = {
    marketing: [
      ['Website', 'The welfareavenue.com site: build, copy, SEO.'],
      ['Content', 'Posts, reels, the calendar, and channel content.'],
      ['Digital advertising and Google Ads', 'Paid and grant-funded advertising, tracking and results.'],
      ['Branding', 'The brand refresh: identity, templates, branded assets.']
    ],
    fundraising: [
      ['Fundraising events', 'Flag Football, Rally Paper, the Gift Basket Drive and friends.'],
      ['Donor program', 'Donor relationships, stewardship, Keela, recurring giving.'],
      ['Grants', 'Applications, reports, and funder relationships.'],
      ['Finance', 'Bookkeeping, statements, budgets and forecasts.']
    ],
    opsmtl: [
      ['General', 'Day to day operations work.'],
      ['Logistics', 'Transport, pickups, equipment and event logistics.'],
      ['Inventory', 'Supplies, storage, and reordering.']
    ],
    progdev: [
      ['General', 'Work that is not tied to one programme.'],
      ['Beneficiary programs', 'Programmes for the people we serve: employment, feedback, referrals.'],
      ['Research and data', 'Surveys, evaluation and the numbers behind the programmes.'],
      ['Community team', 'Community leads, partnerships and outreach.']
    ],
    opsto: [
      ['General', 'Day to day Toronto operations.'],
      ['Logistics', 'Toronto transport, pickups and event logistics.']
    ],
    hr: [
      ['Organizational structure', 'Policies, org chart, roles and accountability.'],
      ['Onboarding and recruitment', 'Finding people and getting them started.'],
      ['Trainings', 'The catalog, training paths, and assignments.'],
      ['Team and community', 'Team bonding, EDI, and community building.']
    ]
  };

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa71css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w71tabs{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:14px 0 10px;}' +
    '.w71tab{border:1px solid var(--wa-line);background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;padding:5px 14px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#1F201E;cursor:pointer;line-height:1.4;}' +
    '.w71tab .n{font-size:10px;font-weight:800;opacity:.55;margin-left:4px;}' +
    '.w71tab.on{color:#FFFAF2;border-color:transparent;}' +
    /* task rows: a calmer card look, same functionality */
    '.checklist li{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:10px;padding:9px 12px;margin:0 0 7px;list-style:none;transition:box-shadow .12s;}' +
    '.checklist li:hover{box-shadow:0 3px 10px rgba(16,63,57,.08);}' +
    '.checklist{padding-left:0;}' +
    '.subsection{margin:0 0 14px;}' +
    '.subsection .subhead{margin-bottom:2px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* ---- the task modal always offers the canonical workstreams ---- */
  try {
    if (typeof SECTIONS !== 'undefined') {
      Object.keys(W71_WS).forEach(function (d) {
        if (!SECTIONS[d]) return;
        var col = SECTIONS[d].color || '#103F39';
        var existing = (SECTIONS[d].subsections || []).map(function (s) { return s.title; });
        var subs = W71_WS[d].map(function (w) {
          return { title: w[0], color: col, summary: w[1] };
        });
        /* keep any pre-existing workstream not in the canonical list */
        (SECTIONS[d].subsections || []).forEach(function (s) {
          if (W71_WS[d].map(function (w) { return w[0]; }).indexOf(s.title) < 0) subs.push(s);
        });
        SECTIONS[d].subsections = subs;
      });
    }
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }

  var WA71_WS = {};      /* dept -> active tab ('All' default) */
  var WA71_ACTIVE = null;

  function w71Canon(id) {
    return (W71_WS[id] || []).map(function (w) { return w[0]; });
  }
  function w71Match(t, ws) {
    if (ws === 'General') return !t.subsection || t.subsection === 'General';
    return t.subsection === ws;
  }
  function w71Tabs(id) {
    var canon = w71Canon(id);
    var tabs = canon.slice();
    if (tabs.indexOf('General') < 0) tabs.push('General');
    /* unknown subsections in the data become their own tabs */
    (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).forEach(function (t) {
      if (t.department === id && t.subsection && tabs.indexOf(t.subsection) < 0) tabs.push(t.subsection);
    });
    return tabs;
  }

  /* ---- the filter lives inside getTasks, so every view obeys it ---- */
  if (typeof getTasks === 'function') {
    var __gt71 = getTasks;
    getTasks = function (id) {
      var rows = __gt71(id);
      var ws = WA71_WS[id];
      if (WA71_ACTIVE !== id || !ws || ws === 'All') return rows;
      return rows.filter(function (t) { return w71Match(t, ws); });
    };
  }

  function w71Inject(id) {
    try {
      var el = document.getElementById(id);
      var card = el ? el.querySelector('.dept-card') : null;
      if (!card || card.querySelector('.w71tabs')) return;
      var col = (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[id]) || '#103F39';
      var cur = WA71_WS[id] || 'All';
      var open = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) {
        return t.department === id && !t.done;
      });
      var tabs = w71Tabs(id);
      var html = '<div class="w71tabs" data-w71d="' + esc(id) + '">'
        + '<button class="w71tab' + (cur === 'All' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w71t="All"'
        + (cur === 'All' ? ' style="background:' + col + '"' : '') + '>All<span class="n">' + open.length + '</span></button>'
        + tabs.map(function (ws) {
          var n = open.filter(function (t) { return w71Match(t, ws); }).length;
          return '<button class="w71tab' + (cur === ws ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w71t="' + esc(ws) + '"'
            + (cur === ws ? ' style="background:' + col + '"' : '') + '>' + esc(ws) + '<span class="n">' + n + '</span></button>';
        }).join('') + '</div>';
      var anchor = card.querySelector('.wa21-ctl');
      if (!anchor) {
        card.querySelectorAll('h3.block').forEach(function (h) {
          if (!anchor && (h.textContent.indexOf('Workstreams') >= 0 || h.textContent.indexOf('Open items') >= 0)) anchor = h;
        });
      }
      if (anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', html);
      else card.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);

      /* on a specific tab, hide the other workstream blocks in list view */
      if (cur !== 'All') {
        card.querySelectorAll('.subsection').forEach(function (s) {
          var h = s.querySelector('.subhead');
          var t = h ? h.textContent.trim() : '';
          var isGen = t.indexOf('General') === 0;
          var show = (t === cur) || (cur === 'General' && isGen);
          s.style.display = show ? '' : 'none';
        });
        card.querySelectorAll('h3.block').forEach(function (h) {
          if (h.textContent.indexOf('Workstreams') >= 0) h.style.display = 'none';
        });
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  if (!document.body.dataset.wa71Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa71Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w71t]');
      if (!b) return;
      var bar = b.closest('.w71tabs');
      var id = bar ? bar.getAttribute('data-w71d') : null;
      if (!id) return;
      WA71_WS[id] = b.getAttribute('data-w71t');
      if (typeof renderSection === 'function') renderSection(id);
    });
  }

  if (typeof renderSection === 'function') {
    var __rs71 = renderSection;
    renderSection = function (id) {
      WA71_ACTIVE = id;
      try { __rs71.apply(this, arguments); }
      finally { WA71_ACTIVE = null; }
      w71Inject(id);
    };
  }
  if (typeof renderAllSections === 'function') {
    var __ras71 = renderAllSections;
    renderAllSections = function () {
      __ras71.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' ? DEPT_ORDER : []).forEach(function (d) { w71Inject(d); });
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v72 people management from the Admin page + ClickUp cleanup
   (04/08/26)
   1. Invite someone: a form on the Admin page creates their profile
      (team_members), sets their access level (hub_roles), and emails
      them a sign-in invitation via the hub-invite edge function. If
      the email cannot be sent, the profile still lands and the admin
      is told to share the link by hand.
   2. Remove someone: a Remove action per row deactivates the profile,
      blocks their access (role 'blocked'; this wave signs blocked
      users out at the gate), and revokes their sign-in.
   3. Easier access: the tab-override modal gets friendly page names,
      grouped sections and one-click presets instead of raw ids.
   4. Founder failsafe (client side, pairs with the DB trigger): the
      founders' emails are always treated as admin even if hub_roles
      misses them, so a lockout like today's cannot recur.
   5. ClickUp remnants disappear from view (approved by Steel): the
      weekly ClickUp chips, "Open this task in ClickUp" links, and
      ClickUp wording in page intros.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa72) return;
  window.__wa72 = 1;

  var FOUNDERS = ['steel.macdonald@welfareavenue.com', 'steelmacdonald@gmail.com', 'suhaib.abdillahi@welfareavenue.com'];

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa72css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '#w72bar{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;background:#F4F2EA;border-radius:12px;padding:10px 14px;margin:0 0 12px;font-size:12.5px;color:#1F201E;}' +
    '#w72bar button{border:none;background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;padding:6px 16px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '[data-w72rm]{color:#b3402f !important;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;}' +
