    '.w72grp{margin:8px 0 4px;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:#103F39;font-weight:800;}' +
    '.w72preset{display:inline-block;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 12px;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;margin:0 6px 8px 0;}' +
    /* hide ClickUp remnants */
    '.info-chip[title*="ClickUp"]{display:none !important;}' +
    'a[href*="clickup.com"]{display:none !important;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* ---- founder failsafe: never locked out again ---- */
  function w72Failsafe() {
    try {
      var em = String((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase();
      if (FOUNDERS.indexOf(em) >= 0 && typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined') {
        if (!WA40_MINE) WA40_MINE = { email: em, role: 'admin', tabs: null };
        else if (WA40_MINE.role !== 'admin') { WA40_MINE.role = 'admin'; WA40_MINE.tabs = null; }
      }
      /* blocked users are shown the gate */
      if (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'blocked') {
        try { db.auth.signOut(); } catch (e2) { /* proceed */ }
        if (typeof showGate === 'function') showGate('denied', em);
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* ---- friendly page names for the access modal ---- */
  var W72_LABELS = {
    mypage: 'My page', overview: 'Decisions', dashboard: 'Dashboard', goals: 'Department goals',
    agendas: 'Agendas', requests: 'Team Requests', importantinfo: 'Important info',
    opsmtl: 'Operations Montreal', opsto: 'Operations Toronto', marketing: 'Marketing',
    fundraising: 'Fundraising', hr: 'Human Resources', board: 'Board & Governance',
    volunteering: 'Volunteering', training: 'Avenue Academy', hrcentre: 'HR centre',
    orgchart: 'Org chart', transcripts: 'Transcript archive', activity: 'Activity',
    finances: 'Finance', create: 'Content drafter', adminpanel: 'Admin', needsme: 'Needs me'
  };
  var W72_GROUPS = [
    ['Home and work', ['mypage', 'overview', 'dashboard', 'goals', 'agendas', 'requests', 'importantinfo', 'create', 'finances']],
    ['Teams', ['opsmtl', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr', 'board']],
    ['People and programs', ['volunteering', 'training', 'hrcentre', 'orgchart']],
    ['Archive and admin', ['transcripts', 'activity', 'adminpanel']]
  ];
  function w72TabModal(email) {
    var row = (typeof WA40_ROLES !== 'undefined' ? WA40_ROLES : []).filter(function (r) { return r.email === email; })[0];
    var cur = row && Array.isArray(row.tabs) ? row.tabs : [];
    var all = (typeof WA40_TABS !== 'undefined' ? WA40_TABS : []).concat(['adminpanel']);
    function box(t) {
      if (all.indexOf(t) < 0) return '';
      return '<label style="display:inline-flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;padding:3px 10px 3px 6px;font-size:12.5px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:999px;margin:0 6px 6px 0;cursor:pointer;background:#FFFAF2">'
        + '<input type="checkbox" class="w40cb" value="' + t + '"' + (cur.indexOf(t) >= 0 ? ' checked' : '') + ' style="width:14px;height:14px" /> '
        + esc(W72_LABELS[t] || t) + '</label>';
    }
    var known = [];
    var html = '<h3>What ' + esc(email.split('@')[0]) + ' can see</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">Leave everything unticked to use the role default (the simple option). Tick pages to hand-pick exactly what they see instead.</p>'
      + '<div style="margin:0 0 6px">'
      + '<span class="w72preset" data-w72p="none">Role default</span>'
      + '<span class="w72preset" data-w72p="all">Everything</span>'
      + '<span class="w72preset" data-w72p="work">Work surfaces only</span>'
      + '</div><div style="max-height:46vh;overflow-y:auto">';
    W72_GROUPS.forEach(function (g) {
      var boxes = g[1].map(function (t) { known.push(t); return box(t); }).join('');
      if (boxes) html += '<div class="w72grp">' + g[0] + '</div>' + boxes;
    });
    var extra = all.filter(function (t) { return known.indexOf(t) < 0; });
    if (extra.length) html += '<div class="w72grp">Other</div>' + extra.map(box).join('');
    html += '</div><div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w40ts">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w40tc">Cancel</button></div>';
    openModal(html);
    document.getElementById('w40tc').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-w72p]').forEach(function (p) {
      p.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var mode = p.getAttribute('data-w72p');
        document.querySelectorAll('.w40cb').forEach(function (c) {
          if (mode === 'none') c.checked = false;
          else if (mode === 'all') c.checked = true;
          else c.checked = ['mypage', 'overview', 'goals', 'agendas', 'requests', 'importantinfo', 'training',
            'opsmtl', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr', 'volunteering', 'transcripts'].indexOf(c.value) >= 0;
        });
      });
    });
    document.getElementById('w40ts').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var picked = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.w40cb:checked')).map(function (c) { return c.value; });
      var r = await db.from('hub_roles').upsert({
        email: email, role: (row && row.role) || 'member',
        tabs: picked.length ? picked : null,
        updated_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'admin', updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      }, { onConflict: 'email' });
      if (r.error) { toast('Could not save it'); return; }
      closeModal();
      if (typeof wa40Load === 'function') await wa40Load();
      toast('Saved');
      if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') wa40Render();
    });
  }
  if (typeof wa40TabModal === 'function') { wa40TabModal = w72TabModal; }

  /* ---- invite and remove ---- */
  function w72InviteModal() {
    var depts = (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' ? DEPT_ORDER : []).map(function (d) {
      return '<option value="' + d + '">' + esc((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d) + '</option>';
    }).join('');
    openModal('<h3>Invite someone to the Hub</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">This creates their profile, sets what they can access, and emails them a sign-in invitation. They can also sign in with Google using this email once invited.</p>'
      + '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Full name</label><input type="text" id="w72n" placeholder="First Last" /></div>'
      + '<div><label>Email</label><input type="text" id="w72e" placeholder="name@welfareavenue.com" /></div></div>'
      + '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Department</label><select id="w72d">' + depts + '</select></div>'
      + '<div><label>Role title</label><input type="text" id="w72rt" placeholder="e.g. Content manager" /></div></div>'
      + '<label>Hub access</label><select id="w72hr">'
      + '<option value="member" selected>Member (the work surfaces)</option>'
      + '<option value="leader">Leader (every page)</option>'
      + '<option value="viewer">Viewer (overview only)</option>'
      + '<option value="admin">Admin (manages everything)</option>'
      + '</select>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w72go">Create the profile and send the invite</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w72cx">Cancel</button></div>');
    document.getElementById('w72cx').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w72go').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var name = (document.getElementById('w72n').value || '').trim();
      var email = (document.getElementById('w72e').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!name || email.indexOf('@') < 1) { toast('A name and a valid email are needed'); return; }
      this.disabled = true;
      this.textContent = 'Inviting...';
      try {
        var r = await db.functions.invoke('hub-invite', { body: {
          action: 'invite', name: name, email: email,
          department: document.getElementById('w72d').value,
          role_title: (document.getElementById('w72rt').value || '').trim(),
          hub_role: document.getElementById('w72hr').value
        } });
        if (r.error || !(r.data && r.data.ok)) throw (r.error || new Error((r.data && r.data.error) || 'failed'));
        closeModal();
        toast(r.data.email_sent
          ? 'Profile created and the invitation email is on its way'
          : 'Profile created. The email could not be sent (' + (r.data.note || 'limit') + '), so share the Hub link with them directly.');
        if (typeof wa40Load === 'function') await wa40Load();
        if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') wa40Render();
      } catch (e) {
        this.disabled = false;
        this.textContent = 'Create the profile and send the invite';
        toast('Could not invite: ' + String(e.message || e).slice(0, 80));
      }
    });
  }
  function w72Remove(email) {
    openModal('<h3>Remove ' + esc(email) + ' from the Hub?</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">Their profile is deactivated, their access is blocked, and their sign-in is revoked. Their tasks, updates and history all stay. You can invite them again later.</p>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w72rmgo" style="background:#b3402f">Remove them</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w72rmc">Cancel</button></div>');
    document.getElementById('w72rmc').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w72rmgo').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      this.disabled = true;
      try {
        var r = await db.functions.invoke('hub-invite', { body: { action: 'remove', email: email } });
        if (r.error || !(r.data && r.data.ok)) throw (r.error || new Error((r.data && r.data.error) || 'failed'));
        closeModal();
        toast('Removed from the Hub' + (r.data.auth_deleted ? ' and signed out everywhere' : ''));
        if (typeof wa40Load === 'function') await wa40Load();
        if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') wa40Render();
      } catch (e) {
        this.disabled = false;
        toast('Could not remove: ' + String(e.message || e).slice(0, 80));
      }
    });
  }

  /* decorate the admin table: invite bar + remove per row */
  function w72Admin() {
    try {
      var body = document.getElementById('w40body');
      if (!body || body.querySelector('#w72bar')) return;
      var table = body.querySelector('table.wa-tbl');
      if (!table) return;
      body.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',
        '<div id="w72bar"><b style="color:#103F39">People</b> Invite someone new, change what each person sees, or remove access.'
        + '<button id="w72inv" style="margin-left:auto">+ Invite someone</button></div>');
      var headRow = table.querySelector('thead tr');
      if (headRow && headRow.children.length === 4) headRow.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<th></th>');
      table.querySelectorAll('tbody tr').forEach(function (tr) {
        if (tr.querySelector('[data-w72rm]')) return;
        var sel = tr.querySelector('[data-w40r]');
        var em = sel ? sel.getAttribute('data-w40r') : null;
        if (!em) { tr.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<td></td>'); return; }
        tr.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
          FOUNDERS.indexOf(em) >= 0
            ? '<td><span style="font-size:11px;color:#797773">founder</span></td>'
            : '<td><a data-w72rm="' + esc(em) + '">Remove</a></td>');
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') {
    var __ar72 = wa40Render;
    wa40Render = async function () {
      await __ar72.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(w72Admin, 40);
    };
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa72Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa72Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w72inv')) { w72InviteModal(); return; }
      var rm = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w72rm]');
      if (rm) { w72Remove(rm.getAttribute('data-w72rm')); return; }
    });
  }

  /* ---- ClickUp wording out of the page intros ---- */
  function w72Copy() {
    try {
      var tp = document.querySelector('#training .card > p.read');
      if (tp && tp.textContent.indexOf('ClickUp') >= 0) {
        tp.textContent = 'The training catalog: browse by path or category, open a card for the details, and track progress. Everyone\'s progress shows on their profile.';
      }
      document.querySelectorAll('.vl-empty').forEach(function (p) {
        if (p.textContent.indexOf('ClickUp') >= 0) {
          p.textContent = 'The master template. Every event gets a copy of exactly this, so editing an event never changes the template.';
        }
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  try {
    var __w72mo;
    new MutationObserver(function () {
      clearTimeout(__w72mo);
      __w72mo = setTimeout(w72Copy, 400);
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa72 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa72();
      setTimeout(w72Failsafe, 200);
      setTimeout(w72Copy, 500);
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v73 the navigation slims down (all six proposals approved)
   (04/08/26)
   New bar: Home | Decisions | Departments | Meetings (Transcripts,
   Agendas) | Strategy | Requests | People (Org chart, Training,
   Volunteering, HR centre) | Important info | More (Finances,
   Activity, Admin).
   1. Dashboard merges into Decisions: its tab disappears and the
      Decisions page gets an "Open the full dashboard" link (the page
      itself still exists at #dashboard, nothing is deleted).
   2. Meetings group: upload a meeting and build an agenda from one
      place.
   3. Activity moves under More with Admin.
   4. Create (content drafter) leaves the bar and becomes a tool
      button on the Marketing page (still at #create).
   5. Volunteering and HR centre join the People group.
   6. The full mobile pass stays queued as its own follow-up.
   Everything is rearranged AFTER wave42 builds the bar, using the same
   classes so the flyout styling, role gating (wa40Enforce) and empty
   group hiding keep working. No page ids or hashes change, so every
   deep link and permission list still holds.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa73) return;
  window.__wa73 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa73css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '#w73dash{display:inline-flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;background:#F4F2EA;border-radius:999px;padding:5px 14px;font-size:12px;color:#1F201E;margin:0 0 10px;}' +
    '#w73dash a{color:#103F39;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  function w73Group(key, label, tabs) {
    var g = document.createElement('span');
    g.className = 'tab-group';
    g.setAttribute('data-w42g', key);
    var h = document.createElement('button');
    h.className = 'tab-group-h';
    h.innerHTML = label + ' <span class="caret">&#9660;</span>';
    var p = document.createElement('div');
    p.className = 'tab-group-p';
    tabs.forEach(function (el) { if (el) p.appendChild(el); });
    g.appendChild(h);
    g.appendChild(p);
    h.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
      ev.stopPropagation();
      var was = g.classList.contains('open');
      document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab-group.open').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('open'); });
      if (!was) g.classList.add('open');
    });
    return g;
  }

  function w73Nav() {
    try {
      var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
      if (!bar || !bar.dataset.wa42 || bar.dataset.w73) return;
      bar.dataset.w73 = '1';
      /* wave134: the menu layout owns the groups and their order; this only keeps the merged tabs hidden */
      if (window.WA_NAV && WA_NAV.layout) {
        ['dashboard', 'create'].forEach(function (id) {
          var hb = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]');
          if (hb) { hb.style.display = 'none'; hb.dataset.w73hide = '1'; }
        });
        if (WA_NAV.applyLayout) WA_NAV.applyLayout();
        if (typeof wa40Enforce === 'function') { try { wa40Enforce(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
        return;
      }

      /* dissolve wave42's groups, keeping the department flyout rows intact */
      var deptRows = [];
      Array.prototype.slice.call(bar.querySelectorAll('.tab-group')).forEach(function (g) {
        Array.prototype.slice.call(g.querySelectorAll(':scope > .tab-group-p > *')).forEach(function (el) {
          if (el.classList && el.classList.contains('w42d')) deptRows.push(el);
          else if (el.classList && el.classList.contains('tab')) bar.appendChild(el);
          else if (el.querySelector && el.querySelector('.tab')) bar.appendChild(el);
        });
        if (g.parentNode) g.parentNode.removeChild(g);
      });
      function grab(id) { return bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]'); }

      /* hide the merged and relocated tabs */
      ['dashboard', 'create'].forEach(function (id) {
        var b = grab(id);
        if (b) { b.style.display = 'none'; b.dataset.w73hide = '1'; }
      });

      var order = [];
      ['mypage', 'overview'].forEach(function (id) { var b = grab(id); if (b) order.push(b); });
      order.push(w73Group('departments', 'Teams', deptRows));
      order.push(w73Group('meetings', 'Meetings', [grab('transcripts'), grab('agendas')]));
      var strat = grab('goals');
      if (strat) order.push(strat);
      var req = grab('requests');
      if (req) order.push(req);
      order.push(w73Group('people', 'People', [grab('orgchart'), grab('training'), grab('volunteering'), grab('hrcentre')]));
      var info = grab('importantinfo');
      if (info) order.push(info);
      var more = w73Group('more', 'More', [grab('finances'), grab('activity'), grab('adminpanel')]);

      /* anything else registered (now or in the future) keeps a home */
      var placed = {};
      order.forEach(function (el) {
        if (el.classList && el.classList.contains('tab')) placed[el.getAttribute('data-tab')] = 1;
        el.querySelectorAll && el.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(function (t) { placed[t.getAttribute('data-tab')] = 1; });
      });
      more.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(function (t) { placed[t.getAttribute('data-tab')] = 1; });
      Array.prototype.slice.call(bar.querySelectorAll(':scope > .tab')).forEach(function (t) {
        var id = t.getAttribute('data-tab');
        if (!placed[id] && !t.dataset.w73hide) order.push(t);
      });
      order.push(more);

      order.forEach(function (el) { if (el) bar.appendChild(el); });
      if (typeof wa40Enforce === 'function') { try { wa40Enforce(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* wa40Enforce re-shows tabs the role allows; keep the merged ones hidden */
  if (typeof wa40Enforce === 'function') {
    var __en73 = wa40Enforce;
    wa40Enforce = function () {
      __en73.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab[data-w73hide]').forEach(function (t) { t.style.display = 'none'; });
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* Decisions carries the dashboard now */
  function w73DashLink() {
    try {
      var panel = document.getElementById('overview');
      if (!panel || document.getElementById('w73dash')) return;
      var card = panel.querySelector('.card');
      if (!card) return;
      card.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',
        '<div id="w73dash">Looking for the numbers view? <a data-w73go="1">Open the full dashboard &raquo;</a></div>');
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa73Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa73Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w73go]')) { location.hash = '#dashboard'; }
    });
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp73 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp73(id);
      if (id === 'overview') setTimeout(w73DashLink, 150);
    };
  }

  /* the Content drafter becomes a Marketing page tool */
  if (typeof wa39DeptTools === 'function') {
    var __dt73 = wa39DeptTools;
    wa39DeptTools = function (id) {
      var html = __dt73.apply(this, arguments);
      if (id === 'marketing' && html && html.indexOf('w39tools') >= 0 && html.indexOf('#create') < 0) {
        html = html.replace(/<\/div>\s*$/,
          '<button class="w39tool" data-w39t="#create"><span class="ic">&#9997;</span>Content drafter</button></div>');
      }
      return html;
    };
  }

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa73 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa73();
      setTimeout(w73Nav, 250);
      setTimeout(w73DashLink, 600);
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v74 My page rebuilt: one clean scan path
   (04/08/26)
   The old page assembled itself from four different waves, which is
   why Mentions and the Online line could double up and the layout
   sprawled. v74 REPLACES renderMyPage with one renderer that draws
   everything once, in a deliberate order:
   - Header: greeting, date, department, a deduped online line, and
     clickable stat chips (tasks, team, requests, decisions, mentions).
   - "Needs your attention": overdue tasks and unread mentions, first.
   - Main column: My tasks grouped (Overdue / Due soon / Later and no
     date, capped with Show all), then Updates for you.
   - Side column: Mentions (compact, Got it), My team folded to one
     line per person (open/overdue counts; click to see their top
     tasks), Requests for your department, Time commitments.
   A render token prevents overlapping renders from double-painting.
   All previous behaviours are kept: chips navigate, updates open the
   department, mentions mark seen ([data-w65got] handler from v65),
   hours load from slack_hours. The section id w65menbox is kept so
   v65's old injector permanently stands down.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa74) return;
  window.__wa74 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa74css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.mp-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.5fr) minmax(0,1fr);gap:14px;align-items:start;}' +
    '@media (max-width:900px){.mp-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr;}}' +
    '.w74col{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:14px;min-width:0;}' +
    '.w74date{font-size:12.5px;color:#797773;}' +
    '.mp-online{font-size:12px;color:#797773;margin:2px 0 0;}' +
    '#w74attn{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid rgba(179,64,47,.35);border-left:4px solid #b3402f;border-radius:12px;padding:12px 15px;margin:0 0 14px;}' +
    '#w74attn h3{margin:0 0 7px;font-size:12px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;color:#b3402f;}' +
    '.w74grp{margin:10px 0 4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w74grp.over{color:#b3402f;}' +
    '.w74more{display:inline-block;margin-top:8px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w74hide{display:none;}' +
    '.w74mem{border-top:1px dashed var(--wa-line);}' +
    '.w74mem:first-of-type{border-top:none;}' +
    '.w74mem summary{list-style:none;cursor:pointer;display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:baseline;padding:7px 0;font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.w74mem summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none;}' +
    '.w74mem summary .nm{font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w74mem summary .meta{color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:11.5px;flex:1;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;}' +
    '.w74mem summary .ct{flex:0 0 auto;font-size:11px;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w74mem summary .ct b{color:#b3402f;}' +
    '.w74mem .mp-task{margin-left:2px;}' +
    '.w74men{padding:8px 0;border-top:1px dashed var(--wa-line);font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.w74men:first-of-type{border-top:none;}' +
    '.w74men.seen{opacity:.55;}' +
    '.w74men .mt{font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w74men .cx{color:#797773;display:-webkit-box;-webkit-line-clamp:2;-webkit-box-orient:vertical;overflow:hidden;}' +
    '.w74new{display:inline-block;background:#DCA527;color:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;padding:0 7px;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;text-transform:uppercase;margin-left:6px;vertical-align:middle;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var WA74 = { seq: 0, showAllTasks: false };

  function w74Overdue(t) { return t.due_date && new Date(t.due_date + 'T23:59:59') < new Date(); }
  function w74Soon(t) {
    if (!t.due_date || w74Overdue(t)) return false;
    return new Date(t.due_date + 'T00:00:00') < new Date(Date.now() + 7 * 86400000);
  }
  function w74TaskLine(t) {
    return (typeof waTaskLine === 'function') ? waTaskLine(t) : '<div class="mp-task">' + esc(t.title) + '</div>';
  }
  function w74Sec(title, inner, id) {
    return '<div class="mp-sec"' + (id ? ' id="' + id + '"' : '') + '><h3>' + title + '</h3>' + inner + '</div>';
  }

  renderMyPage = async function () {
    var body = document.getElementById('mpBody');
    if (!body) return;
    if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) {
      /* wave272: directors get Home (their cards are added by the waves that draw for them) */
      if (typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board') {
        var bh = new Date().getHours(), bn = String((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0];
        body.innerHTML = '<div class="mp-head"><h2>' + (bh < 12 ? 'Good morning' : bh < 17 ? 'Good afternoon' : 'Good evening') + (bn ? ', ' + esc(bn) : '') + '</h2><span class="w74date">' + esc(new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' })) + '</span></div>';
        return;
      }
      body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Your page is available to staff accounts.</p>'; return;
    }
    var token = ++WA74.seq;
    if (typeof waResolveMe === 'function') await waResolveMe();
    var me = (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined') ? WA_ME : null;
    var uname = (me && me.name) || ((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || 'there');
    var myDept = me ? me.department : null;
    var myTasks = me && typeof waTasksOf === 'function' ? waTasksOf(me.name) : [];
    var overdue = myTasks.filter(w74Overdue);
    var team = (typeof WA_MY_TEAM !== 'undefined' ? WA_MY_TEAM : []);
    var teamRows = team.map(function (m) {
      var ts = (typeof waTasksOf === 'function') ? waTasksOf(m.name) : [];
      return { m: m, open: ts.length, overdue: ts.filter(w74Overdue).length, top: ts.slice(0, 3) };
    });
    var teamOpen = 0, teamOver = 0;
    teamRows.forEach(function (r) { teamOpen += r.open; teamOver += r.overdue; });
    var reqs = (typeof REQ_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? REQ_CACHE : []).filter(function (r) { return myDept && r.department === myDept && !r.done; });
    var ft = me && typeof waFirstTok === 'function' ? waFirstTok(me.name) : '@@none@@';
    var upds = (typeof DB_UPDATES !== 'undefined' ? DB_UPDATES : []).filter(function (u) {
      return (myDept && u.department === myDept) || ((u.text || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(ft) >= 0);
    }).sort(function (a, b) { return new Date(b.created_at) - new Date(a.created_at); }).slice(0, 6);

    /* mentions + presence load together */
    var mentions = [];
    try {
      if (me) {
        var mr = await db.from('mentions').select('*').eq('person', me.name).order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(12);
        mentions = (mr && mr.data) || [];
        mentions.sort(function (a, b) { return (a.seen === b.seen) ? 0 : (a.seen ? 1 : -1); });
      }
    } catch (e) { /* optional */ }
    try { if (typeof waLoadPresence === 'function') await waLoadPresence(); } catch (e) { /* optional */ }
    if (token !== WA74.seq) return; /* a newer render took over */

    var unseen = mentions.filter(function (m) { return !m.seen; });
    var hour = new Date().getHours();
    var greet = hour < 12 ? 'Good morning' : hour < 17 ? 'Good afternoon' : 'Good evening';
    var dateLine = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' });

    var onlineNames = [];
    try {
      (typeof waOnlineEmails === 'function' ? waOnlineEmails() : []).forEach(function (p) {
        var n = (typeof waNameForEmail === 'function') ? waNameForEmail(p.email) : p.email;
        if (onlineNames.indexOf(n) < 0) onlineNames.push(n);
      });
    } catch (e) { /* optional */ }
    var meFirst = uname.split(' ')[0];
    var meFlat = uname.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '');
    var others = onlineNames.filter(function (n) {
      var flat = String(n).toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '');
      return flat !== meFlat && flat.indexOf(meFlat) < 0 && n.split(' ')[0] !== meFirst;
    });

    var html = '<div class="mp-head"><h2>' + greet + ', ' + esc(meFirst) + '</h2>'
      + (me && myDept ? '<span class="ag-dep" style="background:' + ((typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[myDept]) || '#797773') + '">' + esc((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[myDept]) || myDept) + '</span>' : '')
      + '<span class="w74date">' + esc(dateLine) + '</span></div>'
      + '<div class="mp-chips">'
      + '<span class="mp-chip' + (overdue.length ? ' warn' : '') + '"><b>' + myTasks.length + '</b>my open tasks' + (overdue.length ? ' (' + overdue.length + ' overdue)' : '') + '</span>'
      + (teamRows.length ? '<span class="mp-chip' + (teamOver ? ' warn' : '') + '"><b>' + teamOpen + '</b>team tasks' + (teamOver ? ' (' + teamOver + ' overdue)' : '') + '</span>' : '')
      + (reqs.length ? '<span class="mp-chip" data-mpgo="' + esc(myDept || '') + '"><b>' + reqs.length + '</b>incoming requests</span>' : '')
      + '<span class="mp-chip" data-mphash="#overview"><b>&#9873;</b>open decisions</span>'
      + (unseen.length ? '<span class="mp-chip warn" data-w74men="1"><b>' + unseen.length + '</b>new ' + (unseen.length === 1 ? 'mention' : 'mentions') + '</span>' : '')
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="mp-online">' + (others.length
        ? '<span class="wa-on"></span>Also on the Hub right now: ' + esc(others.join(', '))
        : 'Nobody else is on the Hub right now.') + '</div>';

    /* needs your attention */
    if (overdue.length || unseen.length) {
      html += '<div id="w74attn"><h3>Needs your attention</h3>'
        + overdue.slice(0, 5).map(w74TaskLine).join('')
        + (overdue.length > 5 ? '<p class="mp-empty">And ' + (overdue.length - 5) + ' more overdue below.</p>' : '')
        + (unseen.length ? '<div class="mp-task"><span class="mp-due soon">' + unseen.length + ' new</span><span>You were mentioned in ' + (unseen.length === 1 ? 'a meeting' : unseen.length + ' meetings') + ' you were not in.</span><a data-w74men="1" style="margin-left:auto;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer">Read them</a></div>' : '')
        + '</div>';
    } else {
      html += '<div style="margin:0 0 14px"></div>';
    }

    /* my tasks, grouped */
    var soon = myTasks.filter(w74Soon);
    var later = myTasks.filter(function (t) { return !w74Overdue(t) && !w74Soon(t); });
    var caps = WA74.showAllTasks ? 999 : 8;
    var shown = 0;
    function grp(label, cls, list) {
      if (!list.length) return '';
      var out = '<div class="w74grp' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + '">' + label + ' (' + list.length + ')</div>';
      list.forEach(function (t) {
        shown++;
        out += (shown > caps ? '<div class="w74hide w74extra">' + w74TaskLine(t) + '</div>' : w74TaskLine(t));
      });
      return out;
    }
    var tasksInner = myTasks.length
      ? grp('Overdue', 'over', overdue) + grp('Due this week', '', soon) + grp('Later and no date', '', later)
        + (myTasks.length > caps && !WA74.showAllTasks ? '<a class="w74more" data-w74all="1">Show all ' + myTasks.length + ' tasks</a>' : '')
      : '<p class="mp-empty">' + (me ? 'Nothing open with your name on it. Enjoy it while it lasts.' : 'I could not match your account to the team roster; ask an admin to add your email to your profile.') + '</p>';

    var updsInner = upds.length ? upds.map(function (u) {
      return '<div class="mp-upd" data-mpdept="' + esc(u.department || '') + '">' + (u.entry_label ? '<b>' + esc(u.entry_label) + ':</b> ' : '') + esc((u.text || '').slice(0, 160)) + ' <span class="when">' + (typeof fmtDay === 'function' ? fmtDay(u.created_at) : '') + '</span></div>';
    }).join('') : '<p class="mp-empty">No recent updates touching your department or name.</p>';

    /* mentions (id kept so the old v65 injector stands down) */
    var menInner = mentions.length ? mentions.slice(0, 5).map(function (m) {
      return '<div class="w74men' + (m.seen ? ' seen' : '') + '">'
        + '<div class="mt">' + esc(m.meeting_title || 'A meeting') + ' <span class="w74date">' + esc(String(m.meeting_date || '')) + '</span>'
        + (m.seen ? '' : '<span class="w74new">new</span>') + '</div>'
        + (m.context ? '<div class="cx">' + esc(m.context) + '</div>' : '')
        + (m.seen ? '' : '<a class="w65got" data-w65got="' + m.id + '" style="font-size:11.5px;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700">Got it</a>')
        + '</div>';
    }).join('') : '<p class="mp-empty">When a meeting you were not in discusses you, it shows up here.</p>';

    var teamInner = teamRows.length ? teamRows.map(function (r) {
      return '<details class="w74mem"><summary><span class="nm">' + esc(r.m.name.split(' ')[0]) + '</span>'
        + '<span class="meta">' + esc(r.m.role || '') + '</span>'
        + '<span class="ct">' + r.open + ' open' + (r.overdue ? '' + ' \u00b7 ' + '<b>' + r.overdue + ' overdue</b>' : '') + '</span></summary>'
        + (r.top.length ? r.top.map(w74TaskLine).join('') : '<p class="mp-empty">Nothing open.</p>')
        + '</details>';
    }).join('') : '';

    var reqInner = reqs.length ? reqs.slice(0, 4).map(function (r) {
      return '<div class="mp-task"><span class="mp-due">' + esc(r.status || '') + '</span><span>' + esc(r.title) + '</span></div>';
    }).join('') + '<div style="margin-top:7px;"><a style="cursor:pointer;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:#103F39" data-mpdept="' + esc(myDept || '') + '">Open the department page &#8594;</a></div>' : '';

    html += '<div class="mp-grid">'
      + '<div class="w74col">'
      + w74Sec('My tasks', tasksInner)
      + w74Sec('Updates for you', updsInner)
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w74col">'
      + w74Sec('Mentions' + (unseen.length ? ' (' + unseen.length + ' new)' : ''), menInner, 'w65menbox')
      + (teamInner ? w74Sec('My team', teamInner) : '')
      + (reqInner ? w74Sec('Requests for ' + esc((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[myDept]) || 'your department'), reqInner) : '')
      + w74Sec('Time commitments (last 3 weeks)', '<div id="mpHours"><p class="mp-empty">Loading hours...</p></div>')
      + '</div></div>';

    if (token !== WA74.seq) return;
    body.innerHTML = html;

    body.querySelectorAll('[data-mpdept]').forEach(function (a) {
      a.addEventListener('click', function () { if (a.dataset.mpdept) location.hash = '#dept/' + a.dataset.mpdept; });
    });
    body.querySelectorAll('[data-mpgo]').forEach(function (a) {
      a.addEventListener('click', function () { if (a.dataset.mpgo) location.hash = '#dept/' + a.dataset.mpgo; });
    });
    body.querySelectorAll('[data-mphash]').forEach(function (a) {
      a.addEventListener('click', function () { location.hash = a.dataset.mphash; });
    });
    body.querySelectorAll('[data-w74men]').forEach(function (a) {
      a.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var box = document.getElementById('w65menbox');
        if (box) box.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
      });
    });
    var all = body.querySelector('[data-w74all]');
    if (all) all.addEventListener('click', function () {
      WA74.showAllTasks = true;
      body.querySelectorAll('.w74extra').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('w74hide'); });
      all.remove();
    });

    /* hours fill, same source as before */
    try {
      var names = [me ? me.name : null].concat(team.map(function (m) { return m.name; })).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 12);
      var slot = document.getElementById('mpHours');
      if (!names.length) { if (slot) slot.innerHTML = '<p class="mp-empty">No roster match for hours.</p>'; return; }
      var since = new Date(Date.now() - 21 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      var hr = await db.from('slack_hours').select('member_name, week_start, hours, sessions').in('member_name', names).gte('week_start', since).order('week_start');
      if (token !== WA74.seq) return;
      var rows = hr.data || [];
      slot = document.getElementById('mpHours');
      if (!slot) return;
      if (!rows.length) { slot.innerHTML = '<p class="mp-empty">No tracked check-ins in the last 3 weeks. Hours come from the Slack check-in channels.</p>'; return; }
      var agg = {};
      rows.forEach(function (x) {
        var a = (agg[x.member_name] = agg[x.member_name] || { h: 0, s: 0 });
        a.h += Number(x.hours || 0); a.s += Number(x.sessions || 0);
      });
      slot.innerHTML = '<table class="mp-hours"><tr><th>Person</th><th>3 week total</th><th>Sessions</th></tr>'
        + names.filter(function (n) { return agg[n]; }).map(function (n) {
            return '<tr><td><b>' + esc(n.split(' ')[0]) + '</b></td><td>' + agg[n].h.toFixed(1) + 'h</td><td>' + agg[n].s + '</td></tr>';
          }).join('')
        + '</table>';
    } catch (e) { /* hours optional */ }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v75 the assistant steps forward (06/08/26)
   Steel: "make the WA chat assistant more apparent, I want users to
   know it's there."
   1. The bottom-right sparkle circle becomes a labelled pill button:
      "Ask the Hub". Same id (#waChatBtn), so wave4's open/close
      listener keeps working untouched.
   2. First-visit nudge: until a person opens the chat once (or closes
      the nudge), the pill pulses gently and a small dark callout above
      it explains what the assistant can do. Remembered per browser in
      localStorage ('wa75nudge').
   3. My page card: a full-width "Ask the Hub anything" card between
      the attention strip and the two columns, with tappable example
      questions that open the chat prefilled. Only rendered when the
      chat button exists (the assistant is staff-gated by wave4).
   4. (Steel, mid-build) Time commitments on the home page are for
      leaders only: the card is removed after render unless the viewer
      is an admin, a hub 'leader', on the board, or has a leadership
      role title (same regex family as v62). The async hours fill
      already bails when #mpHours is gone.
   5. (Steel, mid-build) Steel and Suhaib are board members but also
      operational executives, so the board exemption should not apply
      to them: their minimum is 8h/wk. wave54's wa54Min is IIFE-private
      and keys off department==='board', so this is a post-render patch
      on the Commitment dashboard (#w52hours): a MutationObserver spots
      each rewrite, swaps their 'exempt' chip for 'X.Xh / 8h exec',
      flags + floats the row when under 8, updates the header wording,
      and adds them to the not-checking-in list when fully silent.
   Wiring: decorates after startApp (chat mounts inside wave4's own
   startApp wrap); renderMyPage wrapped outermost after v74's full
   replacement. window.w75Open(prefill) opens the panel and focuses
   the v38 input (falls back to the v4 input if v38 is absent).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa75) return;
  window.__wa75 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa75css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '#waChatBtn{width:auto !important;height:auto !important;border-radius:999px !important;padding:12px 20px 12px 15px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:13px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.4px;text-transform:uppercase;}' +
    '#waChatBtn .w75ic{font-size:17px;line-height:1;}' +
    '#waChatBtn.w75pulse{animation:w75p 2.4s ease-out infinite;}' +
    '@keyframes w75p{0%{box-shadow:0 6px 18px rgba(0,0,0,.28),0 0 0 0 rgba(111,100,171,.45);}70%{box-shadow:0 6px 18px rgba(0,0,0,.28),0 0 0 13px rgba(111,100,171,0);}100%{box-shadow:0 6px 18px rgba(0,0,0,.28),0 0 0 0 rgba(111,100,171,0);}}' +
    '#w75nudge{position:fixed;right:22px;bottom:88px;width:272px;background:#1F201E;color:#F4F2EA;border-radius:14px;padding:14px 16px;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.5;box-shadow:0 12px 34px rgba(0,0,0,.32);z-index:59;}' +
    '#w75nudge:after{content:"";position:absolute;bottom:-7px;right:34px;width:14px;height:14px;background:#1F201E;transform:rotate(45deg);}' +
    '#w75nudge b{display:block;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:3px;}' +
    '#w75nudge .x{position:absolute;top:6px;right:10px;background:none;border:none;color:#F4F2EA;opacity:.6;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;padding:2px;}' +
    '#w75nudge .x:hover{opacity:1;}' +
    '#w75nudge .go{margin-top:9px;background:#6F64AB;color:#FFFAF2;border:none;border-radius:999px;padding:6px 15px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;font-size:12px;}' +
    '#w75card{margin:0 0 14px;border:1px solid #e0d5ff;background:linear-gradient(105deg,#faf8ff,#f3edff);}' +
    '#w75card h3{color:#5a3bbf !important;}' +
    '#w75card p{margin:0 0 4px;font-size:12.5px;color:#4b4560;line-height:1.5;}' +
    '.w75q{display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #d9cbff;color:#5a3bbf;border-radius:999px;padding:6px 13px;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;margin:6px 6px 0 0;}' +
    '.w75q:hover{background:#ece3ff;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  function w75Seen() {
    try { return localStorage.getItem('wa75nudge') === '1'; } catch (e) { return true; }
  }
  function w75Mark() {
    try { localStorage.setItem('wa75nudge', '1'); } catch (e) { /* private mode */ }
  }
  function w75Dismiss() {
    w75Mark();
    var n = document.getElementById('w75nudge');
    if (n && n.parentNode) n.parentNode.removeChild(n);
    var b = document.getElementById('waChatBtn');
    if (b) b.classList.remove('w75pulse');
  }

  window.w75Open = function (prefill) {
    var btn = document.getElementById('waChatBtn');
    var panel = document.getElementById('waChatPanel');
    if (!btn || !panel) return;
    w75Dismiss();
    if (panel.style.display !== 'flex') btn.click();
    var inp = document.getElementById('w38in') || document.getElementById('wacInput');
    if (inp) {
      if (prefill) inp.value = prefill;
      try { inp.focus(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    }
  };

  function w75Decorate() {
    try {
      var btn = document.getElementById('waChatBtn');
      if (!btn || btn.dataset.w75) return;
      btn.dataset.w75 = '1';
      btn.innerHTML = '<span class="w75ic">&#10024;</span>Ask the Hub';
      btn.title = 'Ask the WA assistant anything, or tell it to file a request or task';
      btn.addEventListener('click', w75Dismiss);
      if (!w75Seen()) {
        btn.classList.add('w75pulse');
        if (!document.getElementById('w75nudge')) {
          var n = document.createElement('div');
          n.id = 'w75nudge';
          n.innerHTML = '<button class="x" data-w75x="1" title="Close">&#10005;</button>'
            + '<b>Meet the WA assistant</b>'
            + 'Ask what is happening anywhere in Welfare Avenue, or type a request or task and it will file it for you. You confirm before anything is saved.'
            + '<br /><button class="go" data-w75go="1">Try it</button>';
          document.body.appendChild(n);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  if (!document.body.dataset.wa75Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa75Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      if (!e.target.closest) return;
      if (e.target.closest('[data-w75x]')) { w75Dismiss(); return; }
      if (e.target.closest('[data-w75go]')) { window.w75Open(''); return; }
      var q = e.target.closest('[data-w75q]');
      if (q) window.w75Open(q.getAttribute('data-w75q'));
    });
  }

  /* My page card, only when the assistant exists on this account */
  var W75_EXAMPLES = [
    'What is happening across Welfare Avenue this week?',
    'What is blocking my department right now?',
    'Put in a request to marketing to promote our next event',
    'Add a task for me to follow up with a volunteer'
  ];
  function w75Card() {
    try {
      if (!document.getElementById('waChatBtn')) return;
      var body = document.getElementById('mpBody');
      if (!body || document.getElementById('w75card')) return;
      var grid = body.querySelector('.mp-grid');
      if (!grid) return;
      var card = document.createElement('div');
      card.className = 'mp-sec';
      card.id = 'w75card';
      card.innerHTML = '<h3>&#10024; Ask the Hub anything</h3>'
        + '<p>The WA assistant (bottom right) answers from live Hub data and can file requests and tasks for you. Try one:</p>'
        + W75_EXAMPLES.map(function (x) {
            return '<span class="w75q" data-w75q="' + x.replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + '">' + x + '</span>';
          }).join('');
      grid.parentNode.insertBefore(card, grid);
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* time commitments: leaders only */
  function w75Leader() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE
        && (WA40_MINE.role === 'admin' || WA40_MINE.role === 'leader')) return true;
      var me = (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined') ? WA_ME : null;
      return !!(me && (me.department === 'board' || /\b(lead|leader|director|manager|chair|co-)/i.test(me.role || '')));
    } catch (e) { return true; }
  }
  function w75Hours() {
    try {
      if (w75Leader()) return;
      var slot = document.getElementById('mpHours');
      if (!slot) return;
      var sec = slot.closest ? slot.closest('.mp-sec') : null;
      if (sec && sec.parentNode) sec.parentNode.removeChild(sec);
      else if (slot.parentNode) slot.parentNode.removeChild(slot);
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __mp75 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      await __mp75.apply(this, arguments);
      w75Card();
      w75Hours();
    };
  }

  /* operational executives: board-exempt no more, 8h/wk minimum */
  var W75_EXECS = ['steel', 'suhaib'];
  var W75_EXEC_MIN = 8;
  function w75IsExec(name) {
    var ft = String(name || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0].toLowerCase();
    return W75_EXECS.indexOf(ft) >= 0;
  }
  function w75ExecFix() {
    try {
      var sec = document.getElementById('w52hours');
      if (!sec) return;
      var rows = sec.querySelectorAll('table tbody tr');
      var silentExecs = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
        var row = rows[i];
        var cells = row.children;
        if (cells.length < 4) continue;
        var nameEl = cells[0].querySelector('b');
        var name = nameEl ? nameEl.textContent : cells[0].textContent;
        if (!w75IsExec(name)) continue;
        var avg = parseFloat(cells[2].textContent) || 0;
        var cur = parseFloat(cells[3].textContent) || 0;
        if (avg === 0 && cur === 0) silentExecs.push(name);
        if (row.dataset.w75x) continue;
        row.dataset.w75x = '1';
        var low = avg < W75_EXEC_MIN;
        cells[2].innerHTML = (low
          ? '<span style="color:#b3402f;font-weight:700">' + avg.toFixed(1) + 'h</span>'
          : avg.toFixed(1) + 'h')
          + ' / ' + W75_EXEC_MIN + 'h <span class="w54ex">exec</span>';
        if (low) {
          row.classList.add('w54low');
          var tb = row.parentNode;
          if (tb && tb.firstChild !== row) tb.insertBefore(row, tb.firstChild);
        }
      }
      /* editable-minimums bar note */
      var spans = sec.querySelectorAll('.w54edit span');
      for (var s = 0; s < spans.length; s++) {
        if (spans[s].textContent.indexOf('the board is exempt') === 0 && spans[s].innerHTML.indexOf('executives') < 0) {
          spans[s].innerHTML = 'the board is exempt from check-ins, except operational executives (Steel, Suhaib): ' + W75_EXEC_MIN + 'h minimum';
        }
      }
      /* header + silent-list wording and entries */
      var h4s = sec.querySelectorAll('h4');
      for (var j = 0; j < h4s.length; j++) {
        var h = h4s[j];
        if (h.textContent.indexOf('People (') === 0 && h.innerHTML.indexOf('executives') < 0) {
          h.innerHTML = h.innerHTML.replace('board exempt', 'board exempt, operational executives ' + W75_EXEC_MIN + 'h');
        }
        if (h.textContent.indexOf('Not checking in at all') === 0 && h.innerHTML.indexOf('executives') < 0) {
          h.innerHTML = h.innerHTML.replace('board exempt', 'board exempt except operational executives');
        }
      }
      if (silentExecs.length) {
        var silentBox = null;
        for (var j2 = 0; j2 < h4s.length; j2++) {
          if (h4s[j2].textContent.indexOf('Not checking in at all') === 0) { silentBox = h4s[j2].parentNode; break; }
        }
        if (silentBox) {
          var flags = silentBox.querySelector('.w52flags');
          if (!flags) {
            var empty = silentBox.querySelector('p.m-sub');
            flags = document.createElement('div');
            flags.className = 'w52flags';
            if (empty) silentBox.insertBefore(flags, empty); else silentBox.appendChild(flags);
          }
          silentExecs.forEach(function (n) {
            if (flags.innerHTML.indexOf(n) >= 0) return;
            var sp = document.createElement('span');
            sp.className = 'w52flag';
            sp.innerHTML = n + ' <small>Executive &middot; min ' + W75_EXEC_MIN + 'h/wk</small>';
            flags.appendChild(sp);
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  (function () {
    var queued = false;
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function () {
      if (queued) return;
      queued = true;
      setTimeout(function () { queued = false; w75ExecFix(); }, 120);
    });
    mo.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  })();

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa75 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa75();
      setTimeout(w75Decorate, 200);
      setTimeout(w75Card, 400);
    };
  }
  /* in case chat was already mounted before this wave ran */
  setTimeout(w75Decorate, 800);
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v76 Phase 1 (U5): Slack channel pull lives with Commitment
   =====================================================================
   The header "# Pull Slack" button is gone (base edit). Hours already
   have their own "Update hours from Slack" button in the Commitment
   tab (wave56). This wave adds the channel/DM work pull next to it,
   using the same slack-pull edge function the header button called.
   The ClickUp pull is removed for good: ClickUp is retired.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa76) return; window.__wa76 = true;

  function w76Ensure() {
    var bar = document.getElementById('w56bar');
    if (!bar || bar.querySelector('[data-w76slack]')) return;
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.setAttribute('data-w76slack', '1');
    b.textContent = 'Pull work from Slack channels';
    b.title = 'Read the mapped Slack channels and DMs since the last pull and fold work items into the board';
    var note = bar.querySelector('span:last-child');
    bar.insertBefore(b, note || null);
  }

  var W76_BUSY = false;
  document.body.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w76slack]');
    if (!b || W76_BUSY) return;
    if (!confirm('Pull from the mapped Slack channels now? New digests process automatically.')) return;
    W76_BUSY = true;
    b.disabled = true; b.textContent = 'Pulling from Slack...';
    var r = null;
    try { r = await db.functions.invoke('slack-pull', { body: {} }); } catch (e2) { r = null; }
    if (r && r.data && r.data.accepted) toast('Slack pull started. Results land over the next few minutes.');
    else if (r && r.data && r.data.error === 'tokens_missing') toast('Slack tokens are not set up yet. See GO-LIVE.md.');
    else toast('Slack pull failed to start');
    var n = 0;
    var t = setInterval(async function () {
      n++;
      try {
        await Promise.all([loadTranscriptsData(), loadAll()]);
        renderTranscripts(); renderAllSections(); renderDashboard(); renderWhatsNew();
      } catch (e3) { /* non-fatal */ }
      if (n >= 10) clearInterval(t);
    }, 12000);
    b.disabled = false; b.textContent = 'Pull work from Slack channels';
    W76_BUSY = false;
  });

  /* the Commitment view paints on demand; keep the button present */
  function w76Watch() {
    var target = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!target) return;
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function () { w76Ensure(); });
    mo.observe(target, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    w76Ensure();
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa76 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa76.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w76Watch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v77 Phase 1 (U1): department pages, one clean scan path
   =====================================================================
   Every department page becomes: compact header (banner, this period's
   narrative, priorities, quick tools, folded team) -> ONE task area
   (the workstream tabs) -> collapsible sections below (requests, goals,
   projects, updates, transcripts). Legacy narrative subsections that
   duplicate the tabs and hold no tasks are removed from the DOM.
   Mechanics: a RECONCILER, not a one-shot move. The dept renderers
   re-create the requests card, tools row and projects card on every
   repaint, so a per-panel debounced MutationObserver re-homes fresh
   elements into the folds each time. All wiring is delegated clicks on
   data attributes, so moving nodes keeps everything working. Fold
   open/closed state is remembered per browser in localStorage.
   After each reconcile pass the panel dispatches 'wa77sync' (wave80
   listens to re-apply empty states and counts).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa77) return; window.__wa77 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.w77f{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:14px;margin:14px 0;padding:0 16px;}' +
    '.w77f>summary{cursor:pointer;list-style:none;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-weight:800;font-size:13.5px;color:#103F39;padding:13px 2px;}' +
    '.w77f>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none;}' +
    '.w77f>summary:before{content:"\\25B8";font-size:11px;color:#797773;transition:transform .15s;}' +
    '.w77f[open]>summary:before{transform:rotate(90deg);}' +
    '.w77f>summary .w77n{font-weight:600;font-size:11.5px;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w77f>.w77b{padding:2px 2px 14px;}' +
    '.w77f .card{border:none;box-shadow:none;margin:0;padding:0;background:transparent;}' +
    '.w77f .w39goals{margin:0;}' +
    '.w77team{margin:10px 0 0;}' +
    '.w77team>summary{cursor:pointer;list-style:none;font-weight:800;font-size:12.5px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w77team>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none;}' +
    '.w77team>summary:before{content:"\\25B8";font-size:10px;color:#797773;margin-right:6px;}' +
    '.w77team[open]>summary:before{content:"\\25BE";}' +
    '.w77tools{margin:12px 0 0;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var FOLDS = [
    ['requests', 'Incoming requests'],
    ['goals', 'Quarterly goals'],
    ['projects', 'Projects'],
    ['updates', 'Most recent updates'],
    ['transcripts', 'Transcripts that contributed']
  ];

  function w77Key(dept, name) { return 'wa77.' + dept + '.' + name; }

  function w77MkFold(dept, name, label, defOpen) {
    var d = document.createElement('details');
    d.className = 'w77f';
    d.setAttribute('data-w77', name);
    var saved = null;
    try { saved = localStorage.getItem(w77Key(dept, name)); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    d.open = saved === null ? !!defOpen : saved === '1';
    d.innerHTML = '<summary>' + esc(label) + ' <span class="w77n"></span></summary><div class="w77b"></div>';
    d.addEventListener('toggle', function () {
      try { localStorage.setItem(w77Key(dept, name), d.open ? '1' : '0'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    });
    return d;
  }

  function w77Region(panel, id, card) {
    var region = panel.querySelector('[data-w77r]');
    if (!region) {
      region = document.createElement('div');
      region.setAttribute('data-w77r', '1');
      card.parentNode.insertBefore(region, card.nextSibling);
    } else if (region.previousElementSibling !== card) {
      card.parentNode.insertBefore(region, card.nextSibling);
    }
    FOLDS.forEach(function (f) {
      if (!region.querySelector('[data-w77="' + f[0] + '"]')) {
        region.appendChild(w77MkFold(id, f[0], f[1], false));
      }
    });
    return region;
  }

  function w77Body(region, name) {
    return region.querySelector('[data-w77="' + name + '"] .w77b');
  }
  function w77Num(region, name, n) {
    var el = region.querySelector('[data-w77="' + name + '"] summary .w77n');
    var v = (n === null || n === undefined) ? '' : String(n);
    /* wa116x: only write when it changed; the write itself re-ran the panel observer, seventy times a second across the hidden panels */
    if (el && el.textContent !== v) el.textContent = v;
  }

  function w77TabNames(panel) {
    return Array.prototype.map.call(panel.querySelectorAll('.w71tab'), function (t) {
      return (t.textContent || '').replace(/\s*\d+\s*$/, '').trim().toLowerCase();
    });
  }

  function w77Ensure(id) {
    var panel = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!panel || !panel.classList || !panel.classList.contains('panel')) return;
    var card = panel.querySelector('.card.dept-card');
    if (!card) return;
    var changed = false;
    var region = w77Region(panel, id, card);

    /* ---- 1. legacy narrative subsections out (tabbed depts only) ---- */
    var tabs = w77TabNames(panel);
    if (tabs.length) {
      panel.querySelectorAll('.wa21-list .subsection').forEach(function (ss) {
        var h = ss.querySelector('.subhead');
        if (!h) return;
        var name = (h.textContent || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (tabs.indexOf(name) >= 0) return;
        if (ss.querySelector('[data-task]')) return;
        ss.remove(); changed = true;
      });
    }

    /* ---- 2. fold the team tags ---- */
    var teamH = null;
    card.querySelectorAll('h3.block').forEach(function (h) {
      if ((h.textContent || '').trim() === 'Team') teamH = h;
    });
    var tags = teamH ? card.querySelector('.team-tags') : null;
    if (teamH && tags && !tags.closest('.w77team')) {
      var oldTd = card.querySelector('.w77team');
      if (oldTd) oldTd.remove();
      var td = document.createElement('details');
      td.className = 'w77team';
      td.innerHTML = '<summary>Team (' + tags.querySelectorAll('.team-tag').length + ')</summary>';
      teamH.parentNode.insertBefore(td, teamH);
      teamH.remove();
      td.appendChild(tags);
      changed = true;
    }

    /* ---- 3. quick tools into the header card (fresh copy wins) ---- */
    var freshTools = null;
    panel.querySelectorAll('.w39tools').forEach(function (t) {
      if (!t.classList.contains('w77tools')) freshTools = t;
    });
    if (freshTools) {
      var oldTools = card.querySelector('.w39tools.w77tools');
      if (oldTools && oldTools !== freshTools) oldTools.remove();
      freshTools.classList.add('w77tools');
      freshTools.classList.remove('w39top');
      var anchor = card.querySelector('.w77team') || card.querySelector('ul.prio');
      if (anchor && freshTools.previousElementSibling !== anchor) anchor.parentNode.insertBefore(freshTools, anchor.nextSibling);
      else if (!anchor) card.appendChild(freshTools);
      changed = true;
    }

    /* ---- 4. requests card into its fold (fresh copy wins) ---- */
    var rb = w77Body(region, 'requests');
    var freshReq = null;
    panel.querySelectorAll('.card.req-card').forEach(function (r) {
      if (!rb.contains(r)) freshReq = r;
    });
    if (freshReq) {
      rb.innerHTML = '';
      rb.appendChild(freshReq);
      var rh = freshReq.querySelector('h2'); if (rh) rh.remove();
      changed = true;
    }
    var reqN = rb.querySelectorAll('.req-row').length;
    w77Num(region, 'requests', reqN);
    try {
      if (reqN > 0 && localStorage.getItem(w77Key(id, 'requests')) === null) {
        region.querySelector('[data-w77="requests"]').open = true;
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

    /* ---- 5. quarterly goals bar into its fold ---- */
    var gb = w77Body(region, 'goals');
    var freshGoals = null;
    panel.querySelectorAll('.w39goals').forEach(function (g) {
      if (!gb.contains(g)) freshGoals = g;
    });
    if (freshGoals) {
      gb.innerHTML = '';
      freshGoals.classList.remove('w39top');
      var gh = freshGoals.querySelector('h3'); if (gh) gh.remove();
      gb.appendChild(freshGoals);
      changed = true;
    }
    w77Num(region, 'goals', gb.querySelectorAll('.w39goal').length);

    /* ---- 6. projects card into its fold ---- */
    var pb = w77Body(region, 'projects');
    var freshProj = null;
    panel.querySelectorAll('.w27-card').forEach(function (p) {
      if (!pb.contains(p)) freshProj = p;
    });
    if (freshProj) {
      pb.innerHTML = '';
      pb.appendChild(freshProj);
      var ph = freshProj.querySelector('h2'); if (ph) ph.remove();
      changed = true;
    }

    /* ---- 7. updates details into its fold ---- */
    var ub = w77Body(region, 'updates');
    var freshUpd = null;
    panel.querySelectorAll('details.wa21-upd').forEach(function (u) {
      if (!ub.contains(u) && !u.classList.contains('w77f')) freshUpd = u;
    });
    if (freshUpd) {
      ub.innerHTML = '';
      var uw = freshUpd.querySelector('.wa21-updwrap');
      if (uw) { ub.appendChild(uw); freshUpd.remove(); }
      else ub.appendChild(freshUpd);
      changed = true;
    }

    /* ---- 8. transcripts-that-contributed block into its fold ---- */
    var tb = w77Body(region, 'transcripts');
    var txH = null;
    panel.querySelectorAll('.wa21-list h3.block, .card.dept-card h3.block').forEach(function (h) {
      if (!tb.contains(h) && (h.textContent || '').indexOf('Transcripts that contributed') >= 0) txH = h;
    });
    if (txH) {
      tb.innerHTML = '';
      var node = txH.nextSibling;
      while (node) {
        var next = node.nextSibling;
        if (node.nodeType === 1 && ((node.classList && node.classList.contains('w27-card')) || (node.matches && node.matches('h3.block')))) break;
        tb.appendChild(node);
        node = next;
      }
      txH.remove();
      changed = true;
    }

    if (changed) {
      try { panel.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('wa77sync', { detail: { id: id } })); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    }
  }

  var W77_MO = {};
  var W77_T = {};
  function w77Watch(id) {
    if (W77_MO[id]) return;
    var panel = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!panel) return;
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function () {
      clearTimeout(W77_T[id]);
      W77_T[id] = setTimeout(function () { try { w77Ensure(id); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 200);
    });
    mo.observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    W77_MO[id] = mo;
  }

  function w77All() {
    try {
      (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' ? DEPT_ORDER : []).forEach(function (d) {
        try { w77Ensure(d); w77Watch(d); } catch (e) { /* per-dept non-fatal */ }
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  if (typeof renderSection === 'function') {
    var __rs77 = renderSection;
    renderSection = function (id) {
      __rs77.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w77Ensure(id); w77Watch(id); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa77 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa77.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w77All(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v78 Phase 1 (U2): one personal queue, on Home

   Home's "Needs your attention" strip (wave74) is the one canonical
   personal queue. The old needs-me routes and the header button land

(function () {
  if (window.__wa78) return; window.__wa78 = true;

  /* route the old hashes to Home */
  if (typeof applyHash === 'function') {
    var __ah78 = applyHash;
    applyHash = function () {
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
      var a = h.split('/')[0];
      if (a === 'needs-me' || a === 'needsme') {
        if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) {
          showPanel('mypage');
          if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') renderMyPage();
        } else showPanel('overview');

  /* if anything still shows the needsme panel, it points Home */
  function w78Needsme() {

  /* Decisions page: "Your plate" becomes a compact pointer to Home */

  var w78t = null;
