/* =====================================================================
   WA v79 Phase 1 (U3): one canonical quarter format
   =====================================================================
   Canonical form: "Q1 FY2027" (fiscal year Oct 1 to Sep 30, labelled by
   ending year). Freetext variants like "Q4 2026" or "q4 fy 2026" are
   normalised in memory when goals load (the database migration to match
   ships with this wave). The goal modal's quarter freetext becomes a
   picker, and empty quarter buckets beyond the current and next quarter
   collapse behind a "show empty quarters" link on the Strategy page.
   Org-tier horizons (FY2027, Long term...) stay freetext on purpose.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa79) return; window.__wa79 = true;

  function w79Norm(q) {
    if (!q) return q;
    var s = String(q).trim();
    var m = s.match(/^q\s*([1-4])\s*(?:fy)?\s*'?(\d{2,4})$/i) ||
            s.match(/^(?:fy)?\s*'?(\d{2,4})\s*q\s*([1-4])$/i);
    if (!m) return s;
    var qn, yr;
    if (/^q/i.test(s)) { qn = m[1]; yr = m[2]; } else { qn = m[2]; yr = m[1]; }
    if (yr.length === 2) yr = '20' + yr;
    return 'Q' + qn + ' FY' + yr;
  }
  window.waQNorm = w79Norm;

  /* current + upcoming fiscal quarters (Oct-Sep, labelled by ending year) */
  function w79Quarters(n) {
    var out = [];
    var d = new Date();
    var mth = d.getMonth();                 /* 0..11 */
    var fq = Math.floor(((mth + 3) % 12) / 3) + 1;   /* Oct=Q1 */
    var fy = d.getFullYear() + (mth >= 9 ? 1 : 0);
    for (var i = 0; i < (n || 6); i++) {
      out.push('Q' + fq + ' FY' + fy);
      fq++; if (fq > 4) { fq = 1; fy++; }
    }
    return out;
  }

  /* normalise the cache whenever goals load */
  if (typeof waLoadGoals === 'function') {
    var __lg79 = waLoadGoals;
    waLoadGoals = async function () {
      var r = await __lg79.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).forEach(function (g) {
          if (g && g.quarter) g.quarter = w79Norm(g.quarter);
        });
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }

  /* goal modal: quarter becomes a picker (dept tier only) */
  if (typeof wa29GoalModal === 'function') {
    var __gm79 = wa29GoalModal;
    wa29GoalModal = function () {
      __gm79.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        var inp = document.getElementById('w29q');
        if (!inp || inp.tagName === 'SELECT') return;
        var lab = inp.closest('div') && inp.closest('div').querySelector('label');
        if (lab && /horizon/i.test(lab.textContent || '')) return;   /* org tier stays freetext */
        var cur = w79Norm(inp.value || '');
        var opts = w79Quarters(6);
        if (cur && opts.indexOf(cur) < 0) opts.unshift(cur);
        opts.push('No quarter');
        var sel = document.createElement('select');
        sel.id = 'w29q';
        sel.innerHTML = opts.map(function (q) {
          return '<option value="' + esc(q === 'No quarter' ? '' : q) + '"' +
            ((q === cur || (q === 'No quarter' && !cur)) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(q) + '</option>';
        }).join('');
        inp.parentNode.replaceChild(sel, inp);
        var dl = document.getElementById('w29ql'); if (dl) dl.remove();
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* Strategy page: collapse empty quarters beyond current + next */
  function w79Collapse() {
    var page = document.getElementById('goals');
    if (!page) return;
    var keep = w79Quarters(2);
    var hidden = 0;
    var qs = page.querySelectorAll('details.w29q');
    qs.forEach(function (d) {
      var sm = d.querySelector('summary');
      var label = sm ? (sm.textContent || '').trim().split('\n')[0] : '';
      var qname = (label.match(/^Q[1-4] FY\d{4}/) || [label])[0];
      var n = d.querySelectorAll('.w29ge').length;
      if (n === 0 && keep.indexOf(qname) < 0) { d.style.display = 'none'; d.setAttribute('data-w79hid', '1'); hidden++; }
      else { d.style.display = ''; d.removeAttribute('data-w79hid'); }
    });
    var old = page.querySelector('[data-w79show]');
    if (old) old.remove();
    if (hidden > 0 && qs.length) {
      var a = document.createElement('div');
      a.setAttribute('data-w79show', '1');
      a.style.cssText = 'font-size:12px;margin:4px 0 10px;';
      a.innerHTML = '<a style="cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;color:var(--wa-muted)">Show ' + hidden + ' empty quarter' + (hidden === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</a>';
      a.firstChild.addEventListener('click', function () {
        page.querySelectorAll('[data-w79hid]').forEach(function (d) { d.style.display = ''; d.removeAttribute('data-w79hid'); });
        a.remove();
      });
      var last = qs[qs.length - 1];
      if (last && last.parentNode) last.parentNode.insertBefore(a, last.nextSibling);
    }
  }

  if (typeof renderGoalsPage === 'function') {
    var __gp79 = renderGoalsPage;
    renderGoalsPage = async function () {
      await __gp79.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w79Collapse(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v80 Phase 1 (U4 + audit 7 + audit 8): empty states that act,
   counts that agree, and roles that tell the truth
   =====================================================================
   1. Actionable empty states: wherever a list is empty, the message
      offers the next step (add one, or see how it fills from meetings).
   2. Count sync: visible "(N)" counters recount from the rendered rows
      beside them, so a number can never disagree with its list.
   3. Admin panel: the three founder-failsafe accounts show their
      EFFECTIVE role (admin, enforced server-side) instead of a
      dropdown that can display something else.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa80) return; window.__wa80 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.w80act{font-size:12px;margin-top:4px;}' +
    '.w80act a{cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;color:#103F39;margin-right:12px;}' +
    '.w80chip{display:inline-block;background:#ECEBE3;border:1px solid #cfe0d8;border-radius:999px;padding:2px 10px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w80note{font-size:10.5px;color:#797773;display:block;margin-top:2px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- 1. actionable empty states ---------- */
  function w80Dept(id) {
    var panel = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!panel) return;
    /* empty workstream subsections */
    panel.querySelectorAll('.wa21-list .subsection').forEach(function (ss) {
      if (ss.querySelector('[data-task]') || ss.querySelector('.w80act')) return;
      var emptyEl = null;
      ss.querySelectorAll('p, li').forEach(function (p) { if (/no open tasks/i.test(p.textContent || '')) emptyEl = p; });
      if (!emptyEl) return;
      var act = document.createElement('div');
      act.className = 'w80act';
      act.innerHTML = '<a data-w80add="task">+ Add a task</a><a data-w80how>How tasks arrive from meetings</a>';
      var host = emptyEl.closest('ul') || emptyEl;
      host.parentNode.insertBefore(act, host.nextSibling);
    });
    /* empty folds (wave77) */
    var folds = { goals: ['No quarterly goals here yet.', '#goals', 'Set one on the Department goals page'],
                  requests: ['Nothing waiting on this team.', '#requests', 'File a request'],
                  updates: ['Updates arrive automatically from meeting transcripts.', '#transcripts', 'Upload a transcript'] };
    Object.keys(folds).forEach(function (k) {
      var fold = panel.querySelector('[data-w77="' + k + '"] .w77b');
      if (!fold || fold.querySelector('.w80act')) return;
      var has = fold.querySelector('[data-task], .w39goal, .req-row, li, .wa21-upd-row, .upd-row');
      if (has) return;
      if (!fold.textContent.trim() || fold.textContent.trim().length < 4) {
        var d = folds[k];
        var el = document.createElement('div');
        el.className = 'w80act';
        el.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">' + d[0] + '</span> <a data-w80go="' + d[1] + '">' + d[2] + '</a>';
        fold.appendChild(el);
      }
    });
  }

  document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target;
    if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var add = t.closest('[data-w80add]');
    if (add) {
      var panel = add.closest('.panel');
      var btn = panel && panel.querySelector('[data-add="task"]');
      if (btn) btn.click();
      return;
    }
    var how = t.closest('[data-w80how]');
    if (how) { location.hash = '#transcripts'; return; }
    var go = t.closest('[data-w80go]');
    if (go) { location.hash = go.getAttribute('data-w80go'); return; }
  });

  /* ---------- 2. counts recount from the DOM beside them ---------- */
  function w80Counts() {
    /* Decided (N) on the Decisions page */
    try {
      var done = document.getElementById('decisionDoneList');
      if (done) {
        var n = done.querySelectorAll('[data-decision]').length;
        var wrap = document.getElementById('decDoneWrap');
        var sm = wrap && wrap.querySelector('summary');
        if (sm && /\(\d+\)/.test(sm.textContent)) sm.textContent = sm.textContent.replace(/\(\d+\)/, '(' + n + ')');
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    /* Incoming requests (N) cards on dept pages */
    try {
      document.querySelectorAll('.card.req-card').forEach(function (card) {
        var h = card.querySelector('h2');
        if (!h) return;
        var n = card.querySelectorAll('.req-row').length;
        if (/\(\d+\)/.test(h.textContent)) h.textContent = h.textContent.replace(/\(\d+\)/, '(' + n + ')');
      });
      /* wave77 fold summaries recount too */
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-w77="requests"]').forEach(function (f) {
        var nEl = f.querySelector('summary .w77n');
        if (nEl) nEl.textContent = String(f.querySelectorAll('.req-row').length);
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-w77="goals"]').forEach(function (f) {
        var nEl = f.querySelector('summary .w77n');
        if (nEl) nEl.textContent = String(f.querySelectorAll('.w39goal').length);
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* ---------- 3. founder accounts show the effective role ---------- */
  var W80_FOUNDERS = ['steel.macdonald@welfareavenue.com', 'steelmacdonald@gmail.com', 'suhaib.abdillahi@welfareavenue.com'];
  function w80Admin() {
    W80_FOUNDERS.forEach(function (em) {
      document.querySelectorAll('select[data-w40r="' + em + '"]').forEach(function (sel) {
        var chip = document.createElement('span');
        chip.innerHTML = '<span class="w80chip">admin</span><span class="w80note">founder failsafe: this account is always admin, enforced in the database</span>';
        sel.parentNode.replaceChild(chip, sel);
      });
    });
  }

  /* ---------- run after paints ---------- */
  function w80All() {
    try { (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' ? DEPT_ORDER : []).forEach(w80Dept); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    w80Counts();
    w80Admin();
  }
  if (typeof renderSection === 'function') {
    var __rs80 = renderSection;
    renderSection = function (id) {
      __rs80.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w80Dept(id); w80Counts(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof renderDecisions === 'function') {
    var __rd80 = renderDecisions;
    renderDecisions = function () {
      __rd80.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w80Counts(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') {
    var __ar80 = wa40Render;
    wa40Render = async function () {
      await __ar80.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w80Admin(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa80 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa80.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w80All(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      /* wave77 reconciles dept panels continuously; re-apply after each pass */
      try {
        (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' ? DEPT_ORDER : []).forEach(function (d) {
          var p = document.getElementById(d);
          if (p && !p.dataset.w80wired) {
            p.dataset.w80wired = '1';
            p.addEventListener('wa77sync', function () {
              setTimeout(function () { try { w80Dept(d); w80Counts(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 60);
            });
          }
        });
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v81 Phase 1 (U6): the assistant becomes a proper drawer
   =====================================================================
   Same ids, same wave4 toggle, same wave38 pipeline. The panel is now a
   full-height drawer on the right, and when it opens on an empty
   thread it offers three starter prompts that match the page you are
   on. Clicking a prompt prefills the input; you still press send.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa81) return; window.__wa81 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#waChatPanel{top:0;right:0;bottom:0;height:100vh;max-height:100vh;width:400px;max-width:96vw;border-radius:0;border-top:none;border-bottom:none;border-right:none;box-shadow:-14px 0 40px rgba(0,0,0,.22);}' +
    '.w81chips{padding:4px 12px 10px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;}' +
    '.w81chips .w81h{font-size:10.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;color:#797773;font-weight:800;}' +
    '.w81chip{text-align:left;background:#f2f7f5;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:10px;padding:8px 10px;font-size:12.5px;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w81chip:hover{background:#e8f1ed;}' +
    '#w81close{position:absolute;top:10px;right:12px;background:none;border:none;color:#FFFAF2;font-size:20px;line-height:1;cursor:pointer;opacity:.85;z-index:2;}' +
    '#w81close:hover{opacity:1;}' +
    '#waChatPanel .wac-h{position:relative;padding-right:40px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* the full-height drawer covers the pill, so it needs its own close */
  function w81Close() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('waChatPanel');
    var head = panel && panel.querySelector('.wac-h');
    if (!head || document.getElementById('w81close')) return;
    var x = document.createElement('button');
    x.id = 'w81close';
    x.innerHTML = '&#10005;';
    x.title = 'Close';
    x.addEventListener('click', function () { panel.style.display = 'none'; });
    head.appendChild(x);
  }

  var W81_PROMPTS = {
    mypage: ['What should I focus on this week?', 'What did I miss since last week?', 'Which of my tasks are overdue?'],
    overview: ['Which decisions are still open?', 'What was decided this month?', 'What needs a decision from the EDs?'],
    goals: ['Which goals have no project attached?', 'How are this quarter\'s goals shaping up?', 'Which team is furthest behind on its goals?'],
    transcripts: ['Summarise the last leadership meeting', 'What was discussed about fundraising this month?', 'Which meetings mentioned me?'],
    volunteering: ['What is left to prepare for the next serving event?', 'Which SOP steps have no owner?', 'How many volunteers joined the last event?'],
    hrcentre: ['Who is still mid-onboarding?', 'Who has not checked in hours for two weeks?', 'Which roles are we recruiting for?'],
    requests: ['Which requests are waiting on marketing?', 'What kinds of requests can I file?', 'Which requests have been open longest?'],
    finances: ['How much did we raise this quarter?', 'What were the biggest expenses this quarter?', 'How is spending tracking against budget?'],
    _dept: ['What is blocking DEPT right now?', 'Summarise DEPT progress this period', 'Who carries the most open tasks in DEPT?'],
    _default: ['What happened across Welfare Avenue this week?', 'What needs attention today?', 'Who is mentioned most in recent meetings?']
  };

  function w81Prompts() {
    var active = document.querySelector('.panel.active');
    var id = active ? active.id : '';
    if (W81_PROMPTS[id]) return W81_PROMPTS[id];
    try {
      if (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' && DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(id) >= 0) {
        var label = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[id]) || id;
        return W81_PROMPTS._dept.map(function (p) { return p.replace('DEPT', label); });
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    return W81_PROMPTS._default;
  }

  function w81Input() { return document.getElementById('w38in') || document.getElementById('wacInput'); }

  function w81Inject() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('waChatPanel');
    var msgs = document.getElementById('wacMsgs');
    if (!panel || !msgs || panel.style.display !== 'flex') return;
    var old = panel.querySelector('.w81chips');
    if (old) old.remove();
    if (msgs.querySelectorAll('.wac-m').length > 1) return;   /* real thread already */
    var box = document.createElement('div');
    box.className = 'w81chips';
    box.innerHTML = '<span class="w81h">Try asking</span>' + w81Prompts().map(function (p) {
      return '<button class="w81chip">' + esc(p) + '</button>';
    }).join('');
    var inRow = panel.querySelector('.wac-in');
    panel.insertBefore(box, inRow || null);
    box.querySelectorAll('.w81chip').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var inp = w81Input();
        if (inp) { inp.value = b.textContent; inp.focus(); }
      });
    });
  }

  /* runs after wave4's toggle listener on the same button */
  function w81Wire() {
    var btn = document.getElementById('waChatBtn');
    if (!btn || btn.dataset.w81) return;
    btn.dataset.w81 = '1';
    btn.addEventListener('click', function () { setTimeout(function () { w81Close(); w81Inject(); }, 30); });
    w81Close();
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa81 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa81.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w81Wire(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof window.w75Open === 'function') {
    var __o81 = window.w75Open;
    window.w75Open = function () {
      __o81.apply(this, arguments);
      try { setTimeout(w81Inject, 30); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v82 Phase 1 (U7): the header box searches everything
   =====================================================================
   renderSearch is replaced (the base version is superseded): on top of
   decisions, tasks, updates and transcripts it now searches people,
   goals, projects, trainings, agendas and requests, grouped, from the
   caches already in memory. Transcript deep search still streams in
   from the search_transcripts RPC exactly as before.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa82) return; window.__wa82 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent = '.sr-type{min-width:86px;}.search-pop{max-height:60vh;overflow-y:auto;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  renderSearch = function (q) {
    var ql = q.toLowerCase();
    var out = [];
    function has(s) { return (s || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(ql) >= 0; }

    DB_DECISIONS.filter(function (d) { return has(d.title); }).slice(0, 4)
      .forEach(function (d) { out.push({ t: 'Decision', label: d.title, hash: '#decision/' + d.id }); });
    DB_TASKS.filter(function (t) { return has((t.title || '') + ' ' + (t.owner || '')); }).slice(0, 5)
      .forEach(function (t) { out.push({ t: (DEPT_LABEL[t.department] || 'Task') + (t.done ? ', done' : ''), label: t.title, hash: '#task/' + t.id }); });
    try {
      DB_MEMBERS.filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false && has((m.name || '') + ' ' + (m.role || '')); }).slice(0, 4)
        .forEach(function (m) { out.push({ t: 'Person', label: m.name + (m.role ? ', ' + m.role : ''), hash: '#person/' + m.id }); });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    try {
      (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).filter(function (g) { return has((g.title || '') + ' ' + (g.quarter || '')); }).slice(0, 3)
        .forEach(function (g) { out.push({ t: 'Goal', label: (g.quarter ? g.quarter + ': ' : '') + g.title, hash: '#goals' }); });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    try {
      (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []).filter(function (p) { return has(p.name); }).slice(0, 3)
        .forEach(function (p) { out.push({ t: 'Project', label: p.name, hash: '#project/' + p.id }); });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    try {
      (typeof WA36_TRAININGS !== 'undefined' ? WA36_TRAININGS : []).filter(function (tr) { return has((tr.title || tr.name || '') + ' ' + (tr.description || '')); }).slice(0, 3)
        .forEach(function (tr) { out.push({ t: 'Training', label: tr.title || tr.name, hash: '#training' }); });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    try {
      (typeof AGENDAS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? AGENDAS_CACHE : []).filter(function (a) { return has((a.title || '') + ' ' + (a.purpose || '')); }).slice(0, 3)
        .forEach(function (a) { out.push({ t: 'Agenda', label: a.title || 'Agenda', hash: '#agendas' }); });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    try {
      (typeof REQ_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? REQ_CACHE : []).filter(function (r) {
        return has((r.status || '') + ' ' + JSON.stringify(r.details || {}));
      }).slice(0, 3).forEach(function (r) {
        var rt = (typeof REQ_TYPES !== 'undefined' ? REQ_TYPES : []).filter(function (x) { return x.id === r.type_id || x.key === r.type; })[0];
        out.push({ t: 'Request', label: ((rt && rt.label) || 'Request') + (r.status ? ', ' + r.status : ''), hash: '#requests' });
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    DB_UPDATES.filter(function (u) { return has(u.text); }).slice(0, 3)
      .forEach(function (u) { out.push({ t: (DEPT_LABEL[u.department] || '') + ' update', label: (u.entry_label ? u.entry_label + ': ' : '') + (u.text || '').slice(0, 90), hash: '#dept/' + u.department }); });
    txSearchResults.slice(0, 4)
      .forEach(function (r) { out.push({ t: 'Transcript', label: (r.title || 'Untitled') + (r.snippet ? ': ' + r.snippet.slice(0, 70) : ''), hash: '#tx/' + r.id }); });

    var pop = document.getElementById('searchResults');
    pop.innerHTML = out.length
      ? out.map(function (o) { return '<div class="sr" data-hash="' + o.hash + '"><span class="sr-type">' + esc(o.t) + '</span>' + esc(o.label) + '</div>'; }).join('')
      : '<div class="sr" style="color:var(--wa-muted);">No matches anywhere in the Hub</div>';
    pop.style.display = 'block';
    pop.querySelectorAll('.sr[data-hash]').forEach(function (s) {
      s.addEventListener('click', function () {
        pop.style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('globalSearch').value = '';
        location.hash = s.dataset.hash;
      });
    });
  };
})();

/* =====================================================================
   WA v84 Phase 1 (U9): a 60 second first-visit tour, by role
   =====================================================================
   On the first visit (per browser, localStorage 'wa84tour') a small
   card walks the person through the Hub in 4-5 steps that match their
   role: members see Home, their department and the assistant; leaders
   also see the HR centre and requests; admins also see the Admin page;
   board viewers get the read-only version. Skippable at every step,
   never shown again once finished, and re-openable from any help popup
   footer ("Take the 60 second tour").
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa84) return; window.__wa84 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w84back{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(10,25,20,.55);z-index:400;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:18px;}' +
    '#w84card{background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:16px;max-width:420px;width:100%;padding:22px 22px 16px;box-shadow:0 24px 70px rgba(0,0,0,.35);}' +
    '#w84card h3{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:17px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '#w84card p{margin:0 0 14px;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.55;color:#1F201E;}' +
    '.w84dots{display:flex;gap:5px;margin-bottom:12px;}' +
    '.w84dots i{width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:#DEE0D8;}' +
    '.w84dots i.on{background:#103F39;}' +
    '.w84row{display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:flex-end;align-items:center;}' +
    '.w84row .skip{margin-right:auto;background:none;border:none;color:#797773;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;}' +
    '.w84btn{background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;border:none;border-radius:999px;padding:9px 18px;font-weight:700;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w84btn.ghost{background:#ECEBE3;color:#103F39;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  function w84Role() {
    try { if (typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board') return 'board'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) {
        if (WA40_MINE.role === 'admin') return 'admin';
        if (WA40_MINE.role === 'leader') return 'leader';
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return 'member';
  }

  function w84Steps(role) {
    var s = [];
    s.push({ t: 'Welcome to the Hub', p: 'This is where Welfare Avenue actually runs: meetings turn into tasks, goals and decisions automatically, and everyone can see how the work moves. This tour takes about a minute.', hash: null });
    s.push({ t: 'Home is your queue', p: 'Home shows what needs you: overdue tasks, mentions from meetings, your team and your requests. If you only check one page, check this one.', hash: '#mypage' });
    if (role !== 'board') {
      s.push({ t: 'Your team page', p: 'Open Teams in the menu and pick your team. The tabs in the middle are your workstreams; everything else folds away until you need it.', hash: null });
      s.push({ t: 'Ask the Hub', p: 'The green pill in the corner answers questions from everything in here: meetings, tasks, goals, decisions. Try one of the suggested prompts.', hash: null });
    }
    if (role === 'leader' || role === 'admin') {
      s.push({ t: 'Requests and the HR centre', p: 'Teams ask each other for work through Requests, and the HR centre runs onboarding, training and time commitments. Both are in the top menu.', hash: null });
    }
    if (role === 'admin') {
      s.push({ t: 'The Admin page', p: 'Invite people by email, set what each role can see, and check the audit trail. Its sections are at the bottom of the menu, under Admin.', hash: null });
    }
    if (role === 'board') {
      s.push({ t: 'A read-only window', p: 'As a board viewer you see decisions, goals and progress across the organisation. Nothing here needs anything from you day to day.', hash: null });
    }
    return s;
  }

  function w84Show(force) {
    try { if (!force && localStorage.getItem('wa84tour')) return; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (document.getElementById('w84back')) return;
    var steps = w84Steps(w84Role());
    var i = 0;
    var back = document.createElement('div');
    back.id = 'w84back';
    back.innerHTML = '<div id="w84card"></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(back);
    function done() {
      try { localStorage.setItem('wa84tour', '1'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      back.remove();
    }
    function paint() {
      var st = steps[i];
      var card = document.getElementById('w84card');
      card.innerHTML = '<div class="w84dots">' + steps.map(function (x, k) { return '<i class="' + (k === i ? 'on' : '') + '"></i>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
        '<h3>' + esc(st.t) + '</h3><p>' + esc(st.p) + '</p>' +
        '<div class="w84row"><button class="skip">Skip the tour</button>' +
        (i > 0 ? '<button class="w84btn ghost" data-b>Back</button>' : '') +
        '<button class="w84btn" data-n>' + (i === steps.length - 1 ? 'Done' : 'Next') + '</button></div>';
      card.querySelector('.skip').addEventListener('click', done);
      var b = card.querySelector('[data-b]');
      if (b) b.addEventListener('click', function () { i--; paint(); });
      card.querySelector('[data-n]').addEventListener('click', function () {
        var st2 = steps[i];
        if (st2.hash) { try { location.hash = st2.hash; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
        if (i === steps.length - 1) { done(); return; }
        i++; paint();
      });
    }
    paint();
  }
  window.w84Tour = function () { w84Show(true); };

  /* a "take the tour" line at the bottom of every help popup */
  function w84HelpLink() {
    var box = document.getElementById('modalBox');
    if (!box || box.querySelector('[data-w84t]') || !box.querySelector('.w61wrap, .w60wrap')) return;
    var a = document.createElement('div');
    a.setAttribute('data-w84t', '1');
    a.style.cssText = 'font-size:12px;margin-top:10px;';
    a.innerHTML = '<a style="cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;color:#103F39">New here? Take the 60 second tour</a>';
    a.firstChild.addEventListener('click', function () { try { closeModal(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } w84Show(true); });
    box.appendChild(a);
  }
  var mo = new MutationObserver(function () { w84HelpLink(); });

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa84 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa84.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        var box = document.getElementById('modalBox');
        if (box) mo.observe(box, { childList: true });
        setTimeout(function () { w84Show(false); }, 900);
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v85 Phase 1 (U5 guides + page template): every page says what it
   is for, and the help matches what you are looking at
   =====================================================================
   1. A one-line purpose under each page title (the consistent page
      template: title, purpose, primary action, help).
   2. Refreshed help guides for the pages whose guides predated the
      v68-v77 changes: dept pages (workstream tabs + folds), Strategy
      (four tabs + canonical quarters), Admin (invite/remove), Home
      (v74 rebuild) and Training (the catalog). Registered after the
      v60/v61/v65 help listeners, so this content wins those keys;
      all other keys keep their existing guides.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa85) return; window.__wa85 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent = '.w85p{font-size:12.5px;color:#797773;margin:-6px 0 12px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- 1. purpose lines ---------- */
  var W85_PURPOSE = {
    overview: 'The executive view: the numbers, major announcements, and the decisions that sit with Ian, Shima, Suhaib and Steel.',
    dashboard: 'The numbers behind the work: task movement, hours and momentum per team.',
    transcripts: 'Upload a meeting and the Hub turns it into tasks, updates and decisions automatically.',
    agendas: 'Meeting agendas, drafted from live work and shared before the meeting.',
    goals: 'Every team\'s quarterly goals, SMART, with tasks and comments.',
    requests: 'How teams ask each other for work, with a form for every kind of ask.',
    importantinfo: 'Logins, links and documents the team reaches for most.',
    finances: 'Where the money stands, and what needs someone.',   /* wave164: the first section's line (164_wave164 changes it with the section); wave302: Today */
    orgchart: 'Who reports to whom, team by team, with the open positions. Click anyone for their reporting line and profile.',
    training: 'Your onboarding and the training catalog, tracked per person.',
    volunteering: 'The serving programme: events, volunteers, the master SOP and inventory.',
    hrcentre: 'Onboarding, training assignment, recruitment and time commitments in one place.',
    activity: 'Everything that changed in the Hub, newest first.',
    adminpanel: 'People, roles and access.',
    create: 'Branded content drafts from a prompt.'
  };
  function w85Purpose(id) {
    var txt = W85_PURPOSE[id];
    if (!txt) return;
    var panel = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!panel || panel.querySelector('.w85p')) return;
    var h = null;
    if (id === 'overview') {
      panel.querySelectorAll('h2').forEach(function (x) {
        if (!h && (x.textContent || '').indexOf('Open decisions') >= 0) h = x;
      });
    }
    if (!h) h = panel.querySelector('h2');
    if (!h) return;
    var p = document.createElement('p');
    p.className = 'w85p';
    p.textContent = txt;
    h.parentNode.insertBefore(p, h.nextSibling);
  }
  function w85AllPurpose() { Object.keys(W85_PURPOSE).forEach(w85Purpose); }

  /* some pages repaint their panel after showPanel; keep the line present */
  var W85_MO = {};
  function w85Watch(id) {
    if (W85_MO[id]) return;
    var panel = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!panel) return;
    var t = null;
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function () {
      clearTimeout(t);
      t = setTimeout(function () { try { w85Purpose(id); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 180);
    });
    mo.observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    W85_MO[id] = mo;
  }

  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp85 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp85.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w85Purpose(id); w85Watch(id); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* ---------- 2. refreshed guides ---------- */
  function sec(title, body) {
    return '<div class="w61sec"><div class="t"><b>' + title + '</b><span>' + body + '</span></div></div>';
  }
  function w85Guide(k) {
    var wrap = null;
    if (k === 'dept') {
      wrap = '<h2>How a department page works</h2><div class="w61wrap">' +
        sec('The header is the story', 'Banner, this period\'s narrative, priorities and quick tools. The team list folds away under "Team".') +
        sec('One task area', 'The tabs in the middle are the department\'s workstreams. All shows everything; each tab filters the same list, in list, board or calendar view.') +
        sec('Tasks arrive on their own', 'Upload a meeting on the Meetings page and the Hub files tasks here automatically, with owner, workstream and project. You can always add one by hand with + Add task.') +
        sec('Everything else folds', 'Requests, quarterly goals, projects, updates and source transcripts sit in collapsible sections below the tasks. The Hub remembers which ones you keep open.') +
        '</div><div style="text-align:right;margin-top:8px"><button id="w61c" class="btn ghost mini">Got it</button></div>';
    } else if (k === 'goals') {
      wrap = '<h2>How Department goals work</h2><div class="w61wrap">' +
        sec('Four tabs', 'Projects (the work), Department goals (quarterly targets), Organizational goals (the direction) and The map (how they all connect).') +
        sec('Quarters are picked, not typed', 'Goals use one quarter format, like Q1 FY2027 (the fiscal year runs Oct 1 to Sep 30). Empty far-off quarters stay collapsed until you need them.') +
        sec('The map answers "why"', 'Projects link to goals, department goals roll up to organizational ones. Click any node to pull its whole chain into focus.') +
        sec('Wins are collected for you', 'When a meeting mentions progress on a goal, it appears on the goal as a win, quote included.') +
        '</div><div style="text-align:right;margin-top:8px"><button id="w61c" class="btn ghost mini">Got it</button></div>';
    } else if (k === 'adminpanel') {
      wrap = '<h2>How Admin works</h2><div class="w61wrap">' +
        sec('Invite by email', '+ Invite someone creates the account, files the person in their team and sends the sign-in invite in one step.') +
        sec('Roles decide what people see', 'Viewer, member, leader, admin. The access editor next to each person sets exactly which pages their account shows, with sensible presets.') +
        sec('Founders are always admins', 'The two founder accounts and the co-ED are locked to admin in the database itself, so nobody can lock the organisation out. That is why their role is a chip, not a dropdown.') +
        sec('Removing someone', 'Remove deactivates the person, blocks sign-in and deletes the login. Their history in the Hub stays.') +
        '</div><div style="text-align:right;margin-top:8px"><button id="w61c" class="btn ghost mini">Got it</button></div>';
    } else if (k === 'mypage') {
      wrap = '<h2>How Home works</h2><div class="w61wrap">' +
        sec('Needs your attention', 'The strip at the top is your one personal queue: overdue tasks and meeting mentions you have not seen. Other pages point here.') +
        sec('Your tasks, grouped', 'Overdue, due this week, later. Show all expands past the first eight.') +
        sec('Mentions', 'When a meeting mentions you, it lands on the right with the quote. Got it marks it seen.') +
        sec('Ask the Hub', 'The green pill answers questions from everything in the Hub. The card above your tasks has ready-made prompts to start from.') +
        '</div><div style="text-align:right;margin-top:8px"><button id="w61c" class="btn ghost mini">Got it</button></div>';
    } else if (k === 'training') {
      wrap = '<h2>How Training works</h2><div class="w61wrap">' +
        sec('Your onboarding first', 'Until it is complete, your onboarding checklist sits at the top. Tick steps as you go; changed steps resurface on their own.') +
        sec('A browsable catalog', 'Search, filter by category or path, and open any card for the description and link. Mark done tracks your progress.') +
        sec('Paths', 'Ordered sequences like "Using the Hub". Your progress per path shows on the row.') +
        sec('Leaders assign', 'Leaders and admins can assign trainings from the same cards, one person or in bulk from the HR centre.') +
        '</div><div style="text-align:right;margin-top:8px"><button id="w61c" class="btn ghost mini">Got it</button></div>';
    }
    if (!wrap) return false;
    openModal(wrap);
    var c = document.getElementById('w61c');
    if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    return true;
  }

  /* registered after v60/v61/v65, so this handler's modal wins its keys */
  document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w43help]');
    if (!b) return;
    var k = b.getAttribute('data-w43help');
    if (['dept', 'goals', 'adminpanel', 'mypage', 'training'].indexOf(k) < 0) return;
    setTimeout(function () { try { w85Guide(k); } catch (e2) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 10);
  });

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa85 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa85.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w85AllPurpose(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v86 Phase 2 (S4 + S6 + S7): the Admin page can prove what happened
   =====================================================================
   1. Audit trail: a filterable viewer over change_log (who, what kind,
      free text, 50 at a time) at the bottom of the Admin page.
   2. Security status card: what Phase 2 enforces, when the nightly
      backup last ran, the state of the inventory share link, the
      Anthropic spend cap note, and the 2FA hardening checklist.
   Server truth: change_log and hub_settings reads go through RLS.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa86) return; window.__wa86 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.w86card{margin-top:16px;}' +
    '.w86bar{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin:8px 0 10px;}' +
    '.w86bar input,.w86bar select{padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:9px;font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.w86tbl{width:100%;font-size:12px;border-collapse:collapse;}' +
    '.w86tbl th{text-align:left;color:#797773;font-size:10.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;padding:6px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--wa-line);}' +
    '.w86tbl td{padding:7px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid #ECEBE3;vertical-align:top;}' +
    '.w86tbl td.w{white-space:nowrap;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w86sec{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(240px,1fr));gap:10px;margin-top:8px;}' +
    '.w86s{background:#F4F2EA;border-radius:12px;padding:11px 13px;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.5;}' +
    '.w86s b{display:block;font-size:12px;color:#103F39;margin-bottom:3px;}' +
    '.w86ok{color:#103F39;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w86todo{color:#b3402f;font-weight:700;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var W86 = { rows: [], offset: 0, wired: false };

  function w86Esc(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(String(s == null ? '' : s)) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }

  async function w86Load(reset) {
    if (reset) { W86.rows = []; W86.offset = 0; }
    var q = db.from('change_log').select('*').order('id', { ascending: false }).range(W86.offset, W86.offset + 49);
    var kind = (document.getElementById('w86kind') || {}).value || '';
    var who = (document.getElementById('w86who') || {}).value || '';
    if (kind) q = q.eq('entity_type', kind);
    if (who) q = q.eq('changed_by', who);
    var r = await q;
    var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
    W86.rows = W86.rows.concat(rows);
    W86.offset += rows.length;
    w86Paint(rows.length === 50);
  }

  function w86Paint(more) {
    var body = document.getElementById('w86rows');
    if (!body) return;
    var txt = ((document.getElementById('w86q') || {}).value || '').toLowerCase();
    var rows = W86.rows.filter(function (r) {
      if (!txt) return true;
      return (JSON.stringify(r) || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(txt) >= 0;
    });
    body.innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(function (r) {
      var when = (r.changed_at || r.created_at || '').toString().slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' ');
      return '<tr><td class="w">' + w86Esc(when) + '</td><td class="w">' + w86Esc(r.changed_by) + '</td>' +
        '<td class="w">' + w86Esc(r.entity_type) + (r.field ? ' / ' + w86Esc(r.field) : '') + '</td>' +
        '<td>' + w86Esc((r.new_value || '').toString().slice(0, 140)) + '</td></tr>';
    }).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="4" style="color:var(--wa-muted)">Nothing matches.</td></tr>';
    var mb = document.getElementById('w86more');
    if (mb) mb.style.display = more ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
  }

  async function w86Status() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w86status');
    if (!el) return;
    var backup = 'no run recorded yet';
    try {
      var b = await db.from('change_log').select('entity_id,new_value').eq('entity_type', 'db_backup').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(1);
      if (b && b.data && b.data.length) backup = b.data[0].entity_id + ' (' + b.data[0].new_value + ')';
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    var share = 'unknown';
    try {
      var s = await db.from('hub_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'inventory_share').maybeSingle();
      var v = (s && s.data && s.data.value) || {};
      share = v.token ? ('active' + (v.expires ? ', expires ' + String(v.expires).slice(0, 10) : ', no expiry')) : 'revoked';
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    el.innerHTML =
      '<div class="w86s"><b>Server-side permissions</b><span class="w86ok">Enforced.</span> Roles, blocked accounts, restricted meetings, agenda visibility and the hours allowlist are checked in the database, not just the browser. Signed-out and non-roster accounts read nothing.</div>' +
      '<div class="w86s"><b>Nightly backup</b>Every public table exports to private storage nightly (30 day retention), with a tested restore path. Last run: ' + w86Esc(backup) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w86s"><b>Inventory share link</b>' + w86Esc(share) + '. Links now expire (90 days by default) and can be revoked from the Volunteering page. Bad-link guesses are rate limited.</div>' +
      '<div class="w86s"><b>Security headers</b><span class="w86ok">Live.</span> Content Security Policy, HSTS, frame-deny and referrer policy are served by the host on every page load.</div>' +
      '<div class="w86s"><b>Anthropic spend cap</b><span class="w86todo">Manual step.</span> Set a monthly spend limit in the Anthropic console (console.anthropic.com &gt; Billing &gt; Limits) so the assistant and transcript AI can never run away. Document the amount here once set.</div>' +
      '<div class="w86s"><b>Two-factor sign-in</b><span class="w86todo">Manual step.</span> Turn on 2FA for the Supabase account, the Netlify account, and the Google admin account that owns the OAuth app. Each takes about two minutes in that service\'s account settings.</div>';
  }

  function w86Ensure() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('adminpanel');
    if (!panel || document.getElementById('w86card')) return;
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'card w86card';
    card.id = 'w86card';
    card.innerHTML =
      '<h2>Security and audit</h2>' +
      '<div class="w86sec" id="w86status"></div>' +
      '<h3 class="block" style="margin-top:16px">Audit trail</h3>' +
      '<p class="read" style="font-size:12px">Every change in the Hub, newest first: who did what, and when. Filter by kind or person, or search the text.</p>' +
      '<div class="w86bar">' +
      '<input id="w86q" placeholder="Search the log..." />' +
      '<select id="w86kind"><option value="">All kinds</option></select>' +
      '<select id="w86who"><option value="">Everyone</option></select>' +
      '<button class="btn ghost mini" id="w86refresh">Refresh</button></div>' +
      '<table class="w86tbl"><thead><tr><th>When</th><th>Who</th><th>What</th><th>Detail</th></tr></thead><tbody id="w86rows"></tbody></table>' +
      '<div style="margin-top:8px"><button class="btn ghost mini" id="w86more" style="display:none">Load 50 more</button></div>';
    panel.appendChild(card);

    (async function () {
      try {
        var kinds = await db.from('change_log').select('entity_type').order('entity_type').limit(1000);
        var seen = {};
        ((kinds && kinds.data) || []).forEach(function (r) { seen[r.entity_type] = 1; });
        document.getElementById('w86kind').innerHTML = '<option value="">All kinds</option>' +
          Object.keys(seen).sort().map(function (k) { return '<option>' + w86Esc(k) + '</option>'; }).join('');
        var whos = await db.from('change_log').select('changed_by').order('changed_by').limit(1000);
        var seen2 = {};
        ((whos && whos.data) || []).forEach(function (r) { if (r.changed_by) seen2[r.changed_by] = 1; });
        document.getElementById('w86who').innerHTML = '<option value="">Everyone</option>' +
          Object.keys(seen2).sort().map(function (k) { return '<option>' + w86Esc(k) + '</option>'; }).join('');
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      w86Load(true);
      w86Status();
    })();

    document.getElementById('w86refresh').addEventListener('click', function () { w86Load(true); w86Status(); });
    document.getElementById('w86more').addEventListener('click', function () { w86Load(false); });
    document.getElementById('w86q').addEventListener('input', function () { w86Paint(true); });
    document.getElementById('w86kind').addEventListener('change', function () { w86Load(true); });
    document.getElementById('w86who').addEventListener('change', function () { w86Load(true); });
  }

  /* the admin panel repaints via wa40Render; keep the card present */
  if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') {
    var __ar86 = wa40Render;
    wa40Render = async function () {
      await __ar86.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w86Ensure(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp86 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp86.apply(this, arguments);
      if (id === 'adminpanel') { try { w86Ensure(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa86 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa86.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w86Ensure(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v87 Phase 3 (B1): The Brain
   =====================================================================
   An interactive graph of how work flows through Welfare Avenue:
   people, meetings, tasks, projects, goals and decisions, drawn from
   the tables the Hub already has (no new pipeline). Edges:
     meeting -> task        task_comments where source='transcript'
     meeting -> person      transcripts.speakers + mentions
     person  -> task        dept_tasks.owner + task_assignees
     task    -> project     dept_tasks.project_id
     project -> goal        projects.goal_id (+ project parent_id)
     goal    -> goal        goals.parent_goal_id (+ goals.task_ids)
     meeting -> decision    decisions.raised_by ('AI: <title> - <date>')
   Views: Everything / This month / a week-by-week time-lapse, plus
   per-person and per-goal focus. Click any node to pull its chain
   forward and open the underlying page.
   cytoscape.js loads lazily from cdn.jsdelivr.net (CSP-allowlisted)
   the first time the page opens. Reads go through RLS, so each person
   sees exactly the graph their permissions allow.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa87) return; window.__wa87 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w87cy{height:66vh;min-height:420px;background:#fbfcfc;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:14px;}' +
    '.w87bar{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin:0 0 10px;}' +
    '.w87bar select{padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:9px;font-size:12.5px;background:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '.w87m{background:#ECEBE3;border:1px solid #cfe0d8;border-radius:999px;padding:7px 14px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w87m.on{background:#103F39;border-color:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '.w87leg{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:11.5px;color:#797773;margin:8px 0 0;}' +
    '.w87leg i{display:inline-block;width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:3px;margin-right:4px;vertical-align:-1px;}' +
    '#w87info{position:absolute;right:14px;top:14px;width:250px;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:12px;box-shadow:0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,.14);padding:12px 14px;font-size:12.5px;display:none;z-index:5;}' +
    '#w87info b{display:block;font-size:13px;color:#103F39;margin-bottom:4px;}' +
    '#w87info .k{color:#797773;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;}' +
    '#w87info a{color:#103F39;text-decoration:underline;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w87wrap{position:relative;}' +
    '#w87lapse{font-size:12.5px;color:#797773;font-weight:700;display:none;}' +
    '@media (max-width:760px){#w87cy{height:56vh;}#w87info{width:82vw;left:9vw;right:auto;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var W87 = { cy: null, lib: false, built: false, mode: 'month', mentions: [], txcom: [], timer: null };
  var COLORS = { person: '#103F39', meeting: '#6F64AB', task: '#5c7fc4', project: '#c87a2f', goal: '#a87f2c', decision: '#b3402f' };
  var SHAPES = { person: 'ellipse', meeting: 'diamond', task: 'round-rectangle', project: 'hexagon', goal: 'star', decision: 'triangle' };

  function w87Panel() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('brain');
    if (!sec || sec.dataset.w87) return;
    sec.dataset.w87 = '1';
    sec.innerHTML =
      '<div class="card"><h2 style="color:#6F64AB">The Brain</h2>' +
      '<p class="w85p">How work flows through Welfare Avenue: from a conversation to a task to a project to a goal. Click anything to pull its chain forward.</p>' +
      '<div class="w87bar">' +
      '<button class="w87m" data-w87mode="month">This month</button>' +
      '<button class="w87m" data-w87mode="all">Everything</button>' +
      '<button class="w87m" data-w87mode="lapse">&#9654; Time-lapse</button>' +
      '<span id="w87lapse"></span>' +
      '<select id="w87person"><option value="">Focus a person...</option></select>' +
      '<select id="w87goal"><option value="">Focus a goal...</option></select>' +
      '<button class="w87m" id="w87clear" style="display:none">Clear focus</button>' +
      '<button class="w87m" data-w88open style="margin-left:auto">How work happens</button>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="w87wrap"><div id="w87cy"></div><div id="w87info"></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w87leg">' +
      Object.keys(COLORS).map(function (k) { return '<span><i style="background:' + COLORS[k] + '"></i>' + k.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + k.slice(1) + (k === 'person' ? 's' : 's') + '</span>'; }).join('') +
      '</div></div>';
    sec.querySelectorAll('[data-w87mode]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () { w87Mode(b.getAttribute('data-w87mode')); });
    });
    document.getElementById('w87person').addEventListener('change', function () { w87Focus('p:' + this.value, 1); });
    document.getElementById('w87goal').addEventListener('change', function () { w87Focus('g:' + this.value, 2); });
    document.getElementById('w87clear').addEventListener('click', w87Unfocus);
  }

  function w87Lib(cb) {
    if (window.cytoscape) { cb(); return; }
    if (W87.lib) { setTimeout(function () { w87Lib(cb); }, 300); return; }
    W87.lib = true;
    var s = document.createElement('script');
    s.src = 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/cytoscape@3.30.2/dist/cytoscape.min.js';
    s.onload = cb;
    s.onerror = function () {
      var cyel = document.getElementById('w87cy');
      if (cyel) cyel.innerHTML = '<p class="read" style="padding:20px">The graph library could not load. Check the connection and reopen this page.</p>';
    };
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  }

  async function w87Extra() {
    try {
      var m = await db.from('mentions').select('person, transcript_id').limit(500);
      W87.mentions = (m && m.data) || [];
    } catch (e) { W87.mentions = []; }
    try {
      var c = await db.from('task_comments').select('task_id, source, source_ref').eq('source', 'transcript').limit(1000);
      W87.txcom = (c && c.data) || [];
    } catch (e) { W87.txcom = []; }
  }

  function w87Date(s) { return s ? new Date(String(s).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00') : null; }

  function w87Elements(windowStart, windowEnd) {
    var els = [];
    var seen = {};
    function node(id, type, label, extra) {
      if (seen[id]) return true;
      seen[id] = 1;
      els.push({ data: Object.assign({ id: id, type: type, label: (label || '').slice(0, 34) }, extra || {}) });
      return true;
    }
    function edge(a, b) {
      if (!seen[a] || !seen[b]) return;
      var id = 'e:' + a + '>' + b;
      if (seen[id]) return;
      seen[id] = 1;
      els.push({ data: { id: id, source: a, target: b } });
    }
    function inWin(d) {
      if (!windowStart) return true;
      var t = w87Date(d);
      return t && t >= windowStart && t <= windowEnd;
    }
    var members = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false; });
    var byName = {};
    members.forEach(function (m) {
      node('p:' + m.id, 'person', m.name, { open: '#person/' + m.id });
      byName[(m.name || '').toLowerCase()] = m.id;
      byName[(m.name || '').toLowerCase().split(' ')[0]] = byName[(m.name || '').toLowerCase().split(' ')[0]] || m.id;
    });
    (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).forEach(function (g) {
      node('g:' + g.id, 'goal', (g.quarter ? g.quarter + ': ' : '') + g.title, { open: '#goals' });
    });
    (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).forEach(function (g) {
      if (g.parent_goal_id) edge('g:' + g.id, 'g:' + g.parent_goal_id);
    });
    (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []).forEach(function (p) {
      node('pr:' + p.id, 'project', p.name, { open: '#project/' + p.id });
      if (p.goal_id) edge('pr:' + p.id, 'g:' + p.goal_id);
      if (p.parent_id) edge('pr:' + p.id, 'pr:' + p.parent_id);
    });
    var txs = (typeof txCache !== 'undefined' ? txCache : []).filter(function (t) { return inWin(t.meeting_date || t.created_at); });
    txs.forEach(function (t) {
      node('m:' + t.id, 'meeting', t.title || 'Meeting', { open: '#tx/' + t.id });
      (t.speakers || []).forEach(function (sp) {
        var mid = byName[(sp || '').toLowerCase()] || byName[(sp || '').toLowerCase().split(' ')[0]];
        if (mid) edge('m:' + t.id, 'p:' + mid);
      });
    });
    W87.mentions.forEach(function (mn) {
      var mid = byName[(mn.person || '').toLowerCase()] || byName[(mn.person || '').toLowerCase().split(' ')[0]];
      if (mid && seen['m:' + mn.transcript_id]) edge('m:' + mn.transcript_id, 'p:' + mid);
    });
    var tasks = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) {
      if (!windowStart) return true;
      return inWin(t.opened_at || t.updated_at) || (!t.done && inWin(t.due_date));
    });
    var taskSeen = {};
    tasks.forEach(function (t) {
      node('t:' + t.id, 'task', t.title, { open: '#task/' + t.id });
      taskSeen[t.id] = 1;
      if (t.project_id) edge('t:' + t.id, 'pr:' + t.project_id);
      var mid = byName[(t.owner || '').toLowerCase()] || byName[(t.owner || '').toLowerCase().split(' ')[0]];
      if (mid) edge('p:' + mid, 't:' + t.id);
    });
    (typeof DB_ASSIGN !== 'undefined' ? DB_ASSIGN : []).forEach(function (a) {
      if (taskSeen[a.task_id]) edge('p:' + a.member_id, 't:' + a.task_id);
    });
    W87.txcom.forEach(function (c) {
      var txid = Number(c.source_ref);
      if (taskSeen[c.task_id] && seen['m:' + txid]) edge('m:' + txid, 't:' + c.task_id);
    });
    (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).forEach(function (g) {
      (g.task_ids || []).forEach(function (tid) { if (taskSeen[tid]) edge('t:' + tid, 'g:' + g.id); });
    });
    var decs = (typeof DB_DECISIONS !== 'undefined' ? DB_DECISIONS : []).filter(function (d) { return !windowStart || inWin(d.opened_at || d.updated_at); });
    decs.forEach(function (d) {
      node('d:' + d.id, 'decision', d.title, { open: '#decision/' + d.id });
      var rb = String(d.raised_by || '');
      if (rb.indexOf('AI: ') === 0) {
        var mt = rb.slice(4).split(' - ')[0].toLowerCase();
        txs.forEach(function (t) {
          if ((t.title || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(mt) >= 0) edge('m:' + t.id, 'd:' + d.id);
        });
      }
      var mid = byName[(d.owner || '').toLowerCase()] || byName[(d.owner || '').toLowerCase().split(' ')[0]];
      if (mid) edge('p:' + mid, 'd:' + d.id);
    });
    /* hide fully disconnected nodes; a graph is about connections */
    var deg = {};
    els.forEach(function (e) {
      if (e.data.source) { deg[e.data.source] = (deg[e.data.source] || 0) + 1; deg[e.data.target] = (deg[e.data.target] || 0) + 1; }
    });
    var kept = els.filter(function (e) { return e.data.source || deg[e.data.id]; });
    W87.hidden = els.length - kept.length - els.filter(function (e) { return e.data.source; }).length
      + kept.filter(function (e) { return e.data.source; }).length - kept.filter(function (e) { return e.data.source; }).length;
    W87.hidden = els.filter(function (e) { return !e.data.source && !deg[e.data.id]; }).length;
    return kept;
  }

  function w87Draw(els) {
    var container = document.getElementById('w87cy');
    if (!container) return;
    if (W87.cy) { W87.cy.destroy(); W87.cy = null; }
    W87.cy = cytoscape({
      container: container,
      elements: els,
      wheelSensitivity: 0.2,
      style: [
        { selector: 'node', style: {
          'label': 'data(label)', 'font-size': 8, 'color': '#1F201E',
          'text-valign': 'bottom', 'text-margin-y': 3, 'text-wrap': 'ellipsis', 'text-max-width': 90,
          'width': 16, 'height': 16, 'border-width': 1, 'border-color': '#FFFAF2'
        } },
        { selector: 'node[type="person"]', style: { 'background-color': COLORS.person, 'shape': SHAPES.person, 'width': 22, 'height': 22, 'font-size': 9 } },
        { selector: 'node[type="meeting"]', style: { 'background-color': COLORS.meeting, 'shape': SHAPES.meeting } },
        { selector: 'node[type="task"]', style: { 'background-color': COLORS.task, 'shape': SHAPES.task, 'width': 13, 'height': 13 } },
        { selector: 'node[type="project"]', style: { 'background-color': COLORS.project, 'shape': SHAPES.project, 'width': 24, 'height': 24, 'font-size': 9 } },
        { selector: 'node[type="goal"]', style: { 'background-color': COLORS.goal, 'shape': SHAPES.goal, 'width': 26, 'height': 26, 'font-size': 9 } },
        { selector: 'node[type="decision"]', style: { 'background-color': COLORS.decision, 'shape': SHAPES.decision } },
        { selector: 'edge', style: { 'width': 1, 'line-color': '#cdd9d4', 'curve-style': 'haystack', 'haystack-radius': 0.4 } },
        { selector: '.w87dim', style: { 'opacity': 0.12 } },
        { selector: '.w87hot', style: { 'border-color': '#103F39', 'border-width': 2 } }
      ],
      layout: { name: 'cose', animate: false, nodeOverlap: 8, idealEdgeLength: 60, padding: 20 }
    });
    try {
      var leg = document.querySelector('.w87leg');
      if (leg) {
        var old = leg.querySelector('[data-w87hid]');
        if (old) old.remove();
        if (W87.hidden > 0) leg.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<span data-w87hid style="color:#9aa8a2">' + W87.hidden + ' quiet item' + (W87.hidden === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' hidden (no connections yet)</span>');
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    W87.cy.on('tap', 'node', function (ev) { w87Focus(ev.target.id(), 1, true); });
    W87.cy.on('tap', function (ev) { if (ev.target === W87.cy) w87Unfocus(); });
  }

  function w87Info(node) {
    var el = document.getElementById('w87info');
    if (!el) return;
    if (!node) { el.style.display = 'none'; return; }
    var d = node.data();
    var deg = node.degree();
    el.innerHTML = '<span class="k">' + esc(d.type) + '</span><b>' + esc(node.data('label')) + '</b>' +
      '<span style="color:#797773">' + deg + ' connection' + (deg === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span><br/>' +
      (d.open ? '<a data-w87go="' + esc(d.open) + '">Open</a>' : '');
    el.style.display = 'block';
    var a = el.querySelector('[data-w87go]');
    if (a) a.addEventListener('click', function () { location.hash = a.getAttribute('data-w87go'); });
  }

  function w87Focus(id, hops, fromTap) {
    if (!W87.cy) return;
    var node = W87.cy.getElementById(id);
    if (!node || !node.length) return;
    var hood = node.closedNeighborhood();
    for (var i = 1; i < (hops || 1); i++) hood = hood.closedNeighborhood();
    W87.cy.elements().addClass('w87dim');
    hood.removeClass('w87dim');
    W87.cy.elements().removeClass('w87hot');
    node.addClass('w87hot');
    document.getElementById('w87clear').style.display = 'inline-flex';
    w87Info(node);
    if (!fromTap) W87.cy.animate({ fit: { eles: hood, padding: 40 }, duration: 350 });
  }
  function w87Unfocus() {
    if (!W87.cy) return;
    W87.cy.elements().removeClass('w87dim').removeClass('w87hot');
    document.getElementById('w87clear').style.display = 'none';
    var p = document.getElementById('w87person'); if (p) p.value = '';
    var g = document.getElementById('w87goal'); if (g) g.value = '';
    w87Info(null);
  }

  function w87Fills() {
    var ps = document.getElementById('w87person');
    var gs = document.getElementById('w87goal');
    if (ps && ps.options.length <= 1) {
      (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false; })
        .sort(function (a, b) { return (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || ''); })
        .forEach(function (m) { ps.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<option value="' + m.id + '">' + esc(m.name) + '</option>'); });
    }
    if (gs && gs.options.length <= 1) {
      (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).forEach(function (g) {
        gs.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<option value="' + g.id + '">' + esc((g.quarter ? g.quarter + ': ' : '') + g.title).slice(0, 60) + '</option>');
      });
    }
  }

  function w87Mode(mode) {
    W87.mode = mode;
    clearInterval(W87.timer);
    document.getElementById('w87lapse').style.display = 'none';
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-w87mode]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.classList.toggle('on', b.getAttribute('data-w87mode') === mode);
    });
    var now = new Date();
    if (mode === 'all') { w87Draw(w87Elements(null, null)); return; }
    var start = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1);
    if (mode === 'month') { w87Draw(w87Elements(start, now)); return; }
    /* time-lapse: replay this month week by week */
    var lab = document.getElementById('w87lapse');
    lab.style.display = 'inline';
    var step = 0;
    function tick() {
      step++;
      var end = new Date(start.getTime() + step * 7 * 86400000);
      if (end > now) end = now;
      lab.textContent = 'Up to ' + end.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      w87Draw(w87Elements(start, end));
      if (end >= now) { clearInterval(W87.timer); lab.textContent += ' (done)'; }
    }
    tick();
    W87.timer = setInterval(tick, 2600);
  }

  function w87Show() {
    w87Panel();
    w87Lib(function () {
      if (W87.built) return;
      W87.built = true;
      w87Extra().then(function () {
        w87Fills();
        w87Mode('month');
      });
    });
  }

  function w87Register() {
    if (window.WA_NAV && typeof WA_NAV.register === 'function') {
      WA_NAV.register({ id: 'brain', label: 'The Brain', group: 'more', dot: '#6F64AB', gate: 'member', onShow: function () { w87Show(); } });
    }
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa87 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa87.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w87Register(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  w87Register();
})();

   An animated walkthrough of the Hub's core loop: a meeting happens,
   the Hub reads it, tasks and updates and decisions appear, tasks join
   projects, projects roll up to goals. Five stages, each with a drawn
   arrow and a plain caption. Shown once to new joiners right after the
   first-visit tour finishes (localStorage 'wa88seen'), and always
   available from The Brain ("How work happens") and window.w88Show().
   Pure inline SVG + CSS animation: no assets, no network.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa88) return; window.__wa88 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w88back{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(10,25,20,.6);z-index:410;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:16px;}' +

    '.w88dots{display:flex;gap:5px;margin:10px 0;}' +
    '.w88dots i{width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:#DEE0D8;}' +
    '.w88dots i.on{background:#6F64AB;}' +
    '.w88row{display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:flex-end;align-items:center;}' +
    '.w88row .skip{margin-right:auto;background:none;border:none;color:#797773;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;}' +
