    '.w88btn.ghost{background:#ECEBE3;color:#103F39;}' +
    '#w88svg .st{opacity:.18;transition:opacity .5s;}' +
    '#w88svg .st.on{opacity:1;}' +
    '#w88svg .ar{stroke-dasharray:60;stroke-dashoffset:60;transition:stroke-dashoffset .9s ease;}' +
    '#w88svg .ar.on{stroke-dashoffset:0;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var STAGES = [
    ['A meeting happens', 'Someone records a meeting (Otter, Loom or notes) and drops the transcript into the Hub, on the Transcript archive page under Meetings. That is the only manual step.'],
    ['The Hub reads it', 'The Hub reads the whole conversation and pulls out what matters: who agreed to do what, progress on existing work, open questions.'],
    ['Work appears by itself', 'Tasks land on the right department with an owner. Updates land on the team page. Decisions that need the EDs land on the Executive dashboard. People who were mentioned see it on Home.'],
    ['Tasks join projects', 'Each task files itself into the right project, so nothing floats loose. Comments from later meetings attach to the same task.'],
    ['Projects roll up to goals', 'Projects connect to quarterly goals, and goals roll up to the organisational direction. Goal alignment and The Brain show the whole chain, meeting to mission.']
  ];

  var SVG =
    '<svg id="w88svg" viewBox="0 0 520 190" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
    '<g class="st" data-st="0"><rect x="18" y="70" width="76" height="50" rx="9" fill="#6F64AB"/><text x="56" y="92" text-anchor="middle" fill="#FFFAF2" font-size="11" font-weight="700">Meeting</text><text x="56" y="107" text-anchor="middle" fill="#e6dcff" font-size="8">transcript</text></g>' +
    '<path class="ar" data-ar="1" d="M96 95 L136 95" stroke="#797773" stroke-width="2" fill="none" marker-end="url(#w88a)"/>' +
    '<g class="st" data-st="1"><circle cx="163" cy="95" r="24" fill="#103F39"/><text x="163" y="91" text-anchor="middle" fill="#ffd76d" font-size="13">&#10024;</text><text x="163" y="105" text-anchor="middle" fill="#FFFAF2" font-size="8" font-weight="700">the Hub</text></g>' +
    '<path class="ar" data-ar="2" d="M189 82 L232 48" stroke="#797773" stroke-width="2" fill="none" marker-end="url(#w88a)"/>' +
    '<path class="ar" data-ar="2" d="M191 95 L232 95" stroke="#797773" stroke-width="2" fill="none" marker-end="url(#w88a)"/>' +
    '<path class="ar" data-ar="2" d="M189 108 L232 142" stroke="#797773" stroke-width="2" fill="none" marker-end="url(#w88a)"/>' +
    '<g class="st" data-st="2"><rect x="236" y="28" width="72" height="34" rx="8" fill="#5c7fc4"/><text x="272" y="49" text-anchor="middle" fill="#FFFAF2" font-size="10" font-weight="700">Tasks</text>' +
    '<rect x="236" y="78" width="72" height="34" rx="8" fill="#103F39"/><text x="272" y="99" text-anchor="middle" fill="#FFFAF2" font-size="10" font-weight="700">Updates</text>' +
    '<rect x="236" y="128" width="72" height="34" rx="8" fill="#b3402f"/><text x="272" y="149" text-anchor="middle" fill="#FFFAF2" font-size="10" font-weight="700">Decisions</text></g>' +
    '<path class="ar" data-ar="3" d="M310 45 L352 74" stroke="#797773" stroke-width="2" fill="none" marker-end="url(#w88a)"/>' +
    '<g class="st" data-st="3"><rect x="356" y="70" width="76" height="46" rx="9" fill="#c87a2f"/><text x="394" y="90" text-anchor="middle" fill="#FFFAF2" font-size="10" font-weight="700">Projects</text><text x="394" y="104" text-anchor="middle" fill="#ffe9d2" font-size="8">the work, grouped</text></g>' +
    '<path class="ar" data-ar="4" d="M434 93 L468 93" stroke="#797773" stroke-width="2" fill="none" marker-end="url(#w88a)"/>' +
    '<g class="st" data-st="4"><path d="M492 62 l7 16 17 2 -12 12 3 17 -15 -8 -15 8 3 -17 -12 -12 17 -2 z" fill="#a87f2c"/><text x="492" y="130" text-anchor="middle" fill="#1F201E" font-size="10" font-weight="700">Goals</text></g>' +
    '<defs><marker id="w88a" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" refX="5" refY="3.5" orient="auto"><path d="M0 0 L7 3.5 L0 7 z" fill="#797773"/></marker></defs>' +
    '</svg>';

  function w88Show(auto) {
    if (document.getElementById('w88back')) return;
    var i = 0;
    var back = document.createElement('div');
    back.id = 'w88back';
    back.innerHTML = '<div id="w88card"><h3>How work happens in the Hub</h3>' + SVG +
      '<div class="w88dots">' + STAGES.map(function () { return '<i></i>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<p id="w88cap"></p>' +
      '<div class="w88row"><button class="skip">Close</button>' +
      '<button class="w88btn ghost" data-b style="display:none">Back</button>' +
      '<button class="w88btn" data-n>Next</button></div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(back);
    function done() {
      try { localStorage.setItem('wa88seen', '1'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      clearTimeout(back.__t);
      back.remove();
    }
    function paint() {
      back.querySelectorAll('.w88dots i').forEach(function (d, k) { d.classList.toggle('on', k <= i); });
      back.querySelectorAll('#w88svg .st').forEach(function (g) { g.classList.toggle('on', Number(g.getAttribute('data-st')) <= i); });
      back.querySelectorAll('#w88svg .ar').forEach(function (a) { a.classList.toggle('on', Number(a.getAttribute('data-ar')) <= i); });
      document.getElementById('w88cap').innerHTML = '<b>' + esc(STAGES[i][0]) + '.</b> ' + esc(STAGES[i][1]);
      back.querySelector('[data-b]').style.display = i > 0 ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
      back.querySelector('[data-n]').textContent = i === STAGES.length - 1 ? 'Done' : 'Next';
      if (auto && i < STAGES.length - 1) {
        clearTimeout(back.__t);
        back.__t = setTimeout(function () { if (document.getElementById('w88back')) { i++; paint(); } }, 3400);
      }
    }
    back.querySelector('.skip').addEventListener('click', done);
    back.querySelector('[data-b]').addEventListener('click', function () { auto = false; clearTimeout(back.__t); i--; paint(); });
    back.querySelector('[data-n]').addEventListener('click', function () {
      auto = false; clearTimeout(back.__t);
      if (i === STAGES.length - 1) { done(); return; }
      i++; paint();
    });
    paint();
  }
  window.w88Show = w88Show;

  /* open from The Brain's button (and anywhere else that adds data-w88open) */
  document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w88open]')) w88Show(false);
  });

  /* new joiners: when the first-visit tour closes, show this once */
  function w88AfterTour() {
    try {
      if (localStorage.getItem('wa88seen')) return;
    } catch (e) { return; }
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
      muts.forEach(function (m) {
        Array.prototype.forEach.call(m.removedNodes || [], function (n) {
          if (n && n.id === 'w84back') {
            try {
              if (!localStorage.getItem('wa88seen') && localStorage.getItem('wa84tour') === '1') {
                setTimeout(function () { w88Show(true); }, 500);
              }
            } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
            mo.disconnect();
          }
        });
      });
    });
    mo.observe(document.body, { childList: true });
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa88 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa88.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w88AfterTour(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v89 Phase 4 (R1 UI + R2): notification settings + live Welcome kit
   =====================================================================
   1. Home gains a small "Slack notifications" fold: five per-person
      switches (request filed, task handed to you, meeting suggestion
      accepted, mentioned in a meeting, invited to the Hub) saved to
      wa_notify_prefs (RLS: own row only). A "Send me a test" link
      calls the notify fn so anyone can confirm their delivery.
   2. The HR Welcome kit's channel message gains "Send to their Slack":
      live sending of the (editable) welcome message through the notify
      fn, with the delivery outcome shown inline. Copy buttons stay.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa89) return; window.__wa89 = true;

  var EVENTS = [
    ['request_filed', 'A request is filed for my team'],
    ['task_reassigned', 'A task is handed to me'],
    ['suggestion_accepted', 'A meeting suggestion becomes a request for my team'],
    ['mentioned', 'A meeting mentions me'],
    ['invited', 'Someone I invited joins the Hub']
  ];

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.w89row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:12.5px;padding:4px 0;}' +
    '.w89row input{accent-color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w89note{font-size:11px;color:#797773;margin-top:6px;}' +
    '.w89test{font-size:11.5px;color:#103F39;text-decoration:underline;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '[data-w89kit]{background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;border:none;border-radius:999px;padding:7px 14px;font-weight:700;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;margin-left:6px;}' +
    '.w89out{font-size:11.5px;margin-left:8px;font-weight:700;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- 1. settings fold on Home ---------- */
  var W89 = { disabled: [] };

  async function w89Load() {
    try {
      var em = (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '';
      var r = await db.from('wa_notify_prefs').select('disabled').eq('email', em).maybeSingle();
      W89.disabled = (r && r.data && r.data.disabled) || [];
      if (!Array.isArray(W89.disabled)) W89.disabled = [];
    } catch (e) { W89.disabled = []; }
  }

  async function w89Save() {
    try {
      var em = (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '';
      await db.from('wa_notify_prefs').upsert({ email: em, disabled: W89.disabled, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() });
      toast('Notification settings saved');
    } catch (e) { toast('Could not save settings'); }
  }

  function w89Render() {
    var grid = document.querySelector('#mypage .mp-grid');
    if (!grid || document.getElementById('w89box')) return;
    var right = grid.children[1] || grid;
    var d = document.createElement('details');
    d.className = 'mp-sec';
    d.id = 'w89box';
    d.innerHTML = '<summary style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:800;font-size:13px;color:#103F39;list-style:none">Slack notifications</summary>' +
      '<div style="margin-top:6px">' +
      EVENTS.map(function (ev) {
        var on = W89.disabled.indexOf(ev[0]) < 0;
        return '<label class="w89row"><input type="checkbox" data-w89ev="' + ev[0] + '"' + (on ? ' checked' : '') + '/> ' + ev[1] + '</label>';
      }).join('') +
      '<div class="w89note">Delivered as a Slack DM (or a ping in the dashboard channel until DMs are enabled). <span class="w89test" data-w89test>Send me a test</span></div>' +
      '</div>';
    right.appendChild(d);
    d.querySelectorAll('[data-w89ev]').forEach(function (c) {
      c.addEventListener('change', function () {
        var k = c.getAttribute('data-w89ev');
        W89.disabled = W89.disabled.filter(function (x) { return x !== k; });
        if (!c.checked) W89.disabled.push(k);
        w89Save();
      });
    });
    d.querySelector('[data-w89test]').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      try {
        await db.functions.invoke('notify', { body: { event: 'test' } });
        toast('Test sent, check Slack');
      } catch (e) { toast('Test failed to send'); }
    });
  }

  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __mp89 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      await __mp89.apply(this, arguments);
      try { await w89Load(); w89Render(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* ---------- 2. live Welcome kit sending ---------- */
  function w89KitDecorate() {
    var ta = document.getElementById('w46sm');
    if (!ta || document.querySelector('[data-w89kit]')) return;
    var h3 = document.querySelector('#modalBox h3');
    var name = h3 ? (h3.textContent || '').replace('Welcome kit for', '').trim() : '';
    var row = ta.parentNode.querySelector('div');
    if (!row) return;
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.setAttribute('data-w89kit', name);
    b.textContent = 'Send to their Slack';
    row.appendChild(b);
    var out = document.createElement('span');
    out.className = 'w89out';
    row.appendChild(out);
    b.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var member = null;
      try {
        member = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).find(function (m) {
          return (m.name || '').toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase() && m.email;
        }) || (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).find(function (m) {
          return (m.name || '').toLowerCase().split(' ')[0] === name.toLowerCase().split(' ')[0] && m.email;
        });
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      if (!member || !member.email) { out.textContent = 'No roster email for ' + name; out.style.color = '#b3402f'; return; }
      b.disabled = true; b.textContent = 'Sending...';
      try {
        var r = await db.functions.invoke('notify', { body: { event: 'welcome_kit', email: member.email, text: ta.value } });
        var oc = r && r.data && r.data.outcome;
        if (oc === 'sent' || oc === 'sent_channel') {
          out.textContent = oc === 'sent' ? 'Sent as a DM' : 'Sent (channel ping until DMs are enabled)';
          out.style.color = '#103F39';
        } else {
          out.textContent = 'Not delivered: ' + (oc || 'error') + ' (copy button still works)';
          out.style.color = '#b3402f';
        }
      } catch (e) {
        out.textContent = 'Send failed (copy button still works)';
        out.style.color = '#b3402f';
      }
      b.disabled = false; b.textContent = 'Send to their Slack';
    });
  }
  var mo = new MutationObserver(function () { w89KitDecorate(); });
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa89 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa89.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        var box = document.getElementById('modalBox');
        if (box) mo.observe(box, { childList: true, subtree: true });
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v90 finances upgrade (07/08/26): PDF statement import + charts
   =====================================================================
   1. The Import bank statement modal accepts the bank's PDF directly
      (finance-import v3 reads PDFs natively). CSV/text paths unchanged.
   2. A Charts section on the Finances page: income vs spending by
      month, net position over the year, spending by category, income
      by source, department share of spending, and budget vs actual
      bullet bars. All hand-drawn SVG/HTML, no new dependencies, and
      every figure excludes flagged duplicates, same as the tables.
   3. Add a budget line: staff can create new budget categories that
      flow into the tables, the dropdowns, the charts, and the AI
      importer (finance-import v4 reads them from finance_budgets).
   4. Teach the AI: an editable guidance note (app_config key
      finance_ai_guidance) appended to the importer's prompt, so the
      team controls how donations and expenses get categorised.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa90) return; window.__wa90 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.w90grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(300px,1fr));gap:12px;margin:8px 0 4px;}' +
    '.w90box{background:rgba(127,127,127,.07);border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;min-width:0;}' +
    '.w90box.wide{grid-column:1/-1;}' +
    '.w90t{font-size:11px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);margin-bottom:6px;}' +
    '.w90box svg{width:100%;height:auto;display:block;}' +
    '.w90ax{font-size:9.5px;fill:var(--wa-muted,#797773);}' +
    '.w90row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin:4px 0;font-size:11.5px;}' +
    '.w90row .l{flex:0 0 126px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;}' +
    '.w90row .b{flex:1;height:9px;border-radius:5px;background:rgba(127,127,127,.16);overflow:visible;position:relative;min-width:40px;}' +
    '.w90row .b i{display:block;height:100%;border-radius:5px;}' +
    '.w90row .b s{position:absolute;top:-3px;bottom:-3px;width:2px;background:currentColor;opacity:.5;text-decoration:none;}' +
    '.w90row .v{flex:0 0 auto;font-weight:700;font-size:11px;min-width:54px;text-align:right;}' +
    '.w90leg{display:flex;gap:14px;font-size:10.5px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);margin-top:5px;flex-wrap:wrap;}' +
    '.w90leg i{display:inline-block;width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:2px;margin-right:4px;vertical-align:-1px;}' +
    '@media (max-width:640px){.w90row .l{flex-basis:96px;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- helpers ---------- */
  function w90K(n) {
    n = Math.round(Number(n) || 0);
    var a = Math.abs(n), s = n < 0 ? '-$' : '$';
    if (a >= 100000) return s + Math.round(a / 1000) + 'k';
    if (a >= 10000) return s + (a / 1000).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'k';
    if (a >= 1000) return s + (a / 1000).toFixed(1) + 'k';
    return s + a;
  }
  function w90Nice(v) {
    if (!(v > 0)) return 100;
    var p = Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log(v) / Math.LN10));
    var steps = [1, 1.5, 2, 2.5, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 10];
    for (var i = 0; i < steps.length; i++) { if (steps[i] * p >= v) return steps[i] * p; }
    return 10 * p;
  }
  function w90Months() {
    var y = waFinFYNow() - 1;
    var names = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
    var arr = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      var m = (9 + i) % 12 + 1;
      var yy = m >= 10 ? y : y + 1;
      arr.push({ key: yy + '-' + ('0' + m).slice(-2), label: names[m - 1], inc: 0, spd: 0 });
    }
    return arr;
  }
  function w90Data() {
    var M = w90Months();
    var idx = {};
    M.forEach(function (m, i) { idx[m.key] = i; });
    var byCatEx = {}, byCatIn = {}, byDept = {};
    var any = false;
    WA_FIN_TX.forEach(function (t) {
      if (t.duplicate_of !== null && t.duplicate_of !== undefined) return;
      var a = Number(t.amount);
      var mi = idx[String(t.txn_date).slice(0, 7)];
      any = true;
      if (a >= 0) {
        if (mi !== undefined) M[mi].inc += a;
        byCatIn[t.category || 'Uncategorised'] = (byCatIn[t.category || 'Uncategorised'] || 0) + a;
      } else {
        if (mi !== undefined) M[mi].spd += -a;
        byCatEx[t.category || 'Uncategorised'] = (byCatEx[t.category || 'Uncategorised'] || 0) + -a;
        byDept[t.department || 'org'] = (byDept[t.department || 'org'] || 0) + -a;
      }
    });
    var now = new Date();
    var nowKey = now.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (now.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2);
    var cut = idx[nowKey] !== undefined ? idx[nowKey] : 11;
    for (var i = 11; i > cut; i--) { if (M[i].inc || M[i].spd) { cut = i; break; } }
    /* v92 forecast: when the toggle is on and we are looking at the FY that
       contains today, project the remaining months from the average of the
       last three months that have data. Clearly marked as an estimate. */
    var proj = null;
    try {
      var natFY = ((now.getMonth() + 1) >= 10 ? now.getFullYear() + 1 : now.getFullYear());
      if (window.WA_FIN_FC && waFinFYNow() === natFY) {
        var lastIdx = -1;
        for (var li = 11; li >= 0; li--) { if (M[li].inc || M[li].spd) { lastIdx = li; break; } }
        if (lastIdx >= 0 && lastIdx < 11) {
          var from = Math.max(0, lastIdx - 2), cnt = lastIdx - from + 1, ai = 0, asp = 0;
          for (var pi = from; pi <= lastIdx; pi++) { ai += M[pi].inc; asp += M[pi].spd; }
          ai = ai / cnt; asp = asp / cnt;
          for (var fi = lastIdx + 1; fi <= 11; fi++) { M[fi].pinc = ai; M[fi].pspd = asp; }
          proj = { from: lastIdx + 1, avgInc: ai, avgSpd: asp, months: cnt };
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { proj = null; }
    return { M: M, cut: cut, byCatEx: byCatEx, byCatIn: byCatIn, byDept: byDept, any: any, proj: proj };
  }
  function w90Sorted(obj) {
    return Object.keys(obj).map(function (k) { return { label: k, value: obj[k] }; })
      .filter(function (x) { return x.value > 0.005; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return b.value - a.value; });
  }

  /* ---------- chart builders ---------- */
  function w90ChartMonthly(M, proj) {
    var W = 680, H = 210, padL = 46, padR = 8, padT = 10, padB = 22;
    var max = 0;
    M.forEach(function (m) { max = Math.max(max, m.inc, m.spd, m.pinc || 0, m.pspd || 0); });
    max = w90Nice(max);
    var ih = H - padT - padB, iw = (W - padL - padR) / M.length;
    var bw = Math.max(5, Math.min(13, (iw - 10) / 2));
    var s = '';
    for (var i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
      var y = H - padB - ih * i / 4;
      s += '<line x1="' + padL + '" y1="' + y + '" x2="' + (W - padR) + '" y2="' + y + '" stroke="rgba(127,127,127,.22)" stroke-width="1"/>';
      s += '<text x="' + (padL - 6) + '" y="' + (y + 3) + '" text-anchor="end" class="w90ax">' + w90K(max * i / 4) + '</text>';
    }
    M.forEach(function (m, i) {
      var cx = padL + iw * i + iw / 2;
      var hi = ih * m.inc / max, hs = ih * m.spd / max;
      if (m.inc > 0) s += '<rect x="' + (cx - bw - 1).toFixed(1) + '" y="' + (H - padB - hi).toFixed(1) + '" width="' + bw.toFixed(1) + '" height="' + hi.toFixed(1) + '" rx="1.5" fill="#103F39"><title>' + m.label + ' income: ' + waFinMoney(m.inc, true) + '</title></rect>';
      if (m.spd > 0) s += '<rect x="' + (cx + 1).toFixed(1) + '" y="' + (H - padB - hs).toFixed(1) + '" width="' + bw.toFixed(1) + '" height="' + hs.toFixed(1) + '" rx="1.5" fill="#c96b5f"><title>' + m.label + ' spending: ' + waFinMoney(m.spd, true) + '</title></rect>';
      if (m.pinc) {
        var phi = ih * m.pinc / max, phs = ih * (m.pspd || 0) / max;
        s += '<rect x="' + (cx - bw - 1).toFixed(1) + '" y="' + (H - padB - phi).toFixed(1) + '" width="' + bw.toFixed(1) + '" height="' + phi.toFixed(1) + '" rx="1.5" fill="#103F39" fill-opacity="0.35" stroke="#103F39" stroke-dasharray="3,2" stroke-width="1"><title>' + m.label + ' PROJECTED income: about ' + waFinMoney(m.pinc) + '</title></rect>';
        s += '<rect x="' + (cx + 1).toFixed(1) + '" y="' + (H - padB - phs).toFixed(1) + '" width="' + bw.toFixed(1) + '" height="' + phs.toFixed(1) + '" rx="1.5" fill="#c96b5f" fill-opacity="0.35" stroke="#c96b5f" stroke-dasharray="3,2" stroke-width="1"><title>' + m.label + ' PROJECTED spending: about ' + waFinMoney(m.pspd || 0) + '</title></rect>';
      }
      s += '<text x="' + cx.toFixed(1) + '" y="' + (H - padB + 13) + '" text-anchor="middle" class="w90ax">' + m.label + '</text>';
    });
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + W + ' ' + H + '" role="img" aria-label="Income and spending by month">' + s + '</svg>' +
      '<div class="w90leg"><span><i style="background:#103F39"></i>Income</span><span><i style="background:#c96b5f"></i>Spending</span>' +
      (proj ? '<span><i style="background:rgba(63,156,127,.35);border:1px dashed #103F39"></i>Projected (average of the last ' + proj.months + ' months with data)</span>' : '<span>Hover a bar for the exact amount</span>') +
      '</div>';
  }

  function w90ChartNet(M, cut, proj) {
    var W = 680, H = 190, padL = 46, padR = 52, padT = 12, padB = 22;
    var cum = [], c = 0, i;
    for (i = 0; i <= cut; i++) { c += M[i].inc - M[i].spd; cum.push(c); }
    var end = proj ? 11 : cut;
    if (proj) { cum = cum.slice(0, proj.from); c = cum[cum.length - 1]; }
    var pcum = [], pc = c;
    if (proj) {
      for (i = proj.from; i <= 11; i++) { pc += (M[i].pinc || 0) - (M[i].pspd || 0); pcum.push(pc); }
    }
    var min = 0, max = 0;
    cum.concat(pcum).forEach(function (v) { min = Math.min(min, v); max = Math.max(max, v); });
    if (max - min < 1) { max = min + 1; }
    var span = max - min;
    max += span * 0.08; min -= span * 0.08;
    var ih = H - padT - padB, iwid = W - padL - padR;
    function X(j) { return padL + (end ? iwid * j / end : iwid / 2); }
    function Y(v) { return padT + (max - v) / (max - min) * ih; }
    var s = '';
    var y0 = Y(0);
    s += '<line x1="' + padL + '" y1="' + y0.toFixed(1) + '" x2="' + (W - padR) + '" y2="' + y0.toFixed(1) + '" stroke="rgba(127,127,127,.4)" stroke-width="1" stroke-dasharray="3,3"/>';
    s += '<text x="' + (padL - 6) + '" y="' + (y0 + 3).toFixed(1) + '" text-anchor="end" class="w90ax">$0</text>';
    var pts = cum.map(function (v, j) { return X(j).toFixed(1) + ',' + Y(v).toFixed(1); }).join(' ');
    var last = cum[cum.length - 1];
    var col = last >= 0 ? '#103F39' : '#c96b5f';
    s += '<polyline points="' + pts + '" fill="none" stroke="' + col + '" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round"/>';
    cum.forEach(function (v, j) {
      s += '<circle cx="' + X(j).toFixed(1) + '" cy="' + Y(v).toFixed(1) + '" r="3" fill="' + col + '"><title>End of ' + M[j].label + ': ' + waFinMoney(v, true) + ' net</title></circle>';
    });
    for (i = 0; i <= end; i++) {
      s += '<text x="' + X(i).toFixed(1) + '" y="' + (H - padB + 13) + '" text-anchor="middle" class="w90ax">' + M[i].label + '</text>';
    }
    var endVal = last;
    if (proj && pcum.length) {
      var ppts = [X(cum.length - 1).toFixed(1) + ',' + Y(last).toFixed(1)].concat(pcum.map(function (v, j) {
        return X(proj.from + j).toFixed(1) + ',' + Y(v).toFixed(1);
      })).join(' ');
      var pcol = pcum[pcum.length - 1] >= 0 ? '#103F39' : '#c96b5f';
      s += '<polyline points="' + ppts + '" fill="none" stroke="' + pcol + '" stroke-width="2" stroke-dasharray="5,4" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round" opacity="0.75"/>';
      pcum.forEach(function (v, j) {
        s += '<circle cx="' + X(proj.from + j).toFixed(1) + '" cy="' + Y(v).toFixed(1) + '" r="3" fill="none" stroke="' + pcol + '" stroke-width="1.5"><title>End of ' + M[proj.from + j].label + ' PROJECTED: about ' + waFinMoney(v) + ' net</title></circle>';
      });
      endVal = pcum[pcum.length - 1];
      col = pcol;
    }
    s += '<text x="' + (X(end) + 7).toFixed(1) + '" y="' + (Y(endVal) + 3).toFixed(1) + '" class="w90ax" style="font-weight:700;fill:' + col + '">' + (proj ? '~' : '') + w90K(endVal) + '</text>';
    var note = '';
    if (proj && pcum.length) {
      note = '<div class="w90leg"><span>If the last ' + proj.months + ' months with data continue: about ' + w90K(proj.avgInc) + ' in and ' + w90K(proj.avgSpd) + ' out per month, ending the year near <b>' + w90K(endVal) + '</b> net. An estimate, not a promise.</span></div>';
    }
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + W + ' ' + H + '" role="img" aria-label="Net position month by month">' + s + '</svg>' + note;
  }

  function w90HBars(items, color, total) {
    var max = items.length ? items[0].value : 1;
    return items.map(function (x) {
      var pct = Math.max(1, Math.round(x.value / max * 100));
      var share = total > 0 ? Math.round(x.value / total * 100) : 0;
      return '<div class="w90row" title="' + esc(x.label) + ': ' + waFinMoney(x.value, true) + (total > 0 ? ' (' + share + '% of total)' : '') + '">' +
        '<span class="l">' + esc(x.label) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="b"><i style="width:' + pct + '%;background:' + color + '"></i></span>' +
        '<span class="v">' + w90K(x.value) + (total > 0 ? ' <span style="font-weight:400;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773)">' + share + '%</span>' : '') + '</span></div>';
    }).join('');
  }

  function w90Bullets() {
    var rows = [];
    var max = 1;
    WA_FIN_CATS_EX.forEach(function (c) {
      var bud = waFinBudget('FY', c);
      var act = waFinActualCat('FY', c, false);
      if (bud <= 0 && act <= 0) return;
      max = Math.max(max, bud, act);
      rows.push({ c: c, bud: bud, act: act });
    });
    if (!rows.length) return '<div class="fin-note">Set FY budgets in the table above to see this chart.</div>';
    rows.sort(function (a, b) { return Math.max(b.bud, b.act) - Math.max(a.bud, a.act); });
    return rows.map(function (r) {
      var over = r.bud > 0 && r.act > r.bud;
      var wAct = Math.round(r.act / max * 100);
      var wBud = Math.round(r.bud / max * 100);
      var tip = esc(r.c) + ': spent ' + waFinMoney(r.act, true) + ' of ' + waFinMoney(r.bud) + ' FY budget' + (over ? ' (over budget)' : '');
      return '<div class="w90row" title="' + tip + '">' +
        '<span class="l">' + esc(r.c) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="b"><i style="width:' + wAct + '%;background:' + (over ? '#c96b5f' : '#103F39') + '"></i>' +
        (r.bud > 0 ? '<s style="left:' + wBud + '%"></s>' : '') + '</span>' +
        '<span class="v' + (over ? '" style="color:#c96b5f' : '') + '">' + w90K(r.act) + ' <span style="font-weight:400;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773)">/ ' + w90K(r.bud) + '</span></span></div>';
    }).join('') + '<div class="w90leg"><span><i style="background:#103F39"></i>Spent</span><span><i style="background:#c96b5f"></i>Over budget</span><span>| marks the FY budget</span></div>';
  }

  function w90Charts() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('finances');
    if (!sec) return;
    var kpis = sec.querySelector('.fin-kpis');
    if (!kpis || document.getElementById('w90charts')) return;
    var d = w90Data();
    var html;
    if (!d.any) {
      html = '<div id="w90charts"><h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39">Charts</h3>' +
        '<div class="fin-note">Charts appear once transactions are imported.</div></div>';
    } else {
      var ex = w90Sorted(d.byCatEx), inn = w90Sorted(d.byCatIn), dep = w90Sorted(d.byDept);
      var totEx = ex.reduce(function (n, x) { return n + x.value; }, 0);
      var totIn = inn.reduce(function (n, x) { return n + x.value; }, 0);
      var deptName = {};
      WA_FIN_DEPTS.forEach(function (x) { deptName[x[0]] = x[1]; });
      dep.forEach(function (x) { x.label = deptName[x.label] || x.label; });
      html = '<div id="w90charts"><h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39">Charts</h3>' +
        '<div class="fin-note">The ' + waFinFYLabel() + ' picture at a glance. Duplicates excluded, same as the tables. Hover anything for exact amounts.</div>' +
        '<div class="w90grid">' +
        '<div class="w90box wide"><div class="w90t">Income vs spending by month' + (d.proj ? ' (with forecast)' : '') + '</div>' + w90ChartMonthly(d.M, d.proj) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="w90box wide"><div class="w90t">Net position over the year' + (d.proj ? ' (with forecast)' : '') + '</div>' + w90ChartNet(d.M, d.cut, d.proj) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="w90box"><div class="w90t">Where the money goes</div>' + w90HBars(ex, '#c96b5f', totEx) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="w90box"><div class="w90t">Where the money comes from</div>' + (inn.length ? w90HBars(inn, '#103F39', totIn) : '<div class="fin-note">No income recorded yet this year.</div>') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="w90box"><div class="w90t">Department share of spending</div>' + w90HBars(dep, '#103F39', totEx) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="w90box"><div class="w90t">Budget vs actual (FY, expenses)</div>' + w90Bullets() + '</div>' +
        '</div></div>';
    }
    kpis.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', html);
  }

  if (typeof waFinRender === 'function') {
    var __fr90 = waFinRender;
    waFinRender = function () {
      __fr90.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w90Charts(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      try { w90Buttons(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* ---------- custom budget lines ---------- */
  function w90MergeCats() {
    (WA_FIN_BUD || []).forEach(function (b) {
      if (b.scope !== 'category' || !b.key) return;
      if (WA_FIN_CATS_IN.indexOf(b.key) >= 0 || WA_FIN_CATS_EX.indexOf(b.key) >= 0) return;
      (b.kind === 'income' ? WA_FIN_CATS_IN : WA_FIN_CATS_EX).push(b.key);
    });
  }
  if (typeof waFinLoad === 'function') {
    var __fl90 = waFinLoad;
    waFinLoad = async function () {
      await __fl90.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w90MergeCats(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  function w90BudLineModal() {
    openModal('<h3>Add a budget line</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub">Creates a new category everywhere at once: the budget tables, the allocation dropdowns, the charts, and the Hub\u2019s importer. Set its quarterly budgets in the table afterwards.</p>' +
      '<label>Name</label><input type="text" id="w90bname" maxlength="60" placeholder="7150 Insurance" />' +
      '<label>Type</label><select id="w90bkind"><option value="expense">Expense</option><option value="income">Income</option></select>' +
      '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w90bgo">Add line</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w90bcancel">Cancel</button></div>');
    var cancel = document.getElementById('w90bcancel');
    if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    var go = document.getElementById('w90bgo');
    if (go) go.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var name = (document.getElementById('w90bname').value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      var kind = document.getElementById('w90bkind').value === 'income' ? 'income' : 'expense';
      if (name.length < 2) { toast('Give the line a name'); return; }
      if (WA_FIN_CATS_IN.indexOf(name) >= 0 || WA_FIN_CATS_EX.indexOf(name) >= 0) { toast('That line already exists'); return; }
      go.disabled = true;
      var r = await db.from('finance_budgets').upsert({
        fiscal_year: waFinFYLabel(), period: 'FY', scope: 'category', key: name,
        kind: kind, amount: 0, updated_by: who(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      }, { onConflict: 'fiscal_year,period,scope,key' });
      if (r.error) { go.disabled = false; toast('Could not add the line'); return; }
      closeModal();
      toast('Budget line added');
      await waFinLoad(true);
      waFinRender();
    });
  }

  function w90Buttons() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('finances');
    if (!sec) return;
    var act = sec.querySelector('.fin-actions');
    if (!act || act.querySelector('[data-w90bud]')) return;
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'btn ghost mini';
    b.setAttribute('data-w90bud', '1');
    b.textContent = 'Add a budget line';
    b.addEventListener('click', w90BudLineModal);
    act.appendChild(b);
  }

  /* ---------- PDF-capable import modal ---------- */
  if (typeof waFinImportModal === 'function') {
    waFinImportModal = function () {
      openModal('<h3>Import a bank statement</h3>' +
        '<p class="m-sub">Choose the statement PDF straight from the bank, a CSV export, or paste the text. The Hub extracts every transaction, allocates each one to a department and budget category, and flags duplicates.</p>' +
        '<label>Statement file name</label><input type="text" id="finFile" placeholder="July 31, 2026.pdf" />' +
        '<label>PDF, CSV or text file</label><input type="file" id="finUpload" accept=".pdf,.csv,.txt,application/pdf" />' +
        '<div id="finPdfNote" class="fin-note" style="display:none"></div>' +
        '<div id="finTextWrap"><label>Or paste the statement text</label><textarea id="finText" rows="8" placeholder="Paste the full statement text here"></textarea></div>' +
        '<details id="w90guide" style="margin:10px 0 2px"><summary style="cursor:pointer;font-size:12px;font-weight:700">Teach the Hub how to categorise</summary>' +
        '<p class="m-sub">The Hub already knows the chart of accounts, every budget line you have added, and the usual Welfare Avenue merchants. Add your own rules here, for example: Tim Hortons on event Sundays is volunteer meals, use 6020. E-transfers from board members are 4100 Donation. Saved rules apply to every future import.</p>' +
        '<textarea id="w90gtext" rows="5" maxlength="4000" placeholder="Loading current guidance..."></textarea>' +
        '<div style="margin-top:6px"><button class="btn ghost mini" id="w90gsave">Save guidance</button> <span id="w90gout" class="fin-note" style="display:inline"></span></div>' +
        '</details>' +
        '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="finGo">Import</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="finCancel">Cancel</button></div>');
      (async function () {
        var ta = document.getElementById('w90gtext');
        if (!ta) return;
        try {
          var r = await db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'finance_ai_guidance').maybeSingle();
          ta.value = (r && r.data && r.data.value) || '';
          ta.placeholder = 'Your categorisation rules, one per line';
        } catch (e) { ta.placeholder = 'Could not load saved guidance'; }
        var sv = document.getElementById('w90gsave');
        if (sv) sv.addEventListener('click', async function () {
          var out = document.getElementById('w90gout');
          sv.disabled = true;
          var r2 = await db.from('app_config').upsert({ key: 'finance_ai_guidance', value: ta.value.slice(0, 4000), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() });
          sv.disabled = false;
          if (out) out.textContent = r2.error ? 'Save failed' : 'Saved. The next import uses it.';
        });
      })();
      var pdfB64 = '';
      var note = document.getElementById('finPdfNote');
      var wrap = document.getElementById('finTextWrap');
      function clearPdf() {
        pdfB64 = '';
        if (note) { note.style.display = 'none'; note.innerHTML = ''; }
        if (wrap) wrap.style.display = '';
        var u = document.getElementById('finUpload');
        if (u) u.value = '';
      }
      var up = document.getElementById('finUpload');
      if (up) up.addEventListener('change', function () {
        var f = this.files && this.files[0];
        if (!f) return;
        var isPdf = /\.pdf$/i.test(f.name || '') || (f.type || '').indexOf('pdf') >= 0;
        var fn = document.getElementById('finFile');
        if (fn && !fn.value) fn.value = f.name;
        if (isPdf) {
          if (f.size > 11 * 1024 * 1024) { toast('That PDF is over 11MB. Export a smaller one or paste the text.'); this.value = ''; return; }
          var fr = new FileReader();
          fr.onload = function () {
            var s = String(fr.result || '');
            var comma = s.indexOf(',');
            pdfB64 = comma >= 0 ? s.slice(comma + 1) : '';
            if (!pdfB64) { toast('Could not read that PDF'); clearPdf(); return; }
            if (note) {
              note.innerHTML = 'PDF attached: <b>' + esc(f.name) + '</b> (' + Math.max(1, Math.round(f.size / 1024)) + ' KB). <a class="fin-lnk" id="finPdfClear">Remove</a>';
              note.style.display = '';
              var cl = document.getElementById('finPdfClear');
              if (cl) cl.addEventListener('click', clearPdf);
            }
            if (wrap) wrap.style.display = 'none';
          };
          fr.readAsDataURL(f);
        } else {
          clearPdf();
          if (fn && !fn.value) fn.value = f.name;
          var fr2 = new FileReader();
          fr2.onload = function () {
            var t = document.getElementById('finText');
            if (t) t.value = String(fr2.result || '').slice(0, 80000);
          };
          fr2.readAsText(f);
        }
      });
      var cancel = document.getElementById('finCancel');
      if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
      var go = document.getElementById('finGo');
      if (go) go.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var fname = (document.getElementById('finFile').value || '').trim() || 'statement';
        var body;
        if (pdfB64) {
          body = { pdf_base64: pdfB64, filename: fname };
        } else {
          var text = (document.getElementById('finText').value || '').trim();
          if (text.length < 60) { toast('Choose a PDF or paste the statement text first'); return; }
          body = { statement_text: text, filename: fname };
        }
        go.disabled = true;
        go.textContent = 'Importing (about a minute)...';
        try {
          var r = await db.functions.invoke('finance-import', { body: body });
          if (r.error) throw r.error;
          var d = r.data || {};
          closeModal();
          if (d.all_duplicates) {
            toast('Every transaction in that statement was already in the ledger (re-import). Nothing double-counted.');
          } else {
            toast('Imported ' + (d.imported || 0) + ' transactions' + (d.duplicates ? ', ' + d.duplicates + ' duplicates flagged' : ''));
            if (d.period_warning) setTimeout(function () { toast('Heads up: ' + d.period_warning); }, 2600);
          }
          await waFinLoad(true);
          waFinRender();
        } catch (err) {
          go.disabled = false;
          go.textContent = 'Import';
          toast('Import failed');
        }
      });
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v91 finances insight (07/08/26): explanations + budget math + learning
   =====================================================================
   1. Hover explanations: every budget line, KPI tile, chart row and
      ledger category carries a plain-language note on what it means.
   2. Budget math: editing any quarterly budget recomputes that line's
      FY total automatically (FY = Q1+Q2+Q3+Q4) before the repaint.
   3. Review and learn: a "Needs your review" fold lists the AI's
      low-confidence allocations; fixing or confirming one clears it.
      Whenever staff reallocates ANY transaction, a small prompt offers
      to save the correction as a rule in the AI guidance
      (app_config finance_ai_guidance), so every future statement
      import learns from it. finance-import v4 already reads that key.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa91) return; window.__wa91 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.w91help{cursor:help;border-bottom:1px dotted rgba(127,127,127,.55);}' +
    '#w91review .fin-note{margin:4px 0 8px;}' +
    '#w91review summary{cursor:pointer;font-weight:800;font-size:14px;color:#b07b2e;list-style:none;}' +
    '#w91review{background:rgba(176,123,46,.07);border-radius:10px;padding:10px 14px;margin:12px 0;}' +
    '.w91row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:6px 0;border-top:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.15);font-size:12px;}' +
    '.w91row:first-of-type{border-top:none;}' +
    '.w91row .d{color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);white-space:nowrap;}' +
    '.w91row .n{font-weight:600;flex:1 1 180px;min-width:140px;}' +
    '.w91row .amt{font-weight:700;white-space:nowrap;}' +
    '.w91row .amt.pos{color:#103F39;}.w91row .amt.neg{color:#c96b5f;}' +
    '.w91row select{font-size:11px;padding:2px 4px;border-radius:5px;border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.3);background:transparent;color:inherit;max-width:150px;}' +
    '.w91row select option{color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w91ok{background:transparent;border:1px solid #103F39;color:#103F39;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 10px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '#w91snack{position:fixed;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);bottom:18px;z-index:9999;background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;border-radius:12px;padding:12px 16px;font-size:12.5px;max-width:min(560px,92vw);box-shadow:0 6px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.3);display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;}' +
    '#w91snack b{color:#9fd6c3;}' +
    '#w91snack button{border:none;border-radius:999px;padding:6px 12px;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '#w91snack .yes{background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '#w91snack .no{background:transparent;color:#cfe0da;border:1px solid #4a6a62;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- 1. plain-language explanations ---------- */
  var EXPLAIN = {
    '4100 Donation': 'Money given to the charity: e-transfer donations, cheque deposits, online giving. Income.',
    '4110 FlagPlus Football': 'Income from the FlagPlus football league partnership.',
    '4200 Grants': 'Grant income from governments, cities, municipalities and foundations.',
    'Services': 'Money earned for services provided, including Stripe payouts. Income.',
    '5020 Purchases - COS': 'Food and groceries bought for the Sunday serving events (Instacart, MAXI, Costco and similar). The core cost of serving.',
    'Supplies and materials - COS': 'Non-food supplies and equipment used at serving events: containers, utensils, tables, gear.',
    '6010 Transportation fees': 'Getting people and food where they need to be: gas, parking, rides, van rentals.',
    '6020 Meal and entertainment expenses': 'Restaurant meals, cafes and food that is NOT for serving events: volunteer meals, meetings.',
    '6030 Subscription fees': 'Recurring software and memberships: DigitalOcean, QuickBooks, Loom, Keela, LinkedIn and similar.',
    '7035 Office expenses': 'Office supplies and hardware for admin work, not for events.',
    '7090 Telecommunication': 'Phone and internet, including OpenPhone.',
    '7120 Publicity and Promotion': 'Getting the word out: ads (Meta, Google), printing, event listings.',
    '7145 Lease payments': 'Storage units and space: U-Haul storage, Depotium.',
    'Other': 'Bank fees, government fees, and anything that does not fit the other lines.'
  };
  var KPI_EXPLAIN = {
    'Income FY to date': 'All money that came in since the fiscal year started (Oct 1). Duplicates excluded.',
    'Spend FY to date': 'All money that went out since Oct 1. Duplicates excluded.',
    'Net FY to date': 'Income minus spending so far this fiscal year. Positive means the bank balance grew.'
  };
  function w91Explain(cat) {
    if (EXPLAIN[cat]) return EXPLAIN[cat];
    if (typeof WA_FIN_CATS_IN !== 'undefined' && WA_FIN_CATS_IN.indexOf(cat) >= 0) return 'An income budget line your team added.';
    return 'A budget line your team added.';
  }

  function w91Decorate() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('finances');
    if (!sec) return;
    sec.querySelectorAll('table.fin-tab tbody tr td:first-child').forEach(function (td) {
      var cat = (td.textContent || '').trim();
      if (!cat || cat === 'Total') return;
      td.title = w91Explain(cat);
      td.classList.add('w91help');
    });
    sec.querySelectorAll('.fin-kpi').forEach(function (k) {
      var label = (k.querySelector('.k') || {}).textContent || '';
      var key = Object.keys(KPI_EXPLAIN).filter(function (x) { return label.indexOf(x) === 0; })[0];
      if (key) { k.title = KPI_EXPLAIN[key]; k.classList.add('w91help'); }
      else if (label.indexOf('spend vs budget') > 0) { k.title = 'What this quarter has spent so far, against the total expense budget set for the quarter.'; k.classList.add('w91help'); }
    });
    sec.querySelectorAll('#w90charts .w90row').forEach(function (r) {
      var lab = ((r.querySelector('.l') || {}).textContent || '').trim();
      if (!lab) return;
      var ex = EXPLAIN[lab];
      if (ex && r.title.indexOf(ex) < 0) r.title = (r.title ? r.title + ' | ' : '') + ex;
    });
    sec.querySelectorAll('select[data-ftc]').forEach(function (s) {
      s.title = w91Explain(s.value);
      s.querySelectorAll('option').forEach(function (o) { o.title = w91Explain(o.value); });
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 2. quarter edits recompute the FY total ---------- */
  function w91BudgetMath(sec) {
    if (sec.dataset.w91bud) return;
    sec.dataset.w91bud = '1';
    sec.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
      var el = e.target;
      if (!el || !el.classList || !el.classList.contains('fin-bud')) return;
      var k = el.getAttribute('data-fbud') || '';
      var p = k.split('|')[0];
      var cat = k.split('|').slice(1).join('|');
      if (p === 'FY' || !cat) return;
      var tot = 0;
      ['Q1', 'Q2', 'Q3', 'Q4'].forEach(function (q) {
        tot += (q === p) ? (Number(el.value) || 0) : waFinBudget(q, cat);
      });
      WA_FIN_EDITS['FY|' + cat] = String(Math.round(tot * 100) / 100);
    }, true);
  }

  /* ---------- 3a. needs-review fold ---------- */
  function w91Review() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('finances');
    if (!sec || !isStaff() || document.getElementById('w91review')) return;
    var anchor = document.getElementById('w90charts') || sec.querySelector('.fin-kpis');
    if (!anchor) return;
    var low = (WA_FIN_TX || []).filter(function (t) {
      return (t.duplicate_of === null || t.duplicate_of === undefined) && /low confidence/i.test(t.allocated_by || '');
    });
    if (!low.length) return;
    var shown = low.slice(0, 30);
    var deptOpts = function (cur) {
      return WA_FIN_DEPTS.map(function (x) {
        return '<option value="' + x[0] + '"' + (x[0] === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + x[1] + '</option>';
      }).join('');
    };
    var catOpts = function (cur) {
      return WA_FIN_CATS_IN.concat(WA_FIN_CATS_EX).map(function (c) {
        return '<option value="' + esc(c) + '"' + (c === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(c) + '</option>';
      }).join('');
    };
    var rows = shown.map(function (t) {
      var amt = Number(t.amount);
      var dd = new Date(t.txn_date + 'T12:00:00');
      var dl = dd.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
      return '<div class="w91row">' +
        '<span class="d">' + esc(dl) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="n" title="' + esc(t.description) + '">' + esc(String(t.description).slice(0, 44)) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="amt ' + (amt >= 0 ? 'pos' : 'neg') + '">' + waFinMoney(amt, true) + '</span>' +
        '<select data-w91d="' + t.id + '" title="Department">' + deptOpts(t.department) + '</select>' +
        '<select data-w91c="' + t.id + '" title="' + esc(w91Explain(t.category)) + '">' + catOpts(t.category) + '</select>' +
        '<button class="w91ok" data-w91ok="' + t.id + '">Looks right</button>' +
        '</div>';
    }).join('');
    var more = low.length > shown.length ? '<div class="fin-note">Showing 30 of ' + low.length + '.</div>' : '';
    var d = document.createElement('details');
    d.id = 'w91review';
    d.open = true;
    d.innerHTML = '<summary>Needs your review (' + low.length + ')</summary>' +
      '<div class="fin-note">The Hub was not sure about these allocations. Fix the department or category (hover any category for what it means), or hit Looks right to confirm. Fixing one teaches the Hub for future imports.</div>' +
      rows + more;
    anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', d);
    d.querySelectorAll('[data-w91d]').forEach(function (el) {
      el.addEventListener('change', function () { waFinAlloc(Number(el.getAttribute('data-w91d')), { department: el.value }); });
    });
    d.querySelectorAll('[data-w91c]').forEach(function (el) {
      el.addEventListener('change', function () { waFinAlloc(Number(el.getAttribute('data-w91c')), { category: el.value }); });
    });
    d.querySelectorAll('[data-w91ok]').forEach(function (el) {
      el.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        await waFinAlloc(Number(el.getAttribute('data-w91ok')), {});
      });
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 3b. reallocation teaches the AI ---------- */
  function w91Snack(html) {
    var old = document.getElementById('w91snack');
    if (old) old.remove();
    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.id = 'w91snack';
    s.innerHTML = html;
    document.body.appendChild(s);
    return s;
  }
  function w91LearnPrompt(row) {
    var deptLabel = (WA_FIN_DEPTS.filter(function (x) { return x[0] === row.department; })[0] || ['', row.department])[1];
    var rule = 'Transactions described like "' + String(row.description).slice(0, 80) + '": category "' + row.category + '", department ' + row.department + '.';
    var s = w91Snack('<span>Teach the Hub? Always file <b>' + esc(String(row.description).slice(0, 40)) + '</b> as <b>' + esc(row.category) + '</b> / <b>' + esc(deptLabel) + '</b> on future imports.</span>' +
      '<button class="yes">Save rule</button><button class="no">No thanks</button>');
    var t = setTimeout(function () { s.remove(); }, 15000);
    s.querySelector('.no').addEventListener('click', function () { clearTimeout(t); s.remove(); });
    s.querySelector('.yes').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      clearTimeout(t);
      s.querySelector('.yes').disabled = true;
      try {
        var r = await db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'finance_ai_guidance').maybeSingle();
        var cur = (r && r.data && r.data.value) || '';
        var probe = '"' + String(row.description).slice(0, 40);
        if (cur.indexOf(probe) >= 0) {
          cur = cur.split('\n').filter(function (l) { return l.indexOf(probe) < 0; }).join('\n');
        }
        var next = (cur ? cur.replace(/\s+$/, '') + '\n' : '') + rule;
        if (next.length > 4000) { s.remove(); toast('Guidance is full: trim it in the import window first'); return; }
        var r2 = await db.from('app_config').upsert({ key: 'finance_ai_guidance', value: next, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() });
        s.remove();
        toast(r2.error ? 'Could not save the rule' : 'Saved: future imports will follow it');
      } catch (e) { s.remove(); toast('Could not save the rule'); }
    });
  }
  if (typeof waFinAlloc === 'function') {
    var __al91 = waFinAlloc;
    waFinAlloc = async function (id, patch) {
      await __al91.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        if (!patch || (!('category' in patch) && !('department' in patch))) return;
        var row = (WA_FIN_TX || []).filter(function (t) { return t.id === id; })[0];
        if (!row) return;
        var applied = Object.keys(patch).every(function (k) { return row[k] === patch[k]; });
        if (applied) w91LearnPrompt(row);
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* ---------- wire into the render cycle ---------- */
  if (typeof waFinRender === 'function') {
    var __fr91 = waFinRender;
    waFinRender = function () {
      __fr91.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        var sec = document.getElementById('finances');
        if (!sec) return;
        w91Review();
        w91Decorate();
        w91BudgetMath(sec);
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v92 finances horizons (07/08/26): time frames, forecast, drilldowns,
   nonprofit health
   =====================================================================
   1. Time frame pills at the top of Finances: one pill per fiscal year
      that has data (ready for the bulk upload of past-year PDFs) plus a
      Forecast toggle on the current year. Every table, chart, KPI and
      the ledger follow the selected year (waFinFYNow is wrapped; all of
      wave22/90/91 already derive from it).
   2. Forecast rendering itself lives in wave90's charts (edited 07/08/26):
      dashed/translucent projected bars and a dashed net line from the
      average of the last three months with data, labelled as an estimate.
   3. Merchant drilldowns: click any category row in "Where the money
      goes" / "comes from" to expand the merchants inside it (Instacart
      vs the rest of Purchases, and so on).
   4. Nonprofit health panel: program / fundraising / admin split,
      fundraising cost per dollar raised, net margin, average monthly
      spend with a 3-6 month reserve target, grants reliance; each with
      a plain-language hover note and benchmark, plus a short guidelines
      fold for a young Canadian charity. Benchmarks: Charity Navigator /
      BBB program-ratio guidance, sector reserve guidance, CRA basics.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa92) return; window.__wa92 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.w92pills{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin:2px 0 10px;}' +
    '.w92pills .lbl{font-size:11px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);margin-right:2px;}' +
    '.w92pill{border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.4);background:transparent;color:inherit;border-radius:999px;padding:4px 12px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w92pill.on{background:#103F39;border-color:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '.w92pill.fc{border-style:dashed;}' +
    '.w92pill.fc.on{background:#b07b2e;border-color:#b07b2e;}' +
    '.w92note{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);margin:-4px 0 8px;}' +
    '.w92can .l{cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w92can .l:before{content:"\\25B8 ";font-size:9px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);}' +
    '.w92can.open .l:before{content:"\\25BE ";}' +
    '.w92sub{margin:2px 0 6px 18px;padding-left:8px;border-left:2px solid rgba(127,127,127,.25);}' +
    '.w92sub .w90row{margin:2px 0;font-size:11px;}' +
    '.w92sub .w90row .l{flex-basis:108px;cursor:default;}' +
    '.w92sub .w90row .l:before{content:"";}' +
    '#w92health{margin:12px 0;}' +
    '#w92health h3{margin-bottom:2px;}' +
    '.w92hgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(250px,1fr));gap:10px;margin:8px 0;}' +
    '.w92hcard{background:rgba(127,127,127,.07);border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;cursor:help;}' +
    '.w92hcard .t{font-size:10.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);}' +
    '.w92hcard .v{font-size:17px;font-weight:800;margin:2px 0;}' +
    '.w92hcard .b{font-size:11px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);}' +
    '.w92dot{display:inline-block;width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;margin-right:6px;vertical-align:0;}' +
    '.w92dot.ok{background:#103F39;}.w92dot.warn{background:#b07b2e;}.w92dot.bad{background:#c96b5f;}.w92dot.na{background:rgba(127,127,127,.4);}' +
    '#w92guide{font-size:12px;margin-top:4px;}' +
    '#w92guide summary{cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '#w92guide p{margin:6px 0;font-size:12px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var W92 = { years: null, loading: false };

  /* ---------- time frame plumbing ---------- */
  var __fy92 = waFinFYNow;
  waFinFYNow = function () { return window.WA_FIN_FYSEL || __fy92(); };
  var __qn92 = waFinQNow;
  waFinQNow = function () {
    var nat = __fy92();
    if (window.WA_FIN_FYSEL && window.WA_FIN_FYSEL !== nat) return 'Q4';
    return __qn92();
  };

  async function w92LoadYears() {
    if (W92.years || W92.loading) return;
    W92.loading = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('finance_transactions').select('txn_date').order('txn_date', { ascending: true }).limit(1);
      var nat = __fy92();
      var first = nat;
      if (r && r.data && r.data[0] && r.data[0].txn_date) {
        var d = String(r.data[0].txn_date);
        var y = Number(d.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(d.slice(5, 7));
        first = m >= 10 ? y + 1 : y;
      }
      var ys = [];
      for (var y2 = Math.max(first, nat - 8); y2 <= nat; y2++) ys.push(y2);
      W92.years = ys;
    } catch (e) { W92.years = [__fy92()]; }
    W92.loading = false;
    try { w92Pills(); } catch (e2) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  function w92Pills() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('finances');
    if (!sec) return;
    var head = sec.querySelector('.fin-head');
    if (!head || document.getElementById('w92pills')) return;
    var nat = __fy92();
    var sel = window.WA_FIN_FYSEL || nat;
    var years = W92.years || [nat];
    var html = '<span class="lbl">Time frame</span>' + years.map(function (y) {
      return '<button class="w92pill' + (y === sel ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w92y="' + y + '">FY' + String(y).slice(-2) + '</button>';
    }).join('');
    if (sel === nat) {
      html += '<button class="w92pill fc' + (window.WA_FIN_FC ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w92fc="1" title="Project the rest of the year from the average of the last three months with data. Clearly marked as an estimate.">Forecast</button>';
    }
    var wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className = 'w92pills';
    wrap.id = 'w92pills';
    wrap.innerHTML = html;
    head.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', wrap);
    if (sel !== nat) {
      var note = document.createElement('div');
      note.className = 'w92note';
      note.textContent = 'Viewing FY' + String(sel).slice(-2) + ' (Oct 1 ' + (sel - 1) + ' to Sep 30 ' + sel + '). Quarter columns and charts follow this year.';
      wrap.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', note);
    }
    wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-w92y]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var y = Number(b.getAttribute('data-w92y'));
        if (y === (window.WA_FIN_FYSEL || nat)) return;
        window.WA_FIN_FYSEL = (y === nat ? null : y);
        if (y !== nat) window.WA_FIN_FC = false;
        b.textContent = '...';
        await waFinLoad(true);
        waFinRender();
      });
    });
    var fc = wrap.querySelector('[data-w92fc]');
    if (fc) fc.addEventListener('click', function () {
      window.WA_FIN_FC = !window.WA_FIN_FC;
      waFinRender();
    });
    w92LoadYears();
  }

  /* ---------- merchant drilldowns ---------- */
  function w92Merchant(desc) {
    var s = String(desc || '').toLowerCase()
      .replace(/\([^)]*\)/g, ' ')
      .replace(/[#*]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\d{2,}\b/g, ' ')
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    var words = s.split(' ').slice(0, 2).join(' ');
    return words || 'other';
  }
  function w92Pretty(key) {
    return key.split(' ').map(function (w) { return w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1); }).join(' ');
  }
  function w92SubRows(cat, income) {
    var agg = {}, total = 0;
    (WA_FIN_TX || []).forEach(function (t) {
      if (t.duplicate_of !== null && t.duplicate_of !== undefined) return;
      if ((t.category || '') !== cat) return;
      var a = Number(t.amount);
      if (income ? a < 0 : a >= 0) return;
      var v = Math.abs(a);
      var k = w92Merchant(t.description);
      agg[k] = (agg[k] || 0) + v;
      total += v;
    });
    var items = Object.keys(agg).map(function (k) { return { k: k, v: agg[k] }; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return b.v - a.v; });
    var top = items.slice(0, 8);
    var rest = items.slice(8).reduce(function (n, x) { return n + x.v; }, 0);
    if (rest > 0.005) top.push({ k: 'everything else', v: rest });
    if (!top.length) return '<div class="w90row"><span class="l">No transactions in this view.</span></div>';
    var max = top[0].v;
    return top.map(function (x) {
      var pct = total > 0 ? Math.round(x.v / total * 100) : 0;
      return '<div class="w90row" title="' + esc(w92Pretty(x.k)) + ': ' + waFinMoney(x.v, true) + ' (' + pct + '% of ' + esc(cat) + ')">' +
        '<span class="l">' + esc(w92Pretty(x.k)) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="b"><i style="width:' + Math.max(1, Math.round(x.v / max * 100)) + '%;background:' + (income ? '#103F39' : '#c96b5f') + ';opacity:.7"></i></span>' +
        '<span class="v">' + waFinMoney(x.v) + ' <span style="font-weight:400;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773)">' + pct + '%</span></span></div>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function w92Drill() {
    var charts = document.getElementById('w90charts');
    if (!charts) return;
    charts.querySelectorAll('.w90box').forEach(function (box) {
      var t = (box.querySelector('.w90t') || {}).textContent || '';
      var goes = t.indexOf('Where the money goes') === 0;
      var comes = t.indexOf('Where the money comes from') === 0;
      if (!goes && !comes) return;
      box.querySelectorAll(':scope > .w90row').forEach(function (r) {
        if (r.classList.contains('w92can')) return;
        r.classList.add('w92can');
        var lab = r.querySelector('.l');
        if (!lab) return;
        lab.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
          e.stopPropagation();
          var open = r.classList.toggle('open');
          var next = r.nextElementSibling;
          if (next && next.classList.contains('w92sub')) { next.remove(); return; }
          if (!open) return;
          var cat = (lab.textContent || '').trim();
          var sub = document.createElement('div');
          sub.className = 'w92sub';
          sub.innerHTML = w92SubRows(cat, comes);
          r.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', sub);
        });
      });
      if (!box.querySelector('[data-w92hint]')) {
        var hint = document.createElement('div');
        hint.className = 'w90leg';
        hint.setAttribute('data-w92hint', '1');
        hint.innerHTML = '<span>Click a line to see the merchants inside it.</span>';
        box.appendChild(hint);
      }
    });
  }

  /* ---------- nonprofit health panel ---------- */
  function w92Health() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('finances');
    if (!sec || document.getElementById('w92health')) return;
    var anchor = document.getElementById('w91review') || document.getElementById('w90charts');
    if (!anchor) return;
    var prog = 0, fundr = 0, admin = 0, totSpd = 0, totInc = 0, contrib = 0, grants = 0;
    var monthsWith = {};
    (WA_FIN_TX || []).forEach(function (t) {
      if (t.duplicate_of !== null && t.duplicate_of !== undefined) return;
      var a = Number(t.amount);
      if (a >= 0) {
        totInc += a;
        if (t.category === '4100 Donation' || t.category === '4200 Grants') contrib += a;
        if (t.category === '4200 Grants') grants += a;
      } else {
        var v = -a;
        totSpd += v;
        monthsWith[String(t.txn_date).slice(0, 7)] = 1;
        var d = t.department || 'org';
        if (d === 'opsmtl' || d === 'opsto') prog += v;
        else if (d === 'fundraising' || d === 'marketing') fundr += v;
        else admin += v;
      }
    });
    var nMonths = Object.keys(monthsWith).length || 1;
    var avgMonth = totSpd / nMonths;
    function pct(x) { return Math.round(x * 100) + '%'; }
    function dot(st) { return '<span class="w92dot ' + st + '"></span>'; }
    var cards = [];
    if (totSpd > 0) {
      var pr = prog / totSpd;
      cards.push({
        t: 'Program spending', v: dot(pr >= 0.65 ? 'ok' : (pr >= 0.5 ? 'warn' : 'bad')) + pct(pr) + ' of spending',
        b: 'Target: 65% or more on the mission itself. Ops Montreal + Ops Toronto count as program here.',
        tip: 'Program expense ratio = program spending / total spending. Charity watchdogs (Charity Navigator, BBB) look for 65-70%+. Mapping used: Ops Montreal and Ops Toronto = program; Fundraising and Marketing = fundraising; HR, Board, Org-wide = admin. Reallocate transactions to sharpen this.'
      });
      var ad = admin / totSpd;
      cards.push({
        t: 'Admin overhead', v: dot(ad <= 0.35 ? 'ok' : (ad <= 0.45 ? 'warn' : 'bad')) + pct(ad) + ' of spending',
        b: 'Target: under 35%. Some overhead is healthy; zero overhead is a myth.',
        tip: 'Admin ratio = (HR + Board + Org-wide spending) / total spending. Under 35% is the common watchdog benchmark. Do not starve it to zero: software, insurance and good books are what keep a charity alive.'
      });
    }
    if (contrib > 0) {
      var fe = fundr / contrib;
      cards.push({
        t: 'Cost to raise $1', v: dot(fe <= 0.2 ? 'ok' : (fe <= 0.35 ? 'warn' : 'bad')) + '$' + fe.toFixed(2),
        b: 'Target: under $0.20 spent per dollar of donations and grants.',
        tip: 'Fundraising efficiency = (Fundraising + Marketing spending) / (donations + grants received). Under $0.20 per dollar raised is the standard benchmark.'
      });
    }
    if (totInc > 0) {
      var nm = (totInc - totSpd) / totInc;
      cards.push({
        t: 'Net margin', v: dot(nm >= 0 ? 'ok' : (nm >= -0.1 ? 'warn' : 'bad')) + pct(nm),
        b: nm >= 0 ? 'Running a surplus: money available to reinvest or reserve.' : 'Spending more than comes in this period.',
        tip: 'Net margin = (income - spending) / income. Occasional deficits are normal; sustained ones eat reserves.'
      });
      var gr = grants / totInc;
      cards.push({
        t: 'Grants reliance', v: dot(gr <= 0.5 ? 'ok' : 'warn') + pct(gr) + ' of income',
        b: gr > 0 ? 'Watch diversification if one funder dominates.' : 'No grant income recorded yet. Recategorise grant deposits to 4200 Grants to track this.',
        tip: 'Grants reliance = grant income / total income. Diversified income (individual donations + grants + earned revenue) is safer than depending on one source.'
      });
    }
    cards.push({
      t: 'Average monthly spend', v: waFinMoney(avgMonth),
      b: 'Reserves target: 3 to 6 months = ' + waFinMoney(avgMonth * 3) + ' to ' + waFinMoney(avgMonth * 6) + '. Compare against the actual bank balance.',
      tip: 'Average across the ' + nMonths + ' month(s) with spending in this view. Sector guidance: hold 3 to 6 months of operating costs in unrestricted reserves. The Hub does not know the bank balance; check it against this range.'
    });
    var d = document.createElement('div');
    d.id = 'w92health';
    d.innerHTML = '<h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39">Nonprofit health</h3>' +
      '<div class="fin-note">Standard charity-sector measures computed from this time frame. Hover any card for the formula and benchmark. Department mapping: Ops = program, Fundraising + Marketing = fundraising, HR + Board + Org-wide = admin.</div>' +
      '<div class="w92hgrid">' + cards.map(function (c) {
        return '<div class="w92hcard" title="' + esc(c.tip) + '"><div class="t">' + c.t + '</div><div class="v">' + c.v + '</div><div class="b">' + c.b + '</div></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<details id="w92guide"><summary>Guidelines for a young Canadian charity</summary>' +
      '<p>Spend most of every dollar on the mission: watchdogs look for 65% or more on program work, under 35% on overhead, and under $0.20 to raise each donated dollar. These are guidelines, not law, and a growing charity can sit outside them for a season with a good reason.</p>' +
      '<p>Build a cushion: 3 to 6 months of operating costs in unrestricted reserves is the standard target, so one lost grant or a slow season never threatens payroll or the serving events.</p>' +
      '<p>CRA basics: file the T3010 return within six months of the fiscal year end (WA year ends Sep 30, so file by Mar 31), keep books, records and donation receipts for at least six years, and issue receipts only for true gifts. The disbursement quota (minimum annual charitable spending) only starts to matter once investment assets not used for charity work exceed $100,000.</p>' +
      '<p>Diversify income deliberately: individual donations plus grants plus earned revenue beats any single source. Track grants under 4200 Grants so reliance stays visible.</p>' +
      '</details>';
    anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', d);
  }

  /* ---------- wire into the render cycle ---------- */
  if (typeof waFinRender === 'function') {
    var __fr92 = waFinRender;
    waFinRender = function () {
      __fr92.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w92Pills(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      try { w92Drill(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      try { w92Health(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v93 engagement + finance workspace (07/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   1. Hub engagement tracking: every signed-in load counts a visit and
      a once-a-minute heartbeat counts time while the tab is visible
      (wa_usage table via SECURITY DEFINER RPCs wa_usage_visit /
      wa_usage_beat; counting starts 07/08/26).
   2. HR centre gains a "Hub engagement" card (admins only): per person
      last sign-in (auth), Hub opens and time on Hub (last 30 days),
      and their effective access level, founder failsafe included, so
      the admin flags can be eyeballed in one place.
   3. Finances page: a "Finance tasks" fold at the top pulling open
      tasks from EVERY department whose text looks finance-related
      (budget, statement, audit, reimbursement...). Tasks are
      surfaced here, never moved: each row links to its home page.
      wave164: Finance's own tasks (department finance) have their own
      Tasks section, so this fold lists the money work on other teams'
      boards; grants and donors are Fundraising's, not Finance's.
   4. Finance meetings: the transcript upload gains a "Finance" option
      (wave164: the pipeline files it under Finance; it used to go to
      Fundraising), and the Finances page lists those meetings with
      one-click upload access.
   ===================================================================== */

  /* ---------- 1. usage tracking ---------- */
  function w93Track() {
    try { db.rpc('wa_usage_visit'); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    var owed = 0;
    setInterval(function () {
      try {
        if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') owed += 30;
        if (owed >= 60) { var send = owed; owed = 0; db.rpc('wa_usage_beat', { p_seconds: send }); }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    }, 30000);
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa93 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa93.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w93Track(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* ---------- 2. HR centre engagement card (admins only) ---------- */
  var FOUNDERS93 = ['steel.macdonald@welfareavenue.com', 'steelmacdonald@gmail.com', 'suhaib.abdillahi@welfareavenue.com'];
  function w93Ago(iso) {
    if (!iso) return 'never';
    var ms = Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime();
    var d = Math.floor(ms / 86400000);
    if (d <= 0) return 'today';
    if (d === 1) return 'yesterday';
    if (d < 30) return d + ' days ago';
    return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });
  }
  function w93Dur(sec) {
    sec = Number(sec) || 0;
    if (sec < 60) return sec ? '<1 min' : '0';
    var m = Math.round(sec / 60);
    if (m < 60) return m + ' min';
    return (m / 60).toFixed(1) + ' h';
  }
  async function w93Engagement() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel || document.getElementById('w93eng')) return;
    var isAdmin = false;
    try {
      var em0 = ((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase();
      isAdmin = FOUNDERS93.indexOf(em0) >= 0 || (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin');
    } catch (e) { /* stay false */ }
    if (!isAdmin) return;
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'card';
    card.id = 'w93eng';
    card.innerHTML = '<h2>Hub engagement</h2><p class="read">Who is actually using the Hub. Last sign-in comes from the sign-in system; opens and time on Hub are counted from 07/08/26 onward (last 30 days shown). Visible to admins only.</p><div id="w93engbody">Loading...</div>';
    panel.appendChild(card);
    try {
      var since = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      var r = await Promise.all([
        db.rpc('wa_hub_logins'),
        db.from('wa_usage').select('*').gte('day', since),
        db.from('hub_roles').select('email, role')
      ]);
      var logins = (r[0] && r[0].data) || [];
      var usage = (r[1] && r[1].data) || [];
      var roles = {};
      ((r[2] && r[2].data) || []).forEach(function (x) { roles[x.email] = x.role; });
      var use = {};
      usage.forEach(function (u) {
        var cur = use[u.email] || { sec: 0, visits: 0 };
        cur.sec += Number(u.seconds) || 0;
        cur.visits += Number(u.visits) || 0;
        use[u.email] = cur;
      });
      var names = {};
      try {
        (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []).forEach(function (p) {
          if (p.email) names[String(p.email).toLowerCase()] = p.name;
        });
      } catch (e) { /* names optional */ }
      var emails = {};
      logins.forEach(function (l) { emails[l.email] = 1; });
      Object.keys(use).forEach(function (e2) { emails[e2] = 1; });
      Object.keys(roles).forEach(function (e2) { emails[e2] = 1; });
      var rows = Object.keys(emails).map(function (em) {
        var l = logins.filter(function (x) { return x.email === em; })[0];
        var u = use[em] || { sec: 0, visits: 0 };
        var role = roles[em] || (l ? 'signed in, no role set' : 'invited, never signed in');
        var eff = FOUNDERS93.indexOf(em) >= 0 ? 'admin' : role;
        var effNote = (FOUNDERS93.indexOf(em) >= 0 && role !== 'admin') ? ' (founder failsafe)' : '';
        return { em: em, name: names[em] || em.split('@')[0], last: l ? l.last_sign_in_at : null, sec: u.sec, visits: u.visits, eff: eff, effNote: effNote };
      }).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.last ? new Date(b.last).getTime() : 0) - (a.last ? new Date(a.last).getTime() : 0); });
      var html = '<table><thead><tr><th>Person</th><th>Last sign-in</th><th>Hub opens (30d)</th><th>Time on Hub (30d)</th><th>Access</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
        rows.map(function (x) {
          return '<tr><td><b>' + esc(x.name) + '</b><br/><span class="mut">' + esc(x.em) + '</span></td>' +
            '<td>' + esc(w93Ago(x.last)) + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + (x.visits || 0) + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + w93Dur(x.sec) + '</td>' +
            '<td><span class="rolechip ' + esc(x.eff) + '">' + esc(x.eff) + '</span><span class="mut">' + esc(x.effNote) + '</span></td></tr>';
        }).join('') + '</tbody></table>' +
        '<p class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px">Access shown is the EFFECTIVE level: the three founder accounts are always admin (database failsafe), whatever the roles table says. Change roles in Admin.</p>';
      document.getElementById('w93engbody').innerHTML = html;
    } catch (e) {
      var b = document.getElementById('w93engbody');
      if (b) b.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not load engagement data.</p>';
    }
  }
