  /* ---------- 3 + 4. finance tasks and finance meetings on Finances ---------- */
  var FIN_RX = /(budget|financ|statement|bookkeep|bookeep|audit|quickbook|bank |banking|expense|reimburs|insur|cra\b|t3010|tax|invoice|receipt|payment|payroll|purchase order)/i;   /* wave164: grants and donors are Fundraising's work, not Finance's */
  var DEPT_LABEL93 = { opsmtl: 'Operations Montreal', progdev: 'Program Development', opsto: 'Operations Toronto', marketing: 'Marketing', fundraising: 'Fundraising', hr: 'HR', board: 'Board', org: 'Org-wide' };
  var W93 = { tasks: null, meets: null, at: 0 };

  async function w93FinData() {
    if (W93.tasks && (Date.now() - W93.at) < 300000) return;
    try {
      var r = await Promise.all([
        db.from('dept_tasks').select('id, title, department, owner, due_date, subsection').eq('done', false).limit(600),
        db.from('transcripts').select('id, title, meeting_date, department, processed').ilike('department', 'financ%').order('meeting_date', { ascending: false }).limit(25)
      ]);
      W93.tasks = ((r[0] && r[0].data) || []).filter(function (t) {
        return t.department !== 'finance' && FIN_RX.test((t.title || '') + ' ' + (t.subsection || ''));   /* wave164: Finance's own tasks are in its Tasks section */
      });
      W93.meets = ((r[1] && r[1].data) || []).filter(function (m) { return /^financ/i.test(m.department || ''); });
      W93.at = Date.now();
    } catch (e) { W93.tasks = W93.tasks || []; W93.meets = W93.meets || []; }
  }

  function w93FinFolds() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('finances');
    if (!sec || document.getElementById('w93tasks')) return;
    /* when the wave94 dashboard sections exist, the folds live in Overview */
    var overview = sec.querySelector('.w94sec[data-w94sec="overview"]');
    var anchor = document.getElementById('w92pills');
    anchor = (anchor && anchor.nextElementSibling && anchor.nextElementSibling.classList.contains('w92note')) ? anchor.nextElementSibling : anchor;
    if (!anchor) anchor = sec.querySelector('.fin-head');
    if (!anchor && !overview) return;
    var tasks = W93.tasks || [];
    var meets = W93.meets || [];
    var tHtml = tasks.slice(0, 25).map(function (t) {
      var dep = t.department || 'org';

        '<a data-w93go="' + esc(dep) + '">open the board</a></div>';
    }).join('');
    var mHtml = meets.slice(0, 10).map(function (m) {
      return '<div class="w93row"><span class="t" title="' + esc(m.title || '') + '">' + esc(String(m.title || 'Finance meeting').slice(0, 70)) + '</span>' +
        (m.meeting_date ? '<span class="mut">' + esc(m.meeting_date) + '</span>' : '') +
        '<span class="mut">' + (m.processed ? 'processed' : 'processing') + '</span>' +
        '<a data-w93tx="1">open meetings</a></div>';
    }).join('');
    var t = document.createElement('details');
    t.id = 'w93tasks';
    t.innerHTML = '<summary>Money work on other teams\' boards (' + tasks.length + ')</summary>' +
      '<div class="fin-note">Open tasks on the teams\' own boards that are about money (budgets, statements, receipts, payments, reimbursements). They stay on those boards; Finance\'s own work is in Tasks.</div>' +
      (tHtml || '<div class="w93row"><span class="mut">No money work on other teams\' boards right now.</span></div>') +
      (tasks.length > 25 ? '<div class="fin-note">Showing 25 of ' + tasks.length + '.</div>' : '');
    var m = document.createElement('details');
    m.id = 'w93meet';
    m.innerHTML = '<summary>Finance meetings (' + meets.length + ')</summary>' +
      '<div class="fin-note">Meetings added with the Finance tag: what they decide files under Finance. <a data-w93add style="color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline">Add a finance meeting</a></div>' +
      (mHtml || '<div class="w93row"><span class="mut">None yet. Upload the first one from the Meetings page.</span></div>');
    if (overview) { overview.appendChild(t); overview.appendChild(m); }
    else { anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', m); anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', t); }
    sec.querySelectorAll('[data-w93go]').forEach(function (a) {
      a.addEventListener('click', function () { location.hash = '#team/' + a.getAttribute('data-w93go') + '/tasks'; });
    });
    sec.querySelectorAll('[data-w93tx],[data-w93add]').forEach(function (a) {
      a.addEventListener('click', function () {
        location.hash = '#transcripts';
        setTimeout(function () {
          try {
            var s = document.getElementById('txDept');
            if (s) s.value = 'Finance';
          } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        }, 400);
      });
    });
  }

  /* transcript upload: the Finance option (wave164: both department mappers and apply_transcript_result map it to finance) */
  function w93TxOption() {
    var s = document.getElementById('txDept');
    if (!s || s.querySelector('[data-w93fin]')) return;
    var o = document.createElement('option');
    o.value = 'Finance';
    o.setAttribute('data-w93fin', '1');
    o.textContent = 'Finance (budgets, statements, the books)';
    var after = null;
    s.querySelectorAll('option').forEach(function (x) { if (x.value === 'fundraising') after = x; });
    if (after && after.nextSibling) s.insertBefore(o, after.nextSibling);
    else s.appendChild(o);
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa93b = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa93b.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w93TxOption(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  if (typeof waFinRender === 'function') {
    var __fr93 = waFinRender;
    waFinRender = function () {
      __fr93.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        w93FinFolds();
        w93FinData().then(function () {
          var old = document.getElementById('w93tasks');
          var old2 = document.getElementById('w93meet');
          if (old && old2 && W93.tasks) {
            var openT = old.open, openM = old2.open;
            old.remove(); old2.remove();
            w93FinFolds();
            var nt = document.getElementById('w93tasks'), nm = document.getElementById('w93meet');
            if (nt) nt.open = openT;
            if (nm) nm.open = openM;
          }
        });
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v94 finance dashboard (07/08/26): sections + interactive charts
   =====================================================================
   The Finances page becomes a sectioned dashboard. Nothing is removed:
   every block waves 22 and 90-93 render is MOVED (reconciler-style)
   into one of five sections behind a sub-nav, and the two headline
   charts upgrade to fully interactive Chart.js versions (tooltips on
   every mark, clickable legends, forecast overlays) loaded lazily from
   cdn.jsdelivr.net (CSP already allows it; The Brain set the pattern).
   If the CDN is unreachable the wave90 SVG charts stay: the dashboard
   degrades, never breaks.
   Sections: Overview (KPIs, health, finance tasks + meetings),
   Trends (interactive monthly + net + a new FY-over-FY comparison),
   Money map (where money goes / comes from with merchant drilldowns,
   department share), Budget (tables + bullet bars), Transactions
   (review queue, ledger, imported statements).
   Chart colors follow the validated dataviz palette: income #103F39,
   spending #eb6834, net line #2a78d6, FY series from the fixed
   categorical order. The ledger doubles as the table view, so every
   charted number is reachable without hovering.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa94) return; window.__wa94 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w94nav{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:4px 0 14px;border-bottom:2px solid rgba(127,127,127,.15);padding-bottom:10px;}' +
    '.w94tab{border:none;background:transparent;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);border-radius:999px;padding:6px 14px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w94tab.on{background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '.w94tab:hover:not(.on){background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w94sec{min-height:120px;}' +
    '.w94grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(300px,1fr));gap:12px;margin:8px 0;}' +
    '.w94wide{background:rgba(127,127,127,.07);border-radius:10px;padding:12px 14px;margin:10px 0;}' +
    '.w94wide .w90t{margin-bottom:8px;}' +
    '.w94wide canvas{max-height:280px;}' +
    '.w94empty{font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);padding:20px 0;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var SECS = [
    ['overview', 'Overview'],
    ['tasks', 'Tasks'],   /* wave164: Finance's own board (164_wave164 draws it) */
    ['trends', 'Trends'],
    ['money', 'Money map'],
    ['budget', 'Budget'],
    ['txns', 'Transactions']
  ];
  var CJS_URL = 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.4.3/dist/chart.umd.min.js';
  var W94 = { lib: 'idle', charts: [], all: null, allAt: 0 };

  function w94Cur() {
    try { return localStorage.getItem('wa94.sec') || 'overview'; } catch (e) { return 'overview'; }
  }
  function w94Show(id) {
    try { localStorage.setItem('wa94.sec', id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    document.querySelectorAll('#finances .w94sec').forEach(function (s) {
      s.style.display = s.getAttribute('data-w94sec') === id ? '' : 'none';
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#w94nav .w94tab').forEach(function (t) {
      t.classList.toggle('on', t.getAttribute('data-w94tab') === id);
    });
    if (id === 'trends') w94Interactive();
    try { document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('wa94show', { detail: { id: id } })); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }   /* wave164: the title and the Tasks board follow the section */
  }
  window.w94Show = w94Show; window.w94Cur = w94Cur;

  /* ---------- restructure the rendered page into sections ---------- */
  function w94Build() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('finances');
    if (!sec || document.getElementById('w94nav')) return;
    var card = sec.querySelector(':scope > .card');
    if (!card) return;
    var pills = document.getElementById('w92pills');
    if (!pills) return;

    /* nav goes right after the pills (and the past-FY note if present) */
    var anchor = pills;
    if (anchor.nextElementSibling && anchor.nextElementSibling.classList.contains('w92note')) anchor = anchor.nextElementSibling;
    var nav = document.createElement('div');
    nav.id = 'w94nav';
    nav.innerHTML = SECS.map(function (s) {
      return '<button class="w94tab" data-w94tab="' + s[0] + '">' + s[1] + '</button>';
    }).join('');
    anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', nav);

    var shells = {};
    var frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    SECS.forEach(function (s) {
      var d = document.createElement('div');
      d.className = 'w94sec';
      d.setAttribute('data-w94sec', s[0]);
      shells[s[0]] = d;
      frag.appendChild(d);
    });
    /* insert shells right after the nav, in order */
    var after = nav;
    SECS.forEach(function (s) { after.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', shells[s[0]]); after = shells[s[0]]; });

    function mv(el, home) { if (el && shells[home]) shells[home].appendChild(el); }

    /* fixed-id blocks */
    mv(sec.querySelector('.fin-kpis'), 'overview');
    mv(document.getElementById('w92health'), 'overview');
    mv(document.getElementById('w93tasks'), 'overview');
    mv(document.getElementById('w93meet'), 'overview');
    mv(document.getElementById('w91review'), 'txns');

    /* split the wave90 chart grid: wide boxes -> trends, small -> money map,
       budget bullets -> budget */
    var w90 = document.getElementById('w90charts');
    if (w90) {
      var trendsGrid = document.createElement('div');
      trendsGrid.id = 'w94staticwide';
      var moneyGrid = document.createElement('div');
      moneyGrid.className = 'w94grid';
      var bulletBox = null;
      w90.querySelectorAll('.w90box').forEach(function (box) {
        var t = ((box.querySelector('.w90t') || {}).textContent || '');
        if (box.classList.contains('wide')) trendsGrid.appendChild(box);
        else if (t.indexOf('Budget vs actual') === 0) bulletBox = box;
        else moneyGrid.appendChild(box);
      });
      shells.trends.appendChild(trendsGrid);
      shells.money.appendChild(moneyGrid);
      if (bulletBox) {
        var bg = document.createElement('div');
        bg.className = 'w94grid';
        bg.appendChild(bulletBox);
        shells.budget.appendChild(bg);
      }
      w90.remove();
    }

    /* walk the remaining flat card children and bucket by heading */
    var bucket = null;
    Array.prototype.slice.call(card.children).forEach(function (el) {
      if (el.id === 'w94nav' || el.classList.contains('w94sec') || el.classList.contains('fin-head') || el.id === 'w92pills' || el.classList.contains('w92note')) return;
      var txt = (el.tagName === 'H3') ? (el.textContent || '') : '';
      if (txt.indexOf('Charts') === 0) { el.remove(); return; }
      if (txt.indexOf('Budget vs actual') === 0) bucket = 'budget';
      else if (txt.indexOf('Spending by department') === 0) bucket = 'money';
      else if (txt.indexOf('Transactions') === 0) bucket = 'txns';
      if (bucket) mv(el, bucket);
    });

    /* trends: interactive mount points above the static fallback */
    var mount = document.createElement('div');
    mount.id = 'w94live';
    mount.innerHTML =
      '<div class="w94wide" style="display:none"><div class="w90t">Income vs spending by month (interactive)</div><canvas id="w94c1"></canvas></div>' +
      '<div class="w94wide" style="display:none"><div class="w90t">Net position over the year (interactive)</div><canvas id="w94c2"></canvas></div>' +
      '<div class="w94wide" style="display:none"><div class="w90t">Year over year: how each fiscal year compares</div><canvas id="w94c3"></canvas><div class="fin-note" id="w94c3note"></div></div>';
    shells.trends.insertBefore(mount, shells.trends.firstChild);

    nav.querySelectorAll('.w94tab').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () { w94Show(b.getAttribute('data-w94tab')); });
    });
    w94Show(w94Cur());
  }

  /* ---------- data helpers (independent of wave90 internals) ---------- */
  function w94Months() {
    var y = waFinFYNow() - 1;
    var names = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
    var arr = [], idx = {};
    for (var i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      var m = (9 + i) % 12 + 1;
      var yy = m >= 10 ? y : y + 1;
      var key = yy + '-' + ('0' + m).slice(-2);
      arr.push({ key: key, label: names[m - 1], inc: 0, spd: 0 });
      idx[key] = i;
    }
    (WA_FIN_TX || []).forEach(function (t) {
      if (t.duplicate_of !== null && t.duplicate_of !== undefined) return;
      var i2 = idx[String(t.txn_date).slice(0, 7)];
      if (i2 === undefined) return;
      var a = Number(t.amount);
      if (a >= 0) arr[i2].inc += a; else arr[i2].spd += -a;
    });
    var now = new Date();
    var natFY = (now.getMonth() + 1) >= 10 ? now.getFullYear() + 1 : now.getFullYear();
    var proj = null;
    if (window.WA_FIN_FC && waFinFYNow() === natFY) {
      var last = -1;
      for (var li = 11; li >= 0; li--) { if (arr[li].inc || arr[li].spd) { last = li; break; } }
      if (last >= 0 && last < 11) {
        var from = Math.max(0, last - 2), cnt = last - from + 1, ai = 0, asp = 0;
        for (var p = from; p <= last; p++) { ai += arr[p].inc; asp += arr[p].spd; }
        proj = { from: last + 1, avgInc: ai / cnt, avgSpd: asp / cnt, months: cnt };
      }
    }
    return { M: arr, proj: proj };
  }

  async function w94AllYears() {
    if (W94.all && (Date.now() - W94.allAt) < 300000) return W94.all;
    try {
      var out = [], page = 0, chunk;
      do {
        chunk = await db.from('finance_transactions').select('txn_date, amount, duplicate_of').order('id', { ascending: true }).range(page * 1000, page * 1000 + 999);
        out = out.concat((chunk && chunk.data) || []);
        page += 1;
      } while (chunk && chunk.data && chunk.data.length === 1000 && page < 10);
      var fy = {};
      out.forEach(function (t) {
        if (t.duplicate_of !== null && t.duplicate_of !== undefined) return;
        var d = String(t.txn_date);
        var y = Number(d.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(d.slice(5, 7));
        var f = m >= 10 ? y + 1 : y;
        var row = fy[f] || { inc: 0, spd: 0 };
        var a = Number(t.amount);
        if (a >= 0) row.inc += a; else row.spd += -a;
        fy[f] = row;
      });
      W94.all = Object.keys(fy).map(Number).sort().map(function (y2) {
        return { fy: 'FY' + String(y2).slice(-2), inc: fy[y2].inc, spd: fy[y2].spd };
      });
      W94.allAt = Date.now();
    } catch (e) { W94.all = W94.all || []; }
    return W94.all;
  }

  /* ---------- interactive charts ---------- */
  function w94Money(v) { return waFinMoney(v, true); }
  /* wave164: a canvas can hold one chart: free it before drawing again (Trends opened twice drew on a busy canvas) */
  function w94Free(cv) { try { var ex = cv && window.Chart && window.Chart.getChart ? window.Chart.getChart(cv) : null; if (ex) ex.destroy(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function w94Destroy() {
    W94.charts.forEach(function (c) { try { c.destroy(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
    W94.charts = [];
  }
  function w94LoadLib(cb) {
    if (window.Chart) { W94.lib = 'ok'; cb(); return; }
    if (W94.lib === 'loading' || W94.lib === 'fail') return;
    W94.lib = 'loading';
    var s = document.createElement('script');
    s.src = CJS_URL;
    s.onload = function () { W94.lib = 'ok'; cb(); };
    s.onerror = function () { W94.lib = 'fail'; };
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  }

  var INC = '#103F39', SPD = '#eb6834', NET = '#2a78d6';
  var CAT8 = ['#2a78d6', '#eb6834', '#103F39', '#eda100', '#e87ba4', '#008300', '#4a3aa7', '#e34948'];

  function w94Interactive() {
    var live = document.getElementById('w94live');
    if (!live) return;
    w94LoadLib(function () { w94Mount(); });
    if (window.Chart) w94Mount();
  }

  function w94Mount() {
    var live = document.getElementById('w94live');
    if (!live || !window.Chart || live.dataset.mounted) return;
    live.dataset.mounted = '1';
    var stat = document.getElementById('w94staticwide');
    if (stat) stat.style.display = 'none';
    live.querySelectorAll('.w94wide').forEach(function (b) { b.style.display = ''; });
    w94Destroy();
    var d = w94Months();
    var labels = d.M.map(function (m) { return m.label; });
    var incData = d.M.map(function (m, i) { return (d.proj && i >= d.proj.from) ? null : Math.round(m.inc); });
    var spdData = d.M.map(function (m, i) { return (d.proj && i >= d.proj.from) ? null : Math.round(m.spd); });
    var pInc = d.M.map(function (m, i) { return (d.proj && i >= d.proj.from) ? Math.round(d.proj.avgInc) : null; });
    var pSpd = d.M.map(function (m, i) { return (d.proj && i >= d.proj.from) ? Math.round(d.proj.avgSpd) : null; });
    function money(ctx) { return ' ' + (ctx.dataset.label || '') + ': ' + w94Money(ctx.parsed.y); }
    var c1 = document.getElementById('w94c1');
    if (c1) {
      var sets = [
        { label: 'Income', data: incData, backgroundColor: INC, borderRadius: 3, maxBarThickness: 22 },
        { label: 'Spending', data: spdData, backgroundColor: SPD, borderRadius: 3, maxBarThickness: 22 }
      ];
      if (d.proj) {
        sets.push({ label: 'Projected income', data: pInc, backgroundColor: INC + '59', borderColor: INC, borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 3, maxBarThickness: 22 });
        sets.push({ label: 'Projected spending', data: pSpd, backgroundColor: SPD + '59', borderColor: SPD, borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 3, maxBarThickness: 22 });
      }
      w94Free(c1);
      W94.charts.push(new Chart(c1, {
        type: 'bar',
        data: { labels: labels, datasets: sets },
        options: {
          responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
          plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { boxWidth: 10, font: { size: 11 } } }, tooltip: { callbacks: { label: money } } },
          scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { callback: function (v) { return '$' + Number(v).toLocaleString(); } } }, x: { grid: { display: false } } }
        }
      }));
    }
    var c2 = document.getElementById('w94c2');
    if (c2) {
      var cum = [], c = 0, cut = 11;
      var lastData = -1;
      d.M.forEach(function (m, i) { if (m.inc || m.spd) lastData = i; });
      var now = new Date();
      var nowKey = now.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (now.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2);
      d.M.forEach(function (m, i) { if (m.key === nowKey) cut = i; });
      if (lastData > cut) cut = lastData;
      var end = d.proj ? 11 : cut;
      var endActual = d.proj ? d.proj.from - 1 : cut;
      for (var i3 = 0; i3 <= end; i3++) {
        if (i3 <= endActual) { c += d.M[i3].inc - d.M[i3].spd; cum.push(Math.round(c * 100) / 100); }
        else if (d.proj) { c += d.proj.avgInc - d.proj.avgSpd; cum.push(Math.round(c * 100) / 100); }
      }
      w94Free(c2);
      W94.charts.push(new Chart(c2, {
        type: 'line',
        data: {
          labels: labels.slice(0, end + 1),
          datasets: [{
            label: 'Net position', data: cum, borderColor: NET, backgroundColor: NET,
            pointRadius: 4, pointHoverRadius: 6, borderWidth: 2.5, tension: 0.15,
            segment: { borderDash: function (ctx) { return d.proj && ctx.p1DataIndex > endActual ? [6, 5] : undefined; } },
            pointStyle: function (ctx) { return d.proj && ctx.dataIndex > endActual ? 'circle' : 'circle'; },
            pointBackgroundColor: function (ctx) { return d.proj && ctx.dataIndex > endActual ? '#FFFAF2' : NET; }
          }]
        },
        options: {
          responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
          plugins: {
            legend: { display: false },
            tooltip: { callbacks: { label: function (ctx) { return ' Net' + (d.proj && ctx.dataIndex > endActual ? ' (projected)' : '') + ': ' + w94Money(ctx.parsed.y); } } }
          },
          scales: { y: { ticks: { callback: function (v) { return '$' + Number(v).toLocaleString(); } }, grid: { color: function (ctx2) { return ctx2.tick && ctx2.tick.value === 0 ? 'rgba(0,0,0,.35)' : 'rgba(127,127,127,.15)'; } } }, x: { grid: { display: false } } }
        }
      }));
    }
    w94AllYears().then(function (all) {
      var c3 = document.getElementById('w94c3');
      if (!c3 || !all || all.length < 2) {
        var box = c3 && c3.closest('.w94wide');
        if (box && (!all || all.length < 2)) box.style.display = 'none';
        return;
      }
      w94Free(c3);   /* wave164: the year-over-year data arrives late; a second mount must not collide with the first ("Canvas is already in use") */
      W94.charts.push(new Chart(c3, {
        type: 'bar',
        data: {
          labels: all.map(function (x) { return x.fy; }),
          datasets: [
            { label: 'Income', data: all.map(function (x) { return Math.round(x.inc); }), backgroundColor: INC, borderRadius: 3, maxBarThickness: 34 },
            { label: 'Spending', data: all.map(function (x) { return Math.round(x.spd); }), backgroundColor: SPD, borderRadius: 3, maxBarThickness: 34 }
          ]
        },
        options: {
          responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
          plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { boxWidth: 10, font: { size: 11 } } }, tooltip: { callbacks: { label: money } } },
          scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { callback: function (v) { return '$' + Number(v).toLocaleString(); } } }, x: { grid: { display: false } } }
        }
      }));
      var note = document.getElementById('w94c3note');
      if (note) {
        var latest = all[all.length - 1];
        note.textContent = 'Every fiscal year in the ledger, duplicates excluded. ' + latest.fy + ' is in progress. Hover any bar; click a legend entry to hide a series.';
      }
    });
  }

  /* ---------- wire in (outermost wrapper: runs after waves 90-93) ---------- */
  if (typeof waFinRender === 'function') {
    var __fr94 = waFinRender;
    waFinRender = function () {
      w94Destroy();
      __fr94.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        w94Build();
        var live = document.getElementById('w94live');
        if (live) delete live.dataset.mounted;
        if (w94Cur() === 'trends') w94Interactive();
        /* wave93 refreshes its folds asynchronously by re-inserting them after
           the pills row: scoop any escapees back into the Overview section */
        var scoop = function () {
          var ov = document.querySelector('#finances .w94sec[data-w94sec="overview"]');
          if (!ov) return;
          ['w93tasks', 'w93meet'].forEach(function (id) {
            var el = document.getElementById(id);
            if (el && !ov.contains(el)) ov.appendChild(el);
          });
        };
        setTimeout(scoop, 1200);
        setTimeout(scoop, 3200);
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal: the flat page still renders */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v95 nav tidy (07/08/26): Important info + The Brain into More
   =====================================================================
   Steel's ask: declutter the top bar. The Important info and Brain
   tabs move inside the More flyout (joining Finances, Activity and
   Admin). Buttons keep their ids, handlers and role gating; only the
   parent changes, so deep links and wa40Enforce keep working.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa95) return; window.__wa95 = true;

  function w95Tidy() {
    var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
    if (!bar) return;
    var more = bar.querySelector('.tab-group[data-w42g="more"] .tab-group-p');
    if (!more) return;
    ['importantinfo', 'brain'].forEach(function (id) {
      var t = bar.querySelector(':scope > .tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]');
      if (t) more.appendChild(t);
    });
  }

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa95 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa95.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        w95Tidy();
        /* the Brain tab can register a beat later than the bar build */
        setTimeout(w95Tidy, 800);
        setTimeout(w95Tidy, 2500);
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v96 change the Hub (07/08/26): edit with AI + change requests
   =====================================================================
   A new "Change the Hub" card at the bottom of the Admin page, two
   halves, admins only:
   1. EDIT WITH AI: describe a CONTENT change in plain language
      (important info links, budget amounts, new budget lines, AI
      categorisation rules). The hub-edit fn translates it into a
      bounded list of operations; the admin sees the preview and
      applies it. Operations execute under the admin's OWN permissions
      (RLS), one change_log entry each. Site code is out of scope by
      design: the assistant says so and points at the request lane.
   2. REQUEST A BUILD: describe any bigger change (features, layout,
      code). It is saved to wa_change_requests, posted to Slack
      (notify v6 change_request event), and listed here with a status
      dropdown (open / planned / done / declined) so nothing is lost.
      Builds then run through the tested Cowork deploy pipeline.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa96) return; window.__wa96 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w96card h3{color:#103F39;margin:14px 0 4px;}' +
    '#w96card textarea{width:100%;font-size:12.5px;min-height:64px;}' +
    '#w96ops{margin:10px 0;}' +
    '.w96op{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start;padding:6px 0;border-top:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.15);font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.w96op:first-child{border-top:none;}' +
    '.w96op .tag{font-size:10px;border-radius:7px;padding:2px 8px;font-weight:700;white-space:nowrap;background:rgba(16,63,57,.12);color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w96op .tag.bad{background:rgba(201,107,95,.14);color:#b3402f;}' +
    '.w96req{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:6px 0;border-top:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.15);font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.w96req:first-child{border-top:none;}' +
    '.w96req .t{flex:1 1 260px;min-width:180px;}' +
    '.w96req .mut{font-size:11px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);white-space:nowrap;}' +
    '.w96req select{font-size:11px;padding:2px 4px;border-radius:5px;border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.3);background:transparent;color:inherit;}' +
    '.w96req select option{color:#103F39;}' +
    '#w96card .m-sub{margin:2px 0 8px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var FOUNDERS96 = ['steel.macdonald@welfareavenue.com', 'steelmacdonald@gmail.com', 'suhaib.abdillahi@welfareavenue.com'];
  function w96IsAdmin() {
    try {
      var em = ((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase();
      return FOUNDERS96.indexOf(em) >= 0 || (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin');
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }

  function w96OpLine(o) {
    var lab = '', tag = o.op, bad = false;
    if (o.op === 'add_info_link') lab = 'Add info link "' + (o.label || '') + '"' + (o.url ? ' (' + o.url + ')' : '') + (o.department ? ' under ' + o.department : '');
    else if (o.op === 'update_info_link') lab = 'Update info link #' + o.id + (o.label ? ' to "' + o.label + '"' : '') + (o.url ? ', url ' + o.url : '') + (o.notes ? ', notes updated' : '');
    else if (o.op === 'remove_info_link') lab = 'Remove info link #' + o.id;
    else if (o.op === 'set_budget') lab = 'Set ' + (o.period || 'FY') + ' budget for ' + (o.category || '?') + ' to $' + Number(o.amount || 0).toLocaleString();
    else if (o.op === 'add_budget_line') lab = 'Add ' + (o.line_kind || 'expense') + ' budget line "' + (o.label || o.category || '') + '"';
    else if (o.op === 'add_guidance_rule') lab = 'Teach the importer: ' + (o.rule || '');
    else if (o.op === 'unsupported') { lab = o.reason || 'Not something the content assistant can do.'; tag = 'not possible here'; bad = true; }
    else { lab = 'Unknown operation'; tag = 'skipped'; bad = true; }
    return '<div class="w96op"><span class="tag' + (bad ? ' bad' : '') + '">' + esc(tag) + '</span><span>' + esc(lab) + '</span></div>';
  }

  async function w96Apply(ops) {
    var done = 0, failed = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < ops.length; i++) {
      var o = ops[i];
      try {
        var r = null;
        if (o.op === 'add_info_link') {
          r = await db.from('info_links').insert({ kind: o.kind || 'link', label: String(o.label || '').slice(0, 200), url: o.url || null, department: o.department || null, notes: o.notes || null, added_by: who(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() });
        } else if (o.op === 'update_info_link') {
          var patch = { updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
          if (o.label) patch.label = String(o.label).slice(0, 200);
          if (o.url) patch.url = o.url;
          if (o.department !== undefined && o.department !== null) patch.department = o.department;
          if (o.notes !== undefined && o.notes !== null) patch.notes = o.notes;
          r = await db.from('info_links').update(patch).eq('id', Number(o.id));
        } else if (o.op === 'remove_info_link') {
          r = await db.from('info_links').delete().eq('id', Number(o.id));
        } else if (o.op === 'set_budget') {
          var cat = String(o.category || '');
          var kind = (typeof WA_FIN_CATS_IN !== 'undefined' && WA_FIN_CATS_IN.indexOf(cat) >= 0) ? 'income' : 'expense';
          r = await db.from('finance_budgets').upsert({
            fiscal_year: waFinFYLabel(), period: o.period || 'FY', scope: 'category', key: cat,
            kind: kind, amount: Number(o.amount) || 0, updated_by: who(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
          }, { onConflict: 'fiscal_year,period,scope,key' });
        } else if (o.op === 'add_budget_line') {
          r = await db.from('finance_budgets').upsert({
            fiscal_year: waFinFYLabel(), period: 'FY', scope: 'category', key: String(o.label || o.category || '').slice(0, 60),
            kind: o.line_kind === 'income' ? 'income' : 'expense', amount: 0, updated_by: who(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
          }, { onConflict: 'fiscal_year,period,scope,key' });
        } else if (o.op === 'add_guidance_rule') {
          var g = await db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'finance_ai_guidance').maybeSingle();
          var cur = (g && g.data && g.data.value) || '';
          var next = (cur ? cur.replace(/\s+$/, '') + '\n' : '') + String(o.rule || '').slice(0, 300);
          if (next.length > 4000) { failed += 1; continue; }
          r = await db.from('app_config').upsert({ key: 'finance_ai_guidance', value: next, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() });
        } else { continue; }
        if (r && r.error) { failed += 1; continue; }
        done += 1;
        try {
          await db.from('change_log').insert({
            entity_type: 'hub_edit', entity_id: o.op, field: 'applied',
            old_value: null, new_value: JSON.stringify(o).slice(0, 280), changed_by: who()
          });
        } catch (e) { /* audit only */ }
      } catch (e) { failed += 1; }
    }
    return { done: done, failed: failed };
  }

  function w96EditModal() {
    openModal('<h3>Edit the Hub with AI</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub">Content edits in plain language: important info links, budget amounts, new budget lines, or a rule for the statement importer. Example: "add a link to our new insurance policy doc at drive.google.com/... under org-wide" or "set the Q2 marketing publicity budget to 500". Code and feature changes belong in Request a build below. You will see exactly what will change before anything happens.</p>' +
      '<textarea id="w96in" rows="3" maxlength="2000" placeholder="Describe the content change"></textarea>' +
      '<div id="w96ops"></div>' +
      '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w96go">Preview</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w96cancel">Cancel</button></div>');
    var ops = null;
    var cancel = document.getElementById('w96cancel');
    if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    var go = document.getElementById('w96go');
    if (go) go.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var box = document.getElementById('w96ops');
      if (!ops) {
        var text = (document.getElementById('w96in').value || '').trim();
        if (text.length < 5) { toast('Describe the change first'); return; }
        go.disabled = true; go.textContent = 'Thinking...';
        try {
          var r = await db.functions.invoke('hub-edit', { body: { instruction: text } });
          if (r.error) throw r.error;
          var d = r.data || {};
          ops = (d.operations || []).filter(function (o) { return o && o.op; });
          var real = ops.filter(function (o) { return o.op !== 'unsupported'; });
          box.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub"><b>' + esc(d.summary || 'Proposed changes') + '</b></p>' +
            (ops.length ? ops.map(w96OpLine).join('') : '<div class="w96op"><span>No operations proposed.</span></div>');
          go.disabled = false;
          if (real.length) { go.textContent = 'Apply ' + real.length + (real.length === 1 ? ' change' : ' changes'); }
          else { go.textContent = 'Preview'; ops = null; }
        } catch (e) {
          go.disabled = false; go.textContent = 'Preview';
          box.innerHTML = '<div class="w96op"><span class="tag bad">error</span><span>The assistant could not process that. Try rephrasing, or file it as a build request.</span></div>';
        }
        return;
      }
      go.disabled = true; go.textContent = 'Applying...';
      var real2 = ops.filter(function (o) { return o.op !== 'unsupported'; });
      var res = await w96Apply(real2);
      closeModal();
      toast(res.failed ? res.done + ' applied, ' + res.failed + ' failed' : 'Applied: ' + res.done + (res.done === 1 ? ' change' : ' changes'));
      try { if (typeof WA_FIN_LOADED !== 'undefined' && WA_FIN_LOADED) { await waFinLoad(true); waFinRender(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    });
  }

  function w96RequestModal() {
    openModal('<h3>Request a build</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub">Anything bigger: features, layout, new pages, automations. It gets logged here and posted to Slack, and ships through the tested build pipeline (offline test battery, verified deploys), never straight to the live site.</p>' +
      '<textarea id="w96rin" rows="4" maxlength="2000" placeholder="What should change, and why it helps"></textarea>' +
      '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w96rgo">File the request</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w96rcancel">Cancel</button></div>');
    var cancel = document.getElementById('w96rcancel');
    if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    var go = document.getElementById('w96rgo');
    if (go) go.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var text = (document.getElementById('w96rin').value || '').trim();
      if (text.length < 10) { toast('Say a little more about the change'); return; }
      go.disabled = true;
      var r = await db.from('wa_change_requests').insert({ requested_by: who(), request: text }).select('id').single();
      if (r.error || !r.data) { go.disabled = false; toast('Could not file the request'); return; }
      try { db.functions.invoke('notify', { body: { event: 'change_request', request_id: r.data.id } }); } catch (e) { /* best effort */ }
      closeModal();
      toast('Filed and posted to Slack');
      w96Refresh();
    });
  }

  async function w96Refresh() {
    var list = document.getElementById('w96list');
    if (!list) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('wa_change_requests').select('*').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(30);
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      if (!rows.length) { list.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub">No requests yet.</p>'; return; }
      list.innerHTML = rows.map(function (x) {
        var d = new Date(x.created_at);
        var dl = d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
        return '<div class="w96req"><span class="t" title="' + esc(x.request) + '">' + esc(String(x.request).slice(0, 90)) + '</span>' +
          '<span class="mut">' + esc((x.requested_by || '').split('@')[0]) + ' ' + esc(dl) + '</span>' +
          '<select data-w96st="' + x.id + '">' +
          ['open', 'planned', 'done', 'declined'].map(function (s) {
            return '<option value="' + s + '"' + (s === x.status ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s + '</option>';
          }).join('') + '</select></div>';
      }).join('');
      list.querySelectorAll('[data-w96st]').forEach(function (s) {
        s.addEventListener('change', async function () {
          var res = await db.from('wa_change_requests').update({ status: s.value, updated_by: who(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', Number(s.getAttribute('data-w96st')));
          toast(res.error ? 'Could not update' : 'Request marked ' + s.value);
        });
      });
    } catch (e) { list.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub">Could not load requests.</p>'; }
  }

  function w96Card() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('adminpanel');
    if (!panel || document.getElementById('w96card') || !w96IsAdmin()) return;
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'card';
    card.id = 'w96card';
    card.innerHTML = '<h2>Change the Hub</h2>' +
      '<p class="read">Two lanes. Content edits happen right here with a preview; anything bigger gets filed, posted to Slack, and built through the tested pipeline.</p>' +
      '<h3 class="block">Edit content with AI</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub">Info links, budget amounts, budget lines, importer rules. Preview first, nothing applies until you confirm, everything is change-logged.</p>' +
      '<button class="btn mini" id="w96edit">Edit with AI</button>' +
      '<h3 class="block">Request a build</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub">Features, layout, code. Filed here and posted to Slack; shipped through the tested deploy pipeline with an approval gate.</p>' +
      '<button class="btn ghost mini" id="w96request">Request a build</button>' +
      '<div id="w96list" style="margin-top:10px"><p class="m-sub">Loading requests...</p></div>';
    panel.appendChild(card);
    document.getElementById('w96edit').addEventListener('click', w96EditModal);
    document.getElementById('w96request').addEventListener('click', w96RequestModal);
    w96Refresh();
  }

  /* adminpanel re-renders via wa40Render (global): wrap it */
  if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') {
    var __ar96 = wa40Render;
    wa40Render = async function () {
      var out = await __ar96.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w96Card(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa96 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa96.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w96Card(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v97 Sneat re-skin (07/08/26): the whole Hub, WA green accent
   =====================================================================
   Visual-only wave, no behaviour changes. Adopts the design language of
   the Sneat admin template (ThemeSelection, MIT free edition) requested
   by Steel: Public Sans typography, soft #F4F2EA canvas, borderless
   white cards with the 0 2px 6px rgba(16,63,57,.12) shadow, rounded
   pill navigation, softened inputs, tinted pill badges, and the Sneat
   text scale (#103F39 headings, #1F201E body). The accent stays
   Welfare Avenue green rather than Sneat purple (Steel's call), so the
   brand survives the restyle. Implemented as token overrides on the
   base :root variables plus targeted component restyles; the wave
   loads last, so its CSS wins the cascade. Chart palette untouched
   (it is validated separately).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa97) return; window.__wa97 = true;

  /* Public Sans (CSP already allows fonts.googleapis + fonts.gstatic) */
  try {

    l.href = 'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Public+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap';
    document.head.appendChild(l);
  } catch (e) { /* system font fallback is fine */ }

    /* ---------- top bar + nav ---------- */
    '#tabBar{background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:10px;box-shadow:0 2px 6px 0 rgba(16,63,57,.12);padding:6px 8px;}' +

    /* ---------- buttons ---------- */

    '.btn.ghost{box-shadow:none;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.5);}' +

    'input[type=text],input[type=date],input[type=number],input[type=email],input[type=search],select,textarea{border:1px solid #d9dee3;border-radius:8px;background:#FFFAF2;color:var(--wa-text);padding:8px 12px;font-family:inherit;transition:border-color .15s,box-shadow .15s;}' +

    '#w93eng .rolechip.admin{background:rgba(16,63,57,.14);color:#103F39;}' +

    'details summary{color:var(--wa-ink);}' +

    /* ---------- time pills ---------- */
    '.w92pill{border-color:#d9dee3;color:#1F201E;}' +
    '.w92pill.on{box-shadow:0 2px 4px 0 rgba(16,63,57,.35);}' +

    /* ---------- task rows (Namviek-inspired: card rows, pills, hover) ---------- */
    'ul.checklist li[data-task]{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;margin:8px 0;transition:box-shadow .15s,border-color .15s;border-bottom:1px solid #DEE0D8;}' +
    'ul.checklist li[data-task]:hover{border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.45);box-shadow:0 2px 8px 0 rgba(16,63,57,.14);}' +
    'ul.checklist li[data-task].done{opacity:.6;}' +
    'ul.checklist li[data-task].done .ci-title{text-decoration:line-through;}' +
    'ul.checklist input[type=checkbox]{accent-color:#103F39;width:18px;height:18px;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.ci-title{color:var(--wa-ink);font-weight:600;}' +
    '.ci-sub,.ci-meta{color:var(--wa-muted);}' +
    '.due-badge{background:rgba(133,146,163,.14);color:#1F201E;border-radius:6px;font-weight:600;}' +
    '.due-badge.amber{background:rgba(255,171,0,.16);color:#a06f00;border:none;}' +
    '.due-badge.red{background:rgba(255,62,29,.14);color:#c4402a;border:none;}' +
    '.as-chip{background:rgba(16,63,57,.12);color:#103F39;border-radius:6px;font-weight:600;}' +
    '.cat-tag{border-radius:6px;}' +
    '.edit-btn,.reassign-btn{border:1px solid #e2e5ea;border-radius:6px;background:#FFFAF2;color:#1F201E;text-decoration:none;padding:2px 9px;margin-left:6px;font-weight:600;transition:border-color .15s,color .15s;}' +
    '.edit-btn:hover,.reassign-btn:hover{border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.5);color:var(--wa-green);text-decoration:none;}' +
    '.mp-task{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 10px;margin:6px 0;transition:box-shadow .15s,border-color .15s;}' +
    '.mp-task:hover{border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.45);box-shadow:0 2px 8px 0 rgba(16,63,57,.14);}' +
    '.req-card{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:10px;box-shadow:none;transition:box-shadow .15s;}' +
    '.req-card:hover{box-shadow:0 2px 8px 0 rgba(16,63,57,.14);}' +
    '.subsection{border:1px solid #eef0f4;border-radius:10px;padding:12px 14px;background:#fbfbfd;}' +
    '.subsection ul.checklist li{padding:10px 12px;}' +

    /* ---------- footer ---------- */
    '.footer,footer{color:#a1acb8;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* keep this stylesheet LAST in <head> so later waves' css never outranks
     the skin accidentally, and the skin keeps winning after boot */
  try {
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function () {
      var last = document.head.lastElementChild;
      if (last && last.id !== 'wa97css' && (last.tagName === 'STYLE')) {
        var el = document.getElementById('wa97css');
        if (el) document.head.appendChild(el);
      }
    });
    mo.observe(document.head, { childList: true });
  } catch (e) { /* cascade order at load time still applies */ }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v98 task management pass (07/08/26): Namviek visuals, full depth
   =====================================================================
   Second half of the Namviek inspiration (hudy9x/namviek). Decoration
   only: every dropdown, button and behaviour stays.
   1. Kanban board: Namviek-style columns (soft grey wells, white cards
      with shadow + hover lift, count pills in the column headers).
   2. Colour-coded status: the status chip on board cards and a status
      left-border on every list task row (In progress cyan, Blocked
      red, Waiting amber, Done green); flagged tasks carry the red
      priority edge.
   3. Owner avatars: the wave21 initials bubbles now also appear on
      LIST rows and My page rows, in front of the owner name.
   4. Workstream headers gain live open-task count pills.
   Applied by a debounced MutationObserver over <main>, so wave77's
   continuous repaints re-decorate automatically; every decoration is
   guarded so it never doubles up.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa98) return; window.__wa98 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.id = 'wa98css';
  css.textContent = '' +
    /* board */
    '.wa21-board{gap:12px;}' +
    '.wa21-col{background:#f4f5f9;border:1px solid #eceef4;border-radius:10px;flex:0 0 248px;padding:10px;}' +
    '.wa21-colh{color:#103F39;font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.wa21-colh .n{opacity:1;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #e2e5ea;border-radius:999px;padding:0 8px;font-size:10.5px;color:#1F201E;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.wa21-card{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;box-shadow:0 1px 3px 0 rgba(16,63,57,.1);transition:box-shadow .15s,border-color .15s;}' +
    '.wa21-card:hover{border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.5);box-shadow:0 3px 10px 0 rgba(16,63,57,.16);}' +
    /* status pills */
    '.wa21-st{border:none;font-weight:700;background:rgba(133,146,163,.14);color:#1F201E;}' +
    '.w98st-open{background:rgba(133,146,163,.14);color:#1F201E;}' +
    '.w98st-inprogress{background:rgba(3,195,236,.14);color:#0a8fac;}' +
    '.w98st-blocked{background:rgba(255,62,29,.14);color:#c4402a;}' +
    '.w98st-waiting{background:rgba(255,171,0,.18);color:#a06f00;}' +
    '.w98st-done{background:rgba(16,63,57,.14);color:#103F39;}' +
    /* status edge on list rows */
    'ul.checklist li[data-task]{border-left:3px solid #DEE0D8;}' +
    'ul.checklist li[data-task].w98b-inprogress{border-left-color:#03c3ec;}' +
    'ul.checklist li[data-task].w98b-blocked{border-left-color:#ff3e1d;}' +
    'ul.checklist li[data-task].w98b-waiting{border-left-color:#ffab00;}' +
    'ul.checklist li[data-task].w98b-done{border-left-color:#103F39;}' +
    'ul.checklist li[data-task].w98flag{border-left-color:#ff3e1d;}' +
    /* avatars on list rows */
    '.ci-sub .wa21-bub{vertical-align:-4px;margin-right:5px;}' +
    /* workstream count pills: SIBLINGS of the subhead, never inside it
       (wave77 matches workstreams by the subhead's text; polluting it
       makes legitimate empty workstreams look unknown and get removed) */
    '.subsection h3.subhead{display:inline-block;margin-right:0;}' +
    '.w98count{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-width:20px;height:18px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.12);color:#103F39;border-radius:999px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;margin-left:8px;padding:0 6px;vertical-align:3px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  function w98Status(s, done, fallback) {
    var t = String(s || '').toLowerCase();
    if (t.indexOf('progress') >= 0) return 'inprogress';
    if (t.indexOf('block') >= 0) return 'blocked';
    if (t.indexOf('wait') >= 0) return 'waiting';
    if (t.indexOf('done') >= 0 || done) return 'done';
    return fallback;
  }

  function w98Decorate() {
    /* list rows: status edge + flag edge + owner avatar */
    document.querySelectorAll('li[data-task]').forEach(function (li) {
      if (li.dataset.w98) return;
      li.dataset.w98 = '1';
      var id = Number(li.dataset.task);
      var t = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).find(function (x) { return x.id === id; });
      if (!t) return;
      li.classList.add('w98b-' + w98Status(t.status, t.done, 'open'));
      if (t.flag && !t.done) li.classList.add('w98flag');
      try {
        var sub = li.querySelector('.ci-sub');
        if (sub && t.owner && typeof wa21Bubble === 'function' && !sub.querySelector('.wa21-bub')) {
          sub.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', wa21Bubble(t.owner, true) + ' ');
        }
      } catch (e) { /* avatar is optional */ }
    });
    /* board/status chips: colour by their text */
    document.querySelectorAll('.wa21-st').forEach(function (el) {
      if (el.dataset.w98) return;
      el.dataset.w98 = '1';
      var s = el.getAttribute('title') || el.textContent || '';
      el.classList.add('w98st-' + w98Status(s, false, 'open'));
    });
    /* workstream headers: open-task counts */
    document.querySelectorAll('.subsection').forEach(function (ss) {
      var h = ss.querySelector('h3.subhead');
      if (!h) return;
      var n = ss.querySelectorAll('li[data-task]:not(.done)').length;
      var pill = h.nextElementSibling;
      if (!pill || !pill.classList || !pill.classList.contains('w98count')) {
        pill = document.createElement('span');
        pill.className = 'w98count';
        h.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', pill);
      }
      var txt = String(n);
      if (pill.textContent !== txt) pill.textContent = txt;
    });
  }

  (function () {
    var t98 = null;
    function arm() {
      var main = document.querySelector('main');
      if (!main || main.dataset.w98mo) return;
      main.dataset.w98mo = '1';
      var mo = new MutationObserver(function () {
        clearTimeout(t98);
        t98 = setTimeout(function () { try { w98Decorate(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 250);
      });
      mo.observe(main, { childList: true, subtree: true });
      try { w98Decorate(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    }
    if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
      var __sa98 = startApp;
      startApp = async function () {
        await __sa98.apply(this, arguments);
        try { arm(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      };
    }
  })();
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v99 project pages show every team's tasks (07/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   Bug (Steel, 07/08/26): a named project's page only listed tasks from
   the project's HOME department, because wave27's loader filtered
   .eq('department', dept) before filtering by project. Cross-team
   tasks (a marketing post for the Community Kitchen Pilot, HR's
   rental assessment) were counted on the index cards but invisible on
   the project page itself. Since the round-nine routing pass made
   cross-team assignment the norm, this showed up everywhere.
   Fix: wa27RenderProject is REPLACED (it is a global function): named
   projects now load by project_id across ALL departments; General
   pages stay department-scoped (that is their meaning). Task rows gain
   a small team chip using the department colour vars so cross-team
   tasks are recognisable, mirroring the index cards. Checkbox, move
   link, add-a-task and edit-project behaviours are identical.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa99) return; window.__wa99 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.w99dep{display:inline-block;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;border-radius:6px;padding:1px 7px;margin-left:6px;color:#FFFAF2;vertical-align:1px;white-space:nowrap;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var DEPT_COL = {
    opsmtl: 'var(--c-opsmtl,#103F39)', opsto: 'var(--c-opsto,#3a6ea5)',
    marketing: 'var(--c-mkt,#6F64AB)', fundraising: 'var(--c-fund,#c87a2f)',
    hr: 'var(--c-hr,#b3402f)', board: 'var(--c-board,#4a4f57)'
  };
  function w99DeptChip(t, homeDept) {
    if (!t.department || t.department === homeDept) return '';
    var label = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[t.department]) || t.department;
    return '<span class="w99dep" style="background:' + (DEPT_COL[t.department] || '#797773') + '">' + esc(label) + '</span>';
  }

  if (typeof wa27RenderProject === 'function') {
    wa27RenderProject = async function (ref, deptForGeneral) {
      var sec = document.getElementById('projectpage');
      if (!sec) return;
      await wa27Load(false);
      var isGen = ref === 'general';
      var p = isGen ? null : wa27Find(ref);
      var dept = isGen ? deptForGeneral : (p ? p.department : null);
      if (!dept) { sec.innerHTML = '<div class="card"><p class="read">Project not found.</p></div>'; return; }
      WA27_VIEW = { ref: ref, dept: dept };
      var label = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[dept]) || dept;
      /* THE FIX: named projects load across every department */
      var q = db.from('dept_tasks').select('*').order('done').order('id', { ascending: false });
      if (isGen) q = q.eq('department', dept).is('project_id', null);
      else q = q.eq('project_id', p.id);
      var r = await q;
      var tasks = (r.data) || [];
      if (isGen) tasks = tasks.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; });
      var open = tasks.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; });
      var done = tasks.filter(function (t) { return t.done; });
      var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
      var g = p && p.goal_id ? wa27Goal(p.goal_id) : null;

      function row(t) {
        return '<div class="w27-task' + (t.done ? ' done' : '') + '">'
          + '<input type="checkbox" data-w27t="' + t.id + '"' + (t.done ? ' checked' : '') + (staff ? '' : ' disabled') + ' />'
          + '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><span class="tt">' + esc(t.title || '') + '</span>' + w99DeptChip(t, dept)
          + '<div class="meta">' + esc(t.owner || 'unassigned')
          + (t.due_date ? ' &middot; due ' + esc(t.due_date) : '')
          + (t.flag ? ' &middot; flagged' : '')
          + (staff ? ' &middot; <a class="mv" data-w27mv="' + t.id + '">move</a>' : '')
          + '</div></div></div>';
      }

      var crossCount = open.filter(function (t) { return t.department && t.department !== dept; }).length;
      /* teams involved summary (parity with the index cards) */
      var byTeam = {};
      open.forEach(function (t) { var d2 = t.department || dept; byTeam[d2] = (byTeam[d2] || 0) + 1; });
      var teamLine = Object.keys(byTeam).sort(function (a, b) { return byTeam[b] - byTeam[a]; }).map(function (d2) {
        var l2 = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d2]) || d2;
        return '<span class="w99dep" style="background:' + (DEPT_COL[d2] || '#797773') + ';margin-left:0">' + esc(l2) + ' ' + byTeam[d2] + '</span>';
      }).join(' ');
      var html = '<div class="card">'
        + '<button class="vl-back" data-w27back="' + dept + '">&larr; ' + esc(label) + '</button>'
        + '<h2 style="color:#103F39">' + esc(isGen ? 'General tasks' : wa27Name(p)) + '<span class="deptchip" style="background:#103F39">' + esc(label) + '</span></h2>'
        + (isGen
          ? '<p class="w27-desc">Open tasks with no project yet. Move one into a project with its move link.</p>'
          : '<p class="w27-desc">' + esc(p.description || 'No description yet.') + (staff ? ' <a class="mv" data-w27edit="' + p.id + '" style="color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline">edit project</a>' : '') + '</p>')
        + (g ? '<div class="w27-goal">Rolls up to the goal: <b>' + esc(g.title) + '</b>' + (g.status ? ' <span style="opacity:.7">(' + esc(g.status) + ')</span>' : '') + '</div>' : '')
        + (!isGen && open.length ? '<div style="margin:2px 0 10px;font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773)">Teams involved: ' + teamLine + '</div>' : '')
        + '<h3 class="block">Open (' + open.length + ')</h3>'
        + (crossCount ? '<p class="read" style="margin:0 0 8px">Tasks from every team working on this project. Chips mark tasks owned outside ' + esc(label) + '.</p>' : '')
        + (open.length ? open.map(row).join('') : '<p class="read">Nothing open here right now.</p>')
        + (staff ? '<div class="add-row" style="margin-top:10px"><input type="text" id="w27nt" placeholder="Add a task to ' + esc(isGen ? 'General' : p.name) + '" style="max-width:340px;padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);border-radius:8px;font-size:13px" /> <button class="btn mini" id="w27ntgo">Add</button></div>' : '')
        + (done.length ? '<details class="completed" style="margin-top:14px"><summary>Completed (' + done.length + ')</summary>' + done.map(row).join('') + '</details>' : '')
        + '</div>';
      sec.innerHTML = html;
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v100 project reports + names open profiles (07/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   1. Every named project page gets a "Download report" button (staff):
      the project-report fn writes a grounded narrative (where things
      stand, what each department needs to deliver, who is doing what,
      suggested next steps, watch-outs) from the live tasks; the client
      composes a self-contained HTML document with the full task list
      and downloads it. Nothing stored server-side.
   2. Names open profiles: assignee chips (.as-chip) and initials
      bubbles (.wa21-bub) anywhere become clickable and open the
      wave33 person page (#person/<id>): role, the AI job description,
      open plate and finished work. Staff only (the person route is
      staff-gated already). Resolution: exact roster name, else unique
      first name; no match, no navigation.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa100) return; window.__wa100 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.as-chip,.wa21-bub{cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.as-chip:hover{box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(16,63,57,.3);}' +
    '.wa21-bub:hover{box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(16,63,57,.45);}' +
    '#w100btn{margin-left:8px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- 2. names open profiles ---------- */
  async function w100Members() {
    if (window.__w100mem && (Date.now() - window.__w100memAt) < 300000) return window.__w100mem;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('team_members').select('id, name, active').eq('active', true);
      window.__w100mem = (r && r.data) || [];
      window.__w100memAt = Date.now();
    } catch (e) { window.__w100mem = window.__w100mem || []; }
    return window.__w100mem;
  }
  async function w100Resolve(name) {
    if (!name) return null;
    var rows = await w100Members();
    var lower = String(name).toLowerCase().trim();
    var hit = rows.filter(function (m) { return (m.name || '').toLowerCase() === lower; })[0];
    if (hit) return hit.id;
    var first = lower.split(' ')[0];
    var firsts = rows.filter(function (m) { return (m.name || '').toLowerCase().split(' ')[0] === first; });
    return firsts.length === 1 ? firsts[0].id : null;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var el = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.as-chip, .wa21-bub');
    if (!el) return;
    if (el.closest('.picker-chips')) return; /* assignee pickers keep their behaviour */
    if (typeof isStaff !== 'function' || !isStaff()) return;
    var name = el.classList.contains('wa21-bub') ? (el.getAttribute('title') || '') : (el.textContent || '');
    var id = await w100Resolve(name);
    if (id) { e.stopPropagation(); location.hash = '#person/' + id; }
  }, true);

  /* ---------- 1. project report download ---------- */
  function w100Esc(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;'); }
  function w100Date() {
    var d = new Date();
    return ('0' + d.getDate()).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + String(d.getFullYear()).slice(-2);
  }
  function w100Doc(name, data) {
    var rep = data.report || {};
    var label = function (d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; };
    var open = data.open_tasks || [];
    var doneRows = data.recently_completed || [];
    var deptSecs = (rep.departments || []).map(function (d) {
      return '<h3>' + w100Esc(label(d.department) || d.department) + '</h3>' +
        '<p><b>Owns:</b> ' + w100Esc(d.what_they_own) + '</p>' +
        '<p><b>Needed next:</b> ' + w100Esc(d.needed_next) + '</p>';
    }).join('');
    var people = (rep.people || []).map(function (p) {
      return '<li><b>' + w100Esc(p.name) + '</b>: ' + w100Esc(p.focus) + '</li>';
    }).join('');
    var steps = (rep.next_steps || []).map(function (s) { return '<li>' + w100Esc(s) + '</li>'; }).join('');
    var risks = (rep.watch_outs || []).map(function (s) { return '<li>' + w100Esc(s) + '</li>'; }).join('');
    var taskRow = function (t) {
      return '<tr><td>' + w100Esc(t.title) + '</td><td>' + w100Esc(label(t.department)) + '</td><td>' + w100Esc(t.owner || 'unassigned') + '</td><td>' + w100Esc(t.due_date || '') + '</td></tr>';
    };
    return '<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>' + w100Esc(name) + ' report</title>' +
      '<style>body{font-family:-apple-system,"Segoe UI",Roboto,sans-serif;max-width:820px;margin:32px auto;padding:0 18px;color:#1F201E;line-height:1.55}' +
      'h1{color:#103F39}h2{color:#103F39;margin-top:26px;border-bottom:2px solid #DEE0D8;padding-bottom:4px}h3{color:#103F39;margin:14px 0 4px}' +
      'p{font-size:14px;margin:6px 0}li{font-size:14px;margin:4px 0}' +
      'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12.5px;margin:8px 0}th{text-align:left;color:#797773;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;padding:4px 8px}td{padding:5px 8px;border-top:1px solid #eee}' +
      '.meta{color:#797773;font-size:12.5px}</style></head><body>' +
      '<h1>' + w100Esc(name) + '</h1>' +
      '<p class="meta">Project report, generated ' + w100Esc(w100Date().replace(/-/g, '/')) + ' from live Hub data. Suggested next steps are suggestions, not decisions.</p>' +
      '<h2>Where things stand</h2><p>' + w100Esc(rep.overview || '') + '</p>' +
      '<h2>What each department needs to deliver</h2>' + (deptSecs || '<p>No department breakdown.</p>') +
      '<h2>Who is doing what</h2><ul>' + (people || '<li>No task owners yet.</li>') + '</ul>' +
      '<h2>Suggested next steps</h2><ul>' + (steps || '<li>None suggested.</li>') + '</ul>' +
      '<h2>Watch-outs</h2><ul>' + (risks || '<li>None flagged.</li>') + '</ul>' +
      '<h2>Open tasks (' + open.length + ')</h2>' +
      '<table><thead><tr><th>Task</th><th>Team</th><th>Owner</th><th>Due</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + open.map(taskRow).join('') + '</tbody></table>' +
      (doneRows.length ? '<h2>Completed in the last 45 days (' + doneRows.length + ')</h2>' +
        '<table><thead><tr><th>Task</th><th>Team</th><th>Owner</th><th>Due</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + doneRows.map(taskRow).join('') + '</tbody></table>' : '') +
      '</body></html>';
  }
  async function w100Download(p) {
    var btn = document.getElementById('w100btn');
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Writing the report...'; }
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('project-report', { body: { project_id: p.id } });
      if (r.error || !r.data || r.data.error) throw (r.error || r.data.error);
      var html = w100Doc(p.name, r.data);
      var blob = new Blob([html], { type: 'text/html' });
      var a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      a.download = w100Date() + ' - ' + String(p.name).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9 ]/g, '') + ' report.html';
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      setTimeout(function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); a.remove(); }, 4000);
      toast('Report downloaded');
    } catch (e) { toast('Could not build the report'); }
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Download report'; }
  }
  if (typeof wa27RenderProject === 'function') {
    var __rp100 = wa27RenderProject;
    wa27RenderProject = async function (ref, deptForGeneral) {
      await __rp100.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        if (ref === 'general') return;
        if (typeof isStaff !== 'function' || !isStaff()) return;
        var p = wa27Find(ref);
        if (!p) return;
        var h2 = document.querySelector('#projectpage .card h2');
        if (!h2 || document.getElementById('w100btn')) return;
        var b = document.createElement('button');
        b.className = 'btn ghost mini';
        b.id = 'w100btn';
        b.textContent = 'Download report';
        b.addEventListener('click', function () { w100Download(p); });
        h2.appendChild(b);
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v101 send a project to Slack (07/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   Every named project page gets a "Send to Slack" button (staff): pick
   any channel from the live workspace list, add an optional note, see
   the full message before it goes. The message leads with the project
   name hyperlinked to its Hub page, then the open tasks grouped by
   person with real Slack @mentions (project-slack fn resolves names via
   the wa_slack_names cache; Ian pinned to his welfareavenue.com
   account). Until the Slack app gains the chat:write permission, sends
   land in the Hub notifications channel with a clear explanation; the
   channel picker lights up fully the moment the scope is added, with no
   code change. Every send is change_logged server-side.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa101) return; window.__wa101 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w101btn{margin-left:8px;}' +
    '#w101prev{background:#f8f9fb;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;margin:10px 0;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.5;white-space:pre-wrap;max-height:300px;overflow-y:auto;color:#103F39;}' +
    '#w101warn{font-size:12px;color:#a06f00;margin:6px 0;}' +
    '#w101warn.bad{color:#c4402a;}' +
    '.w101lbl{display:block;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:#103F39;margin:10px 0 4px;}' +
    '#w101ch,#w101note{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var W101_PREV = ''; /* display text without the note, note is spliced in live */

  function w101Render() {
    var box = document.getElementById('w101prev');
    if (!box) return;
    if (!W101_PREV) { box.textContent = 'Building the preview...'; return; }
    var note = (document.getElementById('w101note') || {}).value || '';
    var lines = W101_PREV.split('\n');
    if (note.trim()) lines.splice(1, 0, note.trim());
    box.textContent = lines.join('\n');
  }

  async function w101Open(p) {
    openModal(
      '<h3>Send to Slack</h3>' +
      '<p class="read" style="margin:4px 0 8px">Posts the open tasks for <b>' + esc(p.name) + '</b> to a channel, with the project title linking back to this page and each person @mentioned on their tasks.</p>' +
      '<label class="w101lbl" for="w101ch">Channel</label>' +
      '<select id="w101ch"><option value="">Loading channels...</option></select>' +
      '<label class="w101lbl" for="w101note">Optional note (appears under the title)</label>' +
      '<input type="text" id="w101note" maxlength="600" placeholder="Example: quick status push before Saturday" />' +
      '<div id="w101prev">Building the preview...</div>' +
      '<div id="w101warn"></div>' +
      '<div style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn" id="w101send" disabled>Send to Slack</button> ' +
      '<button class="btn ghost" id="w101cancel">Cancel</button></div>'
    );
    W101_PREV = '';
    document.getElementById('w101cancel').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w101note').addEventListener('input', w101Render);

    var sel = document.getElementById('w101ch');
    var warn = document.getElementById('w101warn');
    var sendBtn = document.getElementById('w101send');

    /* channels and preview load together */
    var chP = db.functions.invoke('project-slack', { body: { action: 'channels' } });
    var pvP = db.functions.invoke('project-slack', { body: { action: 'preview', project_id: p.id } });

    try {
      var ch = await chP;
      var list = (ch && ch.data && ch.data.channels) || [];
      if (ch && (ch.error || (ch.data && ch.data.error)) || !list.length) {
        sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Could not load channels</option>';
      } else {
        var saved = null;
        try { saved = localStorage.getItem('wa101.ch.' + p.id) || localStorage.getItem('wa101.ch'); } catch (e) { /* private mode */ }
        sel.innerHTML = list.map(function (c) {
          var label = '#' + c.name + (c.is_member ? '' : (c.is_private ? ' (private: invite @wa_hub first)' : ' (joins automatically)'));
          return '<option value="' + esc(c.id) + '" data-name="' + esc(c.name) + '"' + (c.id === saved ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(label) + '</option>';
        }).join('');
        sendBtn.disabled = false;
      }
    } catch (e) { sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Could not load channels</option>'; }

    try {
      var pv = await pvP;
      if (pv && pv.data && pv.data.text_display) {
        W101_PREV = pv.data.text_display;
        w101Render();
        var un = pv.data.unresolved || [];
        if (un.length) warn.textContent = 'No Slack account matched: ' + un.join(', ') + '. They appear as plain names, not @mentions.';
      } else {
        document.getElementById('w101prev').textContent = 'Could not build the preview' + (pv && pv.data && pv.data.error ? ': ' + pv.data.error : '.');
      }
    } catch (e) { document.getElementById('w101prev').textContent = 'Could not build the preview.'; }

    sendBtn.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var opt = sel.options[sel.selectedIndex];
      if (!opt || !opt.value) { warn.className = 'bad'; warn.id = 'w101warn'; warn.textContent = 'Pick a channel first.'; return; }
      sendBtn.disabled = true; sendBtn.textContent = 'Sending...';
      warn.classList.remove('bad'); warn.textContent = '';
      try {
        var r = await db.functions.invoke('project-slack', {
          body: {
            action: 'send', project_id: p.id, channel_id: opt.value,
            channel_name: opt.getAttribute('data-name') || opt.value,
            note: (document.getElementById('w101note').value || '').trim()
          }
        });
        var d = (r && r.data) || {};
        if (d.outcome === 'sent') {
          try { localStorage.setItem('wa101.ch.' + p.id, opt.value); localStorage.setItem('wa101.ch', opt.value); } catch (e) { /* private mode */ }
          toast('Posted to #' + (d.channel || 'Slack'));
          closeModal();
        } else if (d.outcome === 'sent_webhook') {
          toast('Posted to the Hub notifications channel');
          warn.textContent = d.note || 'Posted to the Hub notifications channel instead.';
          sendBtn.textContent = 'Send to Slack'; sendBtn.disabled = false;
        } else {
          warn.classList.add('bad');
          warn.textContent = d.error || (r && r.error && 'Could not send.') || 'Could not send.';
          sendBtn.textContent = 'Send to Slack'; sendBtn.disabled = false;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        warn.classList.add('bad'); warn.textContent = 'Could not send.';
        sendBtn.textContent = 'Send to Slack'; sendBtn.disabled = false;
      }
    });
  }

  if (typeof wa27RenderProject === 'function') {
    var __rp101 = wa27RenderProject;
    wa27RenderProject = async function (ref, deptForGeneral) {
      await __rp101.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        if (ref === 'general') return;
        if (typeof isStaff !== 'function' || !isStaff()) return;
        var p = wa27Find(ref);
        if (!p) return;
        var h2 = document.querySelector('#projectpage .card h2');
        if (!h2 || document.getElementById('w101btn')) return;
        var b = document.createElement('button');
        b.className = 'btn ghost mini';
        b.id = 'w101btn';
        b.textContent = 'Send to Slack';
        b.addEventListener('click', function () { w101Open(p); });
        h2.appendChild(b);
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
