/* =====================================================================
   WA v103 scope of work from check-ins (07/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (07/08/26): "pulling information from the check-in-out channels
   for HR to see the scope of everyone's work". The checkin-scope edge fn
   reads #check-in-out-wa (last 30 days) every Monday and writes one row
   per person into wa_checkin_scope (staff-readable): check-in counts,
   days active, first/last seen, and an AI summary of the scope of their
   work (facts only, no performance judgements). This wave renders that
   table as a card in the HR centre with a Refresh button. Injection uses
   the wave93 MutationObserver pattern (wa46Render is IIFE-private).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa103) return; window.__wa103 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w103scope table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12px;}' +
    '#w103scope th{text-align:left;font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);padding:4px 6px;}' +
    '#w103scope td{padding:5px 6px;border-top:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.18);vertical-align:top;}' +
    '#w103scope .mut{color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);font-size:11px;}' +
    '#w103scope details{margin:2px 0 0;}' +
    '#w103scope summary{cursor:pointer;font-size:11.5px;color:#103F39;font-weight:600;}' +
    '#w103scope .sum{font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-text,#1F201E);margin:4px 0 0;line-height:1.5;max-width:640px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  async function w103Render() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w103body');
    if (!body) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('wa_checkin_scope').select('*').order('msg_count', { ascending: false });
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      if (!rows.length) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">No check-in messages found in the window yet. The pull runs every Monday, or press Refresh.</p>'; return; }
      var stamp = rows[0].updated_at ? new Date(rows[0].updated_at).toLocaleDateString('en-CA') : '';
      var html = '<table><thead><tr><th>Person</th><th>Days active</th><th>Check-ins</th><th>Last seen</th><th>Where</th><th>Scope of work</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
      rows.forEach(function (p) {
        html += '<tr><td><b>' + esc(p.name || '') + '</b></td>'
          + '<td>' + (p.days_active || 0) + '</td>'
          + '<td>' + (p.msg_count || 0) + '</td>'
          + '<td class="mut">' + esc(p.last_seen || '') + '</td>'
          + '<td class="mut">' + esc(p.channels || '') + '</td>'
          + '<td>' + (p.summary
            ? '<details><summary>What they worked on</summary><div class="sum">' + esc(p.summary) + '</div></details>'
            : '<span class="mut">Too few messages to summarise.</span>') + '</td></tr>';
      });
      html += '</tbody></table>';
      html += '<p class="mut" style="margin-top:8px">Source: the #check-in-out channels (one per department), last ' + (rows[0].window_days || 30) + ' days' + (stamp ? ', refreshed ' + stamp : '') + '. Summaries describe scope only, never performance.</p>';
      body.innerHTML = html;
    } catch (e) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not load the check-in scope table.</p>'; }
  }

  async function w103Card() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel || document.getElementById('w103scope')) return;
    if (typeof isStaff !== 'function' || !isStaff()) return;
    /* the HR centre has its own access list (wave52): if this person got
       the denied card, they do not get HR data folds either */
    if (!panel.children.length || panel.querySelector('#w52denied')) return;
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'card';
    card.id = 'w103scope';
    card.innerHTML = '<h2>Scope of work from check-ins <button class="btn ghost mini" id="w103ref">Refresh</button></h2>'
      + '<p class="read">What everyone has been working on, read from the per-department check-in channels. Updates automatically every Monday morning.</p>'
      + '<div id="w103body">Loading...</div>';
    panel.appendChild(card);
    document.getElementById('w103ref').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var b = this; b.disabled = true; b.textContent = 'Reading the channel...';
      try {
        var res = await db.functions.invoke('checkin-scope', { body: {} });
        if (res.error || (res.data && res.data.error)) throw (res.error || res.data.error);
        toast('Check-in scope refreshed');
        await w103Render();
      } catch (e) { toast('Refresh failed'); }
      b.disabled = false; b.textContent = 'Refresh';
    });
    w103Render();
  }

  (function () {
    var t103 = null;
    function arm() {
      var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
      if (!panel || panel.dataset.w103mo) return;
      panel.dataset.w103mo = '1';
      var mo = new MutationObserver(function () {
        clearTimeout(t103);
        t103 = setTimeout(function () { try { w103Card(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 300);
      });
      mo.observe(panel, { childList: true });
      if (panel.children.length) { try { w103Card(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
    }
    if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
      var __sa103 = startApp;
      startApp = async function () {
        await __sa103.apply(this, arguments);
        try { arm(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      };
    }
  })();
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v104 project leads, progress bars, contributors (07/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (07/08/26): "assign project lead(s) to each project... add
   progress bars to each project page (tasks complete on all tasks), as
   well as all the contributors to the project".
   Named project pages gain a status strip under the description:
   1. Progress bar: completed / all tasks on the project, every team.
   2. Leads: projects.leads (jsonb list of roster names) shown as chips
      (clickable to their profile via wave100); staff edit them in a
      roster-picker modal; changes are change_logged.
   3. Contributors: everyone with a task on the project (open or done),
      as clickable chips with open-task counts, sorted busiest first.
   Implemented by wrapping the CURRENT wa27RenderProject binding (wave99
   replaced it; 100/101 wrapped it; this wraps again) and injecting after
   the description, so every existing control stays untouched.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa104) return; window.__wa104 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w104{margin:10px 0 14px;padding:12px 14px;background:#fbfbfd;border:1px solid #eef0f4;border-radius:10px;}' +
    '#w104 .row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:7px 0;}' +
    '#w104 .lbl{font-size:10.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;font-weight:700;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);min-width:96px;}' +
    '.w104track{flex:1;min-width:160px;max-width:420px;height:8px;background:#DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;overflow:hidden;}' +
    '.w104fill{height:100%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#103F39,#103F39);border-radius:999px;transition:width .4s;}' +
    '.w104pct{font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;white-space:nowrap;}' +
    '.w104done .w104fill{background:linear-gradient(90deg,#103F39,#103F39);}' +
    '#w104 .as-chip{margin:2px 4px 2px 0;}' +
    '.w104lead{background:rgba(255,171,0,.16)!important;color:#7a5800!important;border:1px solid rgba(255,171,0,.35);}' +
    '.w104lead:before{content:"\\2605  ";font-size:10px;}' +
    '.w104n{font-size:10.5px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);margin-left:3px;}' +
    '.w104edit{font-size:11.5px;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;}' +
    '#w104pick{max-height:260px;overflow-y:auto;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 10px;margin:8px 0;}' +
    '#w104pick label{display:block;font-size:13px;padding:3px 2px;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '#w104pick input{margin-right:7px;accent-color:#103F39;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  function w104Names(v) {
    if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.filter(Boolean).map(String);
    if (typeof v === 'string' && v.trim()) { try { var a = JSON.parse(v); return Array.isArray(a) ? a.filter(Boolean).map(String) : []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
    return [];
  }

  async function w104EditLeads(p, current) {
    var roster = [];
    try {
      var r = await db.from('team_members').select('id, name').eq('active', true).order('name');
      roster = (r && r.data) || [];
    } catch (e) { /* fall through to empty */ }
    var cur = {};
    current.forEach(function (n) { cur[n.toLowerCase()] = 1; });
    openModal(
      '<h3>Project leads: ' + esc(p.name) + '</h3>' +
      '<p class="read" style="margin:4px 0 6px">Pick one or more leads. They show at the top of the project page.</p>' +
      '<input type="text" id="w104filter" placeholder="Filter people..." style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;margin:2px 0 0" />' +
      '<div id="w104pick">' + roster.map(function (m) {
        var on = cur[(m.name || '').toLowerCase()] ? ' checked' : '';
        return '<label data-n="' + esc((m.name || '').toLowerCase()) + '"><input type="checkbox" value="' + esc(m.name) + '"' + on + ' />' + esc(m.name) + '</label>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn" id="w104save">Save leads</button> ' +
      '<button class="btn ghost" id="w104cancel">Cancel</button></div>'
    );
    document.getElementById('w104filter').addEventListener('input', function () {
      var q = this.value.toLowerCase();
      document.querySelectorAll('#w104pick label').forEach(function (l) {
        l.style.display = (l.getAttribute('data-n') || '').indexOf(q) >= 0 ? '' : 'none';
      });
    });
    document.getElementById('w104cancel').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w104save').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var picked = [];
      document.querySelectorAll('#w104pick input:checked').forEach(function (i) { picked.push(i.value); });
      var b = this; b.disabled = true; b.textContent = 'Saving...';
      try {
        var res = await db.from('projects').update({ leads: picked }).eq('id', p.id);
        if (res.error) throw res.error;
        try {
          await db.from('change_log').insert({
            entity_type: 'project', entity_id: String(p.id), field: 'leads',
            old_value: current.join(', ') || null, new_value: picked.join(', ') || null,
            changed_by: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || 'staff'
          });
        } catch (e) { /* audit is best-effort */ }
        closeModal();
        toast(picked.length ? 'Leads saved' : 'Leads cleared');
        if (typeof wa27RenderProject === 'function' && typeof WA27_VIEW !== 'undefined' && WA27_VIEW) {
          wa27RenderProject(WA27_VIEW.ref, WA27_VIEW.dept);
        }
      } catch (e) { b.disabled = false; b.textContent = 'Save leads'; toast('Could not save leads'); }
    });
  }

  function w104Chip(name, extraClass, sub) {
    return '<span class="as-chip' + (extraClass ? ' ' + extraClass : '') + '">' + esc(name) + '</span>' + (sub ? '<span class="w104n">' + esc(sub) + '</span>' : '');
  }

  async function w104Inject(ref) {
    try {
      if (ref === 'general') return;
      var sec = document.getElementById('projectpage');
      if (!sec || document.getElementById('w104')) return;
      var card = sec.querySelector('.card');
      var desc = sec.querySelector('.w27-desc');
      if (!card || !desc) return;
      var p = wa27Find(ref);
      if (!p) return;
      var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();

      var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').select('id, owner, done').eq('project_id', p.id);
      var tasks = (r && r.data) || [];
      var total = tasks.length;
      var done = tasks.filter(function (t) { return t.done; }).length;
      var pct = total ? Math.round(done * 100 / total) : 0;

      /* contributors: everyone with a task, open counts shown */
      var contrib = {};
      tasks.forEach(function (t) {
        String(t.owner || '').split(/\s*(?:\+|&|\/|,| and )\s*/i).forEach(function (n) {
          n = n.trim();
          if (!n) return;
          var c = contrib[n] = contrib[n] || { open: 0, done: 0 };
          if (t.done) c.done++; else c.open++;
        });
      });
      var people = Object.keys(contrib).sort(function (a, b) {
        return (contrib[b].open - contrib[a].open) || (contrib[b].done - contrib[a].done) || a.localeCompare(b);
      });

      var leads = w104Names(p.leads);
      var leadHtml = leads.length
        ? leads.map(function (n) { return w104Chip(n, 'w104lead'); }).join(' ')
        : '<span class="w104n" style="margin-left:0">No lead assigned yet.</span>';
      if (staff) leadHtml += ' <span class="w104edit" id="w104el">' + (leads.length ? 'edit' : 'assign leads') + '</span>';

      var peopleHtml = people.length
        ? people.map(function (n) {
            var c = contrib[n];
            return w104Chip(n, leads.some(function (l) { return l.toLowerCase() === n.toLowerCase(); }) ? 'w104lead' : '', c.open ? c.open + ' open' : 'all done');
          }).join(' ')
        : '<span class="w104n" style="margin-left:0">No tasks assigned yet.</span>';

      var box = document.createElement('div');
      box.id = 'w104';
      box.innerHTML =
        '<div class="row' + (total && done === total ? ' w104done' : '') + '"><span class="lbl">Progress</span>' +
        '<div class="w104track"><div class="w104fill" style="width:' + pct + '%"></div></div>' +
        '<span class="w104pct">' + done + ' of ' + total + ' task' + (total === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' complete (' + pct + '%)</span></div>' +
        '<div class="row"><span class="lbl">Lead' + (leads.length > 1 ? 's' : '') + '</span><span>' + leadHtml + '</span></div>' +
        '<div class="row"><span class="lbl">Contributors</span><span>' + peopleHtml + '</span></div>';
      var goal = sec.querySelector('.w27-goal');
      (goal || desc).insertAdjacentElement('afterend', box);
      var el = document.getElementById('w104el');
      if (el) el.addEventListener('click', function () { w104EditLeads(p, leads); });
    } catch (e) { /* decorative: never break the page */ }
  }

  if (typeof wa27RenderProject === 'function') {
    var __rp104 = wa27RenderProject;
    wa27RenderProject = async function (ref, deptForGeneral) {
      await __rp104.apply(this, arguments);
      try { await w104Inject(ref); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v105 HR tools: time off, policy sign-offs, discipline (07/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   Grounded in the Agency Handbook (Section 3 time-off, Section 5
   discipline) and the Performance & Discipline Tracking doc in Drive.
   1. HR centre (access-list gated, like wave103):
      - Time off: pending requests w/ approve or decline, this-FY totals
        per person and category, and a direct log form for HR.
      - Policies and sign-offs: registry of policy docs (Drive links),
        who has signed, who has not; HR can add a policy.
      - Discipline tracker: the handbook ladders (acknowledgement,
        time commitment, missed meetings, conduct) with per-step levels,
        notes, doc links and clean-slate marking. People can see their
        OWN record (handbook: "Employees can ask to see their record").
   2. My page: "Your time off" (FY totals, own history, Request time off
      modal that also files a Hub request routed to HR) and "Policies to
      review" (open the Drive doc, then one-click sign off).
   3. The sign-off fold also appears on the Training page, so reading
      and signing the policies is part of master onboarding.
   FY runs Oct 1 to Sep 30 (Welfare Avenue fiscal year).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa105) return; window.__wa105 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.w105card table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12px;}' +
    '.w105card th{text-align:left;font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);padding:4px 6px;}' +
    '.w105card td{padding:5px 6px;border-top:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.18);vertical-align:top;}' +
    '.w105mut{color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);font-size:11px;}' +
    '.w105pend{background:rgba(255,171,0,.14);color:#a06f00;border-radius:6px;padding:1px 8px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w105ok{background:rgba(16,63,57,.14);color:#103F39;border-radius:6px;padding:1px 8px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w105no{background:rgba(255,62,29,.12);color:#c4402a;border-radius:6px;padding:1px 8px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w105form{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:flex-end;margin-top:10px;padding-top:10px;border-top:1px dashed #DEE0D8;}' +
    '.w105form label{display:block;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);margin-bottom:3px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em;}' +
    '.w105form select,.w105form input{padding:7px 10px;font-size:13px;}' +
    '.w105sign{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.35);border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;margin:8px 0;}' +
    '.w105sign b{color:#103F39;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var TO_CATS = {
    vacation: 'Vacation', school_break: 'School break', sick: 'Mental health / sick',
    bereavement: 'Bereavement', religious: 'Religious holiday', wedding: 'Wedding', other: 'Other'
  };
  var TO_POLICY = {
    vacation: '4 weeks in years 0-1, 5 weeks in years 1-2, 6 weeks in years 3-5. Volunteering 4 times a year earns an extra week, up to two.',
    school_break: 'Full-time students only: 4 weeks a year, during exam periods, at most 1.5 weeks at a time, half commitment continues.',
    sick: '7 days total a year.', bereavement: 'Two weeks for the loss of a loved one.',
    religious: 'Days for religious holidays (Eid, Hanukkah and others).', wedding: 'Days off for your own or a close family wedding.', other: ''
  };
  var DISC = {
    ack_messages: { label: 'Acknowledgement of messages (24h)', steps: ['Verbal warning', 'Written message on Slack by HR', 'Verbal warning by HR', 'Written email warning', 'Termination'] },
    time_commitment: { label: 'Monthly time commitment', steps: ['Verbal warning', 'Written message on Slack by HR', 'Verbal warning by HR', 'Termination'] },
    missed_meetings: { label: 'Missed 2 meetings in a row', steps: ['Verbal warning', '2nd verbal warning', '1st written warning', 'Termination'] },
    conduct: { label: 'Standards of conduct (Section 4)', steps: ['1st formal warning', '2nd formal warning', '3rd formal warning / termination'] },
    other: { label: 'Other', steps: ['Verbal warning', 'Written warning', 'Final warning'] }
  };
  function w105FY() {
    var n = new Date();
    var y = n.getMonth() >= 9 ? n.getFullYear() : n.getFullYear() - 1;
    return { start: y + '-10-01', label: 'FY' + String(y + 1).slice(2) };
  }
  function w105Days(a, b) {
    var d = (new Date(b) - new Date(a)) / 86400000 + 1;
    return d > 0 ? Math.round(d * 2) / 2 : 0;
  }
  function w105Me() {
    return {
      email: ((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(),
      name: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && (USER.name || USER.email)) || 'me'
    };
  }
  async function w105IsHR() {
    if (window.__w105hr !== undefined) return window.__w105hr;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('wa_hr_access').select('email').eq('email', w105Me().email);
      window.__w105hr = !!(r.data && r.data.length);
    } catch (e) { window.__w105hr = false; }
    return window.__w105hr;
  }

  /* ============ HR centre: time off ============ */
  /* wave212 (Valex: "time off disappeared"): Time off is for the HR access list and the HR access profile (Samson), as the
     database decides (wa_can_timeoff); policies and the discipline tracker stay with the HR access list */
  async function w105CanTimeoff() {
    if (window.__w105to !== undefined) return window.__w105to;
    try { var q = await db.rpc('wa_can_timeoff'); window.__w105to = (q && !q.error && typeof q.data === 'boolean') ? q.data : await w105IsHR(); }
    catch (e) { window.__w105to = await w105IsHR(); }
    return window.__w105to;
  }
  async function w105TimeCard(panel) {
    if (document.getElementById('w105to')) return;
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'card w105card';
    card.id = 'w105to';
    card.innerHTML = '<h2>Time off</h2><p class="read">Requests come from each person\'s My page (and the Requests page); approve or decline them here. Totals count approved days in the current fiscal year (Oct 1 to Sep 30).</p><div id="w105tobody">Loading...</div>';
    panel.appendChild(card);
    await w105TimeRender();
  }
  async function w105TimeRender() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w105tobody');
    if (!body) return;
    try {
      var fy = w105FY();
      var r = await db.from('wa_timeoff').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
      var rows = (r.data) || [];
      window.__w105rows = rows;   /* wave215: the walkthroughs watch the list */
      var pend = rows.filter(function (t) { return t.status === 'requested'; });
      var html = '';
      if (pend.length) {
        html += '<h3 class="block">Waiting on a decision (' + pend.length + ')</h3><table><tbody>';
        pend.forEach(function (t) {
          html += '<tr><td><b>' + esc(t.name) + '</b>' + (t.practice ? ' <span class="w215prac">Practice</span>' : '') + '<div class="w105mut">' + esc(TO_CATS[t.category] || t.category) + (t.note ? ': ' + esc(t.note) : '') + '</div></td>'
            + '<td>' + esc(t.start_date) + ' to ' + esc(t.end_date) + '<div class="w105mut">' + t.days + ' day' + (t.days === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</div></td>'
            + '<td style="white-space:nowrap"><button class="btn mini" data-w105ap="' + t.id + '">Approve</button> <button class="btn ghost mini" data-w105de="' + t.id + '">Decline</button></td></tr>';
        });
        html += '</tbody></table>';
      } else {
        html += '<p class="read">No requests waiting.</p>';
      }
      var appr = rows.filter(function (t) { return t.status === 'approved' && !t.practice && t.start_date >= fy.start; });   /* wave215: practice never counts */
      var per = {};
      appr.forEach(function (t) {
        var p = per[t.name] = per[t.name] || {};
        p[t.category] = (p[t.category] || 0) + Number(t.days || 0);
      });
      var names = Object.keys(per).sort();
      /* wave212: the running total per person, and the mental health and sick days left of 7 */
      html += '<div class="w212toacts"><button class="btn mini" id="w212log">Log time off</button> <button class="btn ghost mini" id="w212mine">Request time off for myself</button></div>';
      if (names.length) {
        var cats = Object.keys(TO_CATS).filter(function (c) { return names.some(function (n) { return per[n][c]; }); });
        html += '<h3 class="block">Days taken this ' + fy.label + ' (approved)</h3><div class="w212towrap"><table class="w212tot"><thead><tr><th>Person</th>' + cats.map(function (c) { return '<th>' + esc(TO_CATS[c]) + '</th>'; }).join('') + '<th>Total</th><th>Sick days left</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
        names.forEach(function (n) {
          var tot = 0; Object.keys(per[n]).forEach(function (c) { tot += per[n][c]; });
          var left = 7 - (per[n].sick || 0);
          html += '<tr><td><b>' + esc(n) + '</b></td>' + cats.map(function (c) { return '<td>' + (per[n][c] || '') + '</td>'; }).join('') + '<td><b>' + tot + '</b></td><td' + (left <= 1 ? ' class="w212low"' : '') + '>' + left + ' of 7</td></tr>';
        });
        html += '</tbody></table></div>';
      } else html += '<p class="read">No approved time off yet this ' + fy.label + '.</p>';
      var past = rows.filter(function (t) { return t.status !== 'requested'; }).slice(0, 12);
      if (past.length) {
        html += '<details class="w212past"><summary>Recent decisions</summary><table><tbody>' + past.map(function (t) {
          return '<tr><td><b>' + esc(t.name) + '</b><div class="w105mut">' + esc(TO_CATS[t.category] || t.category) + '</div></td><td>' + esc(t.start_date) + ' to ' + esc(t.end_date) + ' <span class="w105mut">(' + t.days + 'd)</span></td><td>' + (t.status === 'approved' ? '<span class="w105ok">approved</span>' : '<span class="w105no">declined</span>') + (t.decline_reason ? '<div class="w105mut">' + esc(t.decline_reason) + '</div>' : '') + '</td></tr>';
        }).join('') + '</tbody></table></details>';
      }
      html += '<p class="w105mut" style="margin:6px 0 0">Handbook: vacation 4 to 6 weeks by tenure (plus volunteer bonus weeks), 7 mental health and sick days, 2 weeks bereavement. All time off is mutually exclusive. New requests are sent to Valex and Samson on Slack.</p>';
      body.innerHTML = html;
      body.querySelectorAll('[data-w105ap],[data-w105de]').forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener('click', async function () {
          var ap = this.hasAttribute('data-w105ap');
          var id = Number(this.getAttribute(ap ? 'data-w105ap' : 'data-w105de'));
          var patch = { status: ap ? 'approved' : 'declined', decided_by: w105Me().email, decided_at: new Date().toISOString() };
          if (!ap) { var why = window.prompt('Why is it declined? (they will see this)'); if (why === null) return; why = String(why).trim(); if (!why) { toast('Say why it is declined'); return; } patch.decline_reason = why.slice(0, 300); }   /* wave212 */
          this.disabled = true;
          var u = await db.from('wa_timeoff').update(patch).eq('id', id);
          if (u.error) { toast('Could not save the decision'); this.disabled = false; return; }
          toast(ap ? 'Approved' : 'Declined');
          w105TimeRender();
        });
      });
      var lg = document.getElementById('w212log'); if (lg) lg.addEventListener('click', function () { w105LogModal(); });
      var mn = document.getElementById('w212mine'); if (mn) mn.addEventListener('click', function () { w105ReqModal(); });
    } catch (e) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not load time off.</p>'; }
  }

  /* wave212 (Valex, 06/10/26: the Log time off button only moved the cursor): a proper form, for anyone on the team */
  async function w105LogModal() {
    var ppl = [];
    try { var rr = await db.from('team_members').select('name, email').eq('active', true).order('name'); ppl = (rr.data) || []; } catch (e) { ppl = []; }
    openModal(
      '<h3>Log time off</h3>' +
      '<p class="read" style="margin:4px 0 8px">For time off agreed outside the Hub (a conversation, an email), or a request made for someone.</p>' +
      '<label class="w101lbl">Person</label><select id="w212lp" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box"><option value="">Choose who</option>' + ppl.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + esc((m.email || '').toLowerCase()) + '" data-name="' + esc(m.name) + '">' + esc(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<label class="w101lbl">Type</label><select id="w212lc" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box">' + Object.keys(TO_CATS).map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + c + '">' + TO_CATS[c] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<div id="w212lpol" class="w105mut" style="margin:4px 0 6px">' + esc(TO_POLICY.vacation) + '</div>' +
      '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px"><div><label class="w101lbl">From</label><input type="date" id="w212ls" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" /></div><div><label class="w101lbl">To</label><input type="date" id="w212le" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" /></div></div>' +
      '<p class="w105mut" id="w212ldays" style="margin:4px 0 0"></p>' +
      '<label class="w101lbl">Note (optional)</label><input type="text" id="w212ln" maxlength="300" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
      '<label class="w101lbl">Status</label><select id="w212lst" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box"><option value="approved">Approved (already agreed)</option><option value="requested">A request to decide</option></select>' +
      '<div style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn" id="w212lgo">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w212lx">Cancel</button></div>'
    );
    var days = function () { var s = document.getElementById('w212ls').value, e = document.getElementById('w212le').value, o = document.getElementById('w212ldays'); o.textContent = s && e && e >= s ? w105Days(s, e) + ' day' + (w105Days(s, e) === 1 ? '' : 's') : ''; };
    document.getElementById('w212ls').addEventListener('change', function () { var e = document.getElementById('w212le'); if (!e.value || e.value < this.value) e.value = this.value; days(); });
    document.getElementById('w212le').addEventListener('change', days);
    document.getElementById('w212lc').addEventListener('change', function () { document.getElementById('w212lpol').textContent = TO_POLICY[this.value] || ''; });
    document.getElementById('w212lx').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w212lgo').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var sel = document.getElementById('w212lp'), opt = sel.options[sel.selectedIndex];
      var s = document.getElementById('w212ls').value, e = document.getElementById('w212le').value;
      if (!sel.value) { toast('Choose who'); return; }
      if (!s || !e || e < s) { toast('Pick valid dates'); return; }
      var st = document.getElementById('w212lst').value, n = document.getElementById('w212ln').value.trim();
      var row = { email: sel.value, name: opt.getAttribute('data-name') || 'unknown', category: document.getElementById('w212lc').value || 'other', start_date: s, end_date: e, days: w105Days(s, e), status: st, note: n || null };
      if (st === 'approved') { row.decided_by = w105Me().email; row.decided_at = new Date().toISOString(); }
      if (window.w215 && w215.practice()) row.practice = true;
      this.disabled = true;
      var ins = await db.from('wa_timeoff').insert(row);
      if (ins.error) { this.disabled = false; toast('Could not save it'); return; }
      closeModal(); toast(st === 'approved' ? 'Time off logged' : 'Request added'); w105TimeRender();
    });
  }
  window.w105LogModal = w105LogModal;
  window.w105TimeRender = w105TimeRender;

  /* ============ HR centre: policies and sign-offs ============ */
  async function w105PolCard(panel) {
    if (document.getElementById('w105pol')) return;
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'card w105card';
    card.id = 'w105pol';
    card.innerHTML = '<h2>Policies and sign-offs</h2><p class="read">Each policy links to its document in Google Drive. Everyone signs off on required policies from their My page (part of onboarding); this table shows who has and who has not.</p><div id="w105polbody">Loading...</div>';
    panel.appendChild(card);
    try {
      var res = await Promise.all([
        db.from('wa_policies').select('*').eq('active', true).order('sort'),
        db.from('wa_policy_signoffs').select('email, name, policy_key, signed_at'),
        db.from('team_members').select('name, email').eq('active', true)
      ]);
      var pols = (res[0].data) || [];
      var signs = (res[1].data) || [];
      var roster = ((res[2].data) || []).filter(function (m) { return m.email; });
      var html = '<table><thead><tr><th>Policy</th><th>Signed</th><th>Not yet signed</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
      pols.forEach(function (p) {
        var got = signs.filter(function (s) { return s.policy_key === p.key; });
        var gotEmails = {};
        got.forEach(function (s) { gotEmails[s.email] = 1; });
        var missing = p.required ? roster.filter(function (m) { return !gotEmails[(m.email || '').toLowerCase()]; }).map(function (m) { return m.name; }) : [];
        html += '<tr><td><a href="' + esc(p.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><b>' + esc(p.title) + '</b></a>' + (p.required ? '' : ' <span class="w105mut">(reference only)</span>') + ' <span class="w104edit" data-w105pe="' + esc(p.key) + '" data-t="' + esc(p.title) + '" data-u="' + esc(p.url) + '">edit</span></td>'
          + '<td><span class="w105ok">' + got.length + '</span></td>'
          + '<td class="w105mut">' + (p.required ? (missing.length ? esc(missing.slice(0, 12).join(', ')) + (missing.length > 12 ? ' and ' + (missing.length - 12) + ' more' : '') : 'Everyone with a Hub account has signed.') : '') + '</td></tr>';
      });
      html += '</tbody></table>';
      html += '<div class="w105form"><div><label>Title</label><input type="text" id="w105pt" placeholder="New policy name" style="min-width:200px" /></div>'
        + '<div><label>Drive link</label><input type="text" id="w105pu" placeholder="https://docs.google.com/..." style="min-width:240px" /></div>'
        + '<div><button class="btn mini" id="w105pgo">Add policy</button></div></div>';
      document.getElementById('w105polbody').innerHTML = html;
      card.querySelectorAll('[data-w105pe]').forEach(function (el) {
        el.addEventListener('click', function () {
          var key = this.getAttribute('data-w105pe');
          openModal(
            '<h3>Edit policy</h3>' +
            '<label class="w101lbl">Title</label><input type="text" id="w105et" value="' + esc(this.getAttribute('data-t')) + '" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
            '<label class="w101lbl">Drive link</label><input type="text" id="w105eu" value="' + esc(this.getAttribute('data-u')) + '" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
            '<div style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn" id="w105ego">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w105ex">Cancel</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w105eret" style="float:right">Retire policy</button></div>'
          );
          document.getElementById('w105ex').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
          document.getElementById('w105ego').addEventListener('click', async function () {
            var t = (document.getElementById('w105et') || {}).value || '', u = (document.getElementById('w105eu') || {}).value || '';
            if (t.length < 3 || u.indexOf('http') !== 0) { toast('Keep a name and a valid link'); return; }
            var up = await db.from('wa_policies').update({ title: t, url: u }).eq('key', key);
            if (up.error) { toast('Could not save'); return; }
            closeModal(); toast('Policy updated');
            card.remove(); w105PolCard(panel);
          });
          document.getElementById('w105eret').addEventListener('click', async function () {
            var up = await db.from('wa_policies').update({ active: false }).eq('key', key);
            if (up.error) { toast('Could not retire it'); return; }
            closeModal(); toast('Policy retired (sign-offs kept)');
            card.remove(); w105PolCard(panel);
          });
        });
      });
      document.getElementById('w105pgo').addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var t = (document.getElementById('w105pt') || {}).value || '', u = (document.getElementById('w105pu') || {}).value || '';
        if (t.length < 3 || u.indexOf('http') !== 0) { toast('Give the policy a name and a link'); return; }
        var key = t.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '').slice(0, 40);
        var ins = await db.from('wa_policies').insert({ key: key, title: t, url: u, required: true, sort: 50 });
        if (ins.error) { toast('Could not add it'); return; }
        toast('Policy added; sign-offs start collecting');
        card.remove();
        w105PolCard(panel);
      });
    } catch (e) { document.getElementById('w105polbody').innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not load policies.</p>'; }
  }

  /* ============ HR centre: discipline tracker ============ */
  async function w105DiscCard(panel) {
    if (document.getElementById('w105disc')) return;
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'card w105card';
    card.id = 'w105disc';
    card.innerHTML = '<h2>Discipline tracker</h2><p class="read">Progressive discipline per the handbook (Section 5) and the Performance and Discipline Tracking doc. Every entry is dated and attributed; people can see their own record. A clean slate closes out a category for a person.</p><div id="w105dbody">Loading...</div>';
    panel.appendChild(card);
    await w105DiscRender();
  }
  async function w105DiscRender() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w105dbody');
    if (!body) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('wa_discipline').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
      var rows = (r.data) || [];
      var byP = {};
      rows.forEach(function (d) { (byP[d.name] = byP[d.name] || []).push(d); });
      var html = '';
      var names = Object.keys(byP).sort();
      if (!names.length) html += '<p class="read">Clean record across the team. Nothing tracked yet.</p>';
      names.forEach(function (n) {
        var open = byP[n].filter(function (d) { return !d.clean_slate; });
        html += '<h3 class="block">' + esc(n) + (open.length ? '' : ' <span class="w105ok">clean slate</span>') + '</h3><table><tbody>';
        byP[n].slice(0, 8).forEach(function (d) {
          html += '<tr><td style="white-space:nowrap" class="w105mut">' + esc(d.incident_date) + '</td>'
            + '<td>' + esc((DISC[d.category] || {}).label || d.category) + ': <b>' + esc(d.level) + '</b>'
            + (d.clean_slate ? ' <span class="w105ok">clean slate applied</span>' : '')
            + (d.note ? '<div class="w105mut">' + esc(d.note) + '</div>' : '')
            + (d.doc_url ? ' <a href="' + esc(d.doc_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="w105mut">document</a>' : '') + '</td>'
            + '<td class="w105mut">' + esc((d.recorded_by || '').split('@')[0]) + '</td></tr>';
        });
        html += '</tbody></table>';
      });
      var stepOpts = function (cat) { return (DISC[cat].steps).map(function (s) { return '<option>' + s + '</option>'; }).join(''); };
      html += '<div class="w105form"><div><label>Person</label><select id="w105dp"></select></div>'
        + '<div><label>Category</label><select id="w105dc">' + Object.keys(DISC).map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + c + '">' + DISC[c].label + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>'
        + '<div><label>Step</label><select id="w105dl">' + stepOpts('ack_messages') + '</select></div>'
        + '<div><label>Date</label><input type="date" id="w105dd" /></div>'
        + '<div style="flex:1;min-width:170px"><label>Note</label><input type="text" id="w105dn" placeholder="What happened" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" /></div>'
        + '<div><button class="btn mini" id="w105dgo">Record</button> <button class="btn ghost mini" id="w105dcs">Clean slate</button></div></div>';
      body.innerHTML = html;
      try {
        var rr = await db.from('team_members').select('name, email').eq('active', true).order('name');
        var sel = document.getElementById('w105dp');
        if (sel) sel.innerHTML = ((rr.data) || []).map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + esc((m.email || '').toLowerCase()) + '" data-name="' + esc(m.name) + '">' + esc(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('');
      } catch (e) { /* roster optional */ }
      var dc = document.getElementById('w105dc');
      if (dc) dc.addEventListener('change', function () {
        document.getElementById('w105dl').innerHTML = stepOpts(this.value);
      });
      async function record(clean) {
        var sel2 = document.getElementById('w105dp');
        var opt = sel2 && sel2.options[sel2.selectedIndex];
        if (!opt) return;
        var ins = await db.from('wa_discipline').insert({
          email: opt.value || '', name: opt.getAttribute('data-name') || 'unknown',
          category: (document.getElementById('w105dc') || {}).value || 'other',
          level: clean ? 'Clean slate' : ((document.getElementById('w105dl') || {}).value || 'Verbal warning'),
          incident_date: (document.getElementById('w105dd') || {}).value || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
          note: (document.getElementById('w105dn') || {}).value || null,
          clean_slate: !!clean,
          recorded_by: w105Me().email
        });
        if (ins.error) { toast('Could not record it'); return; }
        toast(clean ? 'Clean slate recorded' : 'Recorded');
        w105DiscRender();
      }
      document.getElementById('w105dgo').addEventListener('click', function () { record(false); });
      document.getElementById('w105dcs').addEventListener('click', function () { record(true); });
    } catch (e) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not load the tracker.</p>'; }
  }

  /* ============ My page: your time off + policy sign-offs ============ */
  function w105ReqModal() {
    var me = w105Me();
    openModal(
      '<h3>Request time off</h3>' +
      '<p class="read" style="margin:4px 0 8px">Goes to HR for a decision, and shows up in the Requests flow. Remember the handbook: tell your director on Slack and set your status.</p>' +
      '<label class="w101lbl">Type</label><select id="w105rc" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box">' + Object.keys(TO_CATS).map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + c + '">' + TO_CATS[c] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<div id="w105rp" class="w105mut" style="margin:4px 0 6px">' + esc(TO_POLICY.vacation) + '</div>' +
      '<label class="w101lbl">From</label><input type="date" id="w105rs" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
      '<label class="w101lbl">To</label><input type="date" id="w105re" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
      '<label class="w101lbl">Note (optional)</label><input type="text" id="w105rn" maxlength="300" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
      '<div style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn" id="w105rgo">Send the request</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w105rx">Cancel</button></div>'
    );
    document.getElementById('w105rc').addEventListener('change', function () {
      document.getElementById('w105rp').textContent = TO_POLICY[this.value] || '';
    });
    document.getElementById('w105rx').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w105rgo').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var c = (document.getElementById('w105rc') || {}).value || 'vacation';
      var s = (document.getElementById('w105rs') || {}).value, e = (document.getElementById('w105re') || {}).value;
      var n = (document.getElementById('w105rn') || {}).value || '';
      if (!s || !e || e < s) { toast('Pick valid dates'); return; }
      this.disabled = true;
      var prac = !!(window.w215 && w215.practice());   /* wave215: a request made in a walkthrough is practice */
      var ins = await db.from('wa_timeoff').insert({ email: me.email, name: me.name, category: c, start_date: s, end_date: e, days: w105Days(s, e), status: 'requested', note: n || null, practice: prac });
      if (ins.error) { this.disabled = false; toast('Could not send it'); return; }
      if (!prac) try {
        await db.from('dept_requests').insert({
          type_key: 'time-off', department: 'hr',
          title: 'Time off: ' + me.name + ', ' + s + ' to ' + e + ' (' + (TO_CATS[c] || c) + ')',
          details: n, status: 'requested', done: false, requested_by: me.name
        });
      } catch (e2) { /* the wa_timeoff row is the source of truth */ }
      closeModal();
      toast(prac ? 'Practice request saved' : 'Request sent to HR');
      w105MyRender();
      if (document.getElementById('w105tobody')) w105TimeRender();   /* wave215: asked from the HR centre's Time off */
    });
  }
  window.w105ReqModal = w105ReqModal; /* wave123: the Requests page opens the time off form */
  async function w105MyCards() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('mypage');
    if (!panel || !panel.children.length || document.getElementById('w105my')) return;
    var me = w105Me();
    if (!me.email) return;
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'card w105card';
    card.id = 'w105my';
    card.innerHTML = '<h2>Your time off <button class="btn mini" id="w105req">Request time off</button></h2><div id="w105mybody">Loading...</div>';
    panel.appendChild(card);
    document.getElementById('w105req').addEventListener('click', w105ReqModal);
    await w105MyRender();
    await w105SignCard(panel, 'w105sign');
  }
  async function w105MyRender() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w105mybody');
    if (!body) return;
    try {
      var me = w105Me();
      var fy = w105FY();
      var r = await db.from('wa_timeoff').select('*').eq('email', me.email).order('start_date', { ascending: false });
      var rows = (r.data) || [];
      var tot = {};
      rows.filter(function (t) { return t.status === 'approved' && t.start_date >= fy.start; })
        .forEach(function (t) { tot[t.category] = (tot[t.category] || 0) + Number(t.days || 0); });
      var totLine = Object.keys(tot).map(function (c) { return '<b>' + tot[c] + '</b> ' + TO_CATS[c].toLowerCase() + ' day' + (tot[c] === 1 ? '' : 's'); }).join(', ');
      var html = '<p class="read" style="margin-top:0">' + (totLine ? 'Taken this ' + fy.label + ': ' + totLine + '.' : 'No time off taken yet this ' + fy.label + '.')
        + ' <span class="w105mut">Vacation runs 4 to 6 weeks by tenure; volunteering 4 times a year adds a week (up to two). 7 mental health and sick days a year.</span></p>';
      if (rows.length) {
        html += '<table><tbody>' + rows.slice(0, 8).map(function (t) {
          var chip = t.status === 'approved' ? '<span class="w105ok">approved</span>' : (t.status === 'declined' ? '<span class="w105no">declined</span>' + (t.decline_reason ? ' <span class="w105mut">' + esc(t.decline_reason) + '</span>' : '') : '<span class="w105pend">waiting</span>');
          return '<tr><td>' + esc(TO_CATS[t.category] || t.category) + '</td><td>' + esc(t.start_date) + ' to ' + esc(t.end_date) + ' <span class="w105mut">(' + t.days + 'd)</span></td><td>' + chip + '</td></tr>';
        }).join('') + '</tbody></table>';
      }
      body.innerHTML = html;
    } catch (e) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not load your time off.</p>'; }
  }

  /* shared: policies-to-sign fold (My page + Training page onboarding).
     prepend=true puts the fold at the TOP of the panel (Steel, round 16:
     policies sit above onboarding and trainings on My trainings). */
  async function w105SignCard(panel, id, prepend) {
    if (document.getElementById(id)) return;
    var me = w105Me();
    if (!me.email) return;
    try {
      var res = await Promise.all([
        db.from('wa_policies').select('*').eq('active', true).eq('required', true).order('sort'),
        db.from('wa_policy_signoffs').select('policy_key, signed_at').eq('email', me.email)
      ]);
      var pols = (res[0].data) || [];
      if (!pols.length) return;
      var mine = {};
      ((res[1].data) || []).forEach(function (s) { mine[s.policy_key] = s.signed_at; });
      var card = document.createElement('div');
      card.className = 'card w105card';
      card.id = id;
      var pending = pols.filter(function (p) { return !mine[p.key]; });
      var html = '<h2>Policies' + (pending.length ? ' <span class="w105pend">' + pending.length + ' to review</span>' : '') + '</h2>'
        + '<p class="read">Reading and signing these is part of onboarding. Open the document, read it, then sign off; your sign-off is recorded for HR with a date.</p>';
      pols.forEach(function (p) {
        if (mine[p.key]) {
          html += '<div class="w105sign"><b>' + esc(p.title) + '</b> <span class="w105ok">signed ' + esc(String(mine[p.key]).slice(0, 10)) + '</span> <a class="w105mut" href="' + esc(p.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">read again</a></div>';
        } else {
          /* wave279: the button waits for the reading time (wa279Pol, parts279/b_hooks.js): "Sign in N min", then the old label */
          var W = window.wa279Pol, OK = 'I have read and reviewed this', v = W ? W.view(p.key, OK, true) : { dis: false, txt: OK };
          html += '<div class="w105sign"><b>' + esc(p.title) + '</b><div style="margin-top:6px"><a class="btn ghost mini" href="' + esc(p.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="text-decoration:none" data-w279polopen="' + esc(p.key) + '">Open and read</a> <button class="btn mini" data-w105sk="' + esc(p.key) + '"' + (W ? W.attrs(p.key, OK, true) : '') + (v.dis ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + esc(v.txt) + '</button></div></div>';
        }
      });
      card.innerHTML = html;
      if (prepend && panel.firstChild) panel.insertBefore(card, panel.firstChild);
      else panel.appendChild(card);
      card.querySelectorAll('[data-w105sk]').forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener('click', async function () {
          this.disabled = true; this.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
          var ins = await db.from('wa_policy_signoffs').insert({ email: me.email, name: me.name, policy_key: this.getAttribute('data-w105sk') });
          /* wave279: the database's refusal ("Read it for N more minutes first") shows as it is */
          if (ins.error) { this.disabled = false; this.removeAttribute('aria-busy'); toast(String(ins.error.message || '') || 'Could not record the sign-off'); if (window.wa279Pol) { window.wa279Pol.reload(); window.wa279Pol.refresh(); } return; }
          toast('Sign-off recorded, thank you');
          card.remove();
          w105SignCard(panel, id, prepend);
        });
      });
    } catch (e) { /* fold is optional */ }
  }

  /* ============ wiring ============ */
  var w105HrBusy = false;   /* wave212: one build at a time (the observer can fire again while the access checks wait) */
  async function w105HrCards() {
    if (w105HrBusy) return;
    w105HrBusy = true;
    try { await w105HrCardsNow(); } finally { w105HrBusy = false; }
  }
  async function w105HrCardsNow() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel || !panel.children.length || panel.querySelector('#w52denied')) return;
    if (typeof isStaff !== 'function' || !isStaff()) return;
    var acc = await Promise.all([w105CanTimeoff(), w105IsHR()]);   /* wave212: both at once, so the cards come as soon as before */
    if (acc[0]) await w105TimeCard(panel);
    if (!acc[1]) return;
    await w105PolCard(panel);
    await w105DiscCard(panel);
  }
  (function () {
    var t105 = null, t105b = null, t105c = null;
    function arm() {
      var hr = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
      if (hr && !hr.dataset.w105mo) {
        hr.dataset.w105mo = '1';
        new MutationObserver(function () {
          clearTimeout(t105);
          t105 = setTimeout(function () { try { w105HrCards(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 320);
        }).observe(hr, { childList: true });
        if (hr.children.length) { try { w105HrCards(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
      }
      var my = document.getElementById('mypage');
      if (my && !my.dataset.w105mo) {
        my.dataset.w105mo = '1';
        new MutationObserver(function () {
          clearTimeout(t105b);
          t105b = setTimeout(function () { try { w105MyCards(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 320);
        }).observe(my, { childList: true });
        if (my.children.length) { try { w105MyCards(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
      }
      var tr = document.getElementById('training');
      if (tr && !tr.dataset.w105mo) {
        tr.dataset.w105mo = '1';
        new MutationObserver(function () {
          clearTimeout(t105c);
          t105c = setTimeout(function () { try { if (tr.children.length && !tr.querySelector('#w52denied')) w105SignCard(tr, 'w105signt', true); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 320);
        }).observe(tr, { childList: true });
        if (tr.children.length) { try { w105SignCard(tr, 'w105signt', true); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
      }
    }
    if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
      var __sa105 = startApp;
      startApp = async function () {
        await __sa105.apply(this, arguments);
        try { arm(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      };
    }
  })();
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v106 renames, HR tabs, event management (07/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel, round sixteen:
   1. The individuals' Training tab becomes "My trainings" (nav label +
      page heading); the HR centre's internal Training tab keeps its name.
      Policy sign-offs already sit at the TOP of My trainings (wave105).
   2. The Volunteering tab becomes "MTL volunteering".
   3. HR centre gains two more section tabs after Commitment, Training,
      Onboarding, Recruitment: "Policies" and "Discipline", and the
      wave105 cards MOVE into them (wave52's Hours-tab pattern).
   4. Events: staff can CANCEL (and restore) an event from the top of
      its page, and ADD new events (any date, 2027 included) from the
      calendar. Codes continue the WA-<yy>-<seq> convention.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa106) return; window.__wa106 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w106cancel{margin-top:10px;}' +
    '.w106sec{display:none;}' +
    '.w106sec.on{display:block;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- 1 + 2. renames ---------- */
  function w106Renames() {
    try {
      var t = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab[data-tab="training"]');
      if (t && t.textContent.indexOf('Avenue Academy') < 0) {
        t.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:#103F39"></span>Avenue Academy';
      }
      var v = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab[data-tab="volunteering"]');
      if (v && v.textContent.indexOf('MTL volunteering') < 0) {
        v.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:#103F39"></span>MTL volunteering';
      }
      var th = document.querySelector('#training .card > h2');
      if (th && th.textContent.trim() === 'Training') th.textContent = 'My trainings';
    } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
  }

  /* ---------- 3. HR centre: Policies + Discipline tabs ---------- */
  function w106HrTabs() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
    if (!body) return;
    var tabs = body.querySelector('.w47tabs');
    if (!tabs) return;
    if (!tabs.querySelector('[data-w47v="policies"]')) {
      tabs.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
        '<button class="w47tab" data-w47v="policies">Policies</button>' +
        '<button class="w47tab" data-w47v="discipline">Discipline</button>');
    }
    if (!document.getElementById('w106polsec')) {
      body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
        '<div id="w106polsec" class="w106sec"></div><div id="w106discsec" class="w106sec"></div>');
    }
    /* adopt the wave105 cards into their sections (listeners survive) */
    var pol = document.getElementById('w105pol');
    var polsec = document.getElementById('w106polsec');
    if (pol && polsec && pol.parentElement !== polsec) polsec.appendChild(pol);
    var disc = document.getElementById('w105disc');
    var discsec = document.getElementById('w106discsec');
    if (disc && discsec && disc.parentElement !== discsec) discsec.appendChild(disc);
    /* deep link support, mirroring the Hours tab */
    var h = String(location.hash || '');
    if ((h.indexOf('#hrcentre/policies') === 0 || h.indexOf('#hrcentre/discipline') === 0)) {
      var want = h.indexOf('policies') > 0 ? 'policies' : 'discipline';
      var sec = document.getElementById(want === 'policies' ? 'w106polsec' : 'w106discsec');
      if (sec && !sec.dataset.w106shown) {
        sec.dataset.w106shown = '1';
        w106Show(want);
        body.querySelectorAll('.w46sec').forEach(function (x) { x.style.display = 'none'; });
        body.querySelectorAll('.w47tab').forEach(function (c) { c.classList.toggle('on', c.getAttribute('data-w47v') === want); });
      }
    }
  }
  function w106Show(view) {
    var pol = document.getElementById('w106polsec');
    var disc = document.getElementById('w106discsec');
    if (pol) pol.classList.toggle('on', view === 'policies');
    if (disc) disc.classList.toggle('on', view === 'discipline');
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa106Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa106Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var tb = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w47tab');
      if (!tb) return;
      w106HrTabs();
      w106Show(tb.getAttribute('data-w47v'));
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 4a. cancel / restore at the top of an event page ---------- */
  function w106EventButtons() {
    try {
      if (typeof isStaff !== 'function' || !isStaff()) return;
      var ev = null;
      (typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENTS : []).forEach(function (e) {
        if (typeof WA_VOL_EVENT !== 'undefined' && e.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ev = e;
      });
      if (!ev) return;
      var hero = document.querySelector('#volunteering .vl-hero');
      if (!hero || document.getElementById('w106cancel')) return;
      var wrap = document.createElement('div');
      wrap.id = 'w106cancel';
      var cancelled = String(ev.status || '').indexOf('cancel') === 0;
      wrap.innerHTML = cancelled
        ? '<button class="btn mini" id="w106rest">Restore this event</button>'
        : '<button class="btn ghost mini" id="w106can">Cancel this serving</button>';
      hero.appendChild(wrap);
      var can = document.getElementById('w106can');
      if (can) can.addEventListener('click', function () {
        openModal(
          '<h3>Cancel this serving?</h3>' +
          '<p class="read" style="margin:4px 0 8px">' + esc(ev.title || ('This serving' + (ev.code ? ' (' + ev.code + ')' : ''))) + ' on ' + esc(ev.event_date) + ' will be marked cancelled. It stays in the calendar history and can be restored.</p>' +
          '<label class="w101lbl">Reason (kept in the event notes)</label>' +
          '<input type="text" id="w106why" maxlength="200" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
          '<div style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn" id="w106go">Cancel the event</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w106no">Keep it</button></div>'
        );
        document.getElementById('w106no').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
        document.getElementById('w106go').addEventListener('click', async function () {
          this.disabled = true;
          var why = (document.getElementById('w106why') || {}).value || '';
          var patch = { status: 'cancelled' };
          if (why) patch.notes = ((ev.notes ? ev.notes + ' | ' : '') + 'Cancelled: ' + why).slice(0, 800);
          var r = await db.from('volunteer_events').update(patch).eq('id', ev.id);
          if (r.error) { this.disabled = false; toast('Could not cancel it'); return; }
          try {
            await db.from('change_log').insert({
              entity_type: 'volunteer_event', entity_id: String(ev.id), field: 'status',
              old_value: ev.status || null, new_value: 'cancelled' + (why ? ': ' + why : ''),
              changed_by: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || 'staff'
            });
          } catch (e2) { /* audit best-effort */ }
          closeModal();
          toast('Event cancelled');
          WA_VOL_LOADED = false; WA_VOL_DETAIL = null;
          waVolRender();
        });
      });
      var rest = document.getElementById('w106rest');
      if (rest) rest.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        this.disabled = true;
        var r = await db.from('volunteer_events').update({ status: 'planned' }).eq('id', ev.id);
        if (r.error) { this.disabled = false; toast('Could not restore it'); return; }
        toast('Event restored');
        WA_VOL_LOADED = false; WA_VOL_DETAIL = null;
        waVolRender();
      });
    } catch (e) { /* decorative */ }
  }

  /* ---------- 4b. add an event from the calendar ---------- */
  function w106NextCode(dateStr) {
    var yy = String(dateStr || '').slice(2, 4) || String(new Date().getFullYear()).slice(2);
    var max = 0;
    (typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENTS : []).forEach(function (e) {
      var m = /^WA-(\d{2})-(\d+)$/.exec(e.code || '');
      if (m && m[1] === yy) max = Math.max(max, Number(m[2]));
    });
    return 'WA-' + yy + '-' + (max + 1);
  }
  function w106AddButton() {
    try {
      if (typeof isStaff !== 'function' || !isStaff()) return;
      var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering');
      if (!sec || document.getElementById('w106add')) return;
      /* only on the calendar view (the hero back-button marks detail views; wave191: the SOP, inventory and recipes views too) */
      if (sec.querySelector('.vl-back')) return;
      if (typeof WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'calendar') return;
      /* anchor above the hero or first month; an EMPTY calendar still gets
         the button (that is exactly when you need to add events) */
      var anchor = sec.querySelector('.vl-hero') || sec.querySelector('.vl-month') || sec.querySelector('.vl-empty');
      if (!anchor) return;
      var btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.className = 'btn ghost mini';
      btn.id = 'w106add';
      btn.textContent = '+ Add an event';
      btn.style.margin = '0 0 10px';
      anchor.parentElement.insertBefore(btn, anchor);
      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
        openModal(
          '<h3>Add an event</h3>' +
          '<p class="read" style="margin:4px 0 8px">Any date works, 2027 included. The code follows on automatically.</p>' +
          '<label class="w101lbl">Date</label><input type="date" id="w106d" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
          '<label class="w101lbl">Title</label><input type="text" id="w106t" placeholder="Food distribution" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
          '<label class="w101lbl">Location</label><input type="text" id="w106l" placeholder="Berri-UQAM" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
          '<label class="w101lbl">Season</label><input type="text" id="w106s" placeholder="Winter" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
          '<label class="w101lbl">Notes (optional)</label><input type="text" id="w106n" maxlength="400" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box" />' +
          '<div style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn" id="w106save">Add the event</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w106x">Cancel</button></div>'
        );
        document.getElementById('w106x').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
        document.getElementById('w106save').addEventListener('click', async function () {
          var d = (document.getElementById('w106d') || {}).value;
          var t = ((document.getElementById('w106t') || {}).value || '').trim();
          if (!d || !t) { toast('A date and a title are the minimum'); return; }
          this.disabled = true;
          var row = {
            code: w106NextCode(d), event_date: d, title: t, kind: 'service', status: 'planned',
            location: ((document.getElementById('w106l') || {}).value || '').trim() || null,
            season: ((document.getElementById('w106s') || {}).value || '').trim() || null,
            notes: ((document.getElementById('w106n') || {}).value || '').trim() || null
          };
          var r = await db.from('volunteer_events').insert(row);
          if (r.error) { this.disabled = false; toast('Could not add it: ' + (r.error.message || '')); return; }
          try {
            await db.from('change_log').insert({
              entity_type: 'volunteer_event', entity_id: row.code, field: 'created',
              old_value: null, new_value: t + ' on ' + d,
              changed_by: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || 'staff'
            });
          } catch (e2) { /* audit best-effort */ }
          closeModal();
          toast('Event added: ' + row.code);
          WA_VOL_LOADED = false;
          waVolRender();
        });
      });
    } catch (e) { /* decorative */ }
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  if (typeof waVolRender === 'function') {
    var __vr106 = waVolRender;
    waVolRender = async function () {
      await __vr106.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w106EventButtons(); w106AddButton(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  (function () {
    var t106 = null;
    function arm() {
      w106Renames();
      setTimeout(w106Renames, 900);
      setTimeout(w106Renames, 2600);
      var hr = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
      if (hr && !hr.dataset.w106mo) {
        hr.dataset.w106mo = '1';
        new MutationObserver(function () {
          clearTimeout(t106);
          t106 = setTimeout(function () { try { w106HrTabs(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 380);
        }).observe(hr, { childList: true, subtree: true });
        try { w106HrTabs(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      }
      var tr = document.getElementById('training');
      if (tr && !tr.dataset.w106mo) {
        tr.dataset.w106mo = '1';
        new MutationObserver(function () { w106Renames(); }).observe(tr, { childList: true });
      }
    }
    if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
      var __sa106 = startApp;
      startApp = async function () {
        await __sa106.apply(this, arguments);
        try { arm(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      };
    }
  })();
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v107 Drive in the Hub (07/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   The wa-hub-drive service account now indexes 8 shared drives into
   wa_drive_docs (nightly drive-pull sync). This wave puts that to work:
   1. Search everything now also searches Drive: file names AND the
      extracted text of Google Docs. Results appear under the Hub's own
      matches with a Drive tag and open the document in a new tab.
      (Staff-scoped by RLS: members simply get no Drive rows.)
   2. Source documents in Drive: the volunteering SOP page and the HR
      centre's Onboarding section link to their source docs, driven by
      app_config 'drive_source_links' so the list is editable without a
      rebuild. "Or at least provide links to them" (Steel): the local
      copies stay (no removals), the links sit on top.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa107) return; window.__wa107 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.sr .sr-type.w107d{background:rgba(66,133,244,.12);color:#3367d6;}' +
    '.w107src{margin:2px 0 10px;font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);}' +
    '.w107src a{color:#103F39;font-weight:600;text-decoration:none;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.35);border-radius:999px;padding:2px 10px;margin:2px 4px 2px 0;display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '.w107src a:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- 1. Drive results in search everything ---------- */
  var W107_SEQ = 0;
  async function w107Drive(q) {
    var seq = ++W107_SEQ;
    try {
      if (!q || q.length < 3) return;
      var ql = q.toLowerCase();
      var r = await db.from('wa_drive_docs')
        .select('file_id, name, url, mime')
        .or('name.ilike.%' + q + '%,text_content.ilike.%' + q + '%')
        .limit(30);
      var rows = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (d) {
        /* belt and braces for environments where ilike is not applied */
        return (d.name || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(ql) >= 0 || true;
      }).slice(0, 5);
      if (seq !== W107_SEQ) return; /* a newer query superseded this one */
      var pop = document.getElementById('searchResults');
      if (!pop || pop.style.display === 'none') return;
      if (pop.querySelector('[data-w107]')) return;
      if (!rows.length) return;
      /* replace the no-matches row if it is all there is */
      var empty = !pop.querySelector('.sr[data-hash]') ? pop.querySelector('.sr') : null;
      if (empty && (empty.textContent || '').indexOf('No matches') >= 0) empty.remove();
      rows.forEach(function (d) {
        var div = document.createElement('div');
        div.className = 'sr';
        div.setAttribute('data-w107', d.file_id);
        var kind = d.mime === 'application/vnd.google-apps.document' ? 'Doc'
          : d.mime === 'application/vnd.google-apps.spreadsheet' ? 'Sheet'
          : d.mime === 'application/vnd.google-apps.form' ? 'Form'
          : (d.mime || '').indexOf('pdf') >= 0 ? 'PDF' : 'Drive';
        div.innerHTML = '<span class="sr-type w107d">Drive ' + esc(kind) + '</span>' + esc(d.name || '');
        div.addEventListener('click', function () {
          pop.style.display = 'none';
          var gs = document.getElementById('globalSearch');
          if (gs) gs.value = '';
          if (d.url) window.open(d.url, '_blank', 'noopener');
        });
        pop.appendChild(div);
      });
    } catch (e) { /* search stays Hub-only on failure */ }
  }
  if (typeof renderSearch === 'function') {
    var __rs107 = renderSearch;
    var t107 = null;
    renderSearch = function (q) {
      __rs107.apply(this, arguments);
      clearTimeout(t107);
      t107 = setTimeout(function () { w107Drive(q); }, 250);
    };
  }

  /* ---------- 2. source documents in Drive ---------- */
  async function w107Links() {
    if (window.__w107links) return window.__w107links;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'drive_source_links');
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      window.__w107links = rows.length ? JSON.parse(rows[0].value) : [];
    } catch (e) { window.__w107links = []; }
    return window.__w107links;
  }
  function w107Strip(links) {
    return '<div class="w107src">Source documents in Drive: ' + links.map(function (l) {
      return '<a href="' + esc(l.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + esc(l.label) + '</a>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  async function w107Sop() {
    try {
      var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering');
      if (!sec || sec.querySelector('.w107src')) return;
      /* the SOP view is identified by its master-template intro line */
      var intro = sec.querySelector('.vl-empty');
      var it = (intro && intro.textContent) || '';
      if (!intro || (it.indexOf('master SOP') < 0 && it.indexOf('master template') < 0)) return;
      var links = (await w107Links()).filter(function (l) { return l.where === 'sop'; });
      if (!links.length) return;
      if (sec.querySelector('.w107src')) return;
      intro.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', w107Strip(links));
    } catch (e) { /* decorative */ }
  }
  async function w107Onb() {
    try {
      var master = document.getElementById('w46master');
      if (!master || document.getElementById('w107onb')) return;
      var links = (await w107Links()).filter(function (l) { return l.where === 'onboarding'; });
      if (!links.length) return;
      if (document.getElementById('w107onb')) return;
      var div = document.createElement('div');
      div.id = 'w107onb';
      div.innerHTML = w107Strip(links);
      master.parentElement.insertBefore(div, master);
    } catch (e) { /* decorative */ }
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  if (typeof waVolRender === 'function') {
    var __vr107 = waVolRender;
    waVolRender = async function () {
      await __vr107.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w107Sop(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  (function () {
    var t = null;
    function arm() {
      /* the SOP view can render without going through waVolRender
         (direct view switches), so watch the panel too */
      var vol = document.getElementById('volunteering');
      if (vol && !vol.dataset.w107mo) {
        vol.dataset.w107mo = '1';
        var tv = null;
        new MutationObserver(function () {
          clearTimeout(tv);
          tv = setTimeout(function () { try { w107Sop(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 300);
        }).observe(vol, { childList: true, subtree: true });
      }
      var hr = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
      if (hr && !hr.dataset.w107mo) {
        hr.dataset.w107mo = '1';
        new MutationObserver(function () {
          clearTimeout(t);
          t = setTimeout(function () { try { w107Onb(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }, 420);
        }).observe(hr, { childList: true, subtree: true });
        try { w107Onb(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      }
    }
    if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
      var __sa107 = startApp;
      startApp = async function () {
        await __sa107.apply(this, arguments);
        try { arm(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      };
    }
  })();
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v108 branded onboarding email builder (07/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "as easy as putting in the right information into a form and
   it inputs information into an html wa branded email welcome."
   The HR welcome kit's email section gains a small form (first name,
   last name, job title, start date, prep sessions, onboarding lead,
   WA email, sender). One button renders BOTH:
   - the plain-text email into the kit's existing textarea (so the
     existing Copy and mail-app buttons keep working, mailto refreshed),
   - a Welfare Avenue branded HTML email (green #103F39 header with the
     wordmark, white card, green step numbers) with Copy as rich text
     (paste straight into Gmail) and Download .html.
   Body copy comes from app_config 'onboarding_email_template' (Steel's
   Google Doc template, Drive id 16PrDJkT0A1A60vXr--aUbFHUsDGiyJKj...),
   so wording edits are a config change, not a rebuild. Wave46's kit is
   untouched: this decorates the open modal (no removals).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa108) return; window.__wa108 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w108f{background:#fbfbfd;border:1px solid #eef0f4;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;margin:0 0 10px;}' +
    '#w108f .grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px;}' +
    '#w108f label{display:block;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);margin:0 0 2px;}' +
    '#w108f input{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;padding:6px 9px;font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '#w108f .wide{grid-column:1 / -1;}' +
    '#w108prev{border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:10px;margin-top:10px;max-height:280px;overflow-y:auto;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var W108_TPL = null;
  async function w108Tpl() {
    if (W108_TPL !== null) return W108_TPL;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'onboarding_email_template');
      W108_TPL = (r.data && r.data[0] && r.data[0].value) || '';
    } catch (e) { W108_TPL = ''; }
    return W108_TPL;
  }
  function w108Fill(tpl, v) {
    return tpl
      .replace(/\{\{first_name\}\}/g, v.first || '[FIRST NAME]')
      .replace(/\{\{job_title\}\}/g, v.title || '[JOB TITLE]')
      .replace(/\{\{start_date\}\}/g, v.start || '[START DATE]')
      .replace(/\{\{prep_day\}\}/g, v.prep || '[PREP DAY]')
      .replace(/\{\{session_1\}\}/g, v.s1 || '[SESSION 1]')
      .replace(/\{\{session_2\}\}/g, v.s2 || '[SESSION 2]')
      .replace(/\{\{wa_email\}\}/g, v.email || '[first.last@welfareavenue.com]')
      .replace(/\{\{sender_name\}\}/g, v.sender || 'The Welfare Avenue team');
  }
  function w108Date(iso) {
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(iso || '')) return '';
    var d = new Date(iso + 'T12:00:00');
    var names = ['January','February','March','April','May','June','July','August','September','October','November','December'];
    var days = ['Sunday','Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday'];
    var n = d.getDate();
    var suf = n % 10 === 1 && n !== 11 ? 'st' : (n % 10 === 2 && n !== 12 ? 'nd' : (n % 10 === 3 && n !== 13 ? 'rd' : 'th'));
    return days[d.getDay()] + ', ' + names[d.getMonth()] + ' ' + n + suf + ', ' + d.getFullYear();
  }
  /* branded HTML email: table layout + inline styles for mail clients */
  function w108Html(v) {
    var G = '#103F39', ACC = '#103F39';

      return '<tr><td style="vertical-align:top;padding:10px 12px 10px 0;width:34px">'
        + '<div style="width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:999px;background:' + ACC + ';color:#FFFAF2;font-weight:bold;text-align:center;line-height:28px;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:14px">' + n + '</div></td>'
        + '<td style="padding:8px 0"><p style="margin:0 0 4px;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:14px;color:#1F201E"><b>' + title + '</b></p>'
        + '<p style="margin:0;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:13px;line-height:1.6;color:#4a5551">' + body + '</p></td></tr>';
    };

      + '</table></td></tr></table></body></html>';
