  function w108Vals() {
    var g = function (id) { return ((document.getElementById(id) || {}).value || '').trim(); };
    var first = g('w108first'), last = g('w108last');
    var email = g('w108mail');
    if (!email && first && last) email = (first + '.' + last).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z.\-]/g, '') + '@welfareavenue.com';
    return {
      first: first, last: last, title: g('w108title'),
      start: w108Date(g('w108start')) || g('w108start'),
      prep: g('w108prep'), s1: g('w108s1'), s2: g('w108s2'),
      lead: g('w108lead'), email: email,
      sender: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || ''
    };
  }
  async function w108Build() {
    var v = w108Vals();
    var tpl = await w108Tpl();
    /* plain text into the kit textarea (existing copy button keeps working) */
    var ta = document.getElementById('w46em');
    if (ta && tpl) {
      ta.value = w108Fill(tpl, v);
      var subj = 'Welcome to Welfare Avenue! Your Next Steps';
      var mail = document.querySelector('#modalBox a[href^="mailto:"]');
      if (mail) mail.setAttribute('href', 'mailto:' + (v.email && v.email.indexOf('[') < 0 ? '' : '') + '?subject=' + encodeURIComponent(subj) + '&body=' + encodeURIComponent(ta.value));
    }
    var html = w108Html(v);
    window.__w108html = html;
    var prev = document.getElementById('w108prev');
    if (prev) {
      prev.innerHTML = '<iframe id="w108if" style="width:100%;height:270px;border:0" sandbox=""></iframe>';
      var f = document.getElementById('w108if');
      f.setAttribute('srcdoc', html);
    }
    var acts = document.getElementById('w108acts');
    if (acts) acts.style.display = '';
    toast('Email built: plain text updated, branded version below');
  }
  async function w108CopyHtml() {
    var html = window.__w108html || '';
    if (!html) return;
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard && window.ClipboardItem) {
        var item = new ClipboardItem({
          'text/html': new Blob([html], { type: 'text/html' }),
          'text/plain': new Blob([(document.getElementById('w46em') || {}).value || ''], { type: 'text/plain' })
        });
        await navigator.clipboard.write([item]);
        toast('Branded email copied: paste into Gmail');
        return;
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fall through */ }
    try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(html); toast('HTML source copied'); } catch (e2) { toast('Copy failed'); }
  }
  function w108Download() {
    var html = window.__w108html || '';
    if (!html) return;
    var blob = new Blob([html], { type: 'text/html' });
    var a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = 'welcome-email.html';
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    setTimeout(function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); a.remove(); }, 3000);
  }

  function w108Inject() {
    var ta = document.getElementById('w46em');
    if (!ta || document.getElementById('w108f')) return;
    /* first name from the kit heading: "Welcome kit for <name>" */
    var h3 = document.querySelector('#modalBox h3');
    var m = /Welcome kit for\s+(.+)$/.exec((h3 && h3.textContent) || '');
    var full = (m && m[1] || '').trim();
    var first = full.split(/\s+/)[0] || '';
    var last = full.split(/\s+/).slice(1).join(' ');
    var box = document.createElement('div');
    box.id = 'w108f';
    box.innerHTML =
      '<label style="font-size:11px;color:#103F39;margin-bottom:6px">Fill the form, press Build: the plain email below updates and a branded version appears.</label>' +
      '<div class="grid">' +
      '<div><label>First name</label><input id="w108first" value="' + esc(first) + '" /></div>' +
      '<div><label>Last name</label><input id="w108last" value="' + esc(last) + '" /></div>' +
      '<div><label>Job title</label><input id="w108title" placeholder="Volunteer Coordinator" /></div>' +
      '<div><label>Start date</label><input id="w108start" type="date" /></div>' +
      '<div><label>Prep day</label><input id="w108prep" placeholder="Friday, May 15th" /></div>' +
      '<div><label>Onboarding lead</label><input id="w108lead" placeholder="Amanda" /></div>' +
      '<div class="wide"><label>Session 1</label><input id="w108s1" placeholder="6:30 PM, team meeting" /></div>' +
      '<div class="wide"><label>Session 2</label><input id="w108s2" placeholder="7:30 PM, onboarding session (software access, job description)" /></div>' +
      '<div class="wide"><label>WA email (blank = first.last@welfareavenue.com)</label><input id="w108mail" placeholder="auto" /></div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div style="margin-top:8px"><button class="btn mini" id="w108go">Build the email</button> ' +
      '<span id="w108acts" style="display:none"><button class="btn ghost mini" id="w108copy">Copy branded email</button> ' +
      '<button class="btn ghost mini" id="w108dl">Download .html</button></span></div>' +
      '<div id="w108prev" style="display:none"></div>';
    ta.parentElement.insertBefore(box, ta);
    document.getElementById('w108go').addEventListener('click', function () {
      document.getElementById('w108prev').style.display = '';
      w108Build();
    });
    document.getElementById('w108copy').addEventListener('click', w108CopyHtml);
    document.getElementById('w108dl').addEventListener('click', w108Download);
  }

  try {
    new MutationObserver(function () {
      try { w108Inject(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* kit keeps its classic email */ }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v109 complete your tasks from the home page (08/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "I want the home page to enable anyone to complete tasks that
   are assigned to them." The My page (home for signed-in staff) lists
   each person's open tasks (wave74 via waTaskLine) but as read-only
   lines. Now:
   1. Every task row on My page carries a checkbox (and a task id).
      Ticking it calls the canonical toggleTask (same save path as the
      department pages: dept_tasks update + change_log + toast), the
      row fades with a strikethrough, and the My page counts refresh.
      An Undo link on the row reopens it for 10 seconds in case of a
      slip, then the row leaves the list on the next repaint.
   2. The same rows become clickable to open the task on its page
      (title text), so context is one tap away.
   Implementation: waTaskLine is WRAPPED (global function; wave74 calls
   it through w74TaskLine) to add the checkbox only while My page is
   rendering (WA109_MY flag set around renderMyPage), so other callers
   of waTaskLine, if any, keep the plain line. Team members' tasks in
   the "your team" fold are NOT ticked by others: the checkbox only
   appears for rows in the signed-in person's own section (owner match).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa109) return; window.__wa109 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '.mp-task.w109{align-items:center;}' +
    '.mp-task.w109 input[type=checkbox]{accent-color:#103F39;width:17px;height:17px;cursor:pointer;flex:0 0 auto;margin:0;}' +
    '.mp-task.w109 .w109t{cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.mp-task.w109 .w109t:hover{text-decoration:underline;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.mp-task.w109.w109done{opacity:.55;}' +
    '.mp-task.w109.w109done .w109t{text-decoration:line-through;}' +
    '.w109undo{font-size:11px;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;margin-left:6px;font-weight:700;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var WA109_MY = false;
  function w109Mine(t) {
    try {
      var me = (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined') ? WA_ME : null;
      if (!me || !me.name) return false;
      var ft = (typeof waFirstTok === 'function') ? waFirstTok(me.name) : me.name.toLowerCase().split(' ')[0];
      return (t.owner || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(ft) >= 0;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }

  if (typeof waTaskLine === 'function') {
    var __tl109 = waTaskLine;
    waTaskLine = function (t) {
      var base = __tl109.apply(this, arguments);
      if (!WA109_MY || !t || !t.id || t.done || !w109Mine(t)) return base;
      /* add id + checkbox + clickable title to the base row */
      return base
        .replace('<div class="mp-task">', '<div class="mp-task w109" data-w109="' + t.id + '"><input type="checkbox" data-w109cb="' + t.id + '" title="Mark done" />')
        .replace('<span>' + esc(t.title) + '</span>', '<span class="w109t" data-w109open="' + t.id + '">' + esc(t.title) + '</span>');
    };
  }
  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __rm109 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      WA109_MY = true;
      try { return await __rm109.apply(this, arguments); }
      finally { WA109_MY = false; }
    };
  }

  /* interactions */
  document.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
    var cb = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('input[data-w109cb]');
    if (!cb) return;
    var id = Number(cb.getAttribute('data-w109cb'));
    var row = cb.closest('.mp-task');
    if (!id || typeof toggleTask !== 'function') return;
    cb.disabled = true;
    var ok = await toggleTask(id, cb.checked);
    cb.disabled = false;
    if (!ok) { cb.checked = !cb.checked; return; }
    if (row) {
      row.classList.toggle('w109done', cb.checked);
      var old = row.querySelector('.w109undo');
      if (old) old.remove();
      if (cb.checked) {
        var u = document.createElement('span');
        u.className = 'w109undo';
        u.textContent = 'Undo';
        u.setAttribute('data-w109undo', String(id));
        row.appendChild(u);
        setTimeout(function () {
          if (u.isConnected && row.classList.contains('w109done')) {
            u.remove();
            /* let the page repaint its counts and sections */
            try { if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') renderMyPage(); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ }
          }
        }, 10000);
      }
    }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var un = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w109undo]');
    if (un) {
      var row = un.closest('.mp-task');
      var cb = row && row.querySelector('input[data-w109cb]');
      if (cb) { cb.checked = false; cb.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true })); }
      return;
    }
    var op = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w109open]');
    if (op) { location.hash = '#task/' + op.getAttribute('data-w109open'); }
  });
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v111 one home for the org view (20/08/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "the task completion dashboard and decisions page should be
   merged... decisions aging could just be, like, buttons where I can
   open a folder and see these things. And I should be able to click
   that these things are done. I don't really care too much for
   overdue tasks."
   The Decisions tab becomes the Hub dashboard, one org-wide home:
   1. The tab is renamed Dashboard (same id, same hash, same gating;
      nothing else keys on the old label).
   2. The wave35 stat strip gains the missing fifth number Steel asked
      for: donations raised this fiscal year (4100 Donation, FY starts
      Oct 1), shown only to roles that could open the old Dashboard.
   3. Three folder buttons under the stats: Decisions waiting (the old
      decision-aging table, now with a check-to-decide box per row
      running through the canonical decideModal), Overdue tasks (check
      to close through toggleTask, with a 10 second Undo), and
      Delivery numbers (the full task-completion dashboard: totals,
      per-department bars, workload table and ClickUp movement,
      rendered by the untouched renderDashboard and mirrored here).
      One folder open at a time; counts on the buttons.
   4. #dashboard now lands on this merged page with the numbers folder
      open, so the wave73 "Open the full dashboard" link and every old
      bookmark keep working. The #dashboard section itself is not
      touched; roles without the dashboard tab (viewers, board) keep
      exactly the page they had, folders and all numbers hidden.
   Nothing is removed: filters, add decision, the decided fold, the
   plate card, purpose lines and all dashboard tables survive. Team
   hours stay in the HR centre where wave52 moved them.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa111) return; window.__wa111 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.id = 'wa111css';
  css.textContent = '' +
    '#w111folds{margin:2px 0 16px;}' +
    '.w111row{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 10px;}' +
    '.w111btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line,#e3e8e5);background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:14px;padding:10px 16px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:#1F201E;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(16,63,57,.04);}' +
    '.w111btn:hover{border-color:#103F39;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w111btn.on{border-color:#103F39;color:#103F39;background:rgba(16,63,57,.07);}' +
    '.w111btn b{background:#f2f5f3;border-radius:999px;padding:2px 9px;font-size:12px;}' +
    '.w111btn.on b{background:rgba(16,63,57,.14);}' +
    '#w111body{margin:0 0 6px;}' +
    '#w111body .wa-tbl td{vertical-align:middle;}' +
    '.w111cb{accent-color:#103F39;width:16px;height:16px;cursor:pointer;margin:0;}' +
    'tr.w111done td{opacity:.55;}' +
    'tr.w111done .w111t{text-decoration:line-through;}' +
    '.w111undo{font-size:11px;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;margin-left:8px;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w111note{font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);margin:8px 0 0;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var W111_OPEN = null;   /* null | aging | overdue | numbers */
  var W111_LOCK = false;  /* re-entrancy guard around renderDashboard */

  function w111Can() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_MINE === 'undefined' || !WA40_MINE) return false;
      return typeof wa40Allowed === 'function' && wa40Allowed().indexOf('dashboard') >= 0;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }

  /* ---------- 1. the tab is renamed Dashboard ---------- */
  function w111Tab() {
    try {
      var b = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab[data-tab="overview"]');
      if (!b || b.dataset.w111) return;
      b.dataset.w111 = '1';
      Array.prototype.slice.call(b.childNodes).forEach(function (n) {
        if (n.nodeType === 3) n.textContent = '';
      });
      b.appendChild(document.createTextNode('Dashboard'));
    } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
  }

  /* ---------- 2. donations tile on the stat strip ---------- */
  function w111FyStart() {
    var now = new Date();
    var y = now.getMonth() + 1 >= 10 ? now.getFullYear() : now.getFullYear() - 1;
    return y + '-10-01';
  }
  async function w111Donations() {
    try {
      if (document.getElementById('w111don') || !w111Can()) return;
      var fy = w111FyStart();
      var r = await db.from('finance_transactions')
        .select('amount, category, txn_date, duplicate_of')
        .eq('category', '4100 Donation').gte('txn_date', fy).limit(1000);
      if (!r || r.error) return;
      /* belt and braces for environments where filters are not applied */
      var sum = (r.data || []).filter(function (x) {
        return x.category === '4100 Donation' && Number(x.amount || 0) > 0
          && !x.duplicate_of && String(x.txn_date || '') >= fy;
      }).reduce(function (s, x) { return s + Number(x.amount || 0); }, 0);
      var tries = 0;
      (function arm() {
        var strip = document.getElementById('w35stats');
        if (!strip) { if (++tries < 30) setTimeout(arm, 300); return; }
        if (document.getElementById('w111don')) return;
        var div = document.createElement('div');
        div.className = 'w35stat';
        div.id = 'w111don';
        div.innerHTML = '<b>$' + Math.round(sum).toLocaleString('en-CA') + '</b><span>raised in donations this fiscal year</span>';
        strip.appendChild(div);
      })();
    } catch (e) { /* the strip stays at four tiles */ }
  }

  /* ---------- 3. the folders ---------- */
  function w111Aging() {
    return (typeof getDecisions === 'function' ? getDecisions() : [])
      .filter(function (d) { return !d.done; })
      .map(function (d) { return { d: d, age: (typeof daysBetween === 'function' ? daysBetween(d.opened, new Date().toISOString()) : null) || 0 }; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return b.age - a.age; });
  }
  function w111Overdue() {
    var today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    return (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : [])
      .filter(function (r) { return !r.done && r.due_date && r.due_date < today; })
      .map(function (r) { return { r: r, over: Math.round((new Date(today) - new Date(r.due_date)) / 86400000) }; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return b.over - a.over; });
  }

  function w111AgingHtml(rows) {
    var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
    var body = rows.map(function (x) {
      return '<tr data-w111dec="' + x.d.ref + '">' +
        (staff ? '<td style="width:34px"><input type="checkbox" class="w111cb" data-w111decide="' + x.d.ref + '" title="Mark decided" /></td>' : '') +
        '<td><a class="w111t" href="#decision/' + x.d.ref + '">' + esc(x.d.title) + '</a>' + (typeof catTag === 'function' ? catTag(x.d.cat) : '') + '</td>' +
        '<td>' + esc(x.d.owner || 'Both') + '</td><td>' + x.age + 'd</td></tr>';
    }).join('');
    return '<div class="card dept-card" style="border-top-color:var(--wa-gold,#DCA527)">' +
      '<h2 style="color:#a87f2c">Decisions waiting</h2>' +
      '<p class="read">Every open executive decision, oldest first. ' + (staff ? 'Tick a box to record what was decided, right here. ' : '') + 'Aging decisions also surface in Slack automatically at 14 and 30 days.</p>' +
      (rows.length
        ? '<table class="wa-tbl"><thead><tr>' + (staff ? '<th></th>' : '') + '<th>Decision</th><th>Decides</th><th>Open</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + body + '</tbody></table>'
        : '<p style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:13px;">No open decisions. Clean slate.</p>') +
      '</div>';
  }

  function w111OverdueHtml(rows) {
    var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
    var body = rows.map(function (x) {
      return '<tr data-w111task="' + x.r.id + '">' +
        (staff ? '<td style="width:34px"><input type="checkbox" class="w111cb" data-w111close="' + x.r.id + '" title="Mark done" /></td>' : '') +
        '<td><a class="w111t" href="#task/' + x.r.id + '">' + esc(x.r.title) + '</a></td>' +
        '<td>' + esc((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[x.r.department]) || '') + '</td>' +
        '<td>' + esc(x.r.owner || '') + '</td><td>' + esc(x.r.due_date || '') + '</td>' +
        '<td><span class="due-badge ' + (x.over > 7 ? 'red' : 'amber') + '">' + x.over + 'd</span></td></tr>';
    }).join('');
    return '<div class="card dept-card" style="border-top-color:var(--wa-flag,#b3402f)">' +
      '<h2 style="color:var(--wa-flag,#b3402f)">Overdue tasks</h2>' +
      '<p class="read">Open tasks past their expected due date, most overdue first. ' + (staff ? 'Tick a box to close one; you get ten seconds to undo. ' : '') + 'Amber up to a week over, red beyond.</p>' +
      (rows.length
        ? '<table class="wa-tbl"><thead><tr>' + (staff ? '<th></th>' : '') + '<th>Task</th><th>Department</th><th>Owner</th><th>Due</th><th>Over</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + body + '</tbody></table>'
        : '<p style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:13px;">Nothing overdue.</p>') +
      '</div>';
  }

  function w111NumbersInto(holder) {
    var dash = document.getElementById('dashboard');
    if (!dash) return;
    if (!dash.children.length) {
      W111_LOCK = true;
      try { if (typeof renderDashboard === 'function') renderDashboard(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      W111_LOCK = false;
    }
    holder.innerHTML = '';
    Array.prototype.slice.call(dash.children).forEach(function (child) {
      var h2 = child.querySelector && child.querySelector('h2');
      var t = (h2 && h2.textContent) || '';
      /* aging and overdue live in their own folders; hours moved to the HR centre (wave52) */
      if (t.indexOf('Decision aging') >= 0 || t.indexOf('Overdue tasks') >= 0 || t === 'Team hours') return;
      var c = child.cloneNode(true);
      c.removeAttribute('id');
      if (c.querySelectorAll) c.querySelectorAll('[id]').forEach(function (n) { n.removeAttribute('id'); });
      /* the old Dashboard page carried a help guide; keep it reachable here
         (clicks are body-delegated by wave43, so a fresh button just works) */
      if (t.indexOf('Task completion dashboard') >= 0 && h2 && !c.querySelector('[data-w43help]')) {
        var ch2 = c.querySelector('h2');
        if (ch2) {
          var hb = document.createElement('button');
          hb.className = 'w43help';
          hb.setAttribute('data-w43help', 'dashboard');
          hb.innerHTML = '&#63; How this page works';
          ch2.appendChild(hb);
        }
      }
      holder.appendChild(c);
    });
    holder.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
      '<p class="w111note">Decisions waiting and overdue tasks have their own folders above. Team hours live in the HR centre.</p>');
  }

  function w111Body() {
    var bodyEl = document.getElementById('w111body');
    if (!bodyEl) return;
    if (!W111_OPEN) { bodyEl.style.display = 'none'; bodyEl.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    bodyEl.style.display = 'block';
    if (W111_OPEN === 'aging') bodyEl.innerHTML = w111AgingHtml(w111Aging());
    else if (W111_OPEN === 'overdue') bodyEl.innerHTML = w111OverdueHtml(w111Overdue());
    else if (W111_OPEN === 'numbers') w111NumbersInto(bodyEl);
  }

  function w111Render() {
    try {
      if (!w111Can()) return;
      var panel = document.getElementById('overview');
      if (!panel) return;
      var host = document.getElementById('w111folds');
      if (!host) {
        host = document.createElement('div');
        host.id = 'w111folds';
        panel.insertBefore(host, panel.firstChild);
      }
      /* keep the folders directly under the stat strip when it exists */
      var strip = document.getElementById('w35stats');
      if (strip && strip.parentElement === panel && host.previousElementSibling !== strip) {
        strip.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', host);
      }
      var nA = w111Aging().length;
      var nO = w111Overdue().length;
      host.innerHTML =
        '<div class="w111row">' +
          '<button type="button" class="w111btn' + (W111_OPEN === 'aging' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w111f="aging">&#128193; Decisions waiting <b>' + nA + '</b></button>' +
          '<button type="button" class="w111btn' + (W111_OPEN === 'overdue' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w111f="overdue">&#128193; Overdue tasks <b>' + nO + '</b></button>' +
          '<button type="button" class="w111btn' + (W111_OPEN === 'numbers' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w111f="numbers">&#128202; Delivery numbers</button>' +
        '</div><div id="w111body" style="display:none"></div>';
      w111Body();
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  function w111Open(which) {
    W111_OPEN = which;
    w111Render();
    var host = document.getElementById('w111folds');
    if (host && host.scrollIntoView) setTimeout(function () { host.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); }, 60);
  }

  /* ---------- interactions ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var fb = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w111f]');
    if (fb) {
      var which = fb.getAttribute('data-w111f');
      W111_OPEN = (W111_OPEN === which) ? null : which;
      w111Render();
      return;
    }
    var un = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w111undo]');
    if (un) {
      var id0 = Number(un.getAttribute('data-w111undo'));
      var tr0 = un.closest('tr');
      if (id0 && typeof toggleTask === 'function') {
        toggleTask(id0, false).then(function (ok) {
          if (!ok || !tr0) return;
          tr0.classList.remove('w111done');
          var cb0 = tr0.querySelector('input[data-w111close]');
          if (cb0) { cb0.checked = false; cb0.disabled = false; }
          un.remove();
          w111Counts();
        });
      }
    }
  });

  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var dc = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('input[data-w111decide]');
    if (dc) {
      var ref = Number(dc.getAttribute('data-w111decide'));
      var d = (typeof getDecisions === 'function' ? getDecisions() : []).find(function (x) { return x.ref === ref; });
      if (d && !d.done && dc.checked && typeof decideModal === 'function') decideModal(d, dc);
      else dc.checked = false;
      return;
    }
    var cb = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('input[data-w111close]');
    if (cb) {
      var id = Number(cb.getAttribute('data-w111close'));
      var tr = cb.closest('tr');
      if (!id || typeof toggleTask !== 'function') { cb.checked = false; return; }
      cb.disabled = true;
      toggleTask(id, true).then(function (ok) {
        cb.disabled = false;
        if (!ok) { cb.checked = false; return; }
        if (tr) {
          tr.classList.add('w111done');
          var old = tr.querySelector('.w111undo');
          if (old) old.remove();
          var u = document.createElement('span');
          u.className = 'w111undo';
          u.textContent = 'Undo';
          u.setAttribute('data-w111undo', String(id));
          var cell = tr.querySelector('.w111t');
          if (cell && cell.parentElement) cell.parentElement.appendChild(u); else tr.appendChild(u);
          setTimeout(function () {
            if (u.isConnected && tr.classList.contains('w111done')) w111Render();
          }, 10000);
        }
        w111Counts();
      });
    }
  }, true);

  function w111Counts() {
    try {
      var host = document.getElementById('w111folds');
      if (!host) return;
      var bs = host.querySelectorAll('.w111btn b');
      if (bs.length >= 2) { bs[0].textContent = String(w111Aging().length); bs[1].textContent = String(w111Overdue().length); }
    } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
  }

  /* ---------- 4. #dashboard lands on the merged page ---------- */
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp111 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      if (id === 'dashboard' && w111Can()) {
        __sp111('overview');
        try { w111Render(); w111Open('numbers'); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
        return;
      }
      __sp111(id);
      if (id === 'overview') { try { w111Render(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
    };
  }

  /* refresh after the canonical write paths repaint */
  if (typeof renderDecisions === 'function') {
    var __rdec111 = renderDecisions;
    renderDecisions = function () {
      __rdec111.apply(this, arguments);
      if (!W111_LOCK) { W111_LOCK = true; try { w111Render(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } W111_LOCK = false; }
    };
  }
  if (typeof renderDashboard === 'function') {
    var __rdash111 = renderDashboard;
    renderDashboard = function () {
      __rdash111.apply(this, arguments);
      if (!W111_LOCK) { W111_LOCK = true; try { w111Render(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } W111_LOCK = false; }
    };
  }

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa111 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa111.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        w111Tab();
        w111Render();
        w111Donations();
        /* a #dashboard deep link resolves before roles load; re-route it now */
        if (typeof WA_CUR_PANEL !== 'undefined' && WA_CUR_PANEL === 'dashboard' && w111Can()) showPanel('dashboard');
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v112 getting-started checklist on My page (24/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "I'd like to add an onboarding experience for users that are
   added to the hub." Chosen shape: a getting-started checklist card on
   My page, shown ONCE to everyone (current staff included), until the
   person finishes it or dismisses it.
   Five steps, each auto-ticked by doing the real thing, and each can
   also be ticked by hand (someone with no tasks yet can still finish):
     profile  open your own person page        (#person/<your id>)
     dept     open your department page        (showPanel <your dept>)
     task     tick off a task                  (toggleTask done=true)
     brain    ask the Hub a question           (send in the chat panel)
     info     read Important info              (showPanel importantinfo)
   Steps the person cannot reach are dropped (ACCESS-GATE RULE: the
   department and Important info steps need their tabs in wa40Allowed).
   Board viewers and non-staff never see the card.
   State lives server-side in wa_onboarding (one row per email, RLS: own
   row for staff, admins read all), so it follows the person across
   browsers. Rows are created lazily on the first tick or dismiss.
   Marks that happen before the row loads are queued and applied after.
   Wrapped (CURRENT bindings at load): renderMyPage, showPanel,
   toggleTask, startApp. Loads BEFORE wave110 in MANIFEST (110 LAST).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa112) return; window.__wa112 = true;

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent =
    '#w112card{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-left:4px solid #103F39;border-radius:14px;padding:16px 18px 12px;margin:0 0 16px;box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(16,63,57,.06);}' +
    '#w112card .w112top{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;}' +
    '#w112card h3{margin:0;font-size:16px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '#w112card .w112count{font-size:12.5px;color:#797773;font-weight:700;}' +
    '#w112card .w112links{margin-left:auto;display:flex;gap:12px;}' +
    '#w112card .w112links a{font-size:12px;color:#797773;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;}' +
    '#w112card .w112bar{height:6px;border-radius:999px;background:#ECEBE3;margin:10px 0 8px;overflow:hidden;}' +
    '#w112card .w112bar i{display:block;height:100%;background:#103F39;border-radius:999px;transition:width .3s;}' +
    '#w112card .w112intro{font-size:13px;color:#1F201E;margin:0 0 6px;line-height:1.5;}' +
    '.w112step{display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px;padding:9px 0;border-top:1px solid #f0f4f2;}' +
    '.w112step:first-of-type{border-top:none;}' +
    '.w112step input[type=checkbox]{accent-color:#103F39;width:17px;height:17px;margin:2px 0 0;cursor:pointer;flex:0 0 auto;}' +
    '.w112step .w112txt{flex:1 1 auto;min-width:0;}' +
    '.w112step .w112t{font-size:13.5px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w112step .w112d{font-size:12.5px;color:#797773;line-height:1.45;margin-top:2px;}' +
    '.w112step.done .w112t{text-decoration:line-through;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w112step.done .w112d{display:none;}' +
    '.w112go{flex:0 0 auto;background:#ECEBE3;color:#103F39;border:none;border-radius:999px;padding:6px 12px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;}' +
    '.w112go:hover{background:#ECEBE3;}' +
    '.w112step.done .w112go{display:none;}' +
    '#w112pill{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;background:#ECEBE3;color:#103F39;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;padding:7px 14px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;margin:0 0 14px;}' +
    '#w112card.w112all{border-left-color:#DCA527;}' +
    '#w112card .w112yay{font-size:13.5px;color:#103F39;margin:6px 0 4px;line-height:1.5;}' +
    '@media (max-width:560px){.w112step{flex-wrap:wrap;}.w112go{margin-left:27px;}#w112card .w112links{margin-left:0;width:100%;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var W112 = {
    loaded: false,     /* row fetched (or confirmed absent) */
    row: null,         /* { email, steps, completed_at, dismissed_at } */
    pending: [],       /* marks queued before load */
    collapsed: false,  /* "Hide for now" this session */
    myId: null,
    justFinished: false
  };
  var DEPTS112 = ['opsmtl', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr', 'board'];

  function w112Email() {
    try { return ((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(); } catch (e) { return ''; }
  }
  function w112Eligible() {
    try { if (typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board') return false; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return false; } catch (e) { return false; }
    return !!w112Email();
  }
  function w112Allowed(tab) {
    try { return typeof wa40Allowed !== 'function' || wa40Allowed().indexOf(tab) >= 0; } catch (e) { return true; }
  }
  function w112MyDept() {
    try { return (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.department) || null; } catch (e) { return null; }
  }

  function w112Steps() {
    var dept = w112MyDept();
    var deptLabel = dept && typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[dept] ? DEPT_LABEL[dept] : 'your team';
    var s = [];
    s.push({ id: 'profile', t: 'Check your profile', d: 'Open your page and make sure your name, role and team look right. If something is off, tell an admin.', go: 'Open my profile' });
    var deptOk = dept ? w112Allowed(dept) : DEPTS112.some(w112Allowed);
    if (deptOk) s.push({ id: 'dept', t: 'Visit your team', d: 'Open the ' + deptLabel + ' page: tasks, workstreams and meeting notes live there.', go: dept ? 'Go to ' + deptLabel : 'Open Teams' });
    s.push({ id: 'task', t: 'Tick off a task', d: 'Finish something with your name on it and tick it, here on My page or on your team page. Nothing assigned yet? Tick this one yourself.', go: 'See my tasks' });
    if (document.getElementById('waChatBtn')) s.push({ id: 'brain', t: 'Ask the Hub a question', d: 'The assistant answers from everything in the Hub. Try: what is my team working on this week?', go: 'Ask now' });
    if (w112Allowed('importantinfo')) s.push({ id: 'info', t: 'Read Important info', d: 'Policies, key contacts and what everyone at Welfare Avenue should know.', go: 'Open Important info' });
    /* wave272: encouraged, and it ticks itself once the Hub is added (or tick it yourself) */
    if (window.w272) s.push({ id: 'app', t: 'Add the Hub as an app', d: 'Keep the Hub one tap away on your phone and computer, with notifications when someone needs you. Optional, and under a minute.', go: 'Show me how' });
    return s;
  }
  function w112Done(id) { return !!(W112.row && W112.row.steps && W112.row.steps[id]); }
  function w112Visible() {
    if (!w112Eligible() || !W112.loaded) return false;
    if (W112.row && W112.row.dismissed_at) return false;
    if (W112.row && W112.row.completed_at && !W112.justFinished) return false;
    return true;
  }

  async function w112Save() {
    var r = W112.row;
    if (!r) return;
    try {
      await db.from('wa_onboarding').upsert({
        email: r.email, steps: r.steps,
        completed_at: r.completed_at || null, dismissed_at: r.dismissed_at || null,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal: the card stays usable; next change retries */ }
  }
  function w112Ensure() {
    if (!W112.row) W112.row = { email: w112Email(), steps: {}, completed_at: null, dismissed_at: null };
    if (!W112.row.steps || typeof W112.row.steps !== 'object') W112.row.steps = {};
    return W112.row;
  }

  function w112Mark(id, on, manual) {
    if (!w112Eligible()) return;
    if (!W112.loaded) { W112.pending.push([id, on !== false]); return; }
    var r = w112Ensure();
    if (r.dismissed_at || (r.completed_at && !W112.justFinished)) return;
    var val = on !== false;
    if (!!r.steps[id] === val) return;
    if (val) r.steps[id] = new Date().toISOString(); else delete r.steps[id];
    var steps = w112Steps();
    var all = steps.every(function (s) { return !!r.steps[s.id]; });
    if (all && !r.completed_at) { r.completed_at = new Date().toISOString(); W112.justFinished = true; }
    if (!all && r.completed_at) { r.completed_at = null; }
    w112Save();
    if (val && !manual) {
      var st = steps.filter(function (s) { return s.id === id; })[0];
      if (st) { try { toast('Getting started: "' + st.t + '" done'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    }
    w112Paint();
  }

  async function w112Load() {
    if (W112.loaded || !w112Eligible()) return;
    try {
      var em = w112Email();
      var res = await db.from('wa_onboarding').select('*').eq('email', em).maybeSingle();
      W112.row = (res && res.data) ? res.data : null;
    } catch (e) { W112.row = null; }
    try {
      var em2 = w112Email();
      var mr = await db.from('team_members').select('id, email').eq('email', em2).maybeSingle();
      W112.myId = (mr && mr.data && mr.data.id) ? mr.data.id : null;
    } catch (e) { W112.myId = null; }
    W112.loaded = true;
    var q = W112.pending.slice(); W112.pending = [];
    q.forEach(function (p) { w112Mark(p[0], p[1], false); });
    w112Paint();
  }

  function w112Html() {
    var steps = w112Steps();
    var n = steps.filter(function (s) { return w112Done(s.id); }).length;
    var pct = steps.length ? Math.round(100 * n / steps.length) : 0;
    if (W112.collapsed) {
      return '<button id="w112pill" data-w112open="1">&#10003; Getting started: ' + n + ' of ' + steps.length + ' done. Show</button>';
    }
    var all = n === steps.length;
    var h = '<div id="w112card"' + (all ? ' class="w112all"' : '') + '>'
      + '<div class="w112top"><h3>' + (all ? 'You are all set' : 'Get started with the Hub') + '</h3>'
      + '<span class="w112count">' + n + ' of ' + steps.length + ' done</span>'
      + '<span class="w112links">' + (all ? '<a data-w112close="1">Close</a>' : '<a data-w112hide="1">Hide for now</a><a data-w112dismiss="1">Don\'t show again</a>') + '</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w112bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div>';
    if (all) {
      h += '<p class="w112yay">Nice work. You know your way around now: Home is your queue, your team page holds the work, and the assistant is one tap away whenever you need it.</p>';
    } else {
      h += '<p class="w112intro">A few quick things to find your way around. Each one ticks itself when you do it.</p>';
      steps.forEach(function (s) {
        var d = w112Done(s.id);
        h += '<div class="w112step' + (d ? ' done' : '') + '">'
          + '<input type="checkbox" data-w112cb="' + s.id + '"' + (d ? ' checked' : '') + ' title="' + (d ? 'Mark not done' : 'Mark done') + '" />'
          + '<div class="w112txt"><div class="w112t">' + esc(s.t) + '</div><div class="w112d">' + esc(s.d) + '</div></div>'
          + '<button class="w112go" data-w112go="' + s.id + '">' + esc(s.go) + '</button>'
          + '</div>';
      });
    }
    return h + '</div>';
  }

  function w112Paint() {
    var body = document.getElementById('mpBody');
    var old = document.getElementById('w112wrap');
    if (!body || !w112Visible()) { if (old) old.remove(); return; }
    var wrap = old;
    if (!wrap) {
      wrap = document.createElement('div');
      wrap.id = 'w112wrap';
      var anchor = body.querySelector('.mp-online');
      if (anchor && anchor.parentNode === body) body.insertBefore(wrap, anchor.nextSibling);
      else body.insertBefore(wrap, body.firstChild);
    }
    wrap.innerHTML = w112Html();
  }

  function w112Go(id) {
    if (id === 'profile') {
      if (W112.myId) { location.hash = '#person/' + W112.myId; }
      else { try { toast('Your profile is not linked yet: ask an admin to add your email to the team roster.'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'dept') {
      var d = w112MyDept();
      if (d && w112Allowed(d)) { location.hash = '#dept/' + d; return; }
      var any = DEPTS112.filter(w112Allowed)[0];
      if (any) location.hash = '#dept/' + any;
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'task') {
      var secs = document.querySelectorAll('#mpBody .mp-sec');
      var target = null;
      secs.forEach(function (s) { var h3 = s.querySelector('h3'); if (!target && h3 && /^My tasks/.test(h3.textContent || '')) target = s; });
      if (target) { try { target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch (e) { target.scrollIntoView(); } }
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'brain') {
      var btn = document.getElementById('waChatBtn');
      var panel = document.getElementById('waChatPanel');
      if (btn && panel && panel.style.display !== 'flex') btn.click();
      setTimeout(function () {
        var inp = document.getElementById('w38in') || document.getElementById('wacInput');
        if (inp) { if (!inp.value) inp.value = 'What is my team working on this week?'; try { inp.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      }, 60);
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'info') { location.hash = '#importantinfo'; }
    if (id === 'app') { try { if (window.w272) window.w272.setupOpen(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }

  /* card interactions (delegated, survives repaints) */
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var cb = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('input[data-w112cb]');
    if (!cb) return;
    w112Mark(cb.getAttribute('data-w112cb'), cb.checked, true);
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target : null;
    if (!t) return;
    var go = t.closest('[data-w112go]');
    if (go) { w112Go(go.getAttribute('data-w112go')); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w112hide]')) { W112.collapsed = true; w112Paint(); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w112open]')) { W112.collapsed = false; w112Paint(); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w112dismiss]')) {
      var r = w112Ensure();
      r.dismissed_at = new Date().toISOString();
      w112Save(); w112Paint();
      try { toast('Checklist hidden. The 60 second tour is still in any help popup.'); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ }
      return;
    }
    if (t.closest('[data-w112close]')) { W112.justFinished = false; w112Paint(); return; }
  });

  /* auto-tick: asking the Hub (capture phase, before send clears the input) */
  function w112AskHook(inp) { if (inp && (inp.value || '').trim()) w112Mark('brain', true, false); }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w38send, #wacSend');
    if (!b) return;
    w112AskHook(document.getElementById(b.id === 'w38send' ? 'w38in' : 'wacInput'));
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key !== 'Enter') return;
    var inp = e.target;
    if (inp && (inp.id === 'w38in' || inp.id === 'wacInput')) w112AskHook(inp);
  }, true);

  /* auto-tick: pages visited */
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp112 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      var out = __sp112.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        if (id === 'importantinfo') w112Mark('info', true, false);
        else if (DEPTS112.indexOf(id) >= 0) {
          var mine = w112MyDept();
          if (!mine || mine === id) w112Mark('dept', true, false);
        } else if (id === 'personpage') {
          var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
          if (W112.myId && h === 'person/' + W112.myId) w112Mark('profile', true, false);
          else if (!W112.loaded) {
            /* id not known yet: re-check once the row and id have loaded */
            var hh = h;
            setTimeout(function () { if (W112.myId && hh === 'person/' + W112.myId) w112Mark('profile', true, false); }, 2500);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) { /* never block navigation */ }
      return out;
    };
  }

  /* auto-tick: finishing a task anywhere in the Hub */
  if (typeof toggleTask === 'function') {
    var __tt112 = toggleTask;
    toggleTask = async function (ref, done) {
      var ok = await __tt112.apply(this, arguments);
      try { if (ok && done === true) w112Mark('task', true, false); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return ok;
    };
  }

  /* paint on every My page render */
  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __rm112 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      var r = await __rm112.apply(this, arguments);
      try { w112Paint(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }

  /* load state once the app (and roles) are up */
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa112 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa112.apply(this, arguments);
      try { await w112Load(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }

  /* wave122: the Training academy shows these steps in its Onboarding section (ticks work there even after the My page card is dismissed) */
  window.w112Api = {
    eligible: w112Eligible,
    loaded: function () { return W112.loaded; },
    load: w112Load,
    steps: w112Steps,
    done: w112Done,
    row: function () { return W112.row; },
    go: w112Go,
    set: function (id, on) {
      if (!w112Eligible() || !W112.loaded) return false;
      var r = w112Ensure(); var val = on !== false;
      if (!!r.steps[id] === val) return true;
      if (val) r.steps[id] = new Date().toISOString(); else delete r.steps[id];
      var all = w112Steps().every(function (s) { return !!r.steps[s.id]; });
      if (all && !r.completed_at) r.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
      if (!all && r.completed_at) r.completed_at = null;
      w112Save(); try { w112Paint(); } catch (e) { /* My page may not be on screen */ }
      return true;
    }
  };
  window.w112Debug = function () { return { loaded: W112.loaded, row: W112.row, myId: W112.myId, steps: w112Steps().map(function (s) { return s.id; }) }; };
})();

/* =====================================================================
   WA v113 People and access: user management inside Admin (24/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "I need to also have a user management module with the
   ability to invite new users." Chosen shape: inside the Admin page,
   one "People and access" card that unifies three things the Hub kept
   apart: the roster (team_members), Hub access (hub_roles) and the
   sign-in system (wa_hub_logins). Every person gets one row with a
   status: Active (has signed in), Invited (account or access exists,
   never signed in), Not invited (has an email, nothing else), No email
   (cannot be invited until one is added), Removed (blocked). Per row:
   invite, resend, copy a one-time sign-in link, add a missing email,
   change access, per-page overrides (the wave72 modal), remove or
   invite again. Checkboxes plus "Invite selected" and an "Invite by
   list" paste box do bulk invites through hub-invite v2 'bulk_invite'.
   Leaders: hub-invite v2 lets a department leader invite members into
   their own department (server forces department and member access);
   the button lives on their department page in the links row. Admins
   see the same button on every department page.
   The classic "who sees what" table is kept (never remove a feature),
   folded under a link on its card; wave110's Access requests card in
   that card stays visible. Slack DM on invite comes from hub-invite v2
   calling notify 'invited'. Wrapped (CURRENT bindings at load):
   wa40Render, showPanel, startApp. Loads BEFORE wave110 in MANIFEST.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
