  document.head.appendChild(css);

  var FOUNDERS113 = ['steel.macdonald@welfareavenue.com', 'steelmacdonald@gmail.com', 'suhaib.abdillahi@welfareavenue.com'];
  var DEPTS113 = ['opsmtl', 'progdev', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr', 'board'];
  var ROLES113 = ['admin', 'leader', 'member', 'viewer'];
  var ROLE_HELP = { member: 'Member (the work surfaces)', leader: 'Leader (every page)', viewer: 'Viewer (overview only)', admin: 'Admin (manages everything)' };
  var STATUS_LABEL = { active: 'Active', invited: 'Invited, not signed in', notinvited: 'Not invited yet', noemail: 'No email yet', removed: 'Removed', blocked: 'Removed' };
  var W113 = { people: [], roles: {}, logins: {}, loaded: false, q: '', dept: '', status: '', sel: {}, loading: null };

  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function isAdmin() {
    if (FOUNDERS113.indexOf(myEmail()) >= 0) return true;
    try { return !!(WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'); } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function isLeader() { try { return !!(WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'leader'); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function myDept() { try { return (WA_ME && WA_ME.department) || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function deptLabel(d) { try { return (DEPT_LABEL && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; } catch (e) { return d || ''; } }
  function siteUrl() { return 'https://hub.welfareavenue.com/'; }   /* wave190: the Hub's own address (the Netlify one still works) */
  function ago(ts) {
    if (!ts) return '';
    var ms = Date.now() - new Date(ts).getTime();
    if (isNaN(ms) || ms < 0) return 'just now';
    var m = Math.floor(ms / 60000);
    if (m < 60) return (m < 1 ? 'just now' : m + ' min ago');
    var h = Math.floor(m / 60);
    if (h < 48) return h + ' h ago';
    var d = Math.floor(h / 24);
    if (d < 60) return d + ' days ago';
    return String(ts).slice(0, 10);
  }
  function copyText(text) {
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) return navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(function () { return true; }, function () { return fallbackCopy(text); });
    } catch (e) { /* fall through */ }
    return Promise.resolve(fallbackCopy(text));
  }
  function fallbackCopy(text) {
    try {
      var ta = document.createElement('textarea');
      ta.value = text; ta.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:-9999px;top:0;';
      document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select();
      var ok = document.execCommand('copy');
      ta.remove();
      return !!ok;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function inviteMessage(name) {
    var first = String(name || '').split(' ')[0] || 'there';
    return 'Hi ' + first + ', you have been added to the Welfare Avenue Hub, the team\'s shared workspace: meetings turn into tasks, goals and decisions, and everyone can see how the work moves.\n\nSign in with your Welfare Avenue Google account at ' + siteUrl() + '\n\nThe 60 second tour starts on your first visit, and a short getting-started checklist on your home page walks you through the rest.';
  }

  /* ---------- data ---------- */
  async function load() {
    if (W113.loading) return W113.loading;
    W113.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await Promise.all([
          db.from('team_members').select('id,name,email,department,role,active').order('name'),
          db.from('hub_roles').select('*'),
          db.rpc('wa_hub_logins')
        ]);
        W113.people = (r[0] && r[0].data) || [];
        W113.roles = {};
        ((r[1] && r[1].data) || []).forEach(function (x) { W113.roles[lower(x.email)] = x; });
        W113.logins = {};
        ((r[2] && r[2].data) || []).forEach(function (l) { W113.logins[lower(l.email)] = l; });
        W113.loaded = true;
      } catch (e) { /* keep whatever we had */ }
      W113.loading = null;
    })();
    return W113.loading;
  }
  function mk(p, em) {
    var role = em ? W113.roles[em] : null;
    var login = em ? W113.logins[em] : null;
    var st;
    if (role && role.role === 'blocked') st = 'blocked';
    else if (p && p.active === false) st = 'removed';
    else if (!em) st = 'noemail';
    else if (login && login.last_sign_in_at) st = 'active';
    else if (login || role) st = 'invited';
    else st = 'notinvited';
    return {
      id: p ? p.id : null,
      name: p ? (p.name || '') : ((role && role.note) || em.split('@')[0]),
      title: p ? (p.role || '') : '',
      dept: p ? (p.department || '') : '',
      email: em,
      role: role ? role.role : '',
      tabs: role && Array.isArray(role.tabs) && role.tabs.length ? role.tabs : null,
      login: login || null,
      status: st,
      founder: FOUNDERS113.indexOf(em) >= 0
    };
  }
  function allRows() {
    var out = [], seen = {};
    W113.people.forEach(function (p) {
      var em = lower(p.email);
      if (em) seen[em] = 1;
      out.push(mk(p, em));
    });
    Object.keys(W113.roles).forEach(function (em) { if (!seen[em]) out.push(mk(null, em)); });
    return out;
  }
  function isRemoved(r) { return r.status === 'removed' || r.status === 'blocked'; }
  function matches(r) {
    if (W113.dept && r.dept !== W113.dept) return false;
    if (W113.status && r.status !== W113.status && !(W113.status === 'removed' && isRemoved(r))) return false;
    if (W113.q) {
      var q = W113.q.toLowerCase();
      if ((r.name + ' ' + r.email + ' ' + r.title + ' ' + deptLabel(r.dept)).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) < 0) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }
  function selectable(r) { return !!r.email && r.status !== 'active'; }
  function rowKey(r) { return r.email || ('id:' + r.id); }

  /* ---------- card ---------- */
  function ensureCard() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('adminpanel');
    if (!panel || !isAdmin()) return null;
    var card = document.getElementById('w113card');
    if (card) return card;
    card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'card';
    card.id = 'w113card';
    card.innerHTML = '<h2>People and access</h2>'
      + '<p class="read">Everyone on the roster, what they can see, and whether they have signed in. Invite people one at a time or in bulk; the Hub emails a sign-in invitation, sends a Slack welcome when it can find them, and gives you a message and a one-time link to share yourself. Department leaders can invite members into their own team from their department page.</p>'
      + '<div id="w113body"><p class="read">Loading people...</p></div>';
    panel.insertBefore(card, panel.firstChild);
    foldClassic(panel);
    return card;
  }
  function foldClassic(panel) {
    try {
      var h2s = panel.querySelectorAll('.card > h2');
      for (var i = 0; i < h2s.length; i++) {
        if ((h2s[i].textContent || '').indexOf('Admin: who sees what') === 0 && !h2s[i].querySelector('[data-w113fold]')) {
          var card = h2s[i].parentNode;
          var p = card.querySelector(':scope > p.read');
          var body = card.querySelector('#w40body');
          if (p) p.style.display = 'none';
          if (body) body.style.display = 'none';
          var a = document.createElement('a');
          a.setAttribute('data-w113fold', '1');
          a.style.cssText = 'font-size:12px;font-weight:400;margin-left:12px;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;';
          a.textContent = 'Show the classic roles table';
          a.addEventListener('click', function () {
            var open = body && body.style.display !== 'none';
            if (p) p.style.display = open ? 'none' : '';
            if (body) body.style.display = open ? 'none' : '';
            a.textContent = open ? 'Show the classic roles table' : 'Hide the classic roles table';
          });
          h2s[i].appendChild(a);
          h2s[i].childNodes[0].textContent = 'Access requests and the classic roles table';
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
  }

  function paint() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w113body');
    if (!body) return;
    if (!W113.loaded) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Loading people...</p>'; return; }
    var rows = allRows();
    var counts = { active: 0, invited: 0, notinvited: 0, noemail: 0, removed: 0 };
    rows.forEach(function (r) { if (isRemoved(r)) counts.removed++; else counts[r.status]++; });
    var chip = function (key, label) {
      return '<span class="w113chip' + (W113.status === key ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w113st="' + key + '"><b>' + counts[key] + '</b>' + label + '</span>';
    };
    var selCount = Object.keys(W113.sel).length;
    var html = '<div class="w113sum">'
      + chip('active', 'active') + chip('invited', 'invited, not signed in') + chip('notinvited', 'not invited yet') + chip('noemail', 'no email yet') + chip('removed', 'removed')
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w113bar">'
      + '<input type="text" id="w113q" placeholder="Search name, email or role" value="' + esc(W113.q) + '" />'
      + '<select id="w113dept"><option value="">All departments</option>' + DEPTS113.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (W113.dept === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(deptLabel(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + '<div class="w113right">'
      + (selCount ? '<button class="w113btn" data-w113bulk="1">Invite selected (' + selCount + ')</button>' : '')
      + '<button class="w113btn ghost" data-w113list="1">Invite by list</button>'
      + '<button class="w113btn" data-w113new="1">+ Invite someone</button>'
      + '</div></div>';
    var live = rows.filter(function (r) { return !isRemoved(r) && matches(r); });
    var removed = rows.filter(function (r) { return isRemoved(r) && (W113.status === 'removed' || W113.status === '') && matches(r); });
    var order = { notinvited: 0, noemail: 1, invited: 2, active: 3 };
    live.sort(function (a, b) {
      var d = (order[a.status] || 0) - (order[b.status] || 0);
      return d || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name));
    });
    var selAll = live.filter(selectable);
    html += '<table id="w113tbl"><thead><tr>'
      + '<th><input type="checkbox" id="w113all" title="Select everyone below who can be invited"' + (selAll.length && selAll.every(function (r) { return W113.sel[rowKey(r)]; }) ? ' checked' : '') + (selAll.length ? '' : ' disabled') + ' /></th>'
      + '<th>Person</th><th class="w113hide">Department</th><th>Email</th><th>Status</th><th>Access</th><th class="w113hide">Pages</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>';
    if (!live.length) html += '<tr><td colspan="8" class="w113mut">Nobody matches.</td></tr>';
    live.forEach(function (r) { html += rowHtml(r); });
    html += '</tbody></table>';
    if (W113.status !== 'removed' && counts.removed) {
      html += '<details' + (W113.status === 'removed' ? ' open' : '') + '><summary>Removed people (' + counts.removed + ')</summary><table id="w113tbl2" style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px;margin-top:8px"><tbody>'
        + removed.map(function (r) { return rowHtml(r); }).join('') + '</tbody></table></details>';
    } else if (W113.status === 'removed') {
      html += (removed.length ? '' : '<p class="w113mut" style="margin-top:8px">Nobody has been removed.</p>');
    }
    html += '<p class="w113mut" style="margin-top:10px">Status: Active means the person has signed in at least once. Invited means an account or access exists but they have not signed in yet: resend the email or copy a sign-in link for them. Access changes apply the next time they load the Hub.</p>';
    body.innerHTML = html;
  }
  function rowHtml(r) {
    var key = rowKey(r);
    var sel = !!W113.sel[key];
    var stTxt = STATUS_LABEL[r.status] || r.status;
    var sub = r.status === 'active' && r.login ? 'signed in ' + ago(r.login.last_sign_in_at) : (r.status === 'invited' && r.login ? 'account created ' + ago(r.login.account_created) : '');
    var roleSel = r.email && !isRemoved(r)
      ? '<select data-w40r="' + esc(r.email) + '" data-w113role="' + esc(r.email) + '">' + ROLES113.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x + '"' + (x === (r.role || 'member') ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + x + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' + (r.founder ? '<div class="w113mut">founder, always admin</div>' : '')
      : '<span class="w113mut">' + (isRemoved(r) ? 'blocked' : 'needs an email') + '</span>';
    var pages = r.email && !isRemoved(r) ? '<a data-w40t="' + esc(r.email) + '" style="font-size:12px;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;white-space:nowrap">' + (r.tabs ? esc(r.tabs.length + ' pages') : 'role default') + '</a>' : '';
    /* wave127: the Access column picks an access profile (or a classic role); Pages says what the profile gives */
    try {
      if (window.w127 && r.email && !isRemoved(r)) {
        if (typeof window.w127.accessCell === 'function') { var ac = window.w127.accessCell(r); if (ac) roleSel = ac + (r.founder ? '<div class="w113mut">founder, always admin</div>' : ''); }
        if (typeof window.w127.pagesCell === 'function') { var pc = window.w127.pagesCell(r); if (pc) pages = pc; }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* the classic cells stay */ }
    var emailCell = r.email ? '<span style="font-size:12px">' + esc(r.email) + '</span>'
      : (r.id ? '<div class="w113em"><input type="text" data-w113emin="' + r.id + '" placeholder="name@welfareavenue.com" /><a data-w113emsave="' + r.id + '" style="font-size:12px;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline">Save</a></div>' : '');
    var acts = [];
    if (isRemoved(r)) {
      if (r.email) acts.push('<a data-w113again="' + esc(r.email) + '">Invite again</a>');
    } else if (r.status === 'notinvited') {
      acts.push('<a data-w113inv="' + esc(r.email) + '">Invite</a>');
      if (!r.founder) acts.push('<a class="rm" data-w113rm="' + esc(r.email) + '">Remove</a>');
    } else if (r.status === 'invited') {
      acts.push('<a data-w113resend="' + esc(r.email) + '">Resend</a>');
      acts.push('<a data-w113link="' + esc(r.email) + '">Copy sign-in link</a>');
      if (!r.founder) acts.push('<a class="rm" data-w113rm="' + esc(r.email) + '">Remove</a>');
    } else if (r.status === 'active') {
      acts.push('<a data-w113link="' + esc(r.email) + '">Sign-in link</a>');
      if (!r.founder) acts.push('<a class="rm" data-w113rm="' + esc(r.email) + '">Remove</a>');
    }
    return '<tr' + (sel ? ' class="w113sel"' : '') + ' data-w113row="' + esc(key) + '">'
      + '<td>' + (selectable(r) ? '<input type="checkbox" data-w113cb="' + esc(key) + '"' + (sel ? ' checked' : '') + ' />' : '') + '</td>'
      + '<td><b>' + esc(r.name) + '</b>' + (r.title ? '<div class="w113mut">' + esc(r.title) + '</div>' : '') + '</td>'
      + '<td class="w113hide">' + esc(deptLabel(r.dept)) + '</td>'
      + '<td>' + emailCell + '</td>'
      + '<td><span class="w113st ' + esc(r.status) + '">' + esc(stTxt) + '</span>' + (sub ? '<div class="w113mut">' + esc(sub) + '</div>' : '') + '</td>'
      + '<td>' + roleSel + '</td>'
      + '<td class="w113hide">' + pages + '</td>'
      + '<td><div class="w113act">' + acts.join('') + '</div></td></tr>';
  }
  async function refresh() {
    await load();
    try { if (typeof wa40Load === 'function') await wa40Load(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    paint();
  }

  /* ---------- server calls ---------- */
  async function call(body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('hub-invite', { body: body });
    if (r.error) {
      var msg = '';
      try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) msg = (await r.error.context.json()).error; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      throw new Error(msg || r.error.message || 'failed');
    }
    if (!r.data) throw new Error('no reply from the Hub');
    if (r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return r.data;
  }
  function findRow(email) { return allRows().filter(function (r) { return r.email === lower(email); })[0] || null; }

  /* ---------- modals ---------- */
  function modal(html) {
    openModal('<div class="w113modal">' + html + '</div>');
  }
  function resultHtml(res, name) {
    var h = '<div class="w113res">'
      + '<div><b class="ok">' + (res.existed ? 'Profile updated' : 'Profile created') + '</b> for ' + esc(name || res.name || res.email) + ' with ' + esc(res.hub_role || '') + ' access.</div>'
      + '<div>Sign-in email: ' + (res.email_sent ? '<b class="ok">sent</b>' : '<b class="no">not sent</b>' + (res.note && res.note !== 'not requested' ? ' <span class="w113mut">(' + esc(String(res.note).slice(0, 110)) + ')</span>' : (res.note === 'not requested' ? ' <span class="w113mut">(you chose not to)</span>' : ''))) + '</div>'
      + '<div>Slack welcome: ' + (res.slack === 'sent' || res.slack === 'sent_channel' ? '<b class="ok">sent</b>' : '<b class="no">not sent</b> <span class="w113mut">(' + esc(res.slack === 'no_slack' ? 'not found in the WA Slack yet' : (res.slack || 'skipped')) + ')</span>') + '</div>'
      + '</div>'
      + '<label>A message you can paste into Slack or email</label>'
      + '<textarea id="w113msg" readonly>' + esc(inviteMessage(name || res.name)) + '</textarea>'
      + '<div class="w113acts">'
      + '<button class="w113btn ghost" data-w113copymsg="1">Copy the message</button>'
      + (isAdmin() ? '<button class="w113btn ghost" data-w113copylink="' + esc(res.email) + '">Copy a one-time sign-in link</button>' : '')
      + '<button class="w113btn" data-w113done="1">Done</button>'
      + '</div><div class="w113ok" id="w113okline"></div>';
    return h;
  }
  function inviteModal(preset) {
    preset = preset || {};
    var leader = !isAdmin() && isLeader();
    var dept = leader ? myDept() : (preset.department || myDept() || DEPTS113[0]);
    var deptField = leader
      ? '<div class="w113lock">' + esc(deptLabel(dept)) + ' <span class="w113mut">(your department)</span></div>'
      : '<select id="w113d">' + DEPTS113.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (d === dept ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(deptLabel(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>';
    var accessField = leader
      ? '<div class="w113lock">Member (the work surfaces)</div>'
      : '<select id="w113hr">' + ROLES113.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return ['member', 'leader', 'viewer', 'admin'].indexOf(a) - ['member', 'leader', 'viewer', 'admin'].indexOf(b); }).map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x + '"' + (x === (preset.hub_role || 'member') ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(ROLE_HELP[x]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>';
    modal('<h3>' + (preset.again ? 'Invite ' + esc(preset.name || preset.email) + ' again' : (preset.email ? 'Invite ' + esc(preset.name || preset.email) : 'Invite someone to the Hub')) + '</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">' + (leader ? 'They join your department as a member. An admin can widen their access later.' : 'This creates their profile, sets what they can see, emails a sign-in invitation and sends a Slack welcome when the Hub can find them. They can also sign in with Google using this email straight away.') + '</p>'
      + '<div class="w113two"><div><label>Full name</label><input type="text" id="w113n" placeholder="First Last" value="' + esc(preset.name || '') + '" /></div>'
      + '<div><label>Email</label><input type="text" id="w113e" placeholder="name@welfareavenue.com" value="' + esc(preset.email || '') + '"' + (preset.email ? ' readonly' : '') + ' /></div></div>'
      + '<div class="w113two"><div><label>Department</label>' + deptField + '</div>'
      + '<div><label>Role title</label><input type="text" id="w113rt" placeholder="e.g. Content manager" value="' + esc(preset.role_title || '') + '" /></div></div>'
      + '<label>Hub access</label>' + accessField
      + '<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-top:12px"><input type="checkbox" id="w113se" checked /> Email them a sign-in invitation now</label>'
      + '<div class="w113acts" id="w113formacts"><button class="w113btn" id="w113go">' + (preset.again ? 'Invite again' : 'Create the profile and invite') + '</button><button class="w113btn ghost" data-w113cancel="1">Cancel</button></div>'
      + '<div id="w113out"></div>');
    var go = document.getElementById('w113go');
    go.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var name = (document.getElementById('w113n').value || '').trim();
      var email = lower(document.getElementById('w113e').value);
      if (!name || email.indexOf('@') < 1) { toast('A name and a valid email are needed'); return; }
      var body = { action: 'invite', name: name, email: email, role_title: (document.getElementById('w113rt').value || '').trim(), send_email: !!document.getElementById('w113se').checked };
      var dsel = document.getElementById('w113d'); if (dsel) body.department = dsel.value;
      var hsel = document.getElementById('w113hr'); if (hsel) body.hub_role = hsel.value;
      go.disabled = true; go.textContent = 'Inviting...';
      try {
        var res = await call(body);
        res.hub_role = hsel ? hsel.value : 'member';
        var form = document.querySelector('.w113modal');
        if (form) form.innerHTML = '<h3>' + esc(name) + ' is on the Hub</h3>' + resultHtml(res, name);
        toast(res.email_sent ? 'Invitation email sent' : 'Profile saved; share the message or a link with them');
        refresh();
      } catch (e) {
        go.disabled = false; go.textContent = preset.again ? 'Invite again' : 'Create the profile and invite';
        toast('Could not invite: ' + String(e.message || e).slice(0, 90));
      }
    });
  }
  function parseList(text) {
    var out = [];
    String(text || '').split(/\n|;/).forEach(function (line) {
      line = line.trim();
      if (!line) return;
      var em = null, name = '';
      var m = line.match(/<([^>]+)>/);
      if (m) { em = lower(m[1]); name = line.slice(0, m.index).replace(/[,"]/g, ' ').trim(); }
      else {
        var parts = line.split(/[,\t]/).map(function (s) { return s.trim(); });
        var hit = parts.filter(function (s) { return s.indexOf('@') > 0; })[0];
        if (hit) { em = lower(hit); name = parts.filter(function (s) { return s !== hit; }).join(' ').trim(); }
        else if (line.indexOf('@') > 0) { var sp = line.split(/\s+/); hit = sp.filter(function (s) { return s.indexOf('@') > 0; })[0]; em = lower(hit); name = sp.filter(function (s) { return s !== hit; }).join(' ').trim(); }
      }
      if (!em) return;
      if (!name) name = em.split('@')[0].split(/[._-]+/).map(function (s) { return s ? s[0].toUpperCase() + s.slice(1) : ''; }).join(' ');
      out.push({ name: name, email: em });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function bulkModal(people, title, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    var leader = !isAdmin() && isLeader();
    var dept = leader ? myDept() : (opts.department || '');
    var list = people.map(function (p) { return '<div><span>' + esc(p.name) + '</span><span class="mut">' + esc(p.email) + (p.department ? ' &middot; ' + esc(deptLabel(p.department)) : '') + '</span></div>'; }).join('');
    modal('<h3>' + esc(title) + '</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">' + people.length + ' ' + (people.length === 1 ? 'person' : 'people') + '. Each gets a profile (or keeps their existing one), the access below, a sign-in email if you leave that on, and a Slack welcome when the Hub can find them.</p>'
      + '<div class="w113rows">' + list + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w113two">'
      + '<div><label>Department' + (opts.perPerson ? ' (only for people without one)' : '') + '</label>' + (leader ? '<div class="w113lock">' + esc(deptLabel(dept)) + '</div>' : '<select id="w113bd">' + (opts.perPerson ? '<option value="">Keep each person\'s department</option>' : '') + DEPTS113.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (d === dept ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(deptLabel(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>') + '</div>'
      + '<div><label>Hub access for this batch</label>' + (leader ? '<div class="w113lock">Member</div>' : '<select id="w113bh"><option value="">Keep each person\'s current access (new people become members)</option>' + ['member', 'leader', 'viewer', 'admin'].map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x + '">Everyone: ' + esc(ROLE_HELP[x]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>') + '</div></div>'
      + '<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-top:12px"><input type="checkbox" id="w113bse" checked /> Email each person a sign-in invitation</label>'
      + '<p class="w113mut" style="margin-top:6px">The email system allows only a few invitation emails per hour; anyone whose email cannot be sent still gets their profile and access, and you can share the sign-in message with them yourself.</p>'
      + '<div class="w113acts"><button class="w113btn" id="w113bgo">Invite ' + people.length + ' ' + (people.length === 1 ? 'person' : 'people') + '</button><button class="w113btn ghost" data-w113cancel="1">Cancel</button></div>');
    document.getElementById('w113bgo').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var go = this;
      var bd = document.getElementById('w113bd'); var bh = document.getElementById('w113bh');
      var se = !!document.getElementById('w113bse').checked;
      var batch = people.map(function (p) {
        var cur = W113.roles[p.email] && W113.roles[p.email].role;
        var role = bh && bh.value ? bh.value : ((cur && cur !== 'blocked') ? cur : 'member');
        return { name: p.name, email: p.email, role_title: p.role_title || '', department: (bd && bd.value) || p.department || '', hub_role: leader ? 'member' : role, send_email: se };
      });
      go.disabled = true; go.textContent = 'Inviting...';
      try {
        var res = await call({ action: 'bulk_invite', people: batch });
        var results = res.results || [];
        var box = document.querySelector('.w113modal');
        box.innerHTML = '<h3>' + res.sent + ' of ' + results.length + ' invited</h3>'
          + '<div class="w113rows">' + results.map(function (x) {
            return '<div><span class="' + (x.ok ? 'ok' : 'no') + '">' + (x.ok ? 'OK' : 'Failed') + '</span><span>' + esc(x.name || x.email) + '</span><span class="mut">'
              + (x.ok ? ('email ' + (x.email_sent ? 'sent' : 'not sent') + ' &middot; Slack ' + (x.slack === 'sent' || x.slack === 'sent_channel' ? 'sent' : 'not sent')) : esc(x.error || 'error')) + '</span></div>';
          }).join('') + '</div>'
          + '<label>A message you can paste for anyone whose email did not go out</label>'
          + '<textarea id="w113msg" readonly>' + esc(inviteMessage('')) + '</textarea>'
          + '<div class="w113acts"><button class="w113btn ghost" data-w113copymsg="1">Copy the message</button><button class="w113btn" data-w113done="1">Done</button></div><div class="w113ok" id="w113okline"></div>';
        W113.sel = {};
        refresh();
      } catch (e) {
        go.disabled = false; go.textContent = 'Invite ' + people.length;
        toast('Could not invite: ' + String(e.message || e).slice(0, 90));
      }
    });
  }
  function listModal() {
    modal('<h3>Invite by list</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">One person per line: "Name, email", "Name &lt;email&gt;" or just the email (the name is guessed from it and can be fixed on their profile later).</p>'
      + '<textarea id="w113paste" placeholder="Amina Diallo, amina.diallo@welfareavenue.com&#10;Jordan Lee &lt;jordan.lee@welfareavenue.com&gt;&#10;sam.ortiz@welfareavenue.com"></textarea>'
      + '<div class="w113acts"><button class="w113btn" id="w113pgo">Review the list</button><button class="w113btn ghost" data-w113cancel="1">Cancel</button></div>');
    document.getElementById('w113pgo').addEventListener('click', function () {
      var people = parseList(document.getElementById('w113paste').value);
      if (!people.length) { toast('No emails found in the list'); return; }
      bulkModal(people, 'Invite ' + people.length + ' from the list', { perPerson: false });
    });
  }
  async function linkFor(email, lineEl) {
    try {
      if (lineEl) lineEl.textContent = 'Generating the link...';
      var res = await call({ action: 'link', email: email });
      var ok = await copyText(res.link);
      if (lineEl) lineEl.innerHTML = (ok ? 'Copied. ' : 'Could not copy automatically, here it is: ') + '<input type="text" readonly value="' + esc(res.link) + '" style="width:100%;margin-top:4px;font-size:11px" onclick="this.select()" /><span class="w113mut">One-time link' + (res.existing_account ? ' for their existing account' : ', creates their account when opened') + '. It expires after the sign-in link timeout; generate a fresh one if it has.</span>';
      toast(ok ? 'Sign-in link copied' : 'Link ready to copy');
    } catch (e) {
      if (lineEl) lineEl.textContent = 'Could not make a link: ' + String(e.message || e).slice(0, 90);
      toast('Could not make a link');
    }
  }
  function linkModal(email) {
    var r = findRow(email);
    modal('<h3>Sign-in link for ' + esc((r && r.name) || email) + '</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">A one-time link that signs them straight in. Paste it into a Slack DM or an email. Anyone with the link can use it once, so send it only to them.</p>'
      + '<div class="w113ok" id="w113okline"></div>'
      + '<label>Or the plain message</label><textarea id="w113msg" readonly>' + esc(inviteMessage(r && r.name)) + '</textarea>'
      + '<div class="w113acts"><button class="w113btn ghost" data-w113copymsg="1">Copy the message</button><button class="w113btn" data-w113done="1">Done</button></div>');
    linkFor(email, document.getElementById('w113okline'));
  }
  function removeModal(email) {
    var r = findRow(email);
    modal('<h3>Remove ' + esc((r && r.name) || email) + ' from the Hub?</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">Their profile is deactivated, their access is blocked, and their sign-in is revoked. Their tasks, updates and history all stay. You can invite them again later from the removed list.</p>'
      + '<div class="w113acts"><button class="w113btn warn" id="w113rmgo">Remove them</button><button class="w113btn ghost" data-w113cancel="1">Cancel</button></div>');
    document.getElementById('w113rmgo').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      this.disabled = true;
      try {
        var res = await call({ action: 'remove', email: email });
        closeModal();
        toast('Removed from the Hub' + (res.auth_deleted ? ' and signed out everywhere' : ''));
        delete W113.sel[email];
        refresh();
      } catch (e) { this.disabled = false; toast('Could not remove: ' + String(e.message || e).slice(0, 90)); }
    });
  }
  async function saveEmail(id) {
    var inp = document.querySelector('input[data-w113emin="' + id + '"]');
    var email = lower(inp && inp.value);
    if (email.indexOf('@') < 1) { toast('Type a valid email first'); return; }
    try {
      var res = await call({ action: 'set_email', member_id: Number(id), email: email });
      toast('Email saved for ' + (res.name || 'the profile') + '; they can be invited now');
      refresh();
    } catch (e) { toast('Could not save: ' + String(e.message || e).slice(0, 90)); }
  }

  /* ---------- department page button (leaders and admins) ---------- */
  function deptButton(d) {
    if (DEPTS113.indexOf(d) < 0) return;
    var panel = document.getElementById(d);
    if (!panel) return;
    var links = panel.querySelector('.wa24-links');
    if (!links || links.querySelector('.w113dept')) return;
    var can = isAdmin() || (isLeader() && myDept() === d);
    if (!can) return;
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'w113dept';
    b.setAttribute('data-w113deptinv', d);
    b.textContent = '+ Invite a team member';
    links.appendChild(b);
  }
  var ARMED113 = {};
  function armDept(d) {
    var panel = document.getElementById(d);
    if (!panel || ARMED113[d]) return;
    ARMED113[d] = 1;
    var t = null;
    try {
      new MutationObserver(function () { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(function () { deptButton(d); }, 350); }).observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    if (e.target && e.target.id === 'w113q') {
      W113.q = e.target.value;
      clearTimeout(W113.qt);
      W113.qt = setTimeout(function () { var el = document.getElementById('w113q'); var pos = el ? el.selectionStart : 0; paint(); var el2 = document.getElementById('w113q'); if (el2) { el2.focus(); try { el2.setSelectionRange(pos, pos); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } }, 220);
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target;
    if (!t) return;
    if (t.id === 'w113dept') { W113.dept = t.value; paint(); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w113all') {
      var rows = allRows().filter(function (r) { return !isRemoved(r) && matches(r) && selectable(r); });
      rows.forEach(function (r) { if (t.checked) W113.sel[rowKey(r)] = 1; else delete W113.sel[rowKey(r)]; });
      paint(); return;
    }
    var cb = t.closest && t.closest('input[data-w113cb]');
    if (cb) {
      var k = cb.getAttribute('data-w113cb');
      if (cb.checked) W113.sel[k] = 1; else delete W113.sel[k];
      paint(); return;
    }
    var rs = t.closest && t.closest('select[data-w113role]');
    if (rs) {
      /* wave40's own handler saves the role; refresh our view once it has */
      var em = rs.getAttribute('data-w113role'), val = rs.value;
      if (W113.roles[em]) W113.roles[em].role = val; else W113.roles[em] = { email: em, role: val };
      setTimeout(refresh, 900);
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target;
    if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w113st]'))) { var k = el.getAttribute('data-w113st'); W113.status = (W113.status === k ? '' : k); paint(); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w113new]')) { inviteModal({}); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w113list]')) { listModal(); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w113bulk]')) {
      var picked = allRows().filter(function (r) { return W113.sel[rowKey(r)]; }).map(function (r) { return { name: r.name, email: r.email, department: r.dept, role_title: r.title }; });
      if (!picked.length) { toast('Tick some people first'); return; }
      bulkModal(picked, 'Invite the selected people', { perPerson: true });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w113inv]'))) { var r1 = findRow(el.getAttribute('data-w113inv')); inviteModal(r1 ? { name: r1.name, email: r1.email, department: r1.dept, role_title: r1.title, hub_role: r1.role || 'member' } : { email: el.getAttribute('data-w113inv') }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w113again]'))) { var r2 = findRow(el.getAttribute('data-w113again')); inviteModal(r2 ? { name: r2.name, email: r2.email, department: r2.dept, role_title: r2.title, hub_role: 'member', again: true } : { email: el.getAttribute('data-w113again'), again: true }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w113resend]'))) { var r3 = findRow(el.getAttribute('data-w113resend')); inviteModal(r3 ? { name: r3.name, email: r3.email, department: r3.dept, role_title: r3.title, hub_role: r3.role || 'member', again: true } : { email: el.getAttribute('data-w113resend'), again: true }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w113link]'))) { linkModal(el.getAttribute('data-w113link')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w113rm]'))) { removeModal(el.getAttribute('data-w113rm')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w113emsave]'))) { saveEmail(el.getAttribute('data-w113emsave')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w113deptinv]'))) { inviteModal({ department: el.getAttribute('data-w113deptinv') }); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w113cancel]')) { try { closeModal(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w113done]')) { try { closeModal(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w113copymsg]')) {
      var ta = document.getElementById('w113msg');
      if (ta) copyText(ta.value).then(function (ok) { var line = document.getElementById('w113okline'); if (line) line.textContent = ok ? 'Message copied.' : 'Could not copy automatically; select the text above and copy it.'; toast(ok ? 'Message copied' : 'Select the text and copy it'); });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w113copylink]'))) { linkFor(el.getAttribute('data-w113copylink'), document.getElementById('w113okline')); return; }
  });

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') {
    var __w40r113 = wa40Render;
    wa40Render = async function () {
      var out = await __w40r113.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        if (ensureCard()) { await load(); paint(); }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp113 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      var out = __sp113.apply(this, arguments);
      try { if (DEPTS113.indexOf(id) >= 0 && (isAdmin() || isLeader())) { armDept(id); setTimeout(function () { deptButton(id); }, 300); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa113 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa113.apply(this, arguments);
      try { if (isAdmin() || isLeader()) DEPTS113.forEach(function (d) { armDept(d); deptButton(d); }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
  window.w113Debug = function () { return { loaded: W113.loaded, rows: allRows().map(function (r) { return { name: r.name, email: r.email, status: r.status, role: r.role, dept: r.dept }; }), sel: W113.sel }; };
})();

/* =====================================================================
   WA v114 the brand skin: cream sidebar, Apple-calm (24/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "apply our brand to the hub and help me choose a nicer
   interface that feels like an Apple easy to use interface." Direction
   A chosen from three mockups: a quiet cream sidebar, one page title in
   Barlow Condensed, Space Grotesk everywhere else, cards that float on
   a cream canvas, gold for the one action that matters, purple for
   highlights. Everyone gets it at once; a "Classic look" switch on My
   page (per browser) brings the previous look back.
   Two halves:
   1. build/brand_remap.py (run by build.sh) rewrites every legacy hex
      and rgba in all earlier waves to the five brand colours and their
      cream/green tints, so nothing off-palette survives the build.
   2. This wave: fonts, tokens, the layout shell (nav.wa-nav becomes a
      fixed left sidebar on desktop and an off-canvas drawer on phones,
      driven by the existing body.wa-mob-open toggle), a bottom tab bar
      on phones (Home, Dashboard, Ask, Teams, More), the wordmark from
      the Hub's image library, and component restyles (cards, buttons,
      chips as stat tiles, inputs, tables, chat, toast, modal, gate,
      boot veil). Visual only: no page is removed or reorganised, every
      existing selector and behaviour is left in place.
   The CSS is appended INSIDE #wa97css (which wave97 keeps last in
   <head>), so it wins the cascade without a second observer fighting.
   Loads BEFORE wave110 in MANIFEST (110 stays last).
   ===================================================================== */

    + 'nav.wa-nav{width:min(84vw,320px);transform:translateX(-102%);transition:transform .25s ease;z-index:98;box-shadow:0 0 50px rgba(16,63,57,.3);}'
    + 'body.wa-mob-open nav.wa-nav{transform:none;}'
    + 'nav.wa-nav #waNavToggle{display:inline-block;margin:0 0 10px 8px;border-radius:999px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.18);background:none;color:' + GREEN + ';font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13px;font-weight:700;}'
    + '#w114back{display:none;position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(16,63,57,.35);z-index:95;}'
    + 'body.wa-mob-open #w114back{display:block;}'
    + '#w114bar{display:flex;position:fixed;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;z-index:90;background:var(--wa-card);border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.10);justify-content:space-around;padding:8px 6px calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));box-shadow:0 -6px 20px rgba(16,63,57,.06);}'
    + '#w114bar button{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:3px;background:none;border:none;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--wa-muted);cursor:pointer;padding:0;min-width:56px;}'
    + '#w114bar button i{width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:7px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.18);display:block;}'
    + '#w114bar button.on{color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '#w114bar button.on i{background:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '#w114bar button.ask i{background:' + GOLD + ';width:46px;height:46px;border-radius:50%;margin-top:-24px;box-shadow:0 8px 20px rgba(16,63,57,.25);}'
    + '#waChatBtn{display:none !important;}'
    + '#waChatPanel{bottom:0;}'
    + '.mp-head h2{font-size:32px;}'
    + '.mp-head::after{display:none;}'
    + '.wa-toolbar .pill{font-size:0;gap:6px;}'
    + '.wa-toolbar .pill b,.wa-toolbar .pill .who-btn{font-size:12.5px;}'
    + '.wa24-hero h2,.dept-banner h2{font-size:21px;line-height:1.15;}'
    + '}';

  /* wave97 keeps #wa97css LAST in <head> with a MutationObserver; a second
     observer fighting for the same spot would loop forever. So the skin rides
     inside that element, after wave97's own rules: always last, no fight. */
  var host = document.getElementById('wa97css');
  if (host) {
    host.textContent += '\n/* wa114 */\n' + css;
  } else {
    var style = document.createElement('style');
    style.id = 'wa114css';
    style.textContent = css;
    document.head.appendChild(style);
  }

  /* ---------- DOM additions: wordmark, drawer backdrop, bottom bar ---------- */
  function brand() {
    var nav = document.querySelector('nav.wa-nav');
    if (!nav || nav.querySelector('.w114brand')) return;
    var a = document.createElement('a');
    a.className = 'w114brand';
    a.href = '#mypage';
    a.innerHTML = '<img src="' + LOGO_GREEN + '" alt="Welfare Avenue" />';
    nav.insertBefore(a, nav.firstChild);
  }
  function closeDrawer() {
    document.body.classList.remove('wa-mob-open');
    var t = document.getElementById('waNavToggle');
    if (t) t.innerHTML = '&#9776; Menu';
  }
  function openDrawer(scrollTo) {
    document.body.classList.add('wa-mob-open');
    var t = document.getElementById('waNavToggle');
    if (t) t.innerHTML = '&#10005; Close';
    if (scrollTo) {
      var g = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab-group[data-w42g="' + scrollTo + '"]');
      var nav = document.querySelector('nav.wa-nav');
      if (g && nav) { try { nav.scrollTop = g.offsetTop - 12; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    }
  }
  function bar() {
    if (document.getElementById('w114bar')) return;
    var back = document.createElement('div');
    back.id = 'w114back';
    back.addEventListener('click', closeDrawer);
    document.body.appendChild(back);
    var b = document.createElement('nav');
    b.id = 'w114bar';
    b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Hub');
    b.innerHTML = '<button data-w114go="mypage"><i></i>Home</button>'
      + '<button data-w114go="overview"><i></i>Dashboard</button>'
      + '<button class="ask" data-w114ask="1"><i></i>Ask</button>'
      + '<button data-w114grp="departments"><i></i>Teams</button>'
      + '<button data-w114grp="more"><i></i>More</button>';
    document.body.appendChild(b);
    b.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var t = e.target.closest('button');
      if (!t) return;
      if (t.hasAttribute('data-w114go')) { closeDrawer(); location.hash = '#' + t.getAttribute('data-w114go'); return; }
      if (t.hasAttribute('data-w114ask')) { closeDrawer(); var c = document.getElementById('waChatBtn'); if (c) c.click(); return; }
      if (t.hasAttribute('data-w114grp')) { if (document.body.classList.contains('wa-mob-open')) closeDrawer(); else openDrawer(t.getAttribute('data-w114grp')); }
    });
    mark();
  }
  function mark() {
    var b = document.getElementById('w114bar');
    if (!b) return;
    var cur = null;
    try { cur = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab.active'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var id = cur ? cur.getAttribute('data-tab') : '';
    var ask = b.querySelector('button[data-w114ask]');
    if (ask) ask.style.display = document.getElementById('waChatBtn') ? '' : 'none';
    b.querySelectorAll('button[data-w114go]').forEach(function (x) {
      var g = x.getAttribute('data-w114go');
      x.classList.toggle('on', g === id || (g === 'overview' && id === 'dashboard'));
    });
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp114 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function () {
      var out = __sp114.apply(this, arguments);
      try { mark(); closeDrawer(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  /* the drawer closes when a tab inside it is chosen (wave42 already clears wa-mob-open on .tab clicks) */
  function init() {
    try { brand(); bar(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init); else init();
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa114 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa114.apply(this, arguments);
      try { brand(); bar(); mark(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
})();

/* =====================================================================
   WA v115 profile menu and photo, top right (24/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "add a profile setting section at the top right with a place
   to add ones photo." The "Signed in: name / Sign out" pill in the top
   row becomes a round avatar button (photo, or initials on a brand
   colour). It opens a small profile menu: the person's name, role and
   department, then Edit profile and photo, My page, Slack notifications
   (wave89 fold), Request more access (wave110) and the existing Sign
   out button (moved into the menu, same element, same handler).
   Edit profile: choose a photo (cropped to a centred square and
   resized to 512px JPEG in the browser), remove it, edit name and role
   title; department and email are read-only here (admins manage those
   in People and access). The photo lives in the public 'avatars'
   storage bucket at u/<email>.jpg (RLS: staff write only their own
   file, admins any), its URL in team_members.avatar_url (new column,
   migration wa_profile_avatars). Photos then show in the top row, the
   Home greeting, the My team rows, the person page header and the
   People and access table. Board viewers get the avatar and Sign out
   only (no roster row to edit). Wrapped (CURRENT bindings): renderMyPage,
   wa33Person, startApp. Loads BEFORE wave110 in MANIFEST.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa115) return; window.__wa115 = true;

    '#w113tbl td .w115nm{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function initials(name) {
    var p = String(name || '').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    if (!p.length) return '?';
    return (p[0][0] + (p.length > 1 ? p[p.length - 1][0] : '')).toUpperCase();
  }
  function palette(name) {
    var h = 0, s = String(name || '');
    for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
    return PAL[h % PAL.length];
  }
  function avatarHtml(person, size, extraCls) {
    var name = (person && person.name) || '';
    var url = person && person.avatar_url;
    if (person && person.id && W115.me && person.id === W115.me.id && W115.preview) url = W115.preview;
    var pal = palette(name);
    var st = 'width:' + size + 'px;height:' + size + 'px;font-size:' + Math.round(size * 0.38) + 'px;';
    if (url) return '<span class="w115av ' + (extraCls || '') + '" style="' + st + '"><img src="' + esc(url) + '" alt="' + esc(name) + '" /></span>';
    return '<span class="w115av ' + (extraCls || '') + '" style="' + st + 'background:' + pal[0] + ';color:' + pal[1] + '">' + esc(initials(name)) + '</span>';
  }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function myName() { try { return (W115.me && W115.me.name) || (USER && USER.name) || myEmail().split('@')[0]; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }

  /* ---------- data ---------- */
  async function load() {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('team_members').select('id,name,email,department,role,avatar_url,color').eq('active', true);
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      W115.byId = {}; W115.byEmail = {}; W115.byName = {};
      rows.forEach(function (m) {
        W115.byId[m.id] = m;
        if (m.email) W115.byEmail[lower(m.email)] = m;
        if (m.name) W115.byName[lower(m.name)] = m;
      });
      W115.me = W115.byEmail[myEmail()] || null;
      if (!W115.me) {
        try { if (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.id && W115.byId[WA_ME.id]) W115.me = W115.byId[WA_ME.id]; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      }
      if (!W115.me) {
        try { if (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.name && W115.byName[lower(WA_ME.name)]) W115.me = W115.byName[lower(WA_ME.name)]; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      }
      W115.loaded = true;
    } catch (e) { /* stays initials */ }
  }
  function personByName(name) {
    var n = lower(name);
    if (!n) return null;
    if (W115.byName[n]) return W115.byName[n];
    var first = n.split(' ')[0];
    var hits = Object.keys(W115.byName).filter(function (k) { return k.split(' ')[0] === first; });
    return hits.length === 1 ? W115.byName[hits[0]] : null;
  }

  /* ---------- top-right button + menu ---------- */
  function mePerson() { return W115.me || { name: myName(), email: myEmail() }; }
  function mount() {
    var who = document.getElementById('whoName');
    if (!who) return;
    var pill = who.closest('.pill');
    if (!pill) return;
    pill.classList.add('w115pill');
    var btn = document.getElementById('w115btn');
    if (!btn) {
      btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.id = 'w115btn';
      btn.type = 'button';
      btn.title = 'Your profile';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-haspopup', 'true');
      pill.insertBefore(btn, pill.firstChild);
      btn.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); toggle(); });
      who.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); toggle(); });
    }
    btn.innerHTML = avatarHtml(mePerson(), 34);
    if (!document.getElementById('w115menu')) {
      var m = document.createElement('div');
      m.id = 'w115menu';
      m.style.display = 'none';
      m.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });
      document.body.appendChild(m);
      document.addEventListener('click', function () { close(); });
      document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') close(); });
      window.addEventListener('resize', function () { if (m.style.display !== 'none') place(); });
    }
  }
  function renderMenu() {
    var m = document.getElementById('w115menu');
    if (!m) return;
    var me = mePerson();
    var dept = '';
    try { dept = me.department ? ((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[me.department]) || me.department) : ''; } catch (e) { dept = me.department || ''; }
    var line = [me.role, dept].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ');
    var so = document.getElementById('signOutBtn');
    m.innerHTML = '<div class="w115head">' + avatarHtml(me, 52) + '<div><b>' + esc(me.name || myName()) + '</b>'
      + (line ? '<div class="w115mut">' + esc(line) + '</div>' : '')
      + '<div class="w115mut">' + esc(me.email || myEmail()) + '</div></div></div>'
      + (W115.me && staff() ? '<button class="w115item" data-w115act="edit">Edit profile and photo</button>' : '')
      + (staff() ? '<button class="w115item" data-w115act="mypage">My page</button>' : '')
      + (staff() ? '<button class="w115item" data-w115act="notify">Slack notifications</button>' : '')
      + (staff() ? '<button class="w115item" data-w115act="access">Request more access</button>' : '');
    if (so) m.appendChild(so); /* the original Sign out button, same handler */
    m.querySelectorAll('[data-w115act]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () { act(b.getAttribute('data-w115act')); });
    });
  }
  function place() {
    var m = document.getElementById('w115menu'), btn = document.getElementById('w115btn');
    if (!m || !btn) return;
    var r = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
    var right = Math.max(8, window.innerWidth - r.right);
    m.style.top = (r.bottom + 8) + 'px';
    m.style.right = right + 'px';
    m.style.left = 'auto';
    if (window.innerWidth < 340) { m.style.left = '8px'; m.style.right = '8px'; m.style.width = 'auto'; }
  }
  function toggle() {
    var m = document.getElementById('w115menu');
    if (!m) return;
    if (m.style.display !== 'none') { close(); return; }
    renderMenu();
    m.style.display = 'block';
    place();
  }
  function close() {
    var m = document.getElementById('w115menu');
    if (m) m.style.display = 'none';
  }
  function act(what) {
    close();
    if (what === 'edit') { editModal(); return; }
    if (what === 'mypage') { location.hash = '#mypage'; return; }
    if (what === 'notify') {
      location.hash = '#mypage';
      setTimeout(function () {
        var box = document.getElementById('w89box');
        if (box) { box.open = true; try { box.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }); } catch (e) { box.scrollIntoView(); } }
      }, 700);
      return;
    }
    if (what === 'access') {
      location.hash = '#mypage';
      setTimeout(function () {
        var b = document.getElementById('w110btn');
        if (b) b.click(); else { try { toast('Open My page to request more access'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      }, 700);
    }
  }

  /* ---------- edit profile ---------- */
  function squareJpeg(file, size) {
    return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
      var img = new Image();
      var u = URL.createObjectURL(file);
      img.onload = function () {
        try {
          var s = Math.min(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight);
          var sx = (img.naturalWidth - s) / 2, sy = (img.naturalHeight - s) / 2;
          var c = document.createElement('canvas');
          c.width = size; c.height = size;
          var ctx = c.getContext('2d');
          ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFAF2'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);
          ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, s, s, 0, 0, size, size);
          URL.revokeObjectURL(u);
          c.toBlob(function (b) { if (b) resolve(b); else reject(new Error('Could not encode the photo')); }, 'image/jpeg', 0.86);
        } catch (e) { reject(e); }
      };
      img.onerror = function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(u); reject(new Error('That file is not an image the browser can read')); };
      img.src = u;
    });
  }
  function editModal() {
    var me = W115.me;
    if (!me) { try { toast('Your account is not linked to a team profile yet; ask an admin.'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return; }
    W115.pending = null; W115.remove = false;
    var dept = '';
    try { dept = me.department ? ((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[me.department]) || me.department) : 'No department set'; } catch (e) { dept = me.department || ''; }
    openModal('<div class="w115edit"><h3>Your profile</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">Your photo shows next to your name across the Hub. Until you choose one, the Hub uses your Slack photo (or your Google one). Department and email are managed in People and access.</p>'
      + '<div class="w115photo"><div id="w115prev">' + avatarHtml(me, 84) + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w115acts"><label class="w115choose">Choose a photo<input type="file" id="w115file" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp" /></label>'
      + ((me.avatar_url || W115.preview) ? '<span class="w115rm" data-w115rm="1">Remove the photo</span>' : '')
      + '<div class="w115hint">JPG, PNG or WebP, up to 2 MB. It is cropped to a centred square.</div></div></div>'
      + '<label>Name</label><input type="text" id="w115n" value="' + esc(me.name || '') + '" />'
      + '<label>Role title</label><input type="text" id="w115rt" value="' + esc(me.role || '') + '" placeholder="e.g. Volunteer coordinator" />'
      + '<label>Department</label><div class="w115lock">' + esc(dept) + '</div>'
      + '<label>Email</label><div class="w115lock">' + esc(me.email || '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w115save"><button class="w115go" id="w115save">Save</button><button class="w115cx" id="w115cancel">Cancel</button></div>'
      + '<div class="w115hint" id="w115msg"></div></div>');
    var file = document.getElementById('w115file');
    file.addEventListener('change', async function () {
      var f = file.files && file.files[0];
      if (!f) return;
      if (f.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024 * 6) { setMsg('That photo is very large; pick one under 12 MB.'); return; }
      setMsg('Preparing the photo...');
      try {
        var blob = await squareJpeg(f, 512);
        W115.pending = blob; W115.remove = false;
        var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        document.getElementById('w115prev').innerHTML = '<span class="w115av" style="width:84px;height:84px"><img src="' + url + '" alt="" /></span>';
        setMsg('Looks good. Save to keep it.');
      } catch (e) { setMsg(String(e.message || e)); }
    });
    var rm = document.querySelector('[data-w115rm]');
    if (rm) rm.addEventListener('click', function () {
      W115.remove = true; W115.pending = null;
      document.getElementById('w115prev').innerHTML = avatarHtml({ name: me.name }, 84);
      setMsg('The photo will be removed when you save, and the Hub will not fill one in from Slack or Google again.');
    });
    document.getElementById('w115cancel').addEventListener('click', function () { try { closeModal(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
    document.getElementById('w115save').addEventListener('click', save);
  }
  function setMsg(t) { var m = document.getElementById('w115msg'); if (m) m.textContent = t || ''; }
  async function save() {
    var me = W115.me;
    if (!me) return;
    var btn = document.getElementById('w115save');
    var name = (document.getElementById('w115n').value || '').trim();
    var role = (document.getElementById('w115rt').value || '').trim();
    if (!name) { setMsg('A name is needed.'); return; }
    btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Saving...';
    var old = { name: me.name, role: me.role, avatar_url: me.avatar_url || null };
    var patch = {};
    if (name !== me.name) patch.name = name;
    if (role !== (me.role || '')) patch.role = role || null;
    try {
      var path = 'u/' + lower(me.email) + '.jpg';
      if (W115.pending) {
        var up = await db.storage.from(BUCKET).upload(path, W115.pending, { upsert: true, contentType: 'image/jpeg', cacheControl: '3600' });
        if (up && up.error) throw new Error('Photo upload failed: ' + (up.error.message || 'storage error'));
        var pub = db.storage.from(BUCKET).getPublicUrl(path);
        patch.avatar_url = ((pub && pub.data && pub.data.publicUrl) || '') + '?v=' + Date.now();
        try { W115.preview = URL.createObjectURL(W115.pending); } catch (e) { W115.preview = null; }
      } else if (W115.remove) {
        patch.avatar_url = ''; /* wave139: '' is no photo by choice, so the daily sync does not bring the Slack or Google photo back */
        W115.preview = null;
        try { await db.storage.from(BUCKET).remove([path]); } catch (e) { /* the row is what matters */ }
      }
      if (Object.keys(patch).length) {
        var r = await db.from('team_members').update(patch).eq('id', me.id);
        if (r && r.error) throw new Error('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || ''));
        Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { me[k] = patch[k]; });
        try { if (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME) { if (patch.name) WA_ME.name = patch.name; if ('role' in patch) WA_ME.role = patch.role; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        try {
          await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'member', entity_id: String(me.id), field: 'profile', old_value: JSON.stringify(old), new_value: JSON.stringify({ name: me.name, role: me.role, avatar_url: me.avatar_url ? 'set' : null }), changed_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : me.name });
        } catch (e) { /* audit only */ }
      }
      try { closeModal(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      W115.pending = null; W115.remove = false;
      var wn = document.getElementById('whoName'); if (wn && patch.name) wn.textContent = patch.name;
      mount(); decorateHome();
      try { toast('Profile saved'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function' && document.getElementById('mypage') && document.getElementById('mypage').classList.contains('active')) renderMyPage(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    } catch (e) {
      btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Save';
      setMsg(String(e.message || e));
    }
  }

  /* ---------- photos across the Hub ---------- */
  function decorateHome() {
    var head = document.querySelector('#mypage .mp-head');
    if (head && !head.querySelector('.w115av')) {
      var h2 = head.querySelector('h2');
      if (h2) h2.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', avatarHtml(mePerson(), 46));
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('#mypage .w74mem summary').forEach(function (s) {
      if (s.querySelector('.w115av')) return;
      var nm = s.querySelector('.nm');
      if (!nm) return;
      var p = personByName(nm.textContent);
      nm.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', avatarHtml(p || { name: nm.textContent }, 22));
    });
  }
  function decoratePerson() {
    var head = document.querySelector('#personpage .w33head');
    if (!head || head.querySelector('.w115av')) return;
    var h2 = head.querySelector('h2');
    var p = h2 ? personByName(h2.textContent) : null;
    if (!p) { var em = (head.textContent.match(/[a-z0-9._%+-]+@[a-z0-9.-]+\.[a-z]{2,}/i) || [])[0]; if (em) p = W115.byEmail[lower(em)] || null; }
    if (!p || !p.avatar_url) return;
    var dot = head.querySelector('.w33dot');
    if (dot) dot.outerHTML = avatarHtml(p, 52); else if (h2) h2.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', avatarHtml(p, 52));
  }
  function decoratePeople() {
    document.querySelectorAll('#w113tbl tr[data-w113row], #w113tbl2 tr[data-w113row]').forEach(function (tr) {
      if (tr.querySelector('.w115av')) return;
      var key = tr.getAttribute('data-w113row') || '';
      var p = W115.byEmail[lower(key)] || null;
      var b = tr.querySelector('td:nth-child(2) b');
      if (!b) return;
      if (!p) { var n = b.textContent; p = personByName(n) || { name: n }; }
      var wrap = document.createElement('span');
      wrap.className = 'w115nm';
      b.parentNode.insertBefore(wrap, b);
      wrap.innerHTML = avatarHtml(p, 26);
      wrap.appendChild(b);
    });
  }
  var pt = null;
  function armPeople() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w113body');
    if (!body || body.dataset.w115mo) return;
    body.dataset.w115mo = '1';
    try { new MutationObserver(function () { clearTimeout(pt); pt = setTimeout(decoratePeople, 120); }).observe(body, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __rm115 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      var r = await __rm115.apply(this, arguments);
      try { if (!W115.loaded) await load(); decorateHome(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa33Person === 'function') {
    var __wp115 = wa33Person;
    wa33Person = async function () {
      var r = await __wp115.apply(this, arguments);
      try { if (!W115.loaded) await load(); decoratePerson(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp115 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      var out = __sp115.apply(this, arguments);
      try { close(); if (id === 'adminpanel') setTimeout(function () { armPeople(); decoratePeople(); }, 500); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa115 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa115.apply(this, arguments);
      try { await load(); mount(); decorateHome(); armPeople(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
  window.w115Debug = function () { return { loaded: W115.loaded, me: W115.me, people: Object.keys(W115.byId).length, preview: !!W115.preview }; };
})();

/* =====================================================================
   WA v116 team pages (24/09/26): one page per team, fed by the hub calendar
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "For the drop down of each department, it should hold a
   different page for each team... each their own Agenda tool (the
   Agenda tab under Meetings is the master agenda tool that only admins
   can view and query), each their own Overview page that holds clear
   bullet point form updates, mentions from other departments, and other
   pertinent KPIs... Operations Montreal should have the Volunteering
   tool under its drop down... Fundraising: Grants, Event Fundraising
   and Donor Relations each get their own task management page
   (ClickUp-like) with their projects (wave164: Finance left Fundraising
   for the Finance app, its tasks there)... sync meeting times to what
   hub@welfareavenue.com has on its calendar... on one's home page they
   should see exactly what meetings they have upcoming."
   1. Routes: #team/<dept> (Overview), #team/<dept>/agenda,
      #team/<dept>/tasks[/<workstream>], #team/fundraising/<board>
      (grants, events, donors) and #team/opsmtl/volunteering.
      wave164: #team/finance[/...] opens the Finance app's Tasks.
      One panel (#team) hosts them all; the classic #dept/<d> page stays
      reachable (Executive decisions, Transcripts, the footer link).
   2. Departments menu: clicking a team opens its Overview; the flyout
      lists the team's pages, then Executive decisions and Transcripts.
   3. Overview: KPI tiles from live data, tracked KPIs (metrics table,
      editable by staff), updates in bullet form, mentions from other
      teams, upcoming meetings (hub calendar), projects, team.
   4. Agenda: the existing agenda tool, scoped to the team (department
      locked, filter hidden). The master Agendas page is admins only.
   5. Calendar: public.hub_events mirrors hub@welfareavenue.com every 30
      minutes (edge fn calendar-sync + cron). Department meeting chips
      read the synced cache instead of each person's own Google Calendar,
      and Home gains "Your upcoming meetings" (who accepted, join link).
   The task boards themselves live in wave116b (w116Board).
   wave287 (06/11/26, edited here; the CSS and motion are in
   287_wave287): projects replace workstreams. The project helpers are
   published (w116IsGeneral, w116GeneralOf, w116ProjOf, w116ProjColor,
   w116Ink, w116Elsewhere, w116ProjForWs); the Overview is rebuilt in
   reading order (header with the clamped read and the tabs' thumb;
   Now: a sentence and three tiles; Needs attention; project cards;
   the Team inbox, Updates, Mentions, decisions; meetings, KPIs, team,
   the pulse, tools); #team/<d>/tasks/<workstream> opens the project
   that replaced the workstream.
   ===================================================================== */

  /* ---------- helpers ---------- */
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function admin() { try { return typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function who0() { try { return who(); } catch (e) { return 'staff'; } }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function label(d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || T.labels[d] || d; }
  function color(d) { return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || '#797773'; }
  function members(d) { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m.department === d && m.active !== false; }); }
  function allowedDept(d) {
    try { var a = (typeof wa40Allowed === 'function') ? wa40Allowed() : null; return !a || a.indexOf(d) >= 0; } catch (e) { return true; }
  }
  var PAL = [['#103F39', '#FFFAF2'], ['#6F64AB', '#FFFAF2'], ['#DCA527', '#1F201E'], ['#2A8C7A', '#FFFAF2'], ['#c87a2f', '#FFFAF2'], ['#3a6ea5', '#FFFAF2'], ['#B3402F', '#FFFAF2']];
  function initials(name) {
    var p = String(name || '').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    if (!p.length) return '?';
    return (p[0][0] + (p.length > 1 ? p[p.length - 1][0] : '')).toUpperCase();
  }
  function palette(name) { var h = 0, s = String(name || ''); for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0; return PAL[h % PAL.length]; }
  function memberByEmail(em) { em = lower(em); if (!em) return null; return members0().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === em; })[0] || null; }
  function memberByName(n) { n = lower(n); if (!n) return null; var all = members0(); return all.filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name) === n; })[0] || all.filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name).split(' ')[0] === n.split(' ')[0]; })[0] || null; }
  function members0() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []); }
  function av(person, size, cls) {
    var name = (person && person.name) || (typeof person === 'string' ? person : '') || '';
    var url = person && person.avatar_url;
    var pal = palette(name);
    var st = 'width:' + size + 'px;height:' + size + 'px;font-size:' + Math.round(size * 0.38) + 'px;';
    if (url) return '<span class="w116av ' + (cls || '') + '" title="' + E(name) + '" style="' + st + '"><img src="' + E(url) + '" alt="' + E(name) + '" /></span>';
    return '<span class="w116av ' + (cls || '') + '" title="' + E(name) + '" style="' + st + 'background:' + pal[0] + ';color:' + pal[1] + '">' + E(initials(name)) + '</span>';
  }
  window.w116Avatar = av;
  function stack(list, size, max) {
    max = max || 6;
    var out = '<span class="w116avs">' + list.slice(0, max).map(function (p) { return av(p.person || p, size, p.cls || ''); }).join('');
    if (list.length > max) out += '<span class="more">+' + (list.length - max) + '</span>';
    return out + '</span>';
  }
  /* the team lives in Montreal and Toronto: every calendar time is shown in that zone, like the department chips */
  var TZ = 'America/Toronto';
  function dayKey(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', timeZone: TZ }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function fmtTime(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit', timeZone: TZ }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function fmtDay(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', timeZone: TZ }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function dayNum(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { day: 'numeric', timeZone: TZ }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function dow(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'short', timeZone: TZ }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function relDay(iso) {
    var k = dayKey(iso), t = dayKey(new Date().toISOString()), tm = dayKey(new Date(Date.now() + 86400000).toISOString());
    if (k === t) return 'Today'; if (k === tm) return 'Tomorrow'; return fmtDay(iso);
  }
  function go(h) { location.hash = h; }

  /* ---------- data: calendar mirror (hub@welfareavenue.com) ---------- */
  async function loadEvents(force) {
    if (!force && W116.eventsAt && Date.now() - W116.eventsAt < 4 * 60000) return W116.events;
    try {
      var sinceMs = Date.now() - 3 * 3600000;
      var r = await db.from('hub_events').select('*').gte('start_at', new Date(Date.now() - 8 * 86400000).toISOString()).order('start_at', { ascending: true }).limit(500);
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      W116.events = rows.filter(function (e) { return e && e.start_at && (e.status || 'confirmed') !== 'cancelled' && Date.parse(e.end_at || e.start_at) >= sinceMs; })
        .sort(function (a, b) { return Date.parse(a.start_at) - Date.parse(b.start_at); });
      W116.eventsAt = Date.now();
      try { var s = await db.from('hub_calendar_state').select('*').limit(3); W116.calState = ((s && s.data) || [])[0] || null; } catch (e2) { /* fine */ }
    } catch (e) { /* offline: keep what we had */ }
    return W116.events;
  }
  function myEvents() {
    var em = myEmail(); var me = memberByEmail(em); var alt = me ? lower(me.email) : '';
    return W116.events.filter(function (e) {
      var at = e.attendee_emails || [];
      return at.indexOf(em) >= 0 || (alt && at.indexOf(alt) >= 0) || lower(e.organizer) === em;
    });
  }
  function teamEvents(d) {
    var emails = members(d).map(function (m) { return lower(m.email); }).filter(Boolean);
    return W116.events.filter(function (e) {
      if (e.department === d) return true;
      if (e.department) return false;
      var at = e.attendee_emails || []; var n = 0;
      at.forEach(function (x) { if (emails.indexOf(x) >= 0) n++; });
      return n >= 2;
    });
  }
  function myStatus(e) {
    var em = myEmail(); var me = memberByEmail(em); var alt = me ? lower(me.email) : '';
    var a = (e.attendees || []).filter(function (x) { return x.email === em || (alt && x.email === alt); })[0];
    return a ? (a.status || 'needsAction') : (lower(e.organizer) === em ? 'accepted' : '');
  }
  function attendeeStack(e, size) {
    var list = (e.attendees || []).map(function (a) {
      var m = memberByEmail(a.email);
      return { person: m || { name: a.name || a.email.split('@')[0] }, cls: 'st-' + (a.status || 'needsAction') };
    });
    return list.length ? stack(list, size || 20, 7) : '';
  }
  function eventRow(e, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    var st = myStatus(e);
    var going = (e.attendees || []).filter(function (a) { return a.status === 'accepted'; }).length;
    var total = (e.attendees || []).length;
    var isToday = dayKey(e.start_at) === dayKey(new Date().toISOString());
    var past = new Date(e.end_at || e.start_at) < new Date();
    var when = e.all_day ? 'All day' : (fmtTime(e.start_at) + (e.end_at ? ' to ' + fmtTime(e.end_at) : ''));
    var rsvp = '';
    if (opts.mine) {
      var map = { accepted: 'Going', tentative: 'Maybe', declined: 'Declined', needsAction: 'No reply yet' };
      /* wave116j: reply from the hub when Google Calendar is connected in Settings; the pill still opens Google Calendar */
      var can = !!(window.w116Conn && window.w116Conn.calendar);
      rsvp = '<span class="w116rsvpw">' + (st ? '<a class="w116rsvp ' + E(st) + (isToday && st === 'accepted' ? ' today' : '') + '" href="' + E(e.html_link || '#') + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" title="Open in Google Calendar">' + (map[st] || st) + '</a>' : '') +
        (!past ? '<span class="w116rsvpb" data-w116rsvp="' + E(e.id) + '">' + (st !== 'accepted' ? '<a data-r="accepted" title="' + (can ? 'Accept from the hub' : 'Turn on Google Calendar in Settings to reply here') + '">Accept</a>' : '') + (st !== 'declined' ? '<a data-r="declined" title="' + (can ? 'Decline from the hub' : 'Turn on Google Calendar in Settings to reply here') + '">Decline</a>' : '') + '</span>' : '') + '</span>';
    } else rsvp = isToday ? '<span class="w116rsvp today">Today</span>' : '<span></span>';
    return '<div class="w116ev' + (past ? ' past' : '') + '" data-w116ev="' + E(e.id) + '">' +
      '<div class="w116evd"><b>' + E(dayNum(e.start_at)) + '</b><span>' + E(dow(e.start_at)) + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w116evb"><div class="t">' + E(e.summary || 'Meeting') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="s"><span>' + E(relDay(e.start_at)) + ' &middot; ' + E(when) + '</span>' +
      (e.department && !opts.hideDept ? '<span class="w116pill" style="background:' + color(e.department) + '">' + E(T.short[e.department] || label(e.department)) + '</span>' : '') +
      (total ? '<span title="' + going + ' of ' + total + ' accepted">' + attendeeStack(e, 18) + ' <span>' + going + '/' + total + ' going</span></span>' : '') +
      (e.meet_link ? '<a href="' + E(e.meet_link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Join</a>' : '') +
      (e.html_link && !opts.mine ? '<a href="' + E(e.html_link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="font-weight:500">Calendar</a>' : '') +
      '</div></div>' + rsvp + '</div>';
  }
  function calNote() {
    var s = W116.calState;
    if (!s) return '';
    if (s.last_ok === false) return '<p class="w116note">Calendar sync is not connected yet' + (admin() ? ': ' + E(s.last_note || '') : '') + '.</p>';
    return '';
  }
