  /* department meeting chips: the synced cache is the source now (no per-person Google Calendar reads) */
  if (typeof loadMeetings === 'function') {
    loadMeetings = async function () {
      try {
        MEETINGS = {};
        (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' ? DEPT_ORDER : []).forEach(function (id) {
          var meta = (typeof DB_META !== 'undefined' && DB_META[id]) || {};
          if (meta.meeting_cache && meta.meeting_cache_at && (Date.now() - new Date(meta.meeting_cache_at).getTime()) < 10 * 86400000) MEETINGS[id] = meta.meeting_cache;
        });
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* ---------- data: mentions, projects, metrics ---------- */
  async function loadMentions() {
    if (W116.mentions) return W116.mentions;
    try { var r = await db.from('mentions').select('*').order('meeting_date', { ascending: false }).limit(400); W116.mentions = (r && r.data) || []; }
    catch (e) { W116.mentions = []; }
    return W116.mentions;
  }
  /* wave116i: one project store for every surface (team pages, boards, Projects
     page, goal pages, the map). Fresh within 30 seconds; an edit anywhere calls
     w116ProjectsChanged and every visible surface repaints from the same rows. */
  var PSTORE = window.__waProjects = window.__waProjects || { rows: null, at: 0, loading: null };
  function syncLegacy() { try { if (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' && PSTORE.rows) WA27_PROJECTS = PSTORE.rows.filter(function (p) { return (p.status || 'active') === 'active'; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  async function loadProjects(force) {
    if (!force && PSTORE.rows && Date.now() - PSTORE.at < 30000) { W116.projects = PSTORE.rows; return PSTORE.rows; }
    if (!PSTORE.loading) {
      PSTORE.loading = (async function () {
        try { var r = await db.from('projects').select('*').order('sort', { ascending: true }); if (r && !r.error) { PSTORE.rows = (r.data || []); PSTORE.at = Date.now(); syncLegacy(); } }
        catch (e) { /* offline */ }
        PSTORE.loading = null;
      })();
    }
    await PSTORE.loading;
    if (!PSTORE.rows) PSTORE.rows = (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' && WA27_PROJECTS) ? WA27_PROJECTS.slice() : [];
    W116.projects = PSTORE.rows;
    return W116.projects;
  }
  window.w116LoadProjects = loadProjects;
  window.w116ProjectsChanged = function (rows) {
    if (Array.isArray(rows)) { PSTORE.rows = rows; PSTORE.at = Date.now(); } else PSTORE.at = 0;
    W116.projects = PSTORE.rows; syncLegacy();
    try { if (W116.cur && document.getElementById('team') && document.getElementById('team').classList.contains('active') && window.w116Route) window.w116Route(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (window.w116Goals && window.w116Goals.refreshLive) window.w116Goals.refreshLive(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('wa:projects')); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  };
  async function loadMetrics(force) {
    if (W116.metrics && !force) return W116.metrics;
    try { var r = await db.from('metrics').select('*').order('sort', { ascending: true }); W116.metrics = (r && r.data) || []; }
    catch (e) { W116.metrics = W116.metrics || []; }
    return W116.metrics;
  }
  window.w116Projects = function () { return PSTORE.rows || W116.projects || (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []) || []; };

  /* ---------- scope helpers shared with the boards ---------- */
  function boardDef(d, key) { return ((T.boards[d]) || []).filter(function (b) { return b.key === key; })[0] || null; }
  /* which board (sub-team) a project belongs to: its own workstream, else its
     parent's, else the static list in WA_TEAMS.boardProjects */
  function boardKeyOf(p, all) {
    if (!p) return null;
    all = all || window.w116Projects();
    var bs = T.boards[p.department] || [];
    var ws = lower(p.workstream || '');
    var par = p.parent_id ? all.filter(function (x) { return Number(x.id) === Number(p.parent_id); })[0] : null;
    if (!ws && par) ws = lower(par.workstream || '');
    for (var i = 0; i < bs.length; i++) { if (ws && lower(bs[i].ws) === ws) return bs[i].key; }
    var keys = Object.keys(T.boardProjects || {});
    for (var k = 0; k < keys.length; k++) { var ids = T.boardProjects[keys[k]] || []; if (ids.indexOf(Number(p.id)) >= 0 || (par && ids.indexOf(Number(par.id)) >= 0)) return keys[k]; }
    return null;
  }
  function boardIds(boardKey) { var all = window.w116Projects(); return all.filter(function (p) { return boardKeyOf(p, all) === boardKey; }).map(function (p) { return Number(p.id); }); }
  window.w116BoardKeyOf = boardKeyOf;
  window.w116BoardIds = boardIds;
  function projectsFor(d, board) {
    var all = window.w116Projects();
    if (board) { return all.filter(function (p) { return boardKeyOf(p, all) === board.key; }); }
    return all.filter(function (p) { return p.department === d; });
  }
  function tasksFor(d, board, ws) {
    var rows = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) { return t.department === d; });
    if (board) {
      var ids = boardIds(board.key);
      rows = rows.filter(function (t) { return t.subsection === board.ws || (t.project_id && ids.indexOf(Number(t.project_id)) >= 0); });
    } else if (ws) rows = rows.filter(function (t) { return (t.subsection || 'General') === ws; });
    return rows;
  }
  window.w116TasksFor = tasksFor;
  window.w116ProjectsFor = projectsFor;

  /* ---------- wave287: projects replace workstreams ---------- */
  /* the team's catch-all: projects.is_general (wa287a_projects.sql); before that column exists, the top-level "General <team>" project */
  function isGeneral(p) {
    if (!p) return false;
    if (p.is_general === true) return true;
    if (p.is_general === false) return false;
    var m = /^general\s+(.+)$/i.exec(String(p.name || '').trim()); if (!m || p.parent_id) return false;
    var rest = lower(m[1]);
    return lower(label(p.department)).indexOf(rest) >= 0 || lower(T.short[p.department] || '').indexOf(rest) >= 0 || rest === lower(p.department);
  }
  function generalOf(d) { return window.w116Projects().filter(function (p) { return p.department === d && (p.status || 'active') === 'active' && isGeneral(p); })[0] || null; }
  function projById(id) { return window.w116Projects().filter(function (p) { return Number(p.id) === Number(id); })[0] || null; }
  /* the project a task counts in: its own, else its team's General (null when the team has none) */
  function projOf(t) { if (!t) return null; var p = t.project_id ? projById(t.project_id) : null; return p || generalOf(t.department); }
  /* colours from the brand set and the Hub's team palette; General wears the team's colour */
  var PCOL = ['#6F64AB', '#DCA527', '#2A8C7A', '#103F39', '#c87a2f', '#3a6ea5', '#b9851b', '#4a4f57'];
  function projColor(p) {
    if (!p) return '#797773';
    if (p.color && /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(p.color)) return p.color;
    if (isGeneral(p)) return color(p.department);
    var i = (Number(p.id) || 0) * 5 % PCOL.length, c = PCOL[i];
    if (lower(c) === lower(color(p.department))) c = PCOL[(i + 1) % PCOL.length];
    return c;
  }
  /* a colour dark enough to set text in on cream (gold and the lighter team colours are darkened) */
  function ink(c) {
    var m = /^#([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(String(c || '')); if (!m) return GREEN;
    var rgb = [0, 2, 4].map(function (i) { return parseInt(m[1].slice(i, i + 2), 16); });
    function lum(a) { var v = a.map(function (x) { x = x / 255; return x <= 0.03928 ? x / 12.92 : Math.pow((x + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4); }); return 0.2126 * v[0] + 0.7152 * v[1] + 0.0722 * v[2]; }
    /* contrast against cream (luminance about 0.955); 4:1 is enough for large titles and small bold labels */
    for (var k = 0; k < 14 && (0.955 + 0.05) / (lum(rgb) + 0.05) < 4; k++) rgb = rgb.map(function (x, j) { return Math.round(x * 0.86 + [31, 32, 30][j] * 0.14); });
    return '#' + rgb.map(function (x) { return ('0' + x.toString(16)).slice(-2); }).join('');
  }
  /* a project that lives on its own page: Grants (wave258) is left out of the Fundraising team page and has #grants */
  /* only while wave258's filter is on (it marks the published w116TasksFor), which is what keeps grants off the team page */
  function elsewhere(p, board) { if (!p || board) return null; if (p.department === 'fundraising' && boardKeyOf(p) === 'grants' && window.w116TasksFor && window.w116TasksFor.__w258) return '#grants'; return null; }
  /* an old #team/<d>/tasks/<workstream> link: the project that replaced the workstream (projects.workstream), else null */
  function projForWs(d, ws) {
    var l = lower(ws); if (!l) return null;
    var hit = window.w116Projects().filter(function (p) { return p.department === d && (p.status || 'active') === 'active' && lower(p.workstream) === l; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return (a.parent_id ? 1 : 0) - (b.parent_id ? 1 : 0) || Number(a.id) - Number(b.id); });
    if (hit[0]) return hit[0];
    if (l === 'general') return generalOf(d);
    return null;
  }
  window.w116IsGeneral = isGeneral;
  window.w116GeneralOf = generalOf;
  window.w116ProjOf = projOf;
  window.w116ProjColor = projColor;
  window.w116Ink = ink;
  window.w116Elsewhere = elsewhere;
  window.w116ProjForWs = projForWs;

  /* ---------- the panel ---------- */
  function host() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('team');
    if (!sec) return null;
    var h = document.getElementById('w116host');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w116host'; sec.appendChild(h); }
    return h;
  }
  function pagesOf(d) {
    var out = [];
    (T.pages[d] || ['overview', 'agenda', 'tasks']).forEach(function (p) {
      if (p === 'overview') out.push({ key: 'overview', label: 'Overview', hash: '#team/' + d });
      else if (p === 'agenda') out.push({ key: 'agenda', label: 'Agenda', hash: '#team/' + d + '/agenda' });
      else if (p === 'tasks') out.push({ key: 'tasks', label: 'Tasks', hash: '#team/' + d + '/tasks' });
      else if (p === 'volunteering') { if (tabVisible('volunteering')) out.push({ key: 'volunteering', label: 'Volunteering', hash: '#volunteering' }); }
      else if (p === 'boards') {
        (T.boards[d] || []).forEach(function (b) { out.push({ key: b.key, label: b.label, hash: '#team/' + d + '/' + b.key, board: b }); });
        out.push({ key: 'tasks', label: 'All tasks', hash: '#team/' + d + '/tasks' });
      }
    });
    if (d === 'hr' && tabVisible('hrcentre')) out.push({ key: 'hrcentre', label: 'HR centre', hash: '#hrcentre', tool: true });
    return out;
  }
  function tabVisible(id) {
    var b = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]');
    return !!b && b.style.display !== 'none';
  }
  function header(d, cur, title, sub) {
    var pages = pagesOf(d);
    var ms = members(d);
    var next = teamEvents(d).filter(function (e) { return new Date(e.end_at || e.start_at) >= new Date(); })[0];
    return '<div class="w116crumb"><a data-w116go="#mypage">Home</a> &middot; Teams &middot; <a data-w116go="#team/' + E(d) + '">' + E(label(d)) + '</a>' + (title ? ' &middot; ' + E(title) : '') + '</div>' +
      /* wave287: the Overview's read is clamped to two lines with More (287 shows More only when the text is cut); the tabs get a sliding thumb */
      '<div class="w116top"><div><h2><span class="dot" style="background:' + color(d) + '"></span>' + E(title || label(d)) + '</h2>' + (sub ? '<p class="sub' + (cur === 'overview' ? ' w287read' : '') + '" id="w287read-' + E(d) + '">' + sub + '</p>' + (cur === 'overview' ? '<button type="button" class="w287more" data-w287more="1" aria-controls="w287read-' + E(d) + '" aria-expanded="false" hidden>More</button>' : '') : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116right">' + (ms.length ? '<span title="' + E(ms.map(function (m) { return m.name; }).join(', ')) + '" data-w116go="#orgchart" style="cursor:pointer">' + stack(ms, 28, 6) + '</span>' : '') +
      (next ? '<span class="w116next"><span class="ic">&#9200;</span><span><b>' + E(relDay(next.start_at)) + '</b> ' + E(next.all_day ? '' : fmtTime(next.start_at)) + ' &middot; ' + E(next.summary) + (next.meet_link ? ' &middot; <a href="' + E(next.meet_link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Join</a>' : '') + '</span></span>' : '') +
      '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="w116seg w287tabs" data-d="' + E(d) + '" style="margin:0 0 18px"><span class="w287thumb" aria-hidden="true"></span>' + pages.map(function (p) { return '<a data-w116go="' + E(p.hash) + '" class="' + (p.key === cur ? 'on' : '') + '"' + (p.key === cur ? ' aria-current="page"' : '') + '>' + E(p.label) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function markNav(d, page) {
    try {
      document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab.active').forEach(function (t) { t.classList.remove('active'); });
      var b = document.querySelector('#tabBar .tab[data-tab="' + d + '"]');
      if (b) b.classList.add('active');
      document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .w42sub a.on').forEach(function (a) { a.classList.remove('on'); });
      var row = b && b.closest ? b.closest('.w42d') : null;
      if (row) { var a = row.querySelector('.w42sub a[data-w116page="' + page + '"]'); if (a) a.classList.add('on'); }
      syncOpen();
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* ---------- Overview ---------- */
  function sentences(text) {
    var s = String(text || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (!s) return [];
    var parts = s.split(/(?<=[.!?])\s+(?=[A-Z0-9"'(])|;\s+/);
    return parts.map(function (p) { return p.trim(); }).filter(function (p) { return p.length > 2; });
  }
  function srcOf(u) {
    var l = lower((u.entry_label || '') + ' ' + (u.added_by || ''));
    if (l.indexOf('slack') >= 0) return ['slack', 'Slack'];
    if (l.indexOf('otter') >= 0 || l.indexOf('loom') >= 0 || l.indexOf('plaud') >= 0 || l.indexOf('ai:') >= 0) return ['meeting', 'Meeting'];
    return ['manual', 'Note'];
  }
  function txFor(u) {
    try {
      var t = String(u.added_by || '').replace(/^AI:\s*/i, '').trim();
      if (!t || typeof txCache === 'undefined') return null;
      return txCache.filter(function (x) { return lower(x.title) === lower(t); })[0] || null;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  var KEYS = {
    opsmtl: /montreal|\bmtl\b|community kitchen|serving/i,
    progdev: /program development|program dev|beneficiar|survey|research/i,
    opsto: /toronto|ontario/i,
    marketing: /marketing|instagram|linkedin|social media|brand|website|newsletter|content/i,
    fundraising: /fundrais|donor|grant|sponsor|keela|flag football|rally paper|gift basket|finance|budget/i,
    hr: /\bhr\b|human resources|onboard|recruit|hiring|volunteer coordinator|policy/i,
    board: /board meeting|governance|board member|\bboard\b/i
  };
  function updatesHtml(d, limit) {
    var rows = (typeof DB_UPDATES !== 'undefined' ? DB_UPDATES : []).filter(function (u) { return u.department === d; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.created_at).localeCompare(String(a.created_at)); }).slice(0, limit || 6);
    if (!rows.length) return '<p class="w116empty">No updates yet. Upload a meeting transcript or add an update on the department page.</p>';
    return '<div class="w116upd">' + rows.map(function (u) {
      var src = srcOf(u); var tx = txFor(u);
      var bullets = sentences(u.text).slice(0, 5);
      return '<div class="u"><div class="hd"><b>' + E(u.entry_label || (u.created_at ? String(u.created_at).slice(0, 10) : '')) + '</b><span class="w116src ' + src[0] + '">' + src[1] + '</span>' +
        (tx ? '<a data-w116go="#tx/' + E(tx.id) + '">' + E(tx.title) + '</a>' : '') +
        (u.confidential ? '<span class="w116src manual">confidential</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<ul>' + bullets.map(function (b) { return '<li>' + E(b) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function mentionsHtml(d) {
    var names = members(d).map(function (m) { return lower(m.name); });
    var firsts = names.map(function (n) { return n.split(' ')[0]; });
    var men = (W116.mentions || []).filter(function (m) {
      if (!m.person || m.department === d) return false;
      var p = lower(m.person); var f = p.split(' ')[0];
      return names.indexOf(p) >= 0 || firsts.indexOf(f) >= 0;
    }).slice(0, 8);
    var re = KEYS[d];
    var cross = (typeof DB_UPDATES !== 'undefined' ? DB_UPDATES : []).filter(function (u) { return u.department !== d && re && re.test(u.text || ''); })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.created_at).localeCompare(String(a.created_at)); }).slice(0, 6);
    if (!men.length && !cross.length) return '<p class="w116empty">When another team\'s meeting or update talks about this team, it shows up here.</p>';
    var html = '<div class="w116men">';
    men.forEach(function (m) {
      var person = memberByName(m.person);
      html += '<div class="m">' + av(person || { name: m.person }, 30) + '<div><div class="t"><b>' + E(m.person) + '</b> was mentioned in ' + E(m.meeting_title || 'a meeting') + '</div>' +
        (m.context || m.quote ? '<div class="q">' + E(m.context || m.quote) + '</div>' : '') +
        '<div class="w"><span class="w116pill" style="background:' + color(m.department) + '">' + E(T.short[m.department] || label(m.department)) + '</span> ' + E(m.meeting_date || '') +
        (m.transcript_id ? ' &middot; <a data-w116go="#tx/' + E(m.transcript_id) + '">open the meeting</a>' : '') + '</div></div></div>';
    });
    cross.forEach(function (u) {
      var hit = sentences(u.text).filter(function (s) { return re.test(s); })[0] || String(u.text || '').slice(0, 200);
      html += '<div class="m"><span class="w116av" style="width:30px;height:30px;font-size:11px;background:' + color(u.department) + '">' + E(initials(T.short[u.department] || u.department)) + '</span>' +
        '<div><div class="t"><b>' + E(label(u.department)) + '</b> update' + (u.entry_label ? ', ' + E(u.entry_label) : '') + '</div><div class="q">' + E(hit) + '</div></div></div>';
    });
    return html + '</div>';
  }
  function kpiTiles(d, board) {
    var tasks = tasksFor(d, board, null);
    var today = dayKey(new Date().toISOString());
    var open = tasks.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; });
    var overdue = open.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date < today; });
    var since = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 86400000).toISOString();
    var done30 = tasks.filter(function (t) { return t.done && ((t.closed_at && t.closed_at >= since) || (!t.closed_at && t.updated_at && t.updated_at >= since)); });
    var projs = projectsFor(d, board).filter(function (p) { return (p.status || 'active') === 'active'; });
    var tx = (typeof txCache !== 'undefined' ? txCache : []).filter(function (x) { return typeof txDeptKey === 'function' && txDeptKey(x.department) === d && x.meeting_date && x.meeting_date >= since.slice(0, 10); });
    var up = teamEvents(d).filter(function (e) { return new Date(e.end_at || e.start_at) >= new Date(); });
    var tHash = board ? '#team/' + d + '/' + board.key : '#team/' + d + '/tasks';
    var tiles = [
      { n: open.length, l: 'open tasks', h: tHash },
      { n: overdue.length, l: 'overdue', h: tHash + (board ? '' : '') , warn: overdue.length > 0, q: 'overdue' },
      { n: done30.length, l: 'done, last 30 days', h: tHash, q: 'done' },
      { n: projs.length, l: 'active projects', h: tHash, q: 'projects' }
    ];
    if (!board) tiles.push({ n: tx.length, l: 'meetings, last 30 days', h: '#transcripts' });
    tiles.push({ n: up.length, l: 'upcoming meetings', h: up.length ? '#w116meet' : '#team/' + d, q: 'meet' });
    return '<div class="w116kpis">' + tiles.map(function (t) {
      return '<div class="w116kpi' + (t.warn ? ' warn' : '') + '" data-w116go="' + E(t.h) + '"' + (t.q ? ' data-w116q="' + t.q + '"' : '') + '><b>' + t.n + '</b><span>' + t.l + '</span></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function metricsHtml(d) {
    var rows = (W116.metrics || []).filter(function (m) { return m.department === d; });
    if (!rows.length) return '<p class="w116empty">No tracked KPIs yet.' + (staff() ? ' Add rows to the metrics table (key, label, target, unit, department) and they appear here.' : '') + '</p>';
    return '<div class="w116met">' + rows.map(function (m) {
      var cur = m.current_value == null ? null : Number(m.current_value); var tgt = m.target_value == null ? null : Number(m.target_value);
      var pct = (cur != null && tgt) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(cur / tgt * 100))) : null;
      return '<div class="row"><span class="lab">' + E(m.label || m.key) + (tgt != null ? ' <small style="color:var(--wa-muted)">target ' + E(tgt.toLocaleString()) + (m.unit ? ' ' + E(m.unit) : '') + '</small>' : '') + '</span>' +
        '<span class="val" data-w116met="' + E(m.id) + '" title="' + (staff() ? 'Click to update' : '') + '">' + (cur == null ? '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:14px">not tracked yet</span>' : E(cur.toLocaleString())) + (m.unit && cur != null ? '<small>' + E(m.unit) + '</small>' : '') + '</span>' +
        (pct != null ? '<div class="bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function projectsHtml(d, board) {
    var projs = projectsFor(d, board).filter(function (p) { return (p.status || 'active') === 'active'; });
    var all = window.w116Projects();
    if (!projs.length) return '<p class="w116empty">No active projects.</p>';
    var byId = {}; all.forEach(function (p) { byId[p.id] = p; });
    return '<div class="w116pro">' + projs.map(function (p) {
      var ts = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) { return Number(t.project_id) === Number(p.id); });
      var done = ts.filter(function (t) { return t.done; }).length;
      var pct = ts.length ? Math.round(done / ts.length * 100) : 0;
      var parent = p.parent_id && byId[p.parent_id] ? byId[p.parent_id].name + ' \u203a ' : '';
      return '<div class="p" data-w116go="#project/' + E(p.id) + '"><div class="n"><span>' + E(parent + p.name) + '</span><span>' + done + '/' + ts.length + '</span></div><div class="bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  /* the classic page's narrative: progress this period, priorities, pulse (dept_meta + dept_sentiment) */
  function progressHtml(d) {
    var meta = (typeof DB_META !== 'undefined' && DB_META[d]) || {};
    var prog = String(meta.progress || '').trim();
    var pri = Array.isArray(meta.priorities) ? meta.priorities.filter(Boolean) : [];
    var pulse = ''; try { pulse = typeof pulseChip === 'function' ? pulseChip(d) : ''; } catch (e) { pulse = ''; }
    if (!prog && !pri.length && !pulse) return '<p class="w116empty">No progress notes yet. They arrive from meeting transcripts' + (staff() ? ', or run the unprocessed ones below.' : '.') + '</p>' + (staff() ? '<div class="w116addrow"><a data-w116act="rerun" data-d="' + E(d) + '">&#8635; Re-run from transcripts</a></div>' : '');
    var pulseTxt = '';
    try {
      if (pulse) { var tmp = document.createElement('div'); tmp.innerHTML = pulse; var txt = String(tmp.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/[\u2661\u2665]/g, '').replace(/Team pulse/gi, '').trim(); var sc = txt.match(/^(\d+\/10\s*[\u2196-\u2199\u2192]?)\s*(?:\u00b7)?\s*(.*)$/); pulseTxt = '<span class="pulse"><b>Team pulse ' + E(sc ? sc[1].trim() : '') + '</b>' + E((sc ? sc[2] : txt).slice(0, 160)) + '</span>'; }
    } catch (e) { pulseTxt = ''; }
    var long = prog.length > 700;
    return '<div class="w116prog' + (long ? ' clamp' : '') + '" data-w116prog="1">' + pulseTxt +
      (prog ? '<p class="body">' + E(prog) + '</p>' + (long ? '<span class="more" data-w116act="unclamp">Read the whole note</span>' : '') : '') +
      (pri.length ? '<h4>High priority</h4><ol>' + pri.map(function (x) { return '<li>' + E(x) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ol>' : '') +
      (staff() ? '<div class="w116addrow"><a data-w116act="rerun" data-d="' + E(d) + '">&#8635; Re-run from transcripts</a></div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  var AREA_DEPT = { 'ops mtl': 'opsmtl', 'program development': 'progdev', 'ops to': 'opsto', 'marketing': 'marketing', 'fundraising': 'fundraising', 'grants': 'fundraising', 'events': 'fundraising', 'finance': 'fundraising', 'hr': 'hr', 'board': 'board' };
  function areaDept(area) {
    var s = lower(area);
    if (AREA_DEPT[s]) return AREA_DEPT[s];
    if (s.indexOf('program dev') >= 0) return 'progdev';
    if (s.indexOf('montreal') >= 0 || s === 'ops mtl') return 'opsmtl';
    if (s.indexOf('toronto') >= 0) return 'opsto';
    if (s.indexOf('fund') >= 0 || s.indexOf('grant') >= 0 || s.indexOf('financ') >= 0 || s.indexOf('event') >= 0) return 'fundraising';
    if (s.indexOf('market') >= 0) return 'marketing';
    if (s.indexOf('human') >= 0 || s === 'hr') return 'hr';
    if (s.indexOf('board') >= 0) return 'board';
    return null;
  }
  function decisionsHtml(d) {
    var rows = (typeof DB_DECISIONS !== 'undefined' ? DB_DECISIONS : []).filter(function (x) { return !x.done && areaDept(x.area) === d; });
    if (!rows.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w116card"><h3>Executive decisions for this team <span class="lnk" data-w116go="#overview">executive dashboard</span></h3><div class="w116dec">' + rows.map(function (x) {
      var ex = window.w116ExecOf ? window.w116ExecOf({ owner: x.owner, area: x.area }) : (x.owner || 'Steel');
      return '<div class="d" data-dec="' + E(x.id) + '"><b>' + E(x.title) + '</b>' +
        '<div class="m"><span class="ex">' + E(ex) + ' decides</span>' + (x.reason_category ? '<span>' + E(x.reason_category) + '</span>' : '') + (x.opened_at ? '<span>open since ' + E(String(x.opened_at).slice(0, 10)) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
        (x.suggested_move ? '<div class="mv">Suggested move: ' + E(x.suggested_move) + '</div>' : '') +
        '<div class="exec-rec" id="w116rec-' + E(d) + '-' + E(x.id) + '">' + (x.recommendation ? '<div class="rec-box">' + E(x.recommendation) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="acts">' + (staff() ? '<button data-rec="' + E(x.id) + '" data-w116act="rec" data-d="' + E(d) + '">' + (x.recommendation ? '&#8635; Refresh the recommendation' : '&#10024; Get a recommendation') + '</button>' : '') + '<a data-w116go="#decision/' + E(x.id) + '">Open on the dashboard &rarr;</a></div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function toolsHtml(d) {
    var tools = [];
    if (d === 'opsmtl' || d === 'opsto') { tools.push(['&#129387;', 'Volunteering manager', '#volunteering']); tools.push(['&#128214;', 'Recipes', '#volunteering/recipes']); }
    if (d === 'hr') { tools.push(['&#127891;', 'Avenue Academy', '#training']); tools.push(['&#128202;', 'Onboarding board', '#training']); if (tabVisible('hrcentre')) tools.push(['&#128101;', 'HR centre', '#hrcentre']); }
    if (d === 'board') tools.push(['&#128101;', 'Org chart', '#orgchart']);
    if (d === 'marketing') { tools.push(['&#9997;', 'Content drafter', '#create']); tools.push(['&#127891;', 'Learning Avenues', '#training']); }
    tools.push(['&#127919;', 'Department goals', '#goals']);
    tools.push(['&#128194;', 'Projects', '#projects']);
    tools.push(['&#128196;', 'Transcripts', '#transcripts']);
    return '<div class="w116tools">' + tools.map(function (t) { return '<a data-w116go="' + E(t[2]) + '">' + t[0] + ' ' + E(t[1]) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function addUpdateModal(d) {
    var when = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
    openModal('<div class="w116pm"><h3>Add an update for ' + E(label(d)) + '</h3><p class="m-sub">A note the whole team sees on this page, in the bullets on the Overview and in the executive digest.</p>' +
      '<label>Label</label><input type="text" id="w116ul" value="' + E(when) + '" placeholder="Jul 2 or Jul 2 (Otter)" />' +
      '<label>What happened</label><textarea id="w116ut" placeholder="Two or three sentences: what moved, what is next."></textarea>' +
      '<label style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;font-size:13px;color:var(--wa-text);margin-top:10px"><input type="checkbox" id="w116uc" style="width:15px;height:15px" /> Confidential (hidden from the board view)</label>' +
      '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" id="w116ux">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="w116us">Add the update</button></div></div>');
    document.getElementById('w116ux').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    document.getElementById('w116us').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var text = document.getElementById('w116ut').value.trim(); if (!text) { toast('Write the update first'); return; }
      var lab = document.getElementById('w116ul').value.trim() || when; var conf = document.getElementById('w116uc').checked;
      var b = document.getElementById('w116us'); b.disabled = true;
      var r = await db.from('dept_updates').insert({ department: d, entry_label: lab, text: text, added_by: who0(), confidential: conf, created_at: new Date().toISOString() });
      if (r && r.error) { b.disabled = false; toast('Save failed'); return; }
      try { logChange('dept_update', d, 'added', null, lab + ': ' + text.slice(0, 200)); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
      closeModal(); toast('Update added');
      try { if (typeof loadAll === 'function') await loadAll(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (typeof renderSection === 'function') renderSection(d); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (typeof renderWhatsNew === 'function') renderWhatsNew(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      if (window.w116Route) window.w116Route();
    });
  }
  function allUpdatesHtml(d) {
    var rows = (typeof DB_UPDATES !== 'undefined' ? DB_UPDATES : []).filter(function (u) { return u.department === d; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.created_at).localeCompare(String(a.created_at)); });
    if (!rows.length) return '<p class="w116empty">No updates yet.</p>';
    return '<div class="w116upd">' + rows.map(function (u) {
      var src = srcOf(u); var tx = txFor(u);
      return '<div class="u"><div class="hd"><b>' + E(u.entry_label || (u.created_at ? String(u.created_at).slice(0, 10) : '')) + '</b><span class="w116src ' + src[0] + '">' + src[1] + '</span>' + (tx ? '<a data-w116go="#tx/' + E(tx.id) + '">' + E(tx.title) + '</a>' : '') + (u.confidential ? '<span class="w116src manual">confidential</span>' : '') + '<span style="margin-left:auto;font-size:11px;color:var(--wa-muted)">' + E(String(u.created_at || '').slice(0, 10)) + '</span></div><ul>' + sentences(u.text).map(function (b) { return '<li>' + E(b) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function canInvite(d) {
    try {
      var r = (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || '';
      if (r === 'admin') return true;
      if (r === 'leader') { var me = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === lower((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || ''); })[0]; return !!(me && me.department === d); }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return false;
  }
  function teamHtml(d) {
    var ms = members(d);
    if (!ms.length) return '<p class="w116empty">No one on the roster yet.</p>';
    return '<div class="w116team">' + ms.map(function (m) {
      return '<div class="r" data-w116go="#person/' + E(m.id) + '">' + av(m, 30) + '<div><b>' + E(m.name) + '</b>' + (m.role ? '<br><span>' + E(m.role) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function eventsHtml(d) {
    var ev = teamEvents(d).filter(function (e) { return new Date(e.end_at || e.start_at) >= new Date(Date.now() - 3600000); }).slice(0, 5);
    if (!ev.length) return '<p class="w116empty">Nothing on the hub calendar for this team yet.' + (admin() ? ' Invite hub@welfareavenue.com to the team meeting and it appears here.' : '') + '</p>' + calNote();
    return '<div class="w116evl">' + ev.map(function (e) { return eventRow(e, { hideDept: true }); }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function boardsHtml(d) {
    var bs = T.boards[d] || [];
    if (!bs.length) return '';
    var today = dayKey(new Date().toISOString());
    return '<div class="w116boards">' + bs.map(function (b) {
      var ts = tasksFor(d, b, null); var open = ts.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; }); var od = open.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date < today; });
      var pr = projectsFor(d, b).filter(function (p) { return (p.status || 'active') === 'active'; });
      return '<div class="w116bcard" style="border-top-color:' + E(b.color) + '" data-w116go="#team/' + E(d) + '/' + E(b.key) + '"><div class="n">' + E(b.label) + '</div><div class="s">' + E(b.blurb || '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k"><span><b>' + open.length + '</b> open</span>' + (od.length ? '<span style="color:' + FLAG + '"><b style="color:' + FLAG + '">' + od.length + '</b> overdue</span>' : '') + '<span><b>' + pr.length + '</b> projects</span>' + (b.tool ? '<span>+ ' + E(b.toolLabel || 'tool') + '</span>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  /* ---------- wave287: the Overview, in the order a lead reads it ---------- */
  function todayKey() { return dayKey(new Date().toISOString()); }
  function plus(iso, n) { var x = new Date(iso + 'T12:00:00'); x.setDate(x.getDate() + n); return x.getFullYear() + '-' + String(x.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(x.getDate()).padStart(2, '0'); }
  function teamRows(d) { return window.w116TasksFor ? window.w116TasksFor(d, null, null) : tasksFor(d, null, null); }   /* the published one: wave258 leaves grants out of Fundraising */
  function requestsOpen(d) {
    try { if (!staff() || !window.w123 || !window.w123.loaded || !window.w123.loaded()) return null; return (window.w123.items(d, 'open') || []).filter(function (it) { return it.k === 'r'; }).length; } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  function nowNumbers(d) {
    var rows = teamRows(d), tdy = todayKey(), wk = plus(tdy, 7), since = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 86400000).toISOString();
    var open = rows.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; });
    return {
      open: open.length,
      overdue: open.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date < tdy; }).length,
      week: open.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date >= tdy && t.due_date <= wk; }).length,
      done30: rows.filter(function (t) { return t.done && ((t.closed_at && t.closed_at >= since) || (!t.closed_at && t.updated_at && t.updated_at >= since)); }).length,
      req: requestsOpen(d)
    };
  }
  function plural(n, one, many) { return n + ' ' + (n === 1 ? one : many); }
  function nowSentence(d) {
    var n = nowNumbers(d), parts = [];
    if (n.req) parts.push(plural(n.req, 'open request', 'open requests'));
    if (!n.open && !n.done30) return (parts.length ? parts[0] + '. ' : '') + 'No open tasks yet.';
    parts.push(n.overdue ? plural(n.overdue, 'task overdue', 'tasks overdue') : 'nothing overdue');
    if (n.week) parts.push(n.week + ' due this week');
    if (n.done30) parts.push(n.done30 + ' done in the last 30 days');
    var s = parts.length > 1 ? parts.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' + parts[parts.length - 1] : parts[0];
    return s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1) + '.';
  }
  function nowHtml(d) {
    var n = nowNumbers(d), base = '#team/' + E(d) + '/tasks';
    function tile(q, num, lab, zero, warn) {
      return '<div class="w116kpi w287tile' + (num ? '' : ' zero') + (warn && num ? ' warn' : '') + '" data-w116go="' + base + '" data-w116q="' + q + '" role="link" tabindex="0">' +
        (num ? '<b>' + num + '</b><span>' + lab + '</span>' : '<span class="z">' + zero + '</span>') + '</div>';
    }
    return '<div class="w287now" id="w287now"><p class="w287sent" data-d="' + E(d) + '">' + E(nowSentence(d)) + '</p>' +
      '<div class="w116kpis w287tiles">' + tile('all', n.open, 'Open', 'No open tasks') + tile('overdue', n.overdue, 'Overdue', 'Nothing overdue', true) + tile('week', n.week, 'Due this week', 'Nothing due this week') + '</div></div>';
  }
  /* Needs attention: up to six overdue or due within seven days, in the task-row style with a working tick */
  function needHtml(d) {
    var tdy = todayKey(), wk = plus(tdy, 7);
    var rows = teamRows(d).filter(function (t) { return !t.done && t.due_date && t.due_date <= wk; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.due_date).localeCompare(String(b.due_date)) || (b.flag ? 1 : 0) - (a.flag ? 1 : 0) || Number(a.id) - Number(b.id); });
    var shown = rows.slice(0, 6);
    var body = !shown.length ? '<p class="w287calm"><span class="ic">&#10003;</span>Nothing overdue or due in the next seven days.</p>'
      : '<div class="w287rows">' + shown.map(function (t) { return window.w116RowHtml ? window.w116RowHtml(t, { dept: d }) : '<div class="w116r" data-tid="' + E(t.id) + '">' + E(t.title) + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
        (rows.length > shown.length ? '<p class="w287more2">And ' + (rows.length - shown.length) + ' more.</p>' : '');
    return '<div class="w116card w287need" id="w287need"><h3>Needs attention <span class="lnk" data-w116go="#team/' + E(d) + '/tasks" data-w116q="today">see all</span></h3>' + body + '</div>';
  }
  /* Projects: a card per project (General first and quieter; sub-projects after their parent) */
  function ring(done, total, c) {
    var r = 15, C = 2 * Math.PI * r, f = total ? done / total : 0;
    return '<svg class="w287ring" viewBox="0 0 36 36" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="18" cy="18" r="' + r + '" fill="none" stroke="rgba(16,63,57,.10)" stroke-width="3.2"/>' +
      (f ? '<circle cx="18" cy="18" r="' + r + '" fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="3.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="' + (C * f).toFixed(2) + ' ' + C.toFixed(2) + '" transform="rotate(-90 18 18)"/>' : '') + '</svg>';
  }
  function projCardsHtml(d) {
    var all = projectsFor(d, null).filter(function (p) { return (p.status || 'active') === 'active'; });
    var ids = {}; all.forEach(function (p) { ids[Number(p.id)] = 1; });
    var gen = all.filter(isGeneral)[0] || null, kids = {}, tops = [];
    all.forEach(function (p) { if (p === gen) return; var par = p.parent_id && ids[Number(p.parent_id)] ? Number(p.parent_id) : 0; if (par) (kids[par] = kids[par] || []).push(p); else tops.push(p); });
    var srt = function (a, b) { return (Number(a.sort == null ? 999 : a.sort) - Number(b.sort == null ? 999 : b.sort)) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); };
    var order = gen ? [gen] : [];
    (function walk(list, depth) { list.sort(srt).forEach(function (p) { order.push(p); if (kids[Number(p.id)] && depth < 4) walk(kids[Number(p.id)], depth + 1); }); })(tops, 0);
    var tdy = todayKey(), tasks = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []);
    var cards = order.map(function (p) {
      var pid = Number(p.id), gp = isGeneral(p);
      var ts = tasks.filter(function (t) { var e = projOf(t); return e && Number(e.id) === pid; });
      var open = ts.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; }), done = ts.length - open.length;
      var od = open.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date < tdy; }).length;
      var next = open.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.due_date).localeCompare(String(b.due_date)); })[0];
      var c = projColor(p), away = elsewhere(p, null);
      var par = p.parent_id && ids[Number(p.parent_id)] ? projById(p.parent_id) : null;
      var leads = (Array.isArray(p.leads) ? p.leads : []).map(function (n) { return memberByName(n) || { name: n }; });
      var du = next && window.w116DueChip ? window.w116DueChip(next) : null;
      return '<div class="w287pc' + (gp ? ' gen' : '') + (par ? ' sub' : '') + '" role="link" tabindex="0" style="--c:' + c + '" ' + (away ? 'data-w116go="' + E(away) + '"' : 'data-w116go="#team/' + E(d) + '/tasks" data-w116q="project:' + pid + '"') + ' title="' + E(p.name) + '">' +
        '<div class="hd"><div class="tl">' + (par ? '<span class="ov">' + E(par.name) + '</span>' : (gp ? '<span class="ov">Catch-all</span>' : '')) + '<b class="nm"><i class="dot"></i>' + E(p.name) + '</b></div>' + ring(done, ts.length, c) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="ct"><span>' + (ts.length ? (open.length ? '<b>' + open.length + '</b> open' : 'All done') + (od ? ', <span class="od">' + od + ' overdue</span>' : '') : 'No tasks yet') + '</span>' + (done ? '<span class="dn">' + done + ' of ' + ts.length + ' done</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
        (next ? '<div class="nx"><span class="w287due ' + (du ? du.cls : '') + '">' + E(du ? du.txt : next.due_date) + '</span><span class="t">' + E(next.title) + '</span></div>' : '') +
        (leads.length ? '<div class="ld">' + stack(leads, 22, 4) + '<span>' + E(leads.map(function (x) { return String(x.name).split(' ')[0]; }).join(', ')) + '</span></div>' : '') + '</div>';
    });
    if (staff()) cards.push('<div class="w287pc new" role="button" tabindex="0" data-w116act="addproj" data-d="' + E(d) + '"><span class="pl">+</span>New project</div>');
    return '<div class="w116card w287proj" id="w287proj"><h3>Projects <span class="lnk" data-w116go="#projects">all projects</span></h3>' +
      (order.length || staff() ? '<div class="w287pgrid">' + cards.join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="w116empty">No active projects.</p>') + '</div>';
  }
  async function renderOverview(d) {
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    var meta = (typeof DB_META !== 'undefined' && DB_META[d]) || {};
    h.innerHTML = header(d, 'overview', null, meta.read ? E(meta.read) : '') + '<p class="w116empty">Loading...</p>';
    await Promise.all([loadEvents(), loadMentions(), loadProjects(), loadMetrics()]);
    if (!W116.cur || W116.cur.d !== d || W116.cur.page !== 'overview') return;
    /* wave287: header, Now (a sentence and three tiles), then the left column (Needs attention, Projects, the Team inbox that wave123
       puts first in the DOM and 287 orders third, Updates, Mentions, Executive decisions) and the right (meetings, KPIs, Team, Where the
       team is, Tools). The ids and classes later waves inject into stay: .w116kpis, .w116grid, .w116col, #w116meet, #w116updcard, .w116dec */
    var html = header(d, 'overview', null, meta.read ? E(meta.read) : '') +
      (d === 'fundraising' ? '' : boardsHtml(d)) +   /* wave232 (Steel, 12/10/26): Fundraising's sub-team cards repeated the Fundraising app; gone */
      nowHtml(d) +
      '<div class="w116grid w287grid"><div class="w116col">' +
      needHtml(d) +
      projCardsHtml(d) +
      '<div class="w116card" id="w116updcard"><h3>Updates <span class="lnk" data-w116act="allupd" data-d="' + E(d) + '">show all</span></h3>' + updatesHtml(d, 4) + (staff() ? '<div class="w116addrow"><a data-w116act="addupd" data-d="' + E(d) + '">+ Add an update</a><a data-w116act="addtask" data-d="' + E(d) + '">+ Add a task</a></div>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116card"><h3>Mentions from other teams</h3>' + mentionsHtml(d) + '</div>' +
      decisionsHtml(d) +
      '</div><div class="w116col">' +
      '<div class="w116card" id="w116meet"><h3>Upcoming meetings <span class="lnk" data-w116go="#team/' + E(d) + '/agenda">agenda</span></h3>' + eventsHtml(d) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116card"><h3>KPIs we track</h3>' + metricsHtml(d) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116card"><h3>Team <span class="lnk" data-w116go="#orgchart">org chart</span></h3>' + teamHtml(d) + (canInvite(d) ? '<div class="w116addrow"><a data-w113deptinv="' + E(d) + '">+ Invite a team member</a></div>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116card"><h3>Where the team is <span class="lnk" data-w116go="#team/' + E(d) + '/tasks">open work</span></h3>' + progressHtml(d) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116card w287tools"><h3>Tools</h3>' + toolsHtml(d) + '</div>' +
      '</div></div>';
    h.innerHTML = html;
    if (d === 'opsmtl') volunteeringTiles(d);
    /* the requests count arrives with wave123's inbox; the sentence picks it up when it does */
    if (requestsOpen(d) == null && staff() && window.w123 && window.w123.load && window.w123.loaded && !window.w123.loaded()) {
      Promise.resolve(window.w123.load()).then(function () { var s = document.querySelector('#w116host .w287sent[data-d="' + d + '"]'); if (s) s.textContent = nowSentence(d); }, function () { /* fine */ });
    }
  }
  window.w116NowSentence = nowSentence;
  async function volunteeringTiles(d) {
    try {
      var today = dayKey(new Date().toISOString());
      var ev = await db.from('volunteer_events').select('id, event_date, status, kind, location').gte('event_date', today).order('event_date', { ascending: true }).limit(12);
      var rows = ((ev && ev.data) || []).filter(function (e) { return e.event_date >= today && (e.status || '') !== 'cancelled' && (e.kind || 'service') === 'service' && (!e.location || lower(e.location).indexOf('toronto') < 0); });
      var next = rows[0];
      var grid = document.querySelector('#w116host .w116kpis');
      if (!grid || !W116.cur || W116.cur.d !== d) return;
      var tile = document.createElement('div');
      tile.className = 'w116kpi';
      tile.setAttribute('data-w116go', next ? '#volunteering/e/' + next.id : '#volunteering');
      tile.innerHTML = '<b>' + (next ? E(new Date(next.event_date + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })) : '\u2014') + '</b><span>next serving event</span>';
      grid.appendChild(tile);
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* ---------- Agenda (the existing tool, scoped) ---------- */
  function mountAgenda(d) {
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    h.innerHTML = header(d, 'agenda', 'Agenda', 'Meeting agendas for ' + E(label(d)) + ', drafted from transcripts, projects and open work.') +
      '<div class="w116agnote"><span>Agendas here are scoped to <b>' + E(label(d)) + '</b>.' + (admin() ? ' You are an admin, so the <a data-w116go="#agendas">master agenda tool</a> shows every team.' : '') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div id="w116agHost"></div>';
    var card = document.querySelector('#agendas .ag-page-card') || W116.agCard;
    var slot = document.getElementById('w116agHost');
    if (!card || !slot) { if (slot) slot.innerHTML = '<p class="w116empty">The agenda tool is not available in this build.</p>'; return; }
    W116.agCard = card;
    slot.appendChild(card);
    try {
      if (typeof waInitAgendasPage === 'function') waInitAgendasPage();
      var tsel = document.getElementById('agNewType'), dsel = document.getElementById('agNewDept'), fsel = document.getElementById('agFilterDept');
      if (tsel) { tsel.value = 'department'; tsel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change')); tsel.style.display = 'none'; }
      if (dsel) { if (!dsel.querySelector('option[value="' + d + '"]')) { var o = document.createElement('option'); o.value = d; o.textContent = label(d); dsel.appendChild(o); } dsel.value = d; dsel.style.display = 'none'; }
      if (fsel) { if (!fsel.querySelector('option[value="' + d + '"]')) { var o2 = document.createElement('option'); o2.value = d; o2.textContent = label(d); fsel.appendChild(o2); } fsel.value = d; fsel.style.display = 'none'; }
      if (typeof waRenderAgendasPage === 'function') waRenderAgendasPage();
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  function unmountAgenda() {
    var card = W116.agCard; var home = document.getElementById('agendas');
    if (!card || !home || card.parentNode === home) return;
    home.appendChild(card);
    try {
      ['agNewType', 'agNewDept', 'agFilterDept'].forEach(function (id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.style.display = ''; });
      var fsel = document.getElementById('agFilterDept'); if (fsel) fsel.value = 'all';
      var tsel = document.getElementById('agNewType'); if (tsel) tsel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* ---------- Tasks and boards (rendered by wave116b) ---------- */
  function renderTasks(d, board, ws) {
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    var title = board ? board.label : 'Tasks';
    var sub = board ? E(board.blurb || '') : 'Every task for ' + E(label(d)) + ', by list and by project.';
    /* wave287: an old #team/<d>/tasks/<workstream> link opens the project that replaced the workstream (else All); the address loses the workstream */
    if (ws && !board) {
      var wp = projForWs(d, ws), away = wp ? elsewhere(wp, null) : null;
      if (away) { try { location.replace(away); } catch (e) { go(away); } return; }
      if (window.w116Board && window.w116Board.preset) window.w116Board.preset(wp ? 'project:' + wp.id : 'all');
      try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#team/' + d + '/tasks'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      if (W116.cur) W116.cur.sub = '';
      ws = null;
    }
    var extra = '';
    if (board && board.tool) {
      extra = '<div class="w116seg" style="margin:-8px 0 16px"><a class="on">Tasks by project</a><a data-w116go="#' + E(board.tool) + '">' + E(board.toolLabel || 'Tool') + ' (QuickBooks, statements, forecast) &#8594;</a></div>';
    }
    h.innerHTML = header(d, board ? board.key : 'tasks', title, sub) + extra + '<div id="w116tasks"><p class="w116empty">Loading tasks...</p></div>';
    var slot = document.getElementById('w116tasks');
    if (window.w116Board && window.w116Board.render) {
      loadProjects().then(function () {
        if (!(W116.cur && W116.cur.d === d)) return;
        window.w116Board.render(slot, { dept: d, board: board, ws: ws || null });
        /* #task/<id> deep links (wave116i) open the task's drawer on its team board */
        if (window.__w116openTask) { var tid = window.__w116openTask; window.__w116openTask = null; setTimeout(function () { try { window.w116Board.open(tid); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 250); }
      });
    } else slot.innerHTML = '<p class="w116empty">The task board is not available in this build.</p>';
  }

  /* ---------- routing ---------- */
  function route(h) {
    var p = String(h || '').split('/');
    var d = p[1] || '', page = p[2] || 'overview', sub = p[3] || '';
    if (d === 'finance') { try { location.replace('#finances/tasks'); } catch (e) { go('#finances/tasks'); } return; }   /* wave164: Finance has no team page; its tasks are in the Finance app */
    if (!d || T.order.indexOf(d) < 0) { go('#mypage'); return; }
    if (!allowedDept(d)) { go('#' + ((window.w260 && window.w260.landing) ? window.w260.landing() : 'overview')); return; }   /* wave260: Home for anyone the Dashboard is not for */
    if (page === 'volunteering') { go('#volunteering'); return; }
    var board = boardDef(d, page);
    var key = board ? page : page;
    if (page !== 'agenda') unmountAgenda();
    W116.cur = { d: d, page: board ? 'board' : page, key: key, sub: sub, board: board };
    try { var hh = host(); if (hh) { hh.classList.toggle('w287ov', !board && page !== 'agenda' && page !== 'tasks'); hh.classList.toggle('w287tasks', !!board || page === 'tasks'); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }   /* wave287 */
    markNav(d, key);
    try { document.title = label(d) + ' \u00b7 Welfare Avenue Hub'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (board) renderTasks(d, board, null);
    else if (page === 'agenda') mountAgenda(d);
    else if (page === 'tasks') renderTasks(d, null, sub ? decodeURIComponent(sub) : null);
    else renderOverview(d);
    if (window.w116Board && window.w116Board.closeDrawer) { try { window.w116Board.closeDrawer(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  window.w116Route = function () { if (W116.cur) route('team/' + W116.cur.d + '/' + (W116.cur.page === 'board' ? W116.cur.key : W116.cur.page) + (W116.cur.sub ? '/' + W116.cur.sub : '')); };
  window.w116Refresh = function () { try { window.w116Route(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } };

  /* ---------- nav: the Departments menu ---------- */
  /* tools that live inside a team's menu: the real tab button moves in, so its wiring and role gating stay intact */
  var ADOPT = { opsmtl: [['volunteering', 'Volunteering']], fundraising: [['finances', 'Finance'], ['grants', 'Grants']],   /* wave258: the Grants page (Steel, 15/10/26 Fundraising meeting) sits in the Fundraising row */   /* wave164: the Finance app's page (hidden from the Hub's menu since wave153; Finance is not Fundraising's) */ hr: [['hrcentre', 'HR centre']] };
  function flyout(d) {
    var pages = pagesOf(d).filter(function (p) { return !p.tool && p.key !== 'volunteering'; });
    var html = pages.map(function (p) {
      return '<a data-w116="' + E(p.hash) + '" data-w116page="' + E(p.key) + '"' + (p.board ? ' class="w116ind"' : '') + '>' + E(p.label) + '</a>';
    }).join('');
    return html;
  }
  function adopt(bar, d, row) {
    var sub = row.querySelector('.w42sub'); if (!sub) return;
    (ADOPT[d] || []).forEach(function (pair) {
      var btn = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + pair[0] + '"]'); if (!btn) return;
      var dot = btn.querySelector('.tabdot');
      btn.innerHTML = (dot ? dot.outerHTML : '') + pair[1];
      btn.classList.add('w116tool');
      sub.appendChild(btn);
    });
  }
  function syncOpen() {
    try {
      document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .w42d').forEach(function (row) {
        var own = row.querySelector(':scope > .tab.active');
        var inner = row.querySelector('.w42sub .tab.active');
        row.classList.toggle('w116open', !!(own || inner));
        row.classList.toggle('w116act', !!(own || inner));
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  function restructureNav() {
    var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar'); if (!bar) return;
    var main = document.querySelector('main');
    T.order.forEach(function (d) {
      var b = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + d + '"]'); if (!b) return;
      var row = b.closest ? b.closest('.w42d') : null;
      if (!row) {
        /* a team the older nav did not wrap: give it the same row shape */
        row = document.createElement('div'); row.className = 'w42d';
        b.parentNode.insertBefore(row, b); row.appendChild(b);
        var sub = document.createElement('div'); sub.className = 'w42sub'; row.appendChild(sub);
      }
      var s = row.querySelector('.w42sub');
      if (s) {
        /* park any adopted tool buttons before the links are rebuilt, so they survive */
        Array.prototype.slice.call(s.querySelectorAll('.tab')).forEach(function (b) { bar.appendChild(b); });
        s.innerHTML = flyout(d); s.dataset.w116sub = '1';
      }
      adopt(bar, d, row);
      if (!row.querySelector('.w116chev')) {
        var ch = document.createElement('span'); ch.className = 'w116chev'; ch.setAttribute('title', 'Show pages'); ch.innerHTML = '&#9660;';
        ch.setAttribute('data-w116chev', d);
        row.insertBefore(ch, s || null);
      }
    });
    syncOpen();
    if (typeof wa40Enforce === 'function') { try { wa40Enforce(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
    /* order inside the Departments group follows the teams registry */
    var grp = bar.querySelector('.tab-group[data-w42g="departments"] .tab-group-p');
    if (grp) T.order.forEach(function (d) { var b = grp.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + d + '"]'); var row = b && b.closest ? b.closest('.w42d') : null; if (row) grp.appendChild(row); });
    /* the group header only shows when a team is visible */
    try { var grpH = bar.querySelector('.tab-group[data-w42g="departments"] .tab-group-h'); if (grpH) grpH.style.display = T.order.some(function (d) { var b = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + d + '"]'); return b && b.style.display !== 'none'; }) ? '' : 'none'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  window.w116Nav = restructureNav;
  /* later waves rebuild the bar on timers; when a tool drifts out of its team menu or the Strategy group dissolves, put it back */
  function navConsistent() {
    var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar'); if (!bar) return true;
    for (var d in ADOPT) {
      var row = (function () { var b = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + d + '"]'); return b && b.closest ? b.closest('.w42d') : null; })();
      if (!row) continue;
      for (var i = 0; i < ADOPT[d].length; i++) {
        var btn = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + ADOPT[d][i][0] + '"]');
        if (btn && !row.contains(btn)) return false;
      }
      var sub = row.querySelector('.w42sub');
      if (!sub || !sub.dataset.w116sub) return false;
    }
    if (window.w116StrategyOk && !window.w116StrategyOk()) return false;
    return true;
  }
  var navTimer = null;
  function watchNav() {
    var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar'); if (!bar || bar.dataset.w116watch) return;
    bar.dataset.w116watch = '1';
    new MutationObserver(function () {
      clearTimeout(navTimer);
      navTimer = setTimeout(function () {
        try { if (!navConsistent()) { restructureNav(); if (window.w116Strategy) window.w116Strategy(); } } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      }, 80);
    }).observe(bar, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa116Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa116Wired = '1';
    /* wave116i: the classic page's actions, now on the Overview */
    document.body.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
      var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w116act]');
      if (!a) return;
      var act = a.getAttribute('data-w116act'); var d = a.getAttribute('data-d') || (W116.cur && W116.cur.d);
      e.preventDefault();
      try {
        if (act === 'unclamp') { var box = a.closest('.w116prog'); if (box) box.classList.remove('clamp'); a.remove(); return; }
        if (act === 'allupd') { var card = document.getElementById('w116updcard'); if (card) { var body = card.querySelector('.w116upd, .w116empty'); if (body) body.outerHTML = allUpdatesHtml(d); a.textContent = 'all updates shown'; } return; }
        if (!staff()) return;
        if (act === 'rerun') { if (typeof rerunDept === 'function') await rerunDept(d); return; }
        if (act === 'addupd') { addUpdateModal(d); return; }
        if (act === 'addtask') { if (typeof taskModal === 'function') taskModal(d, null); return; }
        if (act === 'addproj') { try { var st = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('wa116.projects') || '{}'); st.team = d; localStorage.setItem('wa116.projects', JSON.stringify(st)); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } location.hash = '#projects'; setTimeout(function () { var b = document.querySelector('#projects [data-pact="new"]'); if (b) b.click(); }, 900); return; }
        if (act === 'rec') { var id = Number(a.getAttribute('data-rec')); if (id && typeof waGetRecommendation === 'function') waGetRecommendation(id, d, a); return; }
      } catch (err) { console.warn('wave116 overview action', err); }
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w116],[data-w116go]');
      if (!a) return;
      var h = a.getAttribute('data-w116') || a.getAttribute('data-w116go');
      if (!h) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab-group.open').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('open'); });
      document.body.classList.remove('wa-mob-open');
      if (h === '#w116meet') { var m = document.getElementById('w116meet'); if (m) m.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); return; }
      if (a.hasAttribute('data-w116q') && window.w116Board) { try { window.w116Board.preset(a.getAttribute('data-w116q')); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ } }
      if (location.hash === h) { try { applyHash(); } catch (e3) { /* fine */ } } else location.hash = h;
    });
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var ch = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w116chev]');
      if (!ch) return;
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      var row = ch.closest('.w42d'); if (row) row.classList.toggle('w116open');
    }, true);
    /* clicking a team in the menu opens its Overview (capture phase beats the base tab handler) */
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#tabBar .tab');
      if (!b) return;
      var id = b.getAttribute('data-tab');
      if (T.order.indexOf(id) < 0) return;
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      document.body.classList.remove('wa-mob-open');
      location.hash = '#team/' + id;
    }, true);
    /* tracked KPI values: click to update (staff) */
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var v = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w116met]');
      if (!v || !staff() || v.querySelector('input')) return;
      var id = v.getAttribute('data-w116met');
      var m = (W116.metrics || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0];
      if (!m) return;
      var inp = document.createElement('input'); inp.type = 'number'; inp.value = m.current_value == null ? '' : m.current_value;
      v.innerHTML = ''; v.appendChild(inp); inp.focus();
      var done = false;
      async function save() {
        if (done) return; done = true;
        var val = inp.value === '' ? null : Number(inp.value);
        var r = await db.from('metrics').update({ current_value: val, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', m.id);
        if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not save the KPI'); } else { m.current_value = val; toast('KPI updated'); try { logChange('metric', m.id, 'current_value', null, val); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ } }
        window.w116Refresh();
      }
      inp.addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) { if (ev.key === 'Enter') save(); if (ev.key === 'Escape') { done = true; window.w116Refresh(); } });
      inp.addEventListener('blur', save);
    });
  }

  /* ---------- Home: your upcoming meetings ---------- */
  function homeHtml() {
    var ev = myEvents().filter(function (e) { return new Date(e.end_at || e.start_at) >= new Date(Date.now() - 3600000); }).slice(0, 6);
    var inner;
    if (!W116.eventsAt) inner = '<p class="mp-empty">Loading the hub calendar...</p>';
    else if (!ev.length) inner = '<p class="mp-empty">No meetings with your name on the hub calendar in the next two months.' + (W116.calState && W116.calState.last_ok ? '' : ' The calendar sync has not run successfully yet.') + '</p>';
    else inner = '<div class="w116evl">' + ev.map(function (e) { return eventRow(e, { mine: true }); }).join('') + '</div>';
    return inner + '<p class="w116note">From hub@welfareavenue.com\'s calendar. Accept or decline here once Google Calendar is on in Settings (profile menu), or reply in Google Calendar; either way it updates here within 30 minutes.</p>';
  }
  function injectHome() {
    var page = document.getElementById('mypage');
    if (!page) return;
    var sec = document.getElementById('w116home');
    if (!sec) {
      sec = document.createElement('div'); sec.className = 'mp-sec'; sec.id = 'w116home';
      /* right under the greeting and stat tiles, above the checklists, so the day's meetings are the first thing you read */
      var anchor = page.querySelector('.mp-online') || page.querySelector('.mp-chips') || page.querySelector('.mp-head');
      if (anchor && anchor.parentNode) anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', sec);
      else { var grid = page.querySelector('.mp-grid'); var col = grid ? (grid.querySelector('.w74col') || grid) : null; if (!col) return; col.insertBefore(sec, col.firstChild); }
    }
    sec.innerHTML = '<h3>Your upcoming meetings</h3>' + homeHtml();
  }
  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __rmp116 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      var r = await __rmp116.apply(this, arguments);
      try { injectHome(); loadEvents().then(function () { injectHome(); }); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }

  /* ---------- permissions and registration ---------- */
  function enrol() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_TABS === 'undefined' || typeof WA40_DEFAULTS === 'undefined') return;
      ['progdev', 'team'].forEach(function (id) {
        if (WA40_TABS.indexOf(id) < 0) WA40_TABS.push(id);
        ['admin', 'leader', 'member'].forEach(function (r) { if (WA40_DEFAULTS[r] && WA40_DEFAULTS[r].indexOf(id) < 0) WA40_DEFAULTS[r].push(id); });
      });
      /* the master agenda tool is admins only; teams keep their own scoped agenda */
      ['leader', 'member', 'viewer'].forEach(function (r) {
        var arr = WA40_DEFAULTS[r]; if (!arr) return;
        var i = arr.indexOf('agendas'); if (i >= 0) arr.splice(i, 1);
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  function enrolMine() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_MINE === 'undefined' || !WA40_MINE || !Array.isArray(WA40_MINE.tabs) || !WA40_MINE.tabs.length) return;
      var tabs = WA40_MINE.tabs;
      if (tabs.indexOf('opsmtl') >= 0 && tabs.indexOf('progdev') < 0) tabs.push('progdev');
      if (T.order.some(function (d) { return tabs.indexOf(d) >= 0; }) && tabs.indexOf('team') < 0) tabs.push('team');
      /* the master agenda tool stays admins only even when an older per-person override lists it */
      if (WA40_MINE.role !== 'admin') { var ai = tabs.indexOf('agendas'); if (ai >= 0) tabs.splice(ai, 1); }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  enrol();
  if (window.WA_NAV && WA_NAV.register) {
    WA_NAV.register({ id: 'team', gate: 'member', panel: true, onShow: function (h) { try { route(h); } catch (e) { console.warn('team route', e); } } });
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp116 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function () {
      var out = __sp116.apply(this, arguments);
      try { syncOpen(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  /* leaving the team pages puts the agenda tool back where the master page expects it */
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    var h = String(location.hash || '');
    if (h.indexOf('#team/') !== 0) { unmountAgenda(); if (W116.cur) W116.cur = null; if (window.w116Board && window.w116Board.closeDrawer) { try { window.w116Board.closeDrawer(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  });

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa116 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa116.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        enrol(); enrolMine();
        if (typeof wa40Enforce === 'function') wa40Enforce();
        restructureNav();
        watchNav();
        [400, 1200, 3000].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(function () { try { if (!navConsistent()) { restructureNav(); if (window.w116Strategy) window.w116Strategy(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, ms); });
        loadEvents().then(function () {
          try { if (String(location.hash || '').indexOf('#team/') === 0) window.w116Refresh(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
          try { if (document.getElementById('w116home')) injectHome(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        });
        if (String(location.hash || '').indexOf('#team/') === 0) { try { applyHash(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      } catch (e) { console.warn('wave116 boot', e); }
      return r;
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v116b task boards (24/09/26): the ClickUp-style work surface
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "a clean interface with the robust functionality it already
   has, but additional capabilities like ClickUp or Asana would have."
   One component, w116Board, rendered inside the team pages (wave116):
   - Views: List (grouped), Board (kanban by status), Calendar (by due
     date). Group by project, workstream, status, owner, due window.
   - Filters: mine, owner, status, project, due (overdue, this week),
     flagged, search, show done. Sort: manual, due, updated, title.
     View settings remember themselves per team.
   - Quick add in every group (Enter saves, prefilled from the group),
     inline status and owner changes, drag and drop between statuses,
     projects, workstreams, owners and days, plus drag to reorder.
   - Bulk select: status, owner, due date, project, mark done.
   - Task drawer: title, status, owner, assignees, due, project,
     workstream, executive flag with reason, confidential, comments
     thread, recent activity, open on the department page, delete.
   Every write goes through the same dept_tasks rows, change_log
   entries and in-memory caches the rest of the Hub uses, then the
   classic department page repaints so both stay in step.
   wave287 (06/11/26, edited here; the look and the motion are in
   287_wave287): the page takes the Apple Reminders shape. A sidebar of
   smart lists (Today, Scheduled, All, Flagged, Mine, Completed) and the
   team's projects (General first, sub-projects indented, other teams'
   projects that hold this team's tasks, New project) picks the list;
   B.st.list keeps it ('today'... or 'p<id>'). Projects replace
   workstreams: a task with no project counts in its team's General
   project (projects.is_general), the workstream grouping, the drawer's
   Workstream field and the quick add's workstream are gone (subsection
   stays in the data; the Fundraising boards still read it). Rows have
   a round tick, the title and one line of facts; a tick completes the
   task with an Undo before the row folds away. New in the API: set,
   list, state, complete and the presets today, scheduled, all,
   flagged, mine, completed and week.
   ===================================================================== */

  /* ---------- helpers ---------- */
  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function today() { var d = new Date(); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0'); }
  function addDays(iso, n) { var d = new Date(iso + 'T12:00:00'); d.setDate(d.getDate() + n); return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
  function me() { try { var em = lower(USER && USER.email); return (DB_MEMBERS || []).filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === em; })[0] || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function myName() { var m = me(); if (m) return m.name; try { return (USER && USER.name) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function memberById(id) { try { return MEMBER_BY_ID[id] || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function members(d) { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.department === d ? 0 : 1) - (b.department === d ? 0 : 1) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); }); }
  function av(p, size, cls) { return window.w116Avatar ? window.w116Avatar(p, size, cls) : '<span class="w116av" style="width:' + size + 'px;height:' + size + 'px">' + E(String((p && p.name) || '?').slice(0, 1)) + '</span>'; }
  function assigneeIds(t) { return (typeof DB_ASSIGN !== 'undefined' ? DB_ASSIGN : []).filter(function (a) { return Number(a.task_id) === Number(t.id); }).map(function (a) { return Number(a.member_id); }); }
  function assignees(t) { return assigneeIds(t).map(memberById).filter(Boolean); }
  function bucket(t) {
    if (t.done) return 'Done';
    var s = lower(t.status);
    if (s.indexOf('progress') >= 0) return 'In progress';
    if (s.indexOf('wait') >= 0) return 'Waiting';
    if (s.indexOf('block') >= 0) return 'Blocked';
    return 'Open';
  }
  function stKey(b) { return b.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ''); }
  function stPill(t, extra) { var b = bucket(t); var c = STCOL[b]; return '<span class="w116st ' + (extra || '') + '" data-act="status" style="background:' + c[0] + ';color:' + c[1] + '" title="' + E(t.status || b) + '">' + E(b) + '</span>'; }
  function projects() { return window.w116Projects ? window.w116Projects() : []; }
  function projById(id) { return projects().filter(function (p) { return Number(p.id) === Number(id); })[0] || null; }
  function projLabel(p) { if (!p) return ''; var par = p.parent_id ? projById(p.parent_id) : null; return (par ? par.name + ' \u203a ' : '') + p.name; }
  function dueInfo(t) {
    if (!t.due_date) return { cls: '', txt: '' };
    var d = t.due_date, tdy = today();
    var lbl = new Date(d + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString([], { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
    if (t.done) return { cls: '', txt: lbl };
    if (d < tdy) return { cls: 'over', txt: lbl };
    if (d === tdy) return { cls: 'soon', txt: 'Today' };
    if (d === addDays(tdy, 1)) return { cls: 'soon', txt: 'Tomorrow' };
    if (d <= addDays(tdy, 7)) return { cls: 'soon', txt: lbl };
    return { cls: '', txt: lbl };
  }
  function comments(t) { try { return window.__w116cm && window.__w116cm[String(t.id)] || 0; } catch (e) { return 0; } }
  function wsList(d) {
    var out = [];
    try { (SECTIONS[d] && SECTIONS[d].subsections || []).forEach(function (s) { if (out.indexOf(s.title) < 0) out.push(s.title); }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (out.indexOf('General') < 0) out.unshift('General');
    (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).forEach(function (t) { if (t.department === d && t.subsection && out.indexOf(t.subsection) < 0) out.push(t.subsection); });
    return out;
  }
  function key(scope) { return 'wa116.' + scope.dept + '.' + (scope.board ? scope.board.key : (scope.ws || 'all')); }
  function loadState(scope) {
    var def = { view: 'list', list: 'all', group: 'project', sort: 'manual', mine: false, owner: '', status: '', project: '', due: '', flag: false, done: false, q: '', closed: {}, showf: false };
    try { var s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key(scope)) || 'null'); if (s) Object.keys(def).forEach(function (k) { if (s[k] !== undefined) def[k] = s[k]; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (def.group === 'workstream') def.group = 'project';   /* wave287: workstreams became projects */
    if (!def.list || (def.list.charAt(0) === 'p' && !projById(def.list.slice(1)))) def.list = 'all';
    return def;
  }
  function saveState() { try { var s = Object.assign({}, B.st); s.q = ''; localStorage.setItem(key(B.scope), JSON.stringify(s)); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function boardOf(t) {
    var bs = T.boards[t.department] || [];
    var pk = t.project_id && window.w116BoardKeyOf ? window.w116BoardKeyOf(projById(t.project_id)) : null;
    for (var i = 0; i < bs.length; i++) {
      var b = bs[i]; var ids = T.boardProjects[b.key] || [];
      if (t.subsection === b.ws || pk === b.key || (t.project_id && ids.indexOf(Number(t.project_id)) >= 0)) return b;
    }
    return null;
  }
