  function svg(k, cls) { return '<svg class="' + (cls || 'w287i') + '" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">' + (ICON[k] || '') + '</svg>'; }
  function smartOf(k) { return SMART.filter(function (x) { return x.k === k; })[0] || null; }
  function isGen(p) { return !!(p && window.w116IsGeneral && window.w116IsGeneral(p)); }
  function generalOf(d) { return window.w116GeneralOf ? window.w116GeneralOf(d) : null; }
  /* the project a task counts in: its own, else its team's General, else 0 (No project) */
  function effPid(t) { var p = t.project_id ? projById(t.project_id) : null; if (p) return Number(p.id); var g = generalOf(t.department); return g ? Number(g.id) : 0; }
  function pColor(p) { return p && window.w116ProjColor ? window.w116ProjColor(p) : '#797773'; }
  function ink(c) { return window.w116Ink ? window.w116Ink(c) : c; }
  function elsewhere(p) { return p && window.w116Elsewhere ? window.w116Elsewhere(p, B.scope && B.scope.board) : null; }
  function staffOk() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function teamLabel(d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || (T.labels && T.labels[d]) || d; }
  function mineT(t) {
    var meN = lower(myName()), meM = me();
    return (!!meN && (lower(t.owner) === meN || lower(t.owner).indexOf(meN.split(' ')[0]) === 0)) || (!!meM && assigneeIds(t).indexOf(Number(meM.id)) >= 0);
  }
  function srtP(a, b) { return (Number(a.sort == null ? 999 : a.sort) - Number(b.sort == null ? 999 : b.sort)) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); }
  /* everything the sidebar and the lists read, computed once per render */
  function model() {
    var s = B.scope, d = s.dept;
    var team = window.w116TasksFor ? window.w116TasksFor(d, s.board, s.ws) : (DB_TASKS || []).filter(function (t) { return t.department === d; });
    var open = team.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; });
    var counts = {}; open.forEach(function (t) { var k = effPid(t); counts[k] = (counts[k] || 0) + 1; });
    var ownAll = window.w116ProjectsFor ? window.w116ProjectsFor(d, s.board) : projects().filter(function (p) { return p.department === d; });
    var own = ownAll.filter(function (p) { return (p.status || 'active') === 'active' || counts[Number(p.id)]; });
    var ownIds = {}; own.forEach(function (p) { ownIds[Number(p.id)] = 1; });
    /* other teams' tasks filed in this team's projects are part of those projects */
    var cross = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) { return t.department !== d && t.project_id && ownIds[Number(t.project_id)]; });
    var pcount = {}; Object.keys(counts).forEach(function (k) { pcount[k] = counts[k]; });
    cross.forEach(function (t) { if (!t.done) pcount[Number(t.project_id)] = (pcount[Number(t.project_id)] || 0) + 1; });
    var gen = own.filter(isGen)[0] || null;
    var kids = {}, tops = [];
    own.forEach(function (p) { if (p === gen) return; var par = p.parent_id && ownIds[Number(p.parent_id)] ? Number(p.parent_id) : 0; if (par) (kids[par] = kids[par] || []).push(p); else tops.push(p); });
    var ordered = []; if (gen) ordered.push({ p: gen, depth: 0 });
    (function walk(list, depth) { list.sort(srtP).forEach(function (p) { ordered.push({ p: p, depth: depth }); if (kids[Number(p.id)] && depth < 4) walk(kids[Number(p.id)], depth + 1); }); })(tops, 0);
    var L = (B.st && B.st.list) || 'all';
    var other = Object.keys(counts).filter(function (k) { return Number(k) && !ownIds[Number(k)]; }).map(function (k) { return projById(k); }).filter(Boolean);
    if (L.charAt(0) === 'p' && !ownIds[Number(L.slice(1))] && !other.some(function (p) { return 'p' + p.id === L; })) { var cur = projById(L.slice(1)); if (cur) other.push(cur); }
    other.sort(function (a, b) { return (T.order.indexOf(a.department) - T.order.indexOf(b.department)) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var tdy = today();
    var smart = {
      today: open.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date <= tdy; }).length,
      scheduled: open.filter(function (t) { return !!t.due_date; }).length,
      all: open.length,
      flagged: open.filter(function (t) { return !!t.flag; }).length,
      mine: open.filter(mineT).length,
      completed: team.filter(function (t) { return t.done; }).length
    };
    return { team: team, open: open, cross: cross, own: own, ownIds: ownIds, ordered: ordered, other: other, gen: gen, counts: counts, pcount: pcount, smart: smart, none: counts[0] || 0 };
  }
  function listRows(m, L) {
    if (L.charAt(0) === 'p') {
      var pid = Number(L.slice(1));
      var r = m.team.filter(function (t) { return effPid(t) === pid; });
      return m.ownIds[pid] ? r.concat(m.cross.filter(function (t) { return Number(t.project_id) === pid; })) : r;
    }
    if (L === 'none') return m.team.filter(function (t) { return effPid(t) === 0; });
    return m.team;
  }
  function isProjList(L) { return L === 'none' || L.charAt(0) === 'p'; }
  /* what a task added in this list starts with */
  function listPre() {
    var L = B.st.list || 'all', p = {};
    if (L.charAt(0) === 'p') p.project = Number(L.slice(1));
    if (L === 'today' || L === 'scheduled') p.due = today();
    if (L === 'flagged') p.flag = true;
    if (B.scope.board) p.ws = B.scope.board.ws;
    return p;
  }
  function setList(l) {
    var st = B.st; st.list = l;
    st.due = ''; st.status = ''; st.project = ''; st.flag = false; st.mine = false; st.done = false;
    st.group = isProjList(l) ? 'due' : 'project';
    B.sel = {}; B.qaOpen = null; B.listMoved = true;
    saveState(); render();
  }
  function dueChip(t) {
    if (!t.due_date) return null;
    var d = t.due_date, tdy = today();
    var lbl = new Date(d + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString([], { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
    if (t.done) return { cls: 'past', txt: lbl };
    if (d < tdy) { var n = Math.round((new Date(tdy + 'T12:00:00').getTime() - new Date(d + 'T12:00:00').getTime()) / 86400000); return { cls: 'over', txt: n === 1 ? '1 day overdue' : n + ' days overdue' }; }
    if (d === tdy) return { cls: 'today', txt: 'Today' };
    if (d === addDays(tdy, 1)) return { cls: 'soon', txt: 'Tomorrow' };
    if (d <= addDays(tdy, 6)) return { cls: 'soon', txt: new Date(d + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: 'long' }) };
    return { cls: '', txt: lbl };
  }
  window.w116DueChip = dueChip;

  /* ---------- writes ---------- */
  function who0() { try { return who(); } catch (e) { return 'staff'; } }
  function log(t, field, a, b) { try { logChange('dept_task', t.id, field, a, b); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  async function patch(t, p, logs) {
    var now = new Date().toISOString();
    var full = Object.assign({}, p, { updated_by: who0(), updated_at: now });
    var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').update(full).eq('id', t.id);
    if (r && r.error) { toast('Save failed, check the connection'); return false; }
    Object.keys(full).forEach(function (k) { t[k] = full[k]; });
    (logs || []).forEach(function (l) { log(t, l[0], l[1], l[2]); });
    return true;
  }
  function after() {
    render();
    var d = B.scope && B.scope.dept;
    setTimeout(function () {
      try { if (d && typeof renderSection === 'function') renderSection(d); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (typeof renderNeedsMe === 'function') renderNeedsMe(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }, 40);
  }
  async function setStatus(t, st) {
    var old = bucket(t);
    if (st === old && t.status === st) return;
    var isDone = st === 'Done';
    var p = { status: st, done: isDone, closed_at: isDone ? new Date().toISOString() : null };
    if (await patch(t, p, [[isDone ? 'done' : 'status', old, st]])) { toast(isDone ? 'Marked done' : 'Status: ' + st); after(); }
  }
  async function setOwner(t, name) {
    var old = t.owner || '';
    if (await patch(t, { owner: name || '' }, [['owner', old || 'unassigned', name || 'unassigned']])) { toast(name ? 'Owner: ' + name : 'Owner cleared'); after(); }
  }
  async function setDue(t, d) {
    var old = t.due_date || null;
    if (d === old) return;
    if (await patch(t, { due_date: d || null }, [['due_date', old, d || null]])) { toast(d ? 'Due ' + d : 'Due date cleared'); after(); }
  }
  async function setProject(t, pid) {
    var old = t.project_id ? projLabel(projById(t.project_id)) : 'none';
    var np = pid ? projById(pid) : null;
    if (Number(t.project_id || 0) === Number(pid || 0)) return;
    if (await patch(t, { project_id: pid ? Number(pid) : null }, [['project', old, np ? projLabel(np) : 'none']])) { toast(np ? 'Moved to ' + np.name : 'Removed from project'); after(); }
  }
  async function setWs(t, ws) {
    var old = t.subsection || 'General';
    var nv = (!ws || ws === 'General') ? null : ws;
    if ((t.subsection || null) === nv) return;
    if (await patch(t, { subsection: nv }, [['workstream', old, nv || 'General']])) { toast('Workstream: ' + (nv || 'General')); after(); }
  }
  async function setAssignees(t, ids) {
    var cur = assigneeIds(t);
    var toAdd = ids.filter(function (i) { return cur.indexOf(i) < 0; });
    var toDel = cur.filter(function (i) { return ids.indexOf(i) < 0; });
    if (!toAdd.length && !toDel.length) return;
    if (toAdd.length) { var r1 = await db.from('task_assignees').insert(toAdd.map(function (i) { return { task_id: t.id, member_id: i }; })); if (r1 && r1.error) { toast('Could not add the assignee'); return; } }
    if (toDel.length) { var r2 = await db.from('task_assignees').delete().eq('task_id', t.id).in('member_id', toDel); if (r2 && r2.error) { toast('Could not remove the assignee'); return; } }
    DB_ASSIGN = DB_ASSIGN.filter(function (a) { return !(Number(a.task_id) === Number(t.id) && toDel.indexOf(Number(a.member_id)) >= 0); });
    toAdd.forEach(function (i) { DB_ASSIGN.push({ task_id: t.id, member_id: i }); });
    var nm = function (arr) { return arr.map(function (i) { return (memberById(i) || {}).name; }).filter(Boolean).join(', ') || 'none'; };
    log(t, 'assignees', nm(cur), nm(ids));
    toast('Assignees updated'); after();
  }
  async function addTask(title, pre) {
    title = String(title || '').trim();
    if (!title) return null;
    var d = B.scope.dept;
    /* wave287: the workstream is set only on the Fundraising boards (pre.ws), which still read it; the project comes from the list or the group */
    var row = { department: d, subsection: pre.ws && pre.ws !== 'General' ? pre.ws : null, title: title, owner: pre.owner != null ? pre.owner : (myName() || ''), status: pre.status || 'Open', flag: !!pre.flag, priority: false, done: false, sort: 999, due_date: pre.due || null, project_id: pre.project ? Number(pre.project) : null, confidential: false, updated_by: who0(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), opened_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    if (pre.status === 'Done') { row.done = true; row.closed_at = new Date().toISOString(); }
    var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').insert(row).select('*').single();
    if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not add the task'); return null; }
    var saved = (r && r.data) || row;
    if (!saved.id) saved.id = -Date.now();
    DB_TASKS.push(saved);
    log(saved, 'added', null, title);
    if (pre.assignee) { try { await db.from('task_assignees').insert({ task_id: saved.id, member_id: pre.assignee }); DB_ASSIGN.push({ task_id: saved.id, member_id: pre.assignee }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    toast('Task added');
    after();
    return saved;
  }
  async function delTask(t) {
    if (!confirm('Delete "' + t.title + '"? This cannot be undone.')) return;
    var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').delete().eq('id', t.id);
    if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not delete the task'); return; }
    DB_TASKS = DB_TASKS.filter(function (x) { return Number(x.id) !== Number(t.id); });
    DB_ASSIGN = DB_ASSIGN.filter(function (a) { return Number(a.task_id) !== Number(t.id); });
    try { logChange('dept_task', t.id, 'deleted', t.title, null); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    toast('Task deleted'); closeDrawer(); after();
  }
  async function reorder(groupRows, movedId, beforeId) {
    var ids = groupRows.map(function (t) { return Number(t.id); }).filter(function (i) { return i !== Number(movedId); });
    var at = beforeId ? ids.indexOf(Number(beforeId)) : ids.length;
    if (at < 0) at = ids.length;
    ids.splice(at, 0, Number(movedId));
    var byId = {}; groupRows.forEach(function (t) { byId[Number(t.id)] = t; });
    var writes = [];
    ids.forEach(function (id, i) { var t = byId[id]; var s = (i + 1) * 10; if (t && Number(t.sort) !== s) { t.sort = s; writes.push(db.from('dept_tasks').update({ sort: s }).eq('id', id)); } });
    B.st.sort = 'manual';
    render();
    try { await Promise.all(writes); } catch (e) { toast('Order not saved'); }
  }
  /* wave287: the tick. The circle fills, the task is saved as done, an Undo shows, and the row folds away a moment later */
  function rowsOf(id) { return Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-tid="' + String(id).replace(/"/g, '') + '"]')); }
  /* after a tick or an Undo: the board repaints itself; a team Overview (no board on screen) repaints through wave116's router */
  function afterTick() {
    after();
    if (!B.slot || !document.body.contains(B.slot)) { try { if (String(location.hash || '').indexOf('#team/') === 0 && window.w116Refresh) window.w116Refresh(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function reduced() { try { return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  async function complete(t) {
    if (!t) return false;
    if (t.done) { await setStatus(t, 'Open'); return true; }
    if (B.fold[t.id]) return false;
    var prev = { status: t.status && bucket({ status: t.status }) !== 'Done' ? t.status : 'Open' };
    B.fold[t.id] = 1;
    rowsOf(t.id).forEach(function (r) { r.classList.add('w287tk'); var ck = r.querySelector('.w116done'); if (ck) ck.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'true'); });
    var ok = await patch(t, { status: 'Done', done: true, closed_at: new Date().toISOString() }, [['done', bucket({ status: prev.status }), 'Done']]);
    if (!ok) { delete B.fold[t.id]; rowsOf(t.id).forEach(function (r) { r.classList.remove('w287tk'); }); return false; }
    snack('Done: ' + (t.title.length > 60 ? t.title.slice(0, 58) + '…' : t.title), function () { undoDone(t, prev); });
    clearTimeout(B.foldT[t.id]);
    B.foldT[t.id] = setTimeout(function () {
      rowsOf(t.id).forEach(function (r) { r.classList.add('w287gone'); });
      setTimeout(function () { delete B.fold[t.id]; delete B.foldT[t.id]; afterTick(); }, reduced() ? 120 : 220);
    }, 1400);
    return true;
  }
  async function undoDone(t, prev) {
    clearTimeout(B.foldT[t.id]); delete B.foldT[t.id]; delete B.fold[t.id];
    if (await patch(t, { status: prev.status, done: false, closed_at: null }, [['status', 'Done', prev.status]])) { toast('Back on the list'); afterTick(); }
  }
  function snack(text, undo) {
    var el = document.getElementById('w287snack');
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w287snack'; el.setAttribute('role', 'status'); el.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite'); document.body.appendChild(el);
      el.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w287sn]'); if (!b) return; var fn = el.__undo; hideSnack(); if (b.getAttribute('data-w287sn') === 'undo' && fn) fn(); });
    }
    el.__undo = undo || null;
    el.innerHTML = '<span class="t">' + E(text) + '</span>' + (undo ? '<button type="button" data-w287sn="undo">Undo</button>' : '') + '<button type="button" class="x" data-w287sn="x" aria-label="Dismiss">&#10005;</button>';
    void el.offsetWidth; el.classList.add('on');
    clearTimeout(B.snT); B.snT = setTimeout(hideSnack, 6000);
  }
  function hideSnack() { var el = document.getElementById('w287snack'); if (el) { el.classList.remove('on'); el.__undo = null; } clearTimeout(B.snT); }

  /* ---------- selection of tasks ---------- */
  function scopeRows() {
    var st = B.st;
    var m = B.model = model(); var L = st.list || 'all';
    var rows = listRows(m, L);
    var tdy = today(), wk = addDays(tdy, 7);
    return rows.filter(function (t) {
      if (L === 'completed') { if (!t.done) return false; }
      else if (!st.done && t.done && !(st.status === 'Done') && !B.fold[t.id]) return false;
      if (L === 'today' && !(t.due_date && t.due_date <= tdy)) return false;
      if (L === 'scheduled' && !t.due_date) return false;
      if (L === 'flagged' && !t.flag) return false;
      if (L === 'mine' && !mineT(t)) return false;
      if (st.status && bucket(t) !== st.status) return false;
      if (st.mine && !mineT(t)) return false;
      if (st.owner) { var o = lower(t.owner); if (st.owner === '__none' ? !!o : (o !== lower(st.owner) && (!assignees(t).some(function (m) { return lower(m.name) === lower(st.owner); })))) return false; }
      if (st.project) { if (st.project === '__none' ? effPid(t) !== 0 : effPid(t) !== Number(st.project)) return false; }
      if (st.due === 'overdue' && !(t.due_date && t.due_date < tdy && !t.done)) return false;
      if (st.due === 'today' && t.due_date !== tdy) return false;
      if (st.due === 'week' && !(t.due_date && t.due_date >= tdy && t.due_date <= wk)) return false;
      if (st.due === 'none' && t.due_date) return false;
      if (st.flag && !t.flag) return false;
      if (st.q) { var q = lower(st.q); if (lower(t.title).indexOf(q) < 0 && lower(t.owner).indexOf(q) < 0 && lower(projLabel(projById(effPid(t)))).indexOf(q) < 0) return false; }
      return true;
    }).sort(function (a, b) {
      if (L === 'completed' && st.sort === 'manual') return String(b.closed_at || b.updated_at || '').localeCompare(String(a.closed_at || a.updated_at || ''));
      if (st.sort === 'due') return String(a.due_date || '9999').localeCompare(String(b.due_date || '9999')) || (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0);
      if (st.sort === 'updated') return String(b.updated_at || '').localeCompare(String(a.updated_at || ''));
      if (st.sort === 'title') return String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title));
      return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0) || Number(a.id) - Number(b.id);
    });
  }
  function groupsFor(rows) {
    var st = B.st, s = B.scope, out = [];
    function add(k, name, list, pre, color) { out.push({ key: k, name: name, rows: list, pre: pre || {}, color: color }); }
    var wsB = s.board ? s.board.ws : null;
    if (st.group === 'project' || st.group === 'workstream') {
      /* wave287: one group per project that has tasks in this list, in the sidebar's order (General first, sub-projects after their parent) */
      var m = B.model || model();
      var byP = {}; rows.forEach(function (t) { var k = effPid(t); (byP[k] = byP[k] || []).push(t); });
      m.ordered.map(function (o) { return o.p; }).concat(m.other).forEach(function (p) {
        var k = Number(p.id); if (!byP[k]) return;
        add('p' + k, projLabel(p), byP[k], { project: k, ws: wsB }, pColor(p)); delete byP[k];
      });
      Object.keys(byP).forEach(function (k) { if (Number(k) === 0) return; var p = projById(k); add('p' + k, p ? projLabel(p) : 'Project ' + k, byP[k], { project: Number(k), ws: wsB }, p ? pColor(p) : null); });
      if (byP[0]) add('p0', 'No project', byP[0], { ws: wsB });
      if (!out.length && st.list !== 'completed') {
        /* nothing to show: one empty group to add into (the open project, else General) */
        var L0 = st.list || 'all';
        var tgt = L0.charAt(0) === 'p' ? projById(L0.slice(1)) : (m.gen || (m.ordered[0] && m.ordered[0].p) || null);
        if (tgt) add('p' + tgt.id, projLabel(tgt), [], { project: Number(tgt.id), ws: wsB }, pColor(tgt)); else add('p0', 'No project', [], { ws: wsB });
      }
    } else if (st.group === 'status') {
      STATUSES.forEach(function (b) { add('s' + b, b, rows.filter(function (t) { return bucket(t) === b; }), { status: b }, STCOL[b][1]); });
    } else if (st.group === 'assignee') {
      var names = []; rows.forEach(function (t) { var o = t.owner || ''; if (o && names.indexOf(o) < 0) names.push(o); });
      names.sort().forEach(function (n) { add('a' + n, n, rows.filter(function (t) { return t.owner === n; }), { owner: n }); });
      add('a0', 'Unassigned', rows.filter(function (t) { return !t.owner; }), { owner: '' });
    } else if (st.group === 'due') {
      var tdy = today(), wk = addDays(tdy, 7);
      add('d1', 'Overdue', rows.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date < tdy && !t.done; }), { due: addDays(tdy, -1) }, FLAG);
      add('d2', 'Today', rows.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date === tdy; }), { due: tdy });
      add('d3', 'This week', rows.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date > tdy && t.due_date <= wk; }), { due: addDays(tdy, 3) });
      add('d4', 'Later', rows.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date > wk; }), { due: addDays(tdy, 14) });
      add('d5', 'No date', rows.filter(function (t) { return !t.due_date || (t.done && t.due_date < tdy); }), {});
      /* wave287: Reminders shows the windows that hold something; No date stays as the place to add */
      out = out.filter(function (g) { return g.rows.length || g.key === 'd5'; });
      if (wsB) out.forEach(function (g) { g.pre.ws = wsB; });
    } else if (st.group === 'board') {
      (T.boards[s.dept] || []).forEach(function (b) { add('b' + b.key, b.label, rows.filter(function (t) { return boardOf(t) === b; }), { ws: b.ws }, b.color); });
      add('b0', 'Everything else', rows.filter(function (t) { return !boardOf(t); }), {});
    } else add('all', 'All tasks', rows, { ws: s.board ? s.board.ws : (s.ws || null) });
    return out;
  }

  /* ---------- rendering ---------- */
  /* wave287: a row is a round tick (edged in the project's colour), the title, and one quiet line of facts. ctx.lone: no group around
     it (the Overview's Needs attention), so the project always shows */
  function factsHtml(t, ctx) {
    ctx = ctx || {};
    var as = assignees(t), du = dueChip(t), pid = effPid(t), p = pid ? projById(pid) : null, b = bucket(t);
    var L = (B.st && B.st.list) || 'all', grp = B.st && B.st.group;
    var showP = p && (ctx.lone || ((L.charAt(0) !== 'p' || Number(L.slice(1)) !== pid) && grp !== 'project' && grp !== 'workstream'));
    var dept = ctx.dept || (B.scope && B.scope.dept);
    var oa = t.owner ? av({ name: t.owner, avatar_url: ((DB_MEMBERS || []).filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name) === lower(t.owner); })[0] || {}).avatar_url }, 20) : '';
    var more = as.filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name) !== lower(t.owner); });
    var f = [], tail = [];   /* tail: the set-it-here controls with nothing to show yet; they trail the line and appear on hover */
    var asH = '<span class="w116as' + (oa || more.length ? '' : ' w287none') + '" data-act="owner" title="' + E(t.owner ? 'Owner: ' + t.owner + (more.length ? ', with ' + more.map(function (m) { return m.name; }).join(', ') : '') : 'Set an owner') + '">' + (oa || (more.length ? '' : '<span class="none">+</span>')) + more.slice(0, 3).map(function (m) { return av(m, 20); }).join('') + (oa && !more.length ? '<span class="w287on">' + E(String(t.owner).split(' ')[0]) + '</span>' : '') + '</span>';
    var duH = '<span class="w116due w287due ' + (du ? du.cls : 'nodate') + '" data-act="due" title="' + E(t.due_date ? 'Due ' + t.due_date : 'Set a due date') + '">' + (du ? E(du.txt) : 'Add a date') + '</span>';
    (oa || more.length ? f : tail).push(asH);
    (du ? f : tail).push(duH);
    if (showP) f.push('<span class="w287ptag" title="' + E(projLabel(p)) + '"><i style="background:' + pColor(p) + '"></i><span>' + E(p.name) + '</span></span>');
    if (dept && t.department !== dept) f.push('<span class="w287team">' + E((T.short && T.short[t.department]) || t.department) + '</span>');
    if (t.flag) f.push('<span class="w287flag" title="' + E(t.reason_note || 'Needs an executive decision') + '">' + svg('flag') + 'Exec</span>');
    if (t.confidential) f.push('<span class="w116tag conf">confidential</span>');
    if (window.w132Back && window.w132Back(t)) f.push('<span class="w116tag bank" title="Waited in the bank; back on the board for two weeks now">back from the bank</span>');
    if (comments(t)) f.push('<span class="w287cm" title="Comments">' + svg('cmt') + comments(t) + '</span>');
    if (!t.done) (b === 'Open' ? tail : f).push(stPill(t, b === 'Open' ? 'w287open' : ''));
    return f.concat(tail).join('');
  }
  function rowHtml(t, ctx) {
    var pid = effPid(t), p = pid ? projById(pid) : null, b = bucket(t), tk = !!B.fold[t.id];
    return '<div class="w116r w287r st-' + stKey(b) + (t.done ? ' done' : '') + (tk ? ' w287tk' : '') + (B.sel[t.id] ? ' sel' : '') + (ctx && ctx.lone ? ' w287or' : '') + '" data-tid="' + E(t.id) + '"' + (ctx && ctx.lone ? '' : ' draggable="true"') + ' style="--pc:' + (p ? pColor(p) : 'rgba(16,63,57,.4)') + '">' +
      (ctx && ctx.lone ? '' : '<span class="w116chk"><input type="checkbox" data-act="sel" aria-label="Select this task"' + (B.sel[t.id] ? ' checked' : '') + ' /></span>') +
      '<span class="w116done" data-act="toggle" role="checkbox" tabindex="0" aria-checked="' + (t.done || tk ? 'true' : 'false') + '" aria-label="' + E((t.done ? 'Reopen ' : 'Complete ') + t.title) + '" title="' + (t.done ? 'Reopen' : 'Complete') + '"><i></i>' + svg('check', 'w287ck') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="w116t"><span class="ttl">' + E(t.title) + '</span><span class="w287f">' + factsHtml(t, ctx) + '</span></span></div>';
  }
  window.w116RowHtml = function (t, ctx) { return rowHtml(t, Object.assign({ lone: true }, ctx || {})); };
  function qaHtml(gkey, hint) {
    return '<div class="w116qa" data-qa="' + E(gkey) + '" style="display:none"><span class="w287qc" aria-hidden="true"></span><input type="text" placeholder="New task" aria-label="New task title" /><small>' + E(hint ? 'Enter to save, Esc to stop. ' + hint : 'Enter to save, Esc to stop.') + '</small></div>';
  }
  function groupHtml(g) {
    var open = g.rows.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; }).length;
    var closed = B.st.closed && B.st.closed[g.key];
    var noAdd = B.st.list === 'completed' || g.key === 'd1';
    var pre = []; if (g.pre.project && B.st.group !== 'project') { var gp = projById(g.pre.project); if (gp) pre.push('in ' + E(gp.name)); } if (g.pre.status) pre.push('status ' + g.pre.status); if (g.pre.owner) pre.push('owner ' + E(g.pre.owner)); if (g.pre.due) pre.push('due ' + g.pre.due);
    var lp = listPre(); if (lp.flag) pre.push('flagged');
    return '<section class="w116g w287g' + (closed ? ' closed' : '') + '" data-g="' + E(g.key) + '"' + (g.color ? ' style="--gc:' + g.color + '"' : '') + '>' +
      '<div class="w116gh" data-act="fold" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-expanded="' + (closed ? 'false' : 'true') + '"><span class="chev">' + svg('chev') + '</span>' +
      (g.color ? '<i class="w287gdot"></i>' : '') + '<b>' + E(g.name) + '</b>' + (open ? '<span class="n">' + open + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116rows">' + (g.rows.length ? g.rows.map(function (t) { return rowHtml(t); }).join('') : (B.model && !B.model.emptyList ? '<div class="w116empty2">Nothing here' + (B.st.q || B.st.owner || B.st.status || B.st.due || B.st.mine ? ' with these filters' : '') + '.</div>' : '')) +
      (noAdd ? '' : '<div class="w287add" data-act="qa" data-g="' + E(g.key) + '" role="button" tabindex="0"><span class="w287plus">' + svg('plus') + '</span>New task</div>' + qaHtml(g.key, pre.join(', '))) + '</div></section>';
  }
  function kanbanHtml(rows) {
    return '<div class="w116kb">' + STATUSES.map(function (b) {
      var list = rows.filter(function (t) { return bucket(t) === b; });
      return '<div class="w116kc" data-kst="' + E(b) + '"><div class="w116kch" style="color:' + STCOL[b][1] + '">' + E(b) + '<span class="n">' + list.length + '</span></div>' +
        list.map(function (t) {
          var pid = effPid(t), p = pid ? projById(pid) : null; var du = dueInfo(t); var as = assignees(t);
          return '<div class="w116kd" data-tid="' + E(t.id) + '" draggable="true" style="--pc:' + (p ? pColor(p) : 'transparent') + '"><div class="ttl">' + E(t.title) + '</div><div class="ft">' +
            (p ? '<span class="w287ptag"><i style="background:' + pColor(p) + '"></i><span>' + E(p.name) + '</span></span>' : '<span></span>') +
            '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px">' + (t.flag ? '<span class="w116tag flag">exec</span>' : '') + (du.txt ? '<span class="w116due ' + du.cls + '" style="min-width:0">' + du.txt + '</span>' : '') +
            (t.owner ? av({ name: t.owner, avatar_url: ((DB_MEMBERS || []).filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name) === lower(t.owner); })[0] || {}).avatar_url }, 20) : '') + as.filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name) !== lower(t.owner); }).slice(0, 2).map(function (m) { return av(m, 20); }).join('') + '</span></div></div>';
        }).join('') +
        '<span class="add" data-act="qa" data-g="s' + E(b) + '" style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';cursor:pointer;opacity:.7">+ Add</span>' + qaHtml('s' + b, 'status: ' + b) + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function calHtml(rows) {
    var m0 = B.calMonth || new Date();
    var m = new Date(m0.getFullYear() + '-' + String(m0.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-01T12:00:00'); B.calMonth = m;
    var first = new Date(m.toISOString().slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00'); first.setDate(1 - ((m.getDay() + 6) % 7));
    var tdy = today();
    var byDay = {}; rows.forEach(function (t) { if (t.due_date) (byDay[t.due_date] = byDay[t.due_date] || []).push(t); });
    var cells = '';
    for (var i = 0; i < 42; i++) {
      var d = new Date(first.toISOString().slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00'); d.setDate(first.getDate() + i);
      var k = d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
      var list = byDay[k] || [];
      cells += '<div class="w116cd' + (d.getMonth() !== m.getMonth() ? ' other' : '') + (k === tdy ? ' today' : '') + '" data-day="' + k + '"><div class="d">' + d.getDate() + '</div>' +
        list.slice(0, 3).map(function (t) { return '<span class="w116cp' + (t.done ? ' done' : (t.due_date < tdy ? ' over' : '')) + '" data-tid="' + E(t.id) + '" draggable="true" title="' + E(t.title) + '">' + E(t.title) + '</span>'; }).join('') +
        (list.length > 3 ? '<span class="more" data-act="more" data-day="' + k + '">+' + (list.length - 3) + ' more</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    }
    return '<div class="w116cal"><div class="w116calh"><button data-act="cal" data-n="-1">&#8249;</button><b>' + E(m.toLocaleDateString([], { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })) + '</b><button data-act="cal" data-n="1">&#8250;</button><button data-act="cal" data-n="0" style="margin-left:auto">Today</button></div>' +
      '<div class="w116calg">' + ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'].map(function (x) { return '<div class="dow">' + x + '</div>'; }).join('') + cells + '</div>' +
      '<p class="w116note" style="margin:8px 4px 0">Drag a task onto another day to change its due date. Tasks without a due date are not shown here.</p></div>';
  }
  function toolbarHtml(rows) {
    var st = B.st, s = B.scope;
    /* wave287: grouping by workstream is gone (projects replace it); the filters fold behind one Filters button */
    var groups = [['project', 'Project'], ['status', 'Status'], ['assignee', 'Owner'], ['due', 'Due date'], ['none', 'No grouping']];
    if (s.dept === 'fundraising' && !s.board) groups.push(['board', 'Sub-team']);
    var ms = members(s.dept);
    var m0 = B.model || model();
    var projs = m0.ordered.map(function (o) { return o.p; }).concat(m0.other);
    var nSel = Object.keys(B.sel).length;
    var nF = activeFilters().length;
